## י (id: BDB03727)

י, *Yôdh,* tenth letter; used as numeral 10 in postBHeb.; יא or א“י \= 11; יב, ב“י \= 12, etc.; 14 and 15, however, are not ה“י, ו“י, which might stand for abbrev. of יהוה, but ו“ט, ז“ט, v. ט, and Ges§ 5\. 4\. R. 3 a.

* **Associated Articles:** ט (ID: `BDB03610@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## יָאַב (id: BDB03728)

\[Str2968] † \[יָאַב] **vb.** long, desire (Aram. ܝܐܶܒ, and esp. Ethpa. and deriv.)—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 S. לְמִצְוֺתֶיְ יָאָֽבְתִּי [Psalm 119:131](https://ref.ly/Ps119:131), late Aramaism.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 119:131


## יָאָה (id: BDB03729)

\[Str2969] † \[יָאָה] **vb.** befit, be befitting (NH Targum יָאֵי, יָאֶה adj. *worthy, fitting, fine;* יָאוּת adv. *right, well;* Ph. יא *fair;* Syr. ܝܳܐܷܐ adj. for נאוה [Psalm 33:1](https://ref.ly/Ps33:1), πρέπει [Mt 3:15](https://ref.ly/Matt3:15))—**Qal** *Pf.* לְ יָאָתָה [Je 10:7](https://ref.ly/Jer10:7), *for thee it* (i.e. fear) *is befitting,* late Aramaism.

> \[Str2975] יְאוֺר v. יְאֹר. p. 384

> \[Str2970] יַאֲזנְיָ֫הוּ, יַאֲזַנְיָה v. sub אזן p. 24:b supr.

> \[Str2971–72] יָאִיר, יָאִירִי v. sub אור p. 22:b supr.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:1; Jeremiah 10:7; Matthew 3:15


## I יָאַל (id: BDB03733)

\[Str2973] † I. \[יָאַל] **vb.** be foolish (cf. I. אול)—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. נוֺאֲלוּ [Is 19:3](https://ref.ly/Isa19:3), [Je 5:4](https://ref.ly/Jer5:4); וְנֹאָלוּ [Je 50:36](https://ref.ly/Jer50:36); 1 pl. נוֺאַלְנוּ [Nu 12:11](https://ref.ly/Num12:11); —*do* or *act foolishly:*—**1\.** *shew wicked folly* \= sin חַטָּאת אֲשֶׁר נוֺאַלְנוּ וַאֲשֶׁר חָטָאנוּ [Nu 12:11](https://ref.ly/Num12:11) (J); in [Je 5:4](https://ref.ly/Jer5:4) an exhibition of this folly is ascribed to ignorance. **2\.** *become fools,* lacking insight and judgment: נוֺאֲלוּ שְרֵי צֹעַן [Is 19:13](https://ref.ly/Isa19:13) *the princes of Zoan have become fools* (‖נִשְּׁאוּ שָרֵי נֹף, and, in v:[11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11) אֱוִילִים שָרֵי צֹעַן, etc.); חֶדֶב אֶל־הַבַּדִּים וְנֹאָלוּ [Je 50:36](https://ref.ly/Jer50:36) *a sword is against the praters, and they shall become fools*—be shewn up as such (‖ : חָֽתּוּ).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 19:3; Isaiah 19:11; Isaiah 19:13; Jeremiah 50:36; Jeremiah 5:4; Numbers 12:11


## II יָאַל (id: BDB03734)

\[Str2974] † II. \[יָאַל] **vb.** only **Hiph.** shew willingness, be pleased, determine, undertake to do anything (and do it) (v. Ki Thes; Ar. وَأَلَ is *take refuge with, escape, hasten to* a place; cf. II. אול *be in front of,* NöMBAK 1880\. 775; Sab. ואל in n.pr. מואלת DHMEpigr. Denkm. 53; perh. As *âlû, accept,* LyonSargontexte 72\)—**Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הוֺאִיל [Ho 5:11](https://ref.ly/Hos5:11) \+ 2 t.; 2 ms. הוֺאַלְתָּ [1 Ch 17:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:27); 1 S. הוֺאַלְתִּי [Gn 18:27](https://ref.ly/Gen18:27), [18:31](https://ref.ly/Gen18:31); 1 pl. הוֺאַלְנוּ [Jos 7:7](https://ref.ly/Josh7:7); *Impf.* וְיֹאֵל [Jb 6:9](https://ref.ly/Job6:9) (juss.); וַיּ֫וֹאֶל [Ex 2:21](https://ref.ly/Exod2:21) \+ 4 t.; וַיֹאֶל [1 S 17:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:39) (but v. infr.); *Imv.* הוֺאֵל [2 S 7:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:29), [2 K 5:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:23); הוֺאֶל־נָא Ju [19:6](https://ref.ly/Judg19:6); ה֫וֺאֶל נָא [2 K 6:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:3); הוֺאִ֫ילוּ [Jb 6:28](https://ref.ly/Job6:28); —**1\.** *shew willingness* to do anything, *accept an invitation, acquiesce*, sq. inf. וַי֫וֺאֶל משֶׁה לָשֶׁבֶת אֶת־הָאִישׁ [Ex 2:21](https://ref.ly/Exod2:21) (JE) *and Moses was willing to dwell with the man* (and did so), \= Ju [17:11](https://ref.ly/Judg17:11) (of Levite); elsewh. foll. by vb. fin.; וְלוּ הוֺאַלְנוּ וַנֵּשֶׁב [Jos 7:7](https://ref.ly/Josh7:7) *and would that we had been willing and had stayed;* esp. imv., sq. imv.: הוֺאֶל־נָא וְלִין Ju [19:6](https://ref.ly/Judg19:6) *be willing and spend the night* (kindly accept my invitation) v. Ges§ 110\. 2 a, R 2; 120\. 2 a, [2 K 6:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:3) and (no וְ) [5:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:23); הוֺאִילוּ פְַנוּ־בִי [Jb 6:28](https://ref.ly/Job6:28) i.e. be good enough to look upon me. **2\.** more actively, voluntarily *undertake* to do anything (and do it), sq. inf.: [Gn 18:27](https://ref.ly/Gen18:27) *I have undertaken to speak* \= v:[31](https://ref.ly/Gen18:31) (both J); so [1 S 17:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:39) MT, but rd. וַיֵּלֶא, *and he laboured vainly*, for וַיֹאֶל, so LXX, GeiUrschrift, 377 We Dr (cf. also vb לאֹ אוּכַל לָלֶכֶת בָּאֵלֶּה); sq. vb. fin. הוֺאִיל משֶׁה בֵּאֵר [Dt 1:5](https://ref.ly/Deut1:5) i.e. Moses *took upon himself* to expound the law. **3\.** more actively still, *be pleased, determine* on one's own responsibility, *resolve*, sq. inf.: [Jos 17:12](https://ref.ly/Josh17:12) (JE) \= Ju [1:27](https://ref.ly/Judg1:27) but the Canaanites *were determined* (shewed a determination) to remain in this territory (\= *persisted in dwelling there*); Ju [1:35](https://ref.ly/Judg1:35) *the Amorites persisted in dwelling* in Har\-Ḥeres, etc.; sq. vb. fin., of irreligious action, הואיל הָלַךְ [Ho 5:11](https://ref.ly/Hos5:11) Ephraim *persisted, he walked* after vanity (rd. שׁוא for צו, v. Che); elsewh. only of the divine *good pleasure:* sq. inf. [1 S 12:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:22) ´י *hath been pleased to make you a people for himself;* [1 Ch 17:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:27); sq. vb. fin. הוֺאֵל וּבָרֵךְ [2 S 7:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:29) *be pleased and bless* the house of thy servant; וְיֹאֵל אֱלוֺהַּ וִידַכְּאֵנִי [Jb 6:9](https://ref.ly/Job6:9) *and that Eloah would be pleased and crush me!* (v. Ges§ 120, 2 a DaSynt. § 83 b).

> \[Str2975] † יְאֹר, יְאוֺר **n.m.**:[Ex 7:21](https://ref.ly/Exod7:21) stream of the Nile, stream, canal (Egypt. loan\-word \= Egypt. *’iotr, ’io’r, watercourse*, Copt. *eioor, ior;* also *’iotr‘o, ’io’r‘o*, Copt. *eiero, iaro, Nile*, SteindorffBAs i. 612; in As. *ia’uru, stream, ’Iaru’û, river Nile*, Idib., Hptib. 171 Jägerib 466\)—abs. הַיְאֹר [Gn 41:1](https://ref.ly/Gen41:1) \+ 37 t.; הַיְאוֺר [Is 19:8](https://ref.ly/Isa19:8); הַיְאֹ֫רָה [Ex 1:22](https://ref.ly/Exod1:22); יְאוֺר [Is 19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7) \+ 2 t.; כָּאֹר (err. for כַּיְאֹר) [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8); cstr. כִּיאֹר [Am 9:5](https://ref.ly/Amos9:5); כִּיאוֺר [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8); sf. יְאֹרִי Ez [29:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:3) b (Co יאר, cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:9) and SyrVer Targum); pl. יְאֹרִים [Ex 8:1](https://ref.ly/Exod8:1) \+ 4 t.; cstr. יְאֹרֵי [Is 7:18](https://ref.ly/Isa7:18) \+ 2 t.; יְאוֺרֵי [2 K 19:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:24); sf. יְאֹרֶיְ Ez [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4), [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4), [29:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:5) \+ v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4) (del. LXX Co) \+ [29:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:10) (Co יְאֹרְ); יְאֹרָיו Ez [29:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:3); יְאֹדֵיהֶם [Ex 7:19](https://ref.ly/Exod7:19) [Psalm 78:44](https://ref.ly/Ps78:44); —**1\.** *stream* of the Nile, *river Nile*, usu. c. art. [Gn 41:1](https://ref.ly/Gen41:1), [41:2](https://ref.ly/Gen41:2), [41:3](https://ref.ly/Gen41:3), [41:3](https://ref.ly/Gen41:3), [41:18](https://ref.ly/Gen41:18), [Ex 1:22](https://ref.ly/Exod1:22), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Exod2:3), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Exod2:5), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Exod2:5), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Exod4:9), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Exod4:9), [7:15](https://ref.ly/Exod7:15), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17), [7:18](https://ref.ly/Exod7:18), [7:18](https://ref.ly/Exod7:18), [7:18](https://ref.ly/Exod7:18), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Exod7:20), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Exod7:20), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Exod7:21), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Exod7:21), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Exod7:21), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Exod7:24), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Exod7:24), [7:28](https://ref.ly/Exod7:28), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Exod8:5), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Exod8:7), [17:5](https://ref.ly/Exod17:5) (all JE), [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8), [9:5](https://ref.ly/Amos9:5), [Is 19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Isa19:8), [23:3](https://ref.ly/Isa23:3) (‖שִׁחֹר), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Isa23:10), [Je 46:7](https://ref.ly/Jer46:7), [46:8](https://ref.ly/Jer46:8) (sim. of Egypt. invasion), Ez [29:3b](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:3) (rd. יאר, v. supr.), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:9), [Zc 10:11](https://ref.ly/Zech10:11); more fully יְאֹר מִצְרַיִם [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8), [9:5](https://ref.ly/Amos9:5). **2\.** pl.: **a.** *Nile\-arms, Nile\-canals*, יְאֹרֵי מִצְרַים [Is 7:18](https://ref.ly/Isa7:18); יְאֹרֵי מָצוֺר [19:6](https://ref.ly/Isa19:6) (‖נְהָרוֺת), [37:25](https://ref.ly/Isa37:25) \= [2 K 19:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:24); v. also [Na 3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8), [Ex 7:19](https://ref.ly/Exod7:19), [8:1](https://ref.ly/Exod8:1) (both P; ‖נְהָרֹת, אֲגַמִּים, etc.), [Psalm 78:44](https://ref.ly/Ps78:44) (‖נֹוְלִים), Ez [29:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:3), [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4), [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4), [29:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:5), [29:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:10) \+ v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4) MT (but del. LXX Co), [30:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:12). **b.** *watercourses* in gen., יְאֹרִים רַחֲבֵי יָדָיִם [Is 33:21](https://ref.ly/Isa33:21) (‖נְהָרִים; cf. NH יְאוֺר, late Aram. יְאוֺרָא). **3\.** *shafts*, made in mining [Jb 28:10](https://ref.ly/Job28:10) (בַּצּוּרוֺת). **4\.** יְאֹר, sg., of *Tigris*, [Dn 12:5](https://ref.ly/Dan12:5), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Dan12:5), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Dan12:6), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7) (cf. [10:4](https://ref.ly/Dan10:4)).

## יָאַשׁ (id: BDB03736)

\[Str2976] † \[יָאַשׁ] **vb.** despair (not in **Qal**) (NH Hithp. *despair of*, יֵאוּשׁ *desperation;* Aram. Pa. יָאֵשׁ, etc., *make despair* (so Targum [Ec 2:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:20)); Ar. يَيءِسَ *despair*)—**Niph.** *Pf.* וְנוֺאַש consec. [1 S 27:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:1); *Pt.* נוֺאָשׁ [Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25) \+ 3 t.;—*despair*, מִמֶּנִּי שָׁאוּל ´וְנ לְבַקְּשֵׁנִי [1 S 27:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:1) *and Saul shall despair of me, to seek me;* pt. *desperate, despairing*, of Job, [Jb 6:26](https://ref.ly/Job6:26); elsewh. נוֺאָשׁ (foll. אמר) *desperate! \= there is no hope!* [Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Jer18:12), [Is 57:10](https://ref.ly/Isa57:10). **Pi.** *Inf.* לְיַאֵשׁ אֶת־לִבִּי עַל כָּל־הֶעָמָל [Ec 2:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:20) *to make my heart despair, concerning all the toil.*

> \[Str2977] יאֹשִׁיָּ֫הוּ, יאֹשִׁיָּה v. sub אשׁה p. 78:b supr.

> \[Str2979] † יְאָֽתְרַי **n.pr.m.** an ancestor of Asaph [1 Ch 6:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:6) appar. \= אֶתְנִי v:[26](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:26).

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Samuel 27:1; Jeremiah 18:12; 1 Chronicles 6:26; Jeremiah 2:25; 1 Chronicles 6:6; Ecclesiastes 2:20; Isaiah 57:10; Job 6:26


## יָבַב (id: BDB03739)

\[Str2980] † \[יָבַב] **vb.** only **Pi.** cry shrilly (NH Pi. *lament;* Aram. ܝܰܒܶܒ, יַבֵּב *sound* clarions, *exult;* Eth. የበበ፡ esp. I. 2 )—**Pi.** *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתְּיַבֵּב Ju [5:28](https://ref.ly/Judg5:28) through the windows she looked and *shrilly cried.*

> \[Str3103] † יוֺבָב **n.pr.m.** (Sab. n.pr. יהיבב GlaserSkizze ii. 303 ff.)—**1\.** ‘son’ of Yoḳtân [Gn 10:29](https://ref.ly/Gen10:29) \= [1 Ch 1:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:23), LXX Ιωβαβ. **2\.** a king in Edom [Gn 36:33](https://ref.ly/Gen36:33), [36:34](https://ref.ly/Gen36:34) \= [1 Ch 1:44](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:44); [1:45](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:45), LXX Ιω(α)βαβ. **3\.** king of Madon (Northern Canaan) [Jos 11:1](https://ref.ly/Josh11:1), LXX Ιω(β)αβ. **4\.** Benjamites: **a.** [1 Ch 8:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:9), LXX Ιωβαβ. **b.** [1 Ch 8:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:18), LXX Ιω(β)αβ.

> \[Str2982–83] יְבוּס, יְבוּסִי, יְבֻסִי v. sub ביס p. 101:a supr.

> \[Str2984] יִבְחָר v. sub בחר p. 104:b supr.

> \[Str2985] יָבִין v. sub בין p. 108:a supr.

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 5:28; Genesis 36:34; 1 Chronicles 1:23; 1 Chronicles 8:9; 1 Chronicles 1:45; 1 Chronicles 1:44; 1 Chronicles 8:18; Joshua 11:1; Genesis 10:29; Genesis 36:33


## יָבַל (id: BDB03744)

\[Str2986] † \[יָבַל] **vb. Hiph.** conduct, bear along, esp. in *procession* (Aram. יַבֵּל, אוֺבִיל *id.*, ܝܰܒܶܠ Pa. *lead along*, also Ethpa.; Aph. *lead, conduct;* Zinj. יבל *id.*, DHMSendsch. Gloss.; As. *abâlu* (ובל), *bring, conduct*, COTGloss. DlPr 122 ff.; Ar. وَبَلَ is *run vehemently* (of horse), *pursue sharply, pour* *down rain*, وَبْلٌ *violent rain*)—**Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יוֺבִילֵנִי [Psalm 60:11](https://ref.ly/Ps60:11), [108:11](https://ref.ly/Ps108:11); 1 s. sf. אוֺבִילֵם [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9); 3 mpl. יוֺבִילוּ [Psalm 68:30](https://ref.ly/Ps68:30); יֹבִילוּ [76:12](https://ref.ly/Ps76:12) (juss.); יוֺבִלוּן [Zp 3:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:10); sf. יֹבִלוּהָ [Is 23:7](https://ref.ly/Isa23:7); —**1\.** *bear along* offerings, c. acc. rei, [Zp 3:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:10); c. ל Pers. [Psalm 68:30](https://ref.ly/Ps68:30), [76:12](https://ref.ly/Ps76:12). **2\.** *carry away*, only רַגְלֶיהָ מֵרָחוֺק לָגוּר ´י [Is 23:7](https://ref.ly/Isa23:7) *her feet used to carry her far away to sojourn* (of Tyre as colonizing and trading city). **3\.** *lead, conduct*, c. obj. as sf., of returning exiles [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9); triumphant army, sq. acc. loc., [Psalm 60:11](https://ref.ly/Ps60:11) \= [108:11](https://ref.ly/Ps108:11). **Hoph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. (־)יוּבַל [Is 18:7](https://ref.ly/Isa18:7), [Je 11:19](https://ref.ly/Jer11:19); יוּבָל [Ho 12:2](https://ref.ly/Hos12:2) \+ 3 t.; 3 fs. תּוּבַל [Psalm 45:15](https://ref.ly/Ps45:15); 1 s. אוּבָל [Jb 10:19](https://ref.ly/Job10:19); 3 mpl. יוּבָלוּ [Jb 21:30](https://ref.ly/Job21:30); 3 fpl. תּוּבַלְנָה [Psalm 45:16](https://ref.ly/Ps45:16); 2 mpl. תּוּבָלָוּן [Is 55:12](https://ref.ly/Isa55:12); —**1\.** *be borne along:* of things, sq. לְ, a gift [Is 18:7](https://ref.ly/Isa18:7); idol [Ho 10:6](https://ref.ly/Hos10:6); oil [12:2](https://ref.ly/Hos12:2). **2\.** *be borne* to the grave: מִבֶּטֶן לַקֶּבֶר אוּבָל [Jb 10:19](https://ref.ly/Job10:19); לִקְבָרוֺת יוּבָל [21:32](https://ref.ly/Job21:32). **3\.** *be led, conducted*, sq. לְ, [Psalm 45:15](https://ref.ly/Ps45:15), [Je 11:19](https://ref.ly/Jer11:19), [Is 53:7](https://ref.ly/Isa53:7); abs. [Psalm 45:16](https://ref.ly/Ps45:16); *be led* forth (from captivity in Babylon) [Is 55:12](https://ref.ly/Isa55:12), abs. (‖יצא); the wicked (for judgment) abs. [Jb 21:30](https://ref.ly/Job21:30).

> \[Str2988] † I. \[יָבָל] **n.\[m.]** watercourse, stream (prop. *conduit*), as irrigating;—only pl. cstr. מָיִם(־)יִבְלֵי [Is 30:25](https://ref.ly/Isa30:25) (‖פְּלָגִים), [44:4](https://ref.ly/Isa44:4).

> \[Str3105] † I. יוּבַל **n.\[m.]** stream, [Je 17:8](https://ref.ly/Jer17:8) (‖מַיִם).

> \[Str3106] † II. יוּבָל **n.pr.m.** son of Lamech by Adah, and inventor of musical instr. [Gn 4:21](https://ref.ly/Gen4:21) (J); LXX Ιωβαλ.

[Psalm 78:46](https://ref.ly/Ps78:46) (‖יְגִיעַ); esp. וְנָתְֽנָה הָאָרֶץ יְבוּלָהּ [Lv 26:4](https://ref.ly/Lev26:4) *the land shall yield its produce*, so v:[20](https://ref.ly/Lev26:20), Ez [34:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:27), [Zc 8:2](https://ref.ly/Zech8:2) (all ‖פְּרִי), [Psalm 67:7](https://ref.ly/Ps67:7), [85:13](https://ref.ly/Ps85:13); also, subj. הָאֲדָמָה [Dt 11:17](https://ref.ly/Deut11:17); of grapes only וְאֵין יְבוּל בַּגְּפָנִים Hb [3:17](https://ref.ly/Hab3:17) (‖תְּאֵנָה לאֹ־תִפְרָח and כִּחֵשׁ מַעֲשֵ־ה זַיִת); more generally יְבוּל בֵּיתוֺ [Jb 20:28](https://ref.ly/Job20:28) *the produce* (acquired possessions) *of his house.*

## יבם (id: BDB03757)

יבם (appar. √ of foll.; meaning dubious).

> \[Str2993] † \[יָבָם] **n.m.** husband's brother (NH *id.;* Targum יַבְמָא; v. LagM ii. 78\)—only sf. יְבֶמִי [Dt 25:7](https://ref.ly/Deut25:7), יְבָמָהּ [25:5](https://ref.ly/Deut25:5), in law of levirate marriage (cf. [Gn 38:8](https://ref.ly/Gen38:8); v. also Dr:[Dt 25:5–10](https://ref.ly/Deut25:5-Deut25:10)).

> \[Str2994] † \[יְבֵ֫מֶת] **n.f.** sister\-in\-law (NH יְבָמָה, Targum יְבַמְתָּא, יְבִּימְתָּא; Syr. ܝܺܒܰܡܬܳܐ)—only sf. יְבִמְתּו [Dt 25:7](https://ref.ly/Deut25:7), [25:7](https://ref.ly/Deut25:7), [25:9](https://ref.ly/Deut25:9) *his brother's wife (widow)*, (cf. [Gn 38:8](https://ref.ly/Gen38:8)) in levirate law; יְבִמְתֵּח [Ru 1:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:15), [1:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:15) *thy husband's brother's wife.*

> \[Str2992] † \[יָבַם] **vb. denom.,** only **Pi.** *do the duty of* יָבָם *to* a brother's widow; *Pf.* וְיִבְּמָהּ consec. [Dt 25:5](https://ref.ly/Deut25:5) *and shall do a brother\-in\-law's office to her; Imv.* יַבֵּם אֹתָהּ [Gn 38:8](https://ref.ly/Gen38:8) (i.e. אֵשֶׁת אָחִיְ va); *Inf. sf.* לאֹ אָבָה יַבְּמִי [Dt 25:7](https://ref.ly/Deut25:7) (on nominal sf. as obj. of inf., v. Ges§ 115 R. 2 ad fin.).

> \[Str2999] יַבֹּק v. sub בקק p. 132:b supr.

> \[Str3000] יְבֶרֶכְיָ֫הוּ v. sub ברך p. 140:a supr.

## I יָבֵשׁ (id: BDB03765)

\[Str3001] † I. יָבֵשׁ **vb.** be dry, dried up, withered (NH *id.* (rare), Targum יְבֵישׁ, chiefly Pa. Ithp., syr. ܝܒܡ; Sab. Palm. deriv.; Ar. يَبِسَ; Eth. የብሰ፡) md; **Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. יָבֵשׁ [Jos 9:5](https://ref.ly/Josh9:5) \+ 8 t.; וְיָבֵשׁ consec. [Is 19:5](https://ref.ly/Isa19:5) \+ 4 t.; 3 fs. יָבְֽשָׁה [Gn 8:14](https://ref.ly/Gen8:14); 3 pl. יָבְֽשׁוּ [Je 23:10](https://ref.ly/Jer23:10) \+ 2 t.; יָבֵָשׁוּ [50:38](https://ref.ly/Jer50:38) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִבַשׁ [Is 19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7); יִיבָשׁ [Je 12:4](https://ref.ly/Jer12:4), [Jb 8:12](https://ref.ly/Job8:12); וַיִּיבַשׁ [1 K 17:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:7); וַיִּיבָשׁ [Jon 4:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:7); 3 fs. תִּיבַשׁ Ez [17:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:10); תִּיבָשׁ v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:10) \+ 3 t., etc.; *Inf. cstr.* יבשׁ(בִּ) [Is 27:11](https://ref.ly/Isa27:11); יְבשֶׁת [Gn 8:7](https://ref.ly/Gen8:7); *abs.* יָבוֺשׁ [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17); יָבשׁ Ec 17:10; —**1\.** *be dry, dried up without moisture:* **a.** of bread [Jos 9:5](https://ref.ly/Josh9:5), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Josh9:12) (JE). **b.** of ground lacking rain [Am 4:7](https://ref.ly/Amos4:7), cf. [Am 1:2](https://ref.ly/Amos1:2) (of Carmel at utterance of ´י's voice), [Je 23:10](https://ref.ly/Jer23:10). **c.** of earth after the flood [Gn 8:14](https://ref.ly/Gen8:14) (P), cf. חָרֵב **d.** of grass, herbage, trees and crops (already implied in **b.** \= *wither* [Is 15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7), [27:11](https://ref.ly/Isa27:11), [Je 12:4](https://ref.ly/Jer12:4), [Jb 8:12](https://ref.ly/Job8:12), [Jo 1:12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12), [Jon 4:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:7); in sim. of shortness of life [Psalm 90:6](https://ref.ly/Ps90:6), [102:12](https://ref.ly/Ps102:12), [129:6](https://ref.ly/Ps129:6), v. also [Is 40:7](https://ref.ly/Isa40:7), [40:8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:8) (in these two ‖נָבֵל); of heart under fig. of grass [Psalm 102:5](https://ref.ly/Ps102:5); of Judah under fig. of vine Ez [17:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:9), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:9), [17:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:10), [17:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:10), [17:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:10), cf. [19:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:12); of princes under fig. of tree [Is 40:24](https://ref.ly/Isa40:24); of roots of Ephr. under fig. of tree [Ho 9:16](https://ref.ly/Hos9:16), so of roots of wicked [Jb 18:16](https://ref.ly/Job18:16). **e.** of hand, arm, *dry up, wither*, as judgment from ´ת, [1 K 13:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:4), [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17); in distress יָבֵשׁ כַּחֶרֶשׁ כֹּחִי [Psalm 22:16](https://ref.ly/Ps22:16) *my strength is dried up like the potsherd;* of skin [La 4:8](https://ref.ly/Lam4:8), and (fig.) of bones Ez [37:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:11). **2\.** *be dried up:* of water [Gn 8:7](https://ref.ly/Gen8:7) (J), [Is 19:5](https://ref.ly/Isa19:5), [1 K 17:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:7), [Je 50:38](https://ref.ly/Jer50:38), [Jo 1:20](https://ref.ly/Joel1:20), [Jb 12:15](https://ref.ly/Job12:15), [14:11](https://ref.ly/Job14:11). **Pi.** *make dry, dry up*, only *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. וַיַּבְּשֵׁהוּ [Na 1:4](https://ref.ly/Nah1:4) *and hath made it dry* (´י, the sea); 3 fs. תְּיַבֵּשׁ [Jb 15:30](https://ref.ly/Job15:30) the flame *shall dry up* his branches (fig. of wicked); fig. תְּיַבֶּ־שָׁ\+נּרֶם [Pr 17:22](https://ref.ly/Prov17:22) a broken spirit *maketh dry* (*the*) *bones.* **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הוֺבִישׁ [Jos 2:10](https://ref.ly/Josh2:10) \+ 3 t. \+ [Jo 1:10](https://ref.ly/Joel1:10) (v. infr.); חֹבִישׁ Ez [19:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:12) \+ 2 t. Jo (v. infr.); 3 fs. הוֺבִישָׁה [Jo 1:12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12) (v. infr.); 2 ms. הוֺבַשְׁתָּ [Psalm 74:15](https://ref.ly/Ps74:15); 1 s. הוֺבִשְׁתִּי Ez [17:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:24); וְהוֺבַשְׁתִּ֫י [Je 51:36](https://ref.ly/Jer51:36); 3 pl. וְהֹבִישׁוּ consec. [Zc 10:11](https://ref.ly/Zech10:11); *Impf.* 1 s. אוֺבִישׁ [Is 42:15](https://ref.ly/Isa42:15), [42:15](https://ref.ly/Isa42:15), [44:27](https://ref.ly/Isa44:27); —*dry up, make dry:* **1\.** *dry up water*, ´י subj., [Jos 2:10](https://ref.ly/Josh2:10), [4:23](https://ref.ly/Josh4:23), [4:23](https://ref.ly/Josh4:23), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Josh5:1) (all D), [Je 51:36](https://ref.ly/Jer51:36) (‖הֶחֱרִיב), [Is 42:15](https://ref.ly/Isa42:15), [44:27](https://ref.ly/Isa44:27) (חֳרָבִי in ‖ cl.), [Psalm 74:15](https://ref.ly/Ps74:15). **2\.** *make dry, wither:* herbage, trees, crops, etc., of ´י, [Is 42:15](https://ref.ly/Isa42:15) (‖החריב); obj. Babylon under fig. of tree Ez [17:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:24); of east wind's drying up Judah's fruit Ez [19:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:12). **3\.** *exhibit dryness:* of river\-deeps laid bare [Zc 10:11](https://ref.ly/Zech10:11).—In like manner might be taken [Jo 1:10](https://ref.ly/Joel1:10), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12), [1:17](https://ref.ly/Joel1:17), if fr. יבשׁ; but the sense would be difficult, esp. in v:[10](https://ref.ly/Joel1:10), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12), and הֹבִישׁוּ v:[11](https://ref.ly/Joel1:11) must be fr. בּוֺשׁ, to which all these cases in Jo may be consistently assigned; v. בּוֺשׁ.

> \[Str3002] † II. יָבֵשׁ **adj. verb.** or **Pt.** dry, dried, so, ms. abs. [Na 1:10](https://ref.ly/Nah1:10) \+ 4 t.; fs. יְבֵשָׁה [Nu 11:6](https://ref.ly/Num11:6); mpl. יבֵשִׁים(וִ) [Nu 6:3](https://ref.ly/Num6:3); fpl. יְבֵשׁוֺת Ez [37:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:2), [37:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:4); —**1\.** *dried*, lit. only יֲנָבִיְ יְבֵשִׁיס [Nu 6:3](https://ref.ly/Num6:3) *dried grapes* (P; opp. לַתִים ´ְ); fig. נַפְשֵׁנוּ יְבֵשָׁה [11:6](https://ref.ly/Num11:6) (JE) *our soul* (i.e. our appetite) *is dried up*, viz. for want of fresh, juicy meat. **2\.** *dry*, of chaff, קַשׁ [Na 1:10](https://ref.ly/Nah1:10) in sim. of Ninevites under impending judgment; cf. in fig. of Job, [Jb 13:25](https://ref.ly/Job13:25); of tree Ez [17:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:24) (fig. of Davidic house; opp. עֵץ לָךְ), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3) (in prediction of Judah's devastation by Babylon, opp. *id.*); Is [56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3) fig. of eunuch; of the bones in Ezek.'s vision Ez [37:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:2), [37:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:4).

\[Str3013] † I. \[יָגָה] **vb.** suffer, not in **Qal** (cf. Ar. وَجَى *castravit*, وَجِىَ *be abraded* (of the foot); of horse, *have pain in the hoof*)—**Niph.** *Pt.* fpl. נוּגוֺת (on form v. Köi. 582\) [La 1:4](https://ref.ly/Lam1:4) (‖נֶאֱנָחִים, מַר) *grieved*, mpl. cstr. נוּגֵי (v. Köl.c.) [Zp 3:18](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:18) (sq. prep., cf. Ges§ 130\. 1\); of virgins of Zion [La 1:4](https://ref.ly/Lam1:4); of exiles [Zp 3:18](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:18). **Pi.** *Impf.* וַיַּגֶּה (for וַיְיַגֶּה, v. Köi. 582\. 412\) *grieve*, sq. acc. [La 3:33](https://ref.ly/Lam3:33) (‖עִנָּה). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הוֺגה [La 1:12](https://ref.ly/Lam1:12), [3:32](https://ref.ly/Lam3:32); sf. הוֺגָהּ [La 1:5](https://ref.ly/Lam1:5); *Impf.* 2 mpl. תּוֺגְיוּן [Jb 19:2](https://ref.ly/Job19:2); *Pt.* pl. sf. מוֺגַיִךְ [Is 51:23](https://ref.ly/Isa51:23); —*cause grief* or *sorrow*, abs. [La 3:32](https://ref.ly/Lam3:32) (opp. רִחַם); sq. acc. (Zion) [La 1:5](https://ref.ly/Lam1:5), cf. [Is 51:23](https://ref.ly/Isa51:23), also [La 1:12](https://ref.ly/Lam1:12) (obj. om., but אשׁר of grief \= *wherewith*); sq. נָפְשִׁי [Jb 19:2](https://ref.ly/Job19:2).—On [2 S 20:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:13) v. II. יגה.

## יגן (id: BDB03781)

יגן (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. وَجَنَ *beat* cloth (said of a fuller); in Heb. only in deriv. *wine\-press*).

> \[Str1660] † I. גַּת **n.f.** wine\-press (contracted from …גַּנְתְּ \= …יְגַּנְתְּ)—abs. גַּת Ju [6:11](https://ref.ly/Judg6:11) \+ 3 t.; גִּתּוֺת [Ne 13:15](https://ref.ly/Neh13:15); —*wine\-press*, lit. חֹבֵט חִטִּים בַגַּת Ju [6:11](https://ref.ly/Judg6:11) *beating out wheat in the wine\-press*, to hide it from Midian; the juice of the grapes was pressed out by treading, דֹּרֵךְ בְַּ\+גת [Is 63:2](https://ref.ly/Isa63:2) (in sim.), but also as acc. דֹּרְכִים גִּתּוֺת [Ne 13:15](https://ref.ly/Neh13:15); in fig. of judgment, ´גַּת דָּרַךְ אְדֹבָי לִבְתּוּלַת וגו [La 1:15](https://ref.ly/Lam1:15) *a wine\-press hath Adonay trodden for the virgin daughter of Judah;* also רְדוּ כִּי מָֽלְאָה גַּת [Jo 4:13](https://ref.ly/Joel4:13) (רדה only here with גַּת).—On the form and use of wine\-press, and Heb. synonyms, v. SmithDB (art. *wine\-press*) BenzArch. 212 f. RobBR iii, 137 SchickZPV x, 1887, 146, 150, and Pl. v. vii. AnderlindZPV xi, 1888, 166 f.

> \[Str1661] † II. גַּת **n.pr.loc.** (*wine\-press*)—Philistine city LXX Γεθ, Vulgate *Geth*, JosAnt. vi. 12, 2 etc., Γιττα (ed. Niese), exact site unknown (v. GASmGeog. 194 ff.), named with Gaza and Ashdod [Jos 11:22](https://ref.ly/Josh11:22) as home of remaining ‘Anaḳim; named with Ashdod, Gaza, Ashḳelon and ‘Eḳron [1 S 6:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:17); with Ashḳelon [1 S 1:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:20); with Ashḳelon, yabne and Ashdod [2 Ch 26:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:6); מֵעֶקְרוֺן וְעַד־גַּת [1 S 7:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:14), cf. [17:52](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:52) b and also v:[52](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:52) a (rd. גַּת for גַּיְא LXX We Dr); called גַּח פְּלִשְׁתִּים [Am 6:2](https://ref.ly/Amos6:2); built up by Rehoboam, acc. to [2 Ch 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:8); taken by Hazael [2 K 12:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:18); also [1 S 5:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:8), [17:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:4), [17:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:23) (of Goliath), cf. [2 S 21:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:20), [21:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:22) \= [1 Ch 20:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:6), [20:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:8), [1 S 21:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:11), [21:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:13), [27:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:2), [27:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:3), [27:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:4), [27:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:11), [2 S 15:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:18), [1 K 2:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:39), [2:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:39), [2:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:40), [2:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:41), [Psalm 56:1](https://ref.ly/Ps56:1), [Mi 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mic1:10), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Mic1:14), [1 Ch 7:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:21), [8:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:13), also [18:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:1) (but ‖מֶתֶג אַמָּה [2 S 8:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:1); on change of this into reading in Ch v. We); c. ה loc., גַּ֫חָה [1 K 2:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:40).

> \[Str1662] † גַּת הַחֵ֫פֶר [2 K 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:25) (*wine\-press of digging* home of Jonah, proph.; גִּתָּ֭הַ חֵ֫פֶר [Jos 19:13](https://ref.ly/Josh19:13), perh. mod. *El\-Meshhed*, c. 3 m. E. of N. fr. Nazareth, RobBR ii. 350 BDPal 252\.

> \[Str1665] † גִּתִּית **f.** of foregoing (si vera 1\.) only in phr. עַל־הַגִּתִּית in three Psalm titles: [Psalm 8:1](https://ref.ly/Ps8:1), [81:1](https://ref.ly/Ps81:1), [84:1](https://ref.ly/Ps84:1); *upon the Gittite* (*lyre*) so Targum,*to the Gittite* (*melody*) Ew Ol De, or either of these Hup Pe ; LXX Vulgate הַגִּתֹּת *wine\-presses,* whence Bae al. *at the wine\-presses,* i.e. (Bae) a song for the feast of booths.

(אשׁר) [47:15](https://ref.ly/Isa47:15); sq. בְּ instr. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Isa47:12); abs. [49:4](https://ref.ly/Isa49:4) (לְרִיק, ‖וְהֶבֶל כֹּחִי כִלֵּיתִי), cf. [65:23](https://ref.ly/Isa65:23), [Je 51:58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58) (‖יעף), Hb [2:13](https://ref.ly/Hab2:13) (‖ ), [La 5:5](https://ref.ly/Lam5:5) (‖לאֹ־הוּנַּת־לָנוּ), [Jb 9:29](https://ref.ly/Job9:29) sq. infr. הַיְמָנִית\[ם]יד [Ex 29:20](https://ref.ly/Exod29:20), [Lv 8:23](https://ref.ly/Lev8:23), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Lev8:24), [14:14](https://ref.ly/Lev14:14), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Lev14:17), [14:25](https://ref.ly/Lev14:25), [14:28](https://ref.ly/Lev14:28) (all P); a left\-handed man is אִטֵּר יַד־יְמִינוֺ Ju [3:15](https://ref.ly/Judg3:15), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Judg20:16) (V. אטר); *left hand* (וֺ)יַד שְמאֹל [3:21](https://ref.ly/Judg3:21), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Judg7:20), Ez [39:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:8); both hands of one Pers. are denoted by du., יָדַיִם [Gn 27:22](https://ref.ly/Gen27:22), [2 K 3:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:11) \+ oft.; occasionally \+ numeral, שְׁתֵּי יָדָי [Dt 9:15](https://ref.ly/Deut9:15), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Deut9:17), so [Lv 16:21](https://ref.ly/Lev16:21) (P); du. also of hands of several persons [Gn 5:29](https://ref.ly/Gen5:29), [Ex 29:10](https://ref.ly/Exod29:10), [29:15](https://ref.ly/Exod29:15), [29:19](https://ref.ly/Exod29:19), [Dt 31:29](https://ref.ly/Deut31:29), [2 K 11:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:16) \= [2 Ch 23:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:15), Ez [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12) \+ ; yet sts. sg. of hand of several persons [Gn 19:16](https://ref.ly/Gen19:16), [Ex 29:9](https://ref.ly/Exod29:9), [Lv 8:24](https://ref.ly/Lev8:24), [Dt 1:25](https://ref.ly/Deut1:25), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Deut17:7), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Deut17:7), Ju [7:20](https://ref.ly/Judg7:20), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Judg7:20) \+ **b.** שְׁתֵּי כַּפּוֺת ידיו [1 S 5:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:4) *the two palms of his hands;* כַּפּוֺת הידים [2 K 9:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:35), [Dn 10:10](https://ref.ly/Dan10:10); אַצִּילוֺת יָדֶיְ *armpits* [Je 38:12](https://ref.ly/Jer38:12), Ez [13:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:18); בֹּהֶן יָדָיו [Ex 29:20](https://ref.ly/Exod29:20) (P) \= *his thumbs* (opp. בֹּהֶן רַגְלָם); בְּהֹנוֺת (יו)ורגל (יו)יד Ju [1:6](https://ref.ly/Judg1:6), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Judg1:7); bracelets were worn on *hands,* i.e. wrists [Gn 24:22](https://ref.ly/Gen24:22), [24:30](https://ref.ly/Gen24:30), [24:47](https://ref.ly/Gen24:47) (J), cf. thread bound on *hand* of Zerach [38:28](https://ref.ly/Gen38:28), [38:28](https://ref.ly/Gen38:28), [38:29](https://ref.ly/Gen38:29), [38:30](https://ref.ly/Gen38:30) (J), and cords on *hands* of Samson Ju [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14); the ring was worn on *hand,* i.e. finger [Gn 41:42](https://ref.ly/Gen41:42) (E), [Est 3:10](https://ref.ly/Esth3:10). **c.**as to *hands* in use, note זְרֹעַי ידיו [Gn 49:24](https://ref.ly/Gen49:24) *arms of his hands*, i.e. arms which make his hands serviceable; מִלֵּא יָדוֺ בַּקֶּשֶׁת [2 K 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:24) *he filled his hand with the bow,* i.e. caused his hand to grasp it, seized it; הַרְכֵּב יָדְְֽ עַל־הַקֶּשֶׁת [2 K 13:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:16), [13:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:16); esp. fig. of consecrating or installing (as priest), מִלֵּא יַד *he filled the hand* of any one (perh. orig. gave the selected portions of animal\-sacrifices to, v. [Lv 8:25](https://ref.ly/Lev8:25) ff, so Di), *installed* as priest Ju [17:5](https://ref.ly/Judg17:5), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Judg17:12). [1 K 13:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:33) (sq. וְ subord.); elsewhere only P and late: [Ex 28:41](https://ref.ly/Exod28:41) (‖קִדַּשׁ), [29:9](https://ref.ly/Exod29:9) also v:[29](https://ref.ly/Exod29:29) (‖משׁח), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Exod29:33) קדּשׁ, v:[35](https://ref.ly/Exod29:35), [Lv 8:33](https://ref.ly/Lev8:33), [2 Ch 13:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:9), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Lev16:33), sq. inf. [Lv 16:33](https://ref.ly/Lev16:33) (‖משׁח), [21:10](https://ref.ly/Lev21:10) (‖יוּצַק שֶׁמֶן), [Nu 3:3](https://ref.ly/Num3:3)(‖משׁח); מִלֵּאתֶם יֶרְכֶם ליהוה [2 Ch 29:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:31) \= ye have consecrated yourselves to ´י, is addressed appar. to the whole congregation (otherwise Be Öt, and Di [Ex 32:29](https://ref.ly/Exod32:29)); so the same expression [Ex 32:29](https://ref.ly/Exod32:29); so the same expression [Ex 32:29](https://ref.ly/Exod32:29) (poss.) and [1 Ch 29:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:5) (certainly) of offering gifts to ´י; מִלְאוּ יָד֯ו Ez [43:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:26) of consecrating the altar (‖כִּפֶּר, טִהַר); שָׁלַח יֵד לְ Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26) *stretch out hand to,* so ישׁלח אֶ־ל [2 S 6:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:6) (insert ידו Vrss Dr); hence ם(כ)מִשְׁלַח יד *that to which one puts the hand,* fig., \= undertaking, † [Dt 12:7](https://ref.ly/Deut12:7), [12:18](https://ref.ly/Deut12:18), [15:10](https://ref.ly/Deut15:10), [23:21](https://ref.ly/Deut23:21), [28:8](https://ref.ly/Deut28:8), [28:20](https://ref.ly/Deut28:20) †; שׁלח יד also in hostile sense, c. בְּ, [Ex 22:7](https://ref.ly/Exod22:7), [1 S 24:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:7), [24:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:11), [26:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:11), [Psalm 55:21](https://ref.ly/Ps55:21) \+; cf. מוֺאָב מִשְׁלוֺתֵ יָדָם [Is 11:14](https://ref.ly/Isa11:14); אֶבֶן־יָד [Nu 35:17](https://ref.ly/Num35:17) (P) *a stone* (*thrown from*) *the hand;* כְּלִי עֵץ־יָד v:[18](https://ref.ly/Num35:18) *a weapon in the hand;* מַקֵּל יָד Ez [39:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:9) *a staff in the hand;* idols are ´מַעֲשֵה יְדֵי וגו [Is 2:8](https://ref.ly/Isa2:8) \+ oft. v. מַעֲשֶה; man is work of God's hand [Jb 14:15](https://ref.ly/Job14:15). **d.** special phrases: kissing with the hand וַתִּשַּׁק יָדִי לְפִי [Jb 31:27](https://ref.ly/Job31:27); (in silence) יָדִי שַמְתִּי לְמוֺ פִי [40:4](https://ref.ly/Job40:4), [Mi 7:16](https://ref.ly/Mic7:16) (עַל־פֶּה); the creditor is בַּעַל מַשֵּׁה יָדוֺ [Dt 15:2](https://ref.ly/Deut15:2); the debt מַשָּׁא כָל־יָד [Ne 10:32](https://ref.ly/Neh10:32); תְּרוּמַת יֶרְכֶם [Dt 12:6](https://ref.ly/Deut12:6), [12:11](https://ref.ly/Deut12:11), [12:17](https://ref.ly/Deut12:17) *heave\-offering of your hand;* the hand is placed תחת יָרֵךְ in taking an oath [Gn 24:2](https://ref.ly/Gen24:2) (J); lifted (הרים) to ´י [14:22](https://ref.ly/Gen14:22); so perhaps יָד עַל־כֵּס יָהּ [Ex 17:16](https://ref.ly/Exod17:16) (E) *hand on the throne of Yah !* (but difficult, v. Di VB) ; oft. c. נשא the hand is lifted (נשא) to heaven [Dt 32:40](https://ref.ly/Deut32:40) (of ´י's oath, poem); elsewh. chiefly Ez and P: simply *lift* (נשא) *the hand* (\= נשׁבע), sq, inf. [Ex 6:8](https://ref.ly/Exod6:8), [Nu 14:30](https://ref.ly/Num14:30) (both P), Ez [20:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:28), [20:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:42), [47:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:14); sq. לְ Pers. Ez [20:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:5), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:5) (del. Co as gloss); sq. לְ Pers. \+ inf. Ez [20:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:6), [20:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:15), [20:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:23); abs. [36:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:7); hence (citations) [Ne 9:15](https://ref.ly/Neh9:15), [Psalm 106:26](https://ref.ly/Ps106:26); cf. [Ne 8:6](https://ref.ly/Neh8:6) the people answered אָמֵן אָמֵן בְּמֹעַל יְדֵיהֶם; (ים)נשא יד elsewh. (of men) in prayer [Psalm 28:2](https://ref.ly/Ps28:2); cf. [68:32](https://ref.ly/Ps68:32), and יָדִי לַיְלָה נִגְּרָה [77:3](https://ref.ly/Ps77:3). Also of God, to give a signal [Is 49:29](https://ref.ly/Isa49:29); to rescue [Psalm 10:12](https://ref.ly/Ps10:12). יְנֹפֵף יָדוֺ [Is 10:32](https://ref.ly/Isa10:32) *he brandisheth his hand* (Assyrian, in defiance); הֵנִיף יָדוֺ [Is 11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15) (of ´י in judgment), cf.[19:16](https://ref.ly/Isa19:16), [Zc 2:13](https://ref.ly/Zech2:13) (sq. על); הֵנִיעַ יָדוֺ); [Zp 2:15](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:15) (in derision); הוֺשִׁיעַ ידלְ one's *hand bringing deliverance to, gaining success, by force, for* 1 S [25:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:26) \+ (v. Dr), ins. also v:[31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31) (LXX We Dr); the hand is weary יָגְֽעָה [2 S 23:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:10), it cleaves (דבק) to the sword (אֶל־הַחֶרֶב) v:[10](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:10) (both om. by accident in ‖ [1 Ch 11:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:13), see VB); שֶׁכֶר יָד [Lv 21:9](https://ref.ly/Lev21:9) (H) *fracture of hand* \= arm (‖שֶׁבֶר רגל); after נָתַן: *give a pledge* וִיִּתְּנוּ יָדָם לְהוֺצִיא נְשֵׁיהֶם [Ezr 10:19](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:19); *submit* נָחַן יַד תַּחַת שְׁמֹֽה [1 Ch 29:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:24), i.e. they acknowledged him as their lord; תְּנו־יָד ליהוה; [2 Ch 30:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:8); other phr. c. prep. v. infr. **e.** of hand as strong, helpful, etc.:—(*I*) of man : עֹצֶם יָדִי [Dt 8:17](https://ref.ly/Deut8:17) (‖כֹּחִי); of fighting power of Edom בְּיָד חֲזָקָה [Nu 20:20](https://ref.ly/Num20:20) (JE ; ‖בְּעַם כָּבֵד; cf. infr. of God); קִצְרֵי־יָד *small in power* [2 K 19:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:26) \= [Is 37:27](https://ref.ly/Isa37:27) (cf. infr. of God); Isr. went out of Egypt בְּיָד רָמָה acc. to P, [Ex 14:8](https://ref.ly/Exod14:8), [Nu 33:3](https://ref.ly/Num33:3) i.e. boldly, defiantly ; same phr. of presumptuousness (against ´י) [Nu 15:30](https://ref.ly/Num15:30) (P, cf. יָדֵנוּ רָ֫מָה[Dt 32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27)). Phrases of *strengthening* are: חִוַּק יָדָתו Ju [9:24](https://ref.ly/Judg9:24), [Ne 6:9](https://ref.ly/Neh6:9); חִזֵּק בְּיָדַיִם [Ezr 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:6); החזיק בְּיַ־ד [Jb 8:20](https://ref.ly/Job8:20) (v. חזק); לִהְיוֺת יָדָיו אִתּוֺ [2 K 15:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:19) *that his hands might be with him,* to confirm the kingdom in his hand; note also מִי־הוּא לְיָדִי יִתָּקֵעַ [Jb 17:3](https://ref.ly/Job17:3) *who is he* (that) *will strike his hand into mine,* i.e. give me a pledge (v. sub תקע); strength fails when hands drop: וַיִּרְפּוּ יָדָיו [2 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:1) *then his hands dropped down,* he grew feeble, spiritless (v. רפה); רְפֵי יָדַיִם [17:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:2) *weak\-handed, weak* (‖יָגֵעַ). (*2*) of (mighty) hand of God, pointing to earlier anthropomorphism; as strengthening Joseph [Gn 49:24](https://ref.ly/Gen49:24) (poem in J); as stretched out (שׁלח) to smite [Ex 9:15](https://ref.ly/Exod9:15) (J); so of ´מַלְאַךְ י [2 S 24:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:16), sq. acc. \+ לְ inf., opp. הֶ֫רֶף יָדְֶָ v:[16](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:16); as smiting (נָֽגְעָה בְּ) [1 S 6:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:9), [Jb 19:21](https://ref.ly/Job19:21); as against (בְ *among, in*) cattle, etc., v:[3](https://ref.ly/Job19:3) (J); city [1 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:9); the murrain v:[3](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:3) (J); יַד־יהוה as heavy against (כבדה אֶל) [1 S 5:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:6); שָׁם כָּֽבְרָה v:[11](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:11); קָֽשְׁתה עַל v:[7](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:7) of withdrawal of ´י's chastising hand תָּסוּר יָדוֺ מִכֶּם [1 S 6:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:3); and (´י subj.) יָקֵל אֶת־יָדוֺ מֵעֲלֵיכֶם v:[5](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:5); of ´י's power to deliver his people:—† חֹ֫זֶק יָד [Ex 13:3](https://ref.ly/Exod13:3), [13:14](https://ref.ly/Exod13:14), [13:16](https://ref.ly/Exod13:16) (a11 JE) †; יָד חֲזָקָה [6:1](https://ref.ly/Exod6:1), [6:1](https://ref.ly/Exod6:1) (JE), [13:9](https://ref.ly/Exod13:9) (JE), [Dt 6:21](https://ref.ly/Deut6:21), [7:8](https://ref.ly/Deut7:8), [Jos 4:24](https://ref.ly/Josh4:24) (D); esp: in phr. זְרוֺעַ נְטוּיָה(בִ)יָד חֲזָקָה וּ(בְּ) [Dt 4:34](https://ref.ly/Deut4:34) \+ (v. זְרוֺעַ p. 284\); יד חזקה \+ כֹּחַ גָּדוֺל [Ex 32:11](https://ref.ly/Exod32:11) (JE), [Ne 1:10](https://ref.ly/Neh1:10); תִּקְצָר הֲיַד ´י [Nu 11:23](https://ref.ly/Num11:23) (JE) *is the hand of* ´י *shortened* ? [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), [59:1](https://ref.ly/Isa59:1) (both sq. מִן); יָד נְטוּיָה in judgment [Is 9:11](https://ref.ly/Isa9:11), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Isa9:16), [9:20](https://ref.ly/Isa9:20), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Isa10:4); (ו)עלי ´הָֽיְתָה יַד־י of grasp of ´י's hand in prophetic inspiration Ez [1:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:3), [3:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:22), [37:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:1), [40:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:1), [2 K 3:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:15); עָלַי חזקה ´יַד־י Ez [3:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:14); so בְּחֶזְקַת הַיָּדָ [Is 8:11](https://ref.ly/Isa8:11); of God's (´י's) *good hand* \= favour, (late) יַד (יו)טוֺבָה על (יו)אלה [Ezr 7:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:9), [8:18](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:18), [Ne 2:8](https://ref.ly/Neh2:8), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Neh2:18); without טוֺבָה [Ezr 7:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:6), [7:28](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:28), [8:22](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:22), [8:31](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:31); sq. inf, יַד האלהים לָתֵת לָהֶם [2 Ch 30:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:12); בְּיָֽדְ לְגַדֵּל [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12); in gen. כֹּחַ וּגְבוּרָה בְּיָֽדְ v:[12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12); of protection בְּצֵל יָדוֺ [Is 49:2](https://ref.ly/Isa49:2) *in the shadow of his* (´י's) *hand.*

 **2\.** Fig.\= *strength, power:*—לָנוּס לאֹ הָיָה בָהֶם יָדָיִם [Jos 8:20](https://ref.ly/Josh8:20) (JE) *there was not in them strength to flee;* לאֹ מָֽצְאוּ כָל־אַנְשֵׁי־חַיִל יְדֵיהֶם [Psalm 76:6](https://ref.ly/Ps76:6) *none of the men of might have found their hands,* i.e. their powers are paralyzed in death (‖נָמוּ שְׁנָתָם); with ref. to pecuniary *ability:* תַּ גִּיעַ יָדוֺ דֵּי שֶה [Lv 5:7](https://ref.ly/Lev5:7) (on this and others c. דֵּי v. דַּי); לִשְׁתֵּי תוֺרִים תַּגִּיעַ יָדוֺ v:[11](https://ref.ly/Lev5:11) if *his hand* do not *reach to two turtledoves;* אֵין יָדוֺ מַשֶּגֶת [14:21](https://ref.ly/Lev14:21) if *his hand be unable to reach;* so combinations with נשג Hiph.: v:[22](https://ref.ly/Lev14:22), v:[30](https://ref.ly/Lev14:30), v:[31](https://ref.ly/Lev14:31), v:[32](https://ref.ly/Lev14:32), [Nu 6:21](https://ref.ly/Num6:21) (all foregoing P), [Lv 25:26](https://ref.ly/Lev25:26), [25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47), [25:49](https://ref.ly/Lev25:49), [27:8](https://ref.ly/Lev27:8) (all H), Ez [46:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:7); כְּמִתְּנַת יָדוֺ [Dt 16:17](https://ref.ly/Deut16:17) *according to the giving of his hand,* i.e. his ability; similarly מַתַּת יָדוֺ Ez [46:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:5), [46:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:11); on יֶשׁ־לְאֵל יָדִי [Gn 31:29](https://ref.ly/Gen31:29), etc., v. II. אֵל:a supr.; other phr. c. prep. v. infr.; of *dominion* of king לְהָשִׁיב יָדוֺ בְּ [2 S 8:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:3) rd. prob. as ‖ [1 Ch 18:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:3) לְהַצִּיב יָדוֺ בְּ *to establish his dominion at; hand* \= *display of strength, action* of ´י [Psalm 78:42](https://ref.ly/Ps78:42), [Jb 27:11](https://ref.ly/Job27:11), [Dt 34:12](https://ref.ly/Deut34:12); esp. ´הַיָּד הַיָּד הַגְּדֹלָה אֲשֶׁר עָשָה י Ex [14:31](https://ref.ly/Exod14:31) (J) \= *the great achievement which* ´י *did*. **3\.** Fig.\= *side:* **a.** of way יַד דֶּרֶךְ [1 S 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:13) Qr (Kt יך; but rd. prob. לְיַד השׁער v. Dr.). **b.** of gate בְּעַד יַד־הַשַּׁעַר [1 S 4:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:18) (txt. dub., v. Dr). **c.** of stream or wady כָּל־יַד נַחַל יַבֹּק [Dt 2:37](https://ref.ly/Deut2:37). Esp. du.: **d.** of land, ארץ רַחֲבַת יָדַיִם [Gn 34:21](https://ref.ly/Gen34:21) (P) *the land is wide of (on) both hands,* i.e. in both directions, Ju [18:10](https://ref.ly/Judg18:10), [Is 22:18](https://ref.ly/Isa22:18), [1 Ch 4:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:40); of city [Ne 7:4](https://ref.ly/Neh7:4); of sea [Psalm 104:25](https://ref.ly/Ps104:25); of streams רַחֲבֵי יָדָיִם [Is 33:21](https://ref.ly/Isa33:21); מִיּד כִּתִּים [Nu 24:24](https://ref.ly/Num24:24) (JE) *from the direction of Kittim.*—Other phr. c. prep. v. infr. **e.** *side* \= *place*, properly *place at one side* לְךָ מִחוּץ לַמַּחֲנֶה וְיָד תִּהְיֶה [Dt 23:13](https://ref.ly/Deut23:13); אִישׁ אֶת־יָדוֺ [Je 6:3](https://ref.ly/Jer6:3) *each (in) his place;* so כָּל־אִישׁ עַל־יָדוֺ [Nu 2:17](https://ref.ly/Num2:17) (P);—v. other phrases c. prep. infr. **4\.** יָד is used in various special, technical senses:—**a.** *sign, monument* [1 S 15:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:12), [2 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:18). † **b.** *part, fractional part* or *share:* of seed [Gn 47:24](https://ref.ly/Gen47:24) (J); share in king [2 S 19:44](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:44); of fighting men [2 K 11:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:17); of people [Ne 11:1](https://ref.ly/Neh11:1) † **c.** *time, repetition* [Gn 43:34](https://ref.ly/Gen43:34) (J), [Dn 1:20](https://ref.ly/Dan1:20). Also f. pl. (only in fig. senses):—† **d.** *axle\-trees* [1 K 7:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:32), [7:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:33) (SyrVer ܐܻܝ̈ܕܰܗܳܬܳܐ). † **e.** *stays, supports* for laver [1 K 7:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:35), [7:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:36); *stays* at sides of throne [1 K 10:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:19), [10:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:19) \= [2 Ch 9:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:18), [9:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:18). † **f.** *tenons* on sides of boards of tabernacle שְׁתֵּי יָדוֺת [Ex 26:17](https://ref.ly/Exod26:17) (P), also v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod26:19), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod26:19), [36:22](https://ref.ly/Exod36:22), [36:24](https://ref.ly/Exod36:24), [36:24](https://ref.ly/Exod36:24). **g.** יָד חָזִית [Is 57:8](https://ref.ly/Isa57:8) acc. to Hi De Che Or Brd Du and most \= *a phallus thou beholdest;* this favoured by context but without support in Heb. usage; Di *a* (beckoning) *hand*. **5\.** יַד with prep.:—**a.** אֶל־יַד:—(1\) after vb. of motion, *into the charge, custody, of* [Est 2:3](https://ref.ly/Esth2:3), [2:8](https://ref.ly/Esth2:8), [2:8](https://ref.ly/Esth2:8), [2:14](https://ref.ly/Esth2:14); (2\) אֶל־יַד *by the side of* gate [2 S 18:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:4); אֶל־יָדִי *by my side, next to me* [14:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:30); אֶל־יַד דֶּרֶךְ Ez [48:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:1) a; אֶליַד חֲמָת vb (but on txt. v. Co); cf. also לְיַד, עַל־יַד. **b.** בְּיַד *into the hand:* (1\) נָתַן בְּיַד *give into the hand of,* lit., [Gn 27:17](https://ref.ly/Gen27:17); (2\) *into the possession of* נָתַן בְּיַד [Ex 10:25](https://ref.ly/Exod10:25) (JE); esp. (3\) נתן ביד \= *give into the power of, deliver over to* [Jos 6:2](https://ref.ly/Josh6:2), [7:7](https://ref.ly/Josh7:7) (both JE), [Dt 1:27](https://ref.ly/Deut1:27), Ju [2:14](https://ref.ly/Judg2:14) a, [13:1](https://ref.ly/Judg13:1), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Judg15:12), [1 K 22:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:6), [2 K 18:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:30), [19:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:10); also [Is 64:6](https://ref.ly/Isa64:6), which read וַתְּמַגְּנֵנוּ for MT וַתְּמוּגֵנוּ; (4\) נתן בְּיַד \= *entrust to* [Gn 30:35](https://ref.ly/Gen30:35), [32:17](https://ref.ly/Gen32:17). **c.** בְּיַד *in the hand of:* (1\) lit. מַטְּ אֲשֶׁר בְּיָדְֶָ [Gn 38:18](https://ref.ly/Gen38:18) (J) *thy staff which is in thy hand,* [Ex 7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17) (J), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Exod17:9) (E), [32:15](https://ref.ly/Exod32:15), [Nu 22:23](https://ref.ly/Num22:23), [22:31](https://ref.ly/Num22:31), [Jos 5:13](https://ref.ly/Josh5:13), [8:18](https://ref.ly/Josh8:18) (all JE), [1 S 13:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:22), [14:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:27), [17:50](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:50), [18:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:10), [20:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:10), [2 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:8), [11:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:11) \= [2 Ch 23:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:7), [Am 7:7](https://ref.ly/Amos7:7), [Is 6:6](https://ref.ly/Isa6:6), Ez [40:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:3), [40:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:5), [47:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:3), [Zc 2:5](https://ref.ly/Zech2:5), [8:4](https://ref.ly/Zech8:4); (2\) fig. *in the possession of,* [Ex 21:16](https://ref.ly/Exod21:16) (JE); נִמְצָא בְּיָדִי [1 S 9:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:8) *there is found* *in my hand,* i.e. I happen to have (1/4 shekel); (3\) *in the (physical) power of* [Gn 16:6](https://ref.ly/Gen16:6) (J; [16:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:2); קַח בְּיָֽדְ שְׁלשִׁים אֲנָשִׁים [Je 38:10](https://ref.ly/Jer38:10). **d.** בְּיַד *by the agency* or *instrumentality of* [Gn 38:20](https://ref.ly/Gen38:20) (J), Ju [6:36](https://ref.ly/Judg6:36), [1 S 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:7), [16:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:20), [2 S 3:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:18), [10:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:2), [11:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:4), [12:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:25), [2 K 14:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:27) (the orig. lit. sense of discernible in some of these); \= *near to,* in time, נָכוֺן בְּיָדוֺ יוֺם חשֶׁךְ [Jb 15:23](https://ref.ly/Job15:23) *ready at his hand is a day of darkness* (i.e. near *at hand*). **e.** כְּיַד הַמֶּלֶךְ [1 K 10:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:13) *acc. to the hand of the king,* i.e. his royal munificence, so [Est 2:18](https://ref.ly/Esth2:18) cf. [1:7](https://ref.ly/Esth1:7). **f.** לְיַד *by the side of* [1 S 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:3), [1 Ch 18:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:17), [23:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:28), [Ne 11:24](https://ref.ly/Neh11:24), [Pr 8:3](https://ref.ly/Prov8:3), [Psalm 140:6](https://ref.ly/Ps140:6). **g.** מִיַּד *out of the hand:*—(1\) *out of power of,* often nearly \= מִן, used idomat. c. many vbs.: c. הִצִּיל Gn [32:12](https://ref.ly/Gen32:12), [32:12](https://ref.ly/Gen32:12) (J), [Ex 2:19](https://ref.ly/Exod2:19), [18:9](https://ref.ly/Exod18:9), [18:10](https://ref.ly/Exod18:10), [18:10](https://ref.ly/Exod18:10) (all JE), [1 S 17:37](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:37); מִתַּחַת יַד [Ex 18:10](https://ref.ly/Exod18:10) (JE; so c. פשׁע [2 Ch 21:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:8), [21:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:10), [21:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:10); cf. **i.** infr.); of animals מִיַּד [1 S 17:37](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:37), [17:37](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:37), מִיַּד־כֶּלֶב [Psalm 22:21](https://ref.ly/Ps22:21); מִידֵי חֶרֶב ´פ [Jb 5:20](https://ref.ly/Job5:20); pregn. יִשְׁפְּטֵנִי מִיָּדְֶָ [1 S 24:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:16) *may he* (´י) *judge* (and save) *me out of thy hand;* so [2 S 18:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:19) and (with רִיב for שׁפט) [1 S 25:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:39); [Mal 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mal1:10), [1:13](https://ref.ly/Mal1:13); רָצוֺן מִיַּד לָקַח [2:13](https://ref.ly/Mal2:13). **h.** (י)עַל־יד:—(1\) *upon the hand(s) of* תְּנָה אֹתוֺ עַל־יָדִי [Gn 42:37](https://ref.ly/Gen42:37) \= *entrust him to me* (E; lit. *put him upon my hand*); חָרֶב יַגִּירֻהוּ עַל־יְדֵ־י [Psalm 63:11](https://ref.ly/Ps63:11) *they shall pour him out upon the hands of the sword* \= *deliver him up to the sword;* so [Je 18:21](https://ref.ly/Jer18:21), Ez [35:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:5); (2\) עַל־יַד, עַל־יְדֵי *acc. to the hand(s) of* \= at the guidance, direction of: עַל־יַד [1 Ch 25:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:2) a, [2 Ch 26:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:13); עַל־יְדֵי [Je 5:31](https://ref.ly/Jer5:31), [33:13](https://ref.ly/Jer33:13), and esp. Chr: [1 Ch 25:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:2) b, [25:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:3), [25:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:6), [25:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:6); עַל־יְדֵי דָוִיד [2 Ch 23:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:18), [Ezr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:10); עַל־יְדֵי כְּלִי דָוִיד [2 Ch 29:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:27) *acc. to the guidance of the instruments of David;* (3\) *by the side of,* way הַשַּׁעַר עַל־יַד דֶּרֶךְ [2 S 15:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:2); river (היאר)עַל־יַד [Ex 2:5](https://ref.ly/Exod2:5) (E), [Nu 13:29](https://ref.ly/Num13:29) (JE; ‖עַל־הַיָּם), [Je 46:6](https://ref.ly/Jer46:6), [Dn 10:4](https://ref.ly/Dan10:4); city [Jos 15:46](https://ref.ly/Josh15:46) (JE); person ( \= *in the company of*) [2 S 15:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:18); people [2 Ch 21:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:16); esp. late עַל־יַד *next to* (in a series) [17:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:15), [17:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:16), [17:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:18), [31:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:15), [Ne 3:2](https://ref.ly/Neh3:2), [3:2](https://ref.ly/Neh3:2) \+ 13 t. Ne 3, [13:13](https://ref.ly/Neh13:13); עַל־יְדֵי *by the side of* Ju [11:26](https://ref.ly/Judg11:26); people (אדום)עַל־יְדֵי [Nu 34:3](https://ref.ly/Num34:3) (P), [1 Ch 7:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:29); cattle [Jb 1:14](https://ref.ly/Job1:14); שִׁיר הֶעֱמִיד עַל־יְדֵי [1 Ch 6:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:16) *he stationed them by the side of song,* i.e. to watch over the singing. **i.** תַּחַת יַד *under the hand* \= in the possession, at the disposal of; ´וְאִין יֶשׁ־פֹּה תַחַת־יָֽדְ חֲנִית וגו [1 S 21:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:9); \= in the power of, subject to [Is 3:6](https://ref.ly/Isa3:6) come, thou shalt be our ruler and this ruin *under thy hand;* pl. sq. vb., וְהִתְעַנִּי תַּחַת יָדֶיהָ [Gn 16:9](https://ref.ly/Gen16:9) (J) *and submit thyself under her hands,* her authority, [Is 3:6](https://ref.ly/Isa3:6).—מִתַּחַת יַד v. **g.** (1\) supr., and sub תַּחַת.

## I יָדַד (id: BDB03800)

\[Str3032] † I. \[יָדַד] **vb.** cast a lot (cf. Eth. ወደደ፡ *immittere,* etc, DI935)—only **Qal** *Pf.* 3 mpl. יַדוּ גוֺרָל עַל [Na 3:10](https://ref.ly/Nah3:10), Ob:[11](https://ref.ly/Obad1:11); יַדוּ גוֺרָל אֶ־ל [Jos 4:3](https://ref.ly/Josh4:3). (Poss. wrongly pointed pfs. of ידה **Pi.** q.v.)

* **Associated Passages:** Nahum 3:10; Joshua 4:3


## II ידד (id: BDB03801)

II. ידד (√ of foll.; *love,* cf. Ar. وَدَّ *love;* Aram. Pa. ܝܰܕܶܕ *love,* also deriv.; Sab. epith. f. ודת *loving\-one, friend, amie,* DHMZMG 1883, 391; v. also דוד).

> \[Str3039] † \[יָדִיד] **adj.** (poet.) beloved (NH *id.*, Aram. ܝܰܕܺܝܕܳܐ)—cstr. יְדִיד [Dt 33:12](https://ref.ly/Deut33:12); sf. לִידִידִי [Is 5:1](https://ref.ly/Isa5:1), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Isa5:1), [Je 11:15](https://ref.ly/Jer11:15); לִידִידוֺ [Psalm 127:2](https://ref.ly/Ps127:2); pl. sf. יְדִידֶיְ [60:7](https://ref.ly/Ps60:7), [108:7](https://ref.ly/Ps108:7); f. יְדִידֹת [45:1](https://ref.ly/Ps45:1); יְדִידוֺת [84:2](https://ref.ly/Ps84:2); —**1\.** *my beloved,* used by proph. of ´י under fig. of husbandman [Is 5:1](https://ref.ly/Isa5:1), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Isa5:1); so *my* (thy, his) *beloved* [Je 11:15](https://ref.ly/Jer11:15), [Psalm 60:7](https://ref.ly/Ps60:7) \= [108:7](https://ref.ly/Ps108:7), [127:2](https://ref.ly/Ps127:2); *beloved,* of Benj. as beloved by ´י [Dt 33:12](https://ref.ly/Deut33:12). **2\.** *lovely,* מַה־יְּדִידוֺת מִשְׁכְּנוֺתֶיְ [Psalm 84:2](https://ref.ly/Ps84:2) *how lovely are thy habitations !* **3\.** fpl. as abstr. subst. שִׁיר יְדִידֹת [Psalm 45:1](https://ref.ly/Ps45:1) \= *a song of love*.

> \[Str3033] † יְדִדוּת **n.f.** love \= obj. of love;—only cstr. יְדִדוּת נַפְשִׁי [Je 12:7](https://ref.ly/Jer12:7) (´י speaks) *love* (i.e. beloved) *of my soul*.

> \[Str3040] † יְדִידָה **n.pr.f.** (*beloved*) mother of Josiah [2 K 22:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:1); LXX Ιεδεια, LXXL Ιεδιδα.

> \[Str3041] † יְדִידְיָה **n.pr.m.** (*beloved of Yah;* cf. Sab. ודדאל DHMZMG 1875, 604; 1883, 15\)—name given to Solomon by Nathan; יהוה וַיִּקְרָא אֶת־שְׁמוֺ יְדִידְיָהַָ בַּעֲבוּר [2 S 12:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:25); LXX Ιδεδει, LXXL Ιεδδιδια.

> \[Str3035] † יִדּוֺ **n.pr.m. 1\.** a Manassite, [1 Ch 27:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:21), LXX Ιαδδαι. **2\.** one of those who took foreign wives [Ezr 10:43](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:43) Kt (Qr יַדַּי; cf. Palm. ידי \= *dilectus* VogNo. 5\), LXX Δια, A Ιαδει, LXXL Ιαδιαι.

> \[Str3035] יַדַּי **n.pr.m.** v. יִדּוֺ. above

> \[Str4312] † מֵידָד **n.pr.m.** one who (with אֶלְדָּד) prophesied in the camp of Isr. [Nu 11:26](https://ref.ly/Num11:26), [11:27](https://ref.ly/Num11:27) (JE), LXX Μωδαδ.

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 11:15; Psalms 108:7; 2 Kings 22:1; Psalms 45:1; Numbers 11:26; 2 Samuel 12:25; Numbers 11:27; Psalms 60:7; Ezra 10:43; Psalms 84:2; Jeremiah 12:7; Deuteronomy 33:12; Isaiah 5:1; Psalms 127:2; 1 Chronicles 27:21


## יָדָה (id: BDB03809)

\[Str3034] † \[יָדָה] **vb.** throw, cast (Ar. وَدَى *exeruit, emisit,* II. IV. *emisit* (all now in special senses); Eth. ወደየ፡ *throw, cast* on or in (very oft.; cf. ወደደ፡); where **Hiph.** *give thanks, confess* (orig. *acknowledge* ?) is commonly derived, perhaps from gestures accompanying the act, v. Thes LagOr. ii. 22, yet connexion uncertain; Aram. Pa. וַדֵּי, Aph. אוֺדִּי *confess,* ܐܰܘܕܺܝ, ܐܫܬܘܕܝ *id.;* but Eth. አስተዋደየ፡ *accuse,* perh. also fr. gesture; Palm. מודא *render thanks,* oft. in votive inscrr., see Vog on No. 79; עבד ומודא VogNo. 101 \= εὐχαρίστως ἀνέθηκε; מודן כל יום VogNo. 93\. 1; ידתא *pious* VogNo. 29\)—**Qal** *Imv.* יְדוּ אֶ־ל *shoot* (arrows) *at* [Je 50:14](https://ref.ly/Jer50:14) (rd. prob., with some Codd. יְרוּ). **Pi.** *Impf.* וַיַּדּוּ בִּי [La 3:53](https://ref.ly/Lam3:53) *and they cast* (stones) *on me; Inf.* יַדּוֺת [Zc 2:4](https://ref.ly/Zech2:4) *to cast down* (the horns of the nations).

[Ru 3:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:4); c. בְּ, יָדַעְתָּ בְּצָרוֺת נַפְשִׁי [Psalm 31:8](https://ref.ly/Ps31:8) (‖רָאָה אֶ־ת). **c.** ‖ראה, imv. דע וראה *perceive and see* [1 S 12:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:17), [24:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:12); *find out and discern* [14:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:38), [23:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:22); in reverse order, ראו ודעו only [23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23) (v. **a** supr.), [Je 5:1](https://ref.ly/Jer5:1) (v. [Gn 4:1](https://ref.ly/Gen4:1), [4:17](https://ref.ly/Gen4:17), [4:25](https://ref.ly/Gen4:25), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Gen24:16), [38:26](https://ref.ly/Gen38:26) (all J), [1 S 1:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:19), Ju [19:25](https://ref.ly/Judg19:25), [1 K 1:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:4); woman subj. [Gn 19:8](https://ref.ly/Gen19:8) (J), [Nu 31:17](https://ref.ly/Num31:17), [31:18](https://ref.ly/Num31:18), [31:35](https://ref.ly/Num31:35) (all P), Ju [11:39](https://ref.ly/Judg11:39); יֹדַעַת מִשְׁכַּב זָכָר Ju [21:11](https://ref.ly/Judg21:11); לאֹ יָדְֽעָה אִישׁ לְמִשְׁכַּב זָכָר v:[12](https://ref.ly/Judg21:12); man subj. and obj. (of sodomy) [Gn 19:5](https://ref.ly/Gen19:5) (J), Ju [19:22](https://ref.ly/Judg19:22). **4\. a.** *know how* to do a thing, be able to do it, oft. c. לאֹ and sq. inf. לאֹ יָדְֽעוּ עֲשוֺ־ת נְכֹחָה [Am 3:10](https://ref.ly/Amos3:10) *they know not how to do right;* לאֹ יָדַעְתִּי דַבֵּר [Je 1:6](https://ref.ly/Jer1:6), [6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15), [1 K 3:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:7), [Is 56:11](https://ref.ly/Isa56:11), [56:11](https://ref.ly/Isa56:11), c. לְ [50:4](https://ref.ly/Isa50:4), [Ec 10:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:15); לאֹ יָדַע לְהִזָּהֵר [Ec 4:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:13) *know not how to be admonished* (v:[17](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:1) know *that*); inf. abs. ידע מָאוֺס בָּרָע וּבָחוֺר בַּטּוֺב [Is 7:15](https://ref.ly/Isa7:15), [7:16](https://ref.ly/Isa7:16). **b.** *be skilful in,*esp. pt., sq. acc. יֹדֵעַ צַיִד [Gn 25:27](https://ref.ly/Gen25:27) (J) *skilled in hunting;* ידע סֵפֶר *skilled in a book, learned*[Is 29:11](https://ref.ly/Isa29:11), [29:12](https://ref.ly/Isa29:12), [29:12](https://ref.ly/Isa29:12); as pl. cstr. יוֺדְעֵי ֶ\+נהִי [Am 5:16](https://ref.ly/Amos5:16) *knowers of lamentation,*professional mourners; יֹדְעֵי הַיָּם [1 K 9:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:27) *skilled in the sea,* [2 Ch 8:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:18); esp. sq. inf. יֹדֵעַ נַגֵּן [1 S 16:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:18) *skilled in playing,*[1 K 5:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:20), [2 Ch 2:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:6), [2:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:7), [2:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:13): יֹדֵעַ מְנַגֵּן בַּכִנּוֺר [1 S 16:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:16) *knowing,* (as) *a player on the lyre* (v. Dr). **5\.** abs. *have knowledge, be wise:* לַיֹּדְעִים [Ec 9:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:11) (‖חֲכָמִים, נְבֹנִים); esp. have knowledge of God and duty, or in practical affairs [Is 1:3](https://ref.ly/Isa1:3) (‖בין), [56:10](https://ref.ly/Isa56:10); oft. c. acc. cogn.: דַּעַת [Pr 17:27](https://ref.ly/Prov17:27), [30:3](https://ref.ly/Prov30:3); בִּינָה [Is 29:24](https://ref.ly/Isa29:24), [Pr 4:1](https://ref.ly/Prov4:1), [2 Ch 2:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:12), cf. יוֺדְעֵי בִינָה לָעִתִּים [1 Ch 12:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:33) (Baer), [Est 1:13](https://ref.ly/Esth1:13); also יֹדְעֵי דָּת וָדִין v:[13](https://ref.ly/Esth1:13); יֹדְעֵי דַעַת וּמְבִינֵי מַדָּע [Dn 1:4](https://ref.ly/Dan1:4); obj. שֶכֶל וּבִינָה [2 Ch 2:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:11) (‖בֵּן חָכָם); חָכְמָה וּמוּסָר [Pr 1:2](https://ref.ly/Prov1:2) (‖הבין אִמְרֵי בִּינָה); חָכְמָה [Pr 24:14](https://ref.ly/Prov24:14), [Ec 8:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:16).

> \[Str1843] † \[דֵּעַ] **n.\[m.]** knowledge, opinion (late)—only sf. דֵּעִי [Jb 32:6](https://ref.ly/Job32:6) \+ 3 t., and pl. דֵּעִים [37:6](https://ref.ly/Job37:6); all in speech of Elihu. **1\.** *knowledge,* ´תְּמִים ד [Jb 37:16](https://ref.ly/Job37:16) *one perfect in knowledge* (of God). **2\.** *judgement, opinion* [Jb 36:3](https://ref.ly/Job36:3); חַוֹּת דֵּעִי [32:6](https://ref.ly/Job32:6) *to declare my opinion,* so v:[10](https://ref.ly/Job32:10), v:[17](https://ref.ly/Job32:17).

Dr); יֵשׁ דֵּעָה בְעֶלְיוֺן [Psalm 73:11](https://ref.ly/Ps73:11) (‖אֵיכָה יָדַע־אֵל); *knowledge,* with ´י as obj. (plausibly) רָעֹה, after LXX, v. also Gie.

Inf., which appears clearly [Gn 2:9](https://ref.ly/Gen2:9), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Gen2:17), [Je 22:16](https://ref.ly/Jer22:16))—abs. דַּ֫עַת [Gn 2:9](https://ref.ly/Gen2:9) \+ 35 t.; דָּעַת [Jos 20:3](https://ref.ly/Josh20:3) \+ 35 t.; cstr. דַּ֫עַת [Nu 24:16](https://ref.ly/Num24:16) \+ 10 t.; sf. דַּעְתִּי [Pr 22:17](https://ref.ly/Prov22:17); דַּעְתְּ [Jb 10:7](https://ref.ly/Job10:7); דַּעְתֵח [Is 47:10](https://ref.ly/Isa47:10); דַּעְתּוֺ [Pr 3:20](https://ref.ly/Prov3:20), [Is 53:11](https://ref.ly/Isa53:11); דַּעְתְּכֶם [Jb 13:2](https://ref.ly/Job13:2); דַּעְתָּם [Is 44:25](https://ref.ly/Isa44:25); —**1\. a.** *knowledge, perception* in phrase ´בִּבְלִי ד [Dt 4:42](https://ref.ly/Deut4:42) \= *unintenionally,* [19:4](https://ref.ly/Deut19:4), [Jos 20:3](https://ref.ly/Josh20:3) (where P's synonym is בִּשְׁגָגָה v:[3](https://ref.ly/Josh20:3), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Josh20:9), cf. [Nu 35:11](https://ref.ly/Num35:11), [35:15](https://ref.ly/Num35:15)), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Num35:5); *without knowing* ´מִבְּלִי ד [Is 5:13](https://ref.ly/Isa5:13) ( \= before they knew it, suddenly). **b.** \= *skill* (in work\-manship) [Ex 31:3](https://ref.ly/Exod31:3), [35:31](https://ref.ly/Exod35:31) (both P; both ‖תְּבוּנָה and חָכְמָה), [1 K 7:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:14) (sq. inf.); creative skill [Is 40:14](https://ref.ly/Isa40:14) (‖אֹרַח מִשְׁפָּט, דֶּרֶח תְּכוּנוֺת). **c.** of proph. knowledge עליון ´יֹדֵעַ ד [Nu 24:16](https://ref.ly/Num24:16) (JE), cf. [Psalm 19:3](https://ref.ly/Ps19:3). **d.** esp. knowledge with moral quality [Gn 2:9](https://ref.ly/Gen2:9), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Gen2:17) (J; in both verbal force, מוֺב וָרַע ´הַדּ *the knowing good and evil*). **e.** *knowledge* possessed by God [Jb 10:7](https://ref.ly/Job10:7), [Psalm 139:6](https://ref.ly/Ps139:6), [Pr 3:20](https://ref.ly/Prov3:20) (‖חָכְמָה and תְּבוּנָה), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Prov21:12); taught by God to men [Psalm 94:10](https://ref.ly/Ps94:10), [119:66](https://ref.ly/Ps119:66) (‖טוּב טַעַם), [Pr 2:6](https://ref.ly/Prov2:6) (‖תְּבוּנָה and חָכְמָה). **f.** דַּעַת־רוּחַ \= *windy* (unreal) *knowledge* [Jb 15:2](https://ref.ly/Job15:2). **2\.** esp. in WisdLt \= *discernment, understanding, wisdom:* **a.** [Jb 13:2](https://ref.ly/Job13:2), [33:3](https://ref.ly/Job33:3) (שְפָתַי ´ד), לאֹ בְּדַעַת [Jb 34:35](https://ref.ly/Job34:35)(‖חכמה), [Pr 8:9](https://ref.ly/Prov8:9), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Prov8:10) (‖מוסר), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Prov10:4), [11:9](https://ref.ly/Prov11:9), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Prov18:15), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Prov18:15), [22:12](https://ref.ly/Prov22:12), [29:7](https://ref.ly/Prov29:7), cf. > \[Str3048] † יְדַעְיָה **n.pr.m.** (Sab. n.pr. ידעאל DHMZMG 1875, 605\) priestly name;—**1\.** [1 Ch 9:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:10) LXX Ιωαδε, [24:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:7) LXX (Ανε)ιδεια. **2\.** [Ezr 2:36](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:36), [Ne 7:39](https://ref.ly/Neh7:39) LXX Ιεουδα, (Ι)εδδουα, Ιωαδε. **3\.** [Ne 11:10](https://ref.ly/Neh11:10), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Neh12:6), [12:19](https://ref.ly/Neh12:19) LXX Δαδεια, Ιαδιασς, Ιδειας, etc. **4\.** [Ne 12:7](https://ref.ly/Neh12:7), [12:21](https://ref.ly/Neh12:21) LXXL Ωδουιας. **5\.** [Zc 6:10](https://ref.ly/Zech6:10), [6:14](https://ref.ly/Zech6:14) LXX ((παρὰ)τὠ ν ἑεγνωκότων ά̔τήν.

> \[Str3037] † יַדוּעַ **n.pr.m. 1\.** a chief of the people, one of those sealed [Ne 10:22](https://ref.ly/Neh10:22) LXXL Ιεδδαυα. **2\.** a priest, prob. the high priest in time of Alexander the Great [Ne 12:11](https://ref.ly/Neh12:11), [12:22](https://ref.ly/Neh12:22) LXX Ιαδου, LXXL Ιεδδου (whence LagBN 113 rds. יִדּוּעַ).

> \[Str3043] † יְדִיעֲאֵל **n.pr.m.** (Palm. ידיבעל DHM in MV10, 983 cf. Sab. n.pr. with ידע DHMZMG 1875, 612\)—**1\.** a Benjamite [1 Ch 7:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:6), [7:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:10), [7:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:11), LXX Αδειηλ, Ιεδιηλ, etc. **2\.** one of David's heroes acc. to [1 Ch 11:45](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:45) appar. \= the Manassite captain of [12:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:21) (Baer; van d. H v:[20](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:20)), LXX Ελθειηλ, Ιεδιηλ. **3\.** a Korahite porter [1 Ch 26:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:2) LXX Ιδερηλ, Ιεδιηλ.

> \[Str1845] † דְּעוּאֵל **n.pr.m.** a Gadite, [Nu 1:14](https://ref.ly/Num1:14), [7:42](https://ref.ly/Num7:42), [7:47](https://ref.ly/Num7:47), [10:20](https://ref.ly/Num10:20), but LXX in all Παγουηλ, so Sam SyrVer; \= רְעוּאֵל [Nu 2:14](https://ref.ly/Num2:14), where, however, Vulgate and Heb. Codd. ´ד (all P); v. further sub רעה. p. 944 ff

> \[Str3049] † יִדְּעֹנִי **n.m.** familiar spirit (prop. either as *knowing, wise* (acquainted with secrets of unseen world), Ew *vielwisserisch;* or as *intimate acquaintance* of soothsayer, v. RSJPh xiv.1885\. 127\)—abs. יִדְּעֹנִי [Dt 18:11](https://ref.ly/Deut18:11) \+ 2 t.; pl. יִדְּעֹנִים [1 S 28:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:3) \+ 6 t.; הַיִּדְּעֹנִי מִן [1 S 28:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:9), rd. הַיִּדְּעֹנִים מִן (v. LXX Th We Klo Dr); *familiar spirit,* always ‖אוֺב, אֹבוֺת [1 S 28:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:3), [28:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:9), [Is 8:19](https://ref.ly/Isa8:19), [19:3](https://ref.ly/Isa19:3), [2 K 21:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:6) (עשה אוֺב; ‖נִחֵשׁ, עוֺנֵן) \= [2 Ch 33:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:6), [2 K 23:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:24), [Lv 19:31](https://ref.ly/Lev19:31), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Lev20:6), [20:27](https://ref.ly/Lev20:27) (all H; on last two cf. [Acts 16:16](https://ref.ly/Acts16:16) and Dr:[Dt 18:11](https://ref.ly/Deut18:11)), [Dt 18:11](https://ref.ly/Deut18:11)(‖אוֺב \+ דֹּרֵשׁ אֶל־הַמֵּתִים).

> \[Str4129] † מוֺדַע, מֹדָע **n.m.** kinsman, מידע לְ [Ru 2:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:1) Kt, but Qr מוֺדַע *a kinsman* of her husband; fig. מֹדָע לַבִּינָה תִקְרָא [Pr 7:4](https://ref.ly/Prov7:4) *a kinsman shalt thou call understanding* (‖אֱמֹר לְחָכְמָה אֲחֹתִי אָתְּ).

> \[Str4130] † \[מֹדַ֫עַת] **n.f.** kindred, kinship—sg. sf. מֹדַעְתָּנוּ [Ru 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:2) is not Boaz (of) *our kindred ?*

> \[Str4093] † מַדָּע **n.m.**:[2 Ch 1:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:12) knowledge, thought (late); מַדָּע [2 Ch 1:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:10) \+ 4 t.; מִדָּֽעְֲ [Ec 10:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:20); —**1\.** *knowledge* (‖חָכְמָה) of Solomon, [2 Ch 1:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:10), [1:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:11), [1:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:12), [Dn 1:4](https://ref.ly/Dan1:4) (‖חָכְמָה and דַּעַת), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Dan1:7) (‖הַשְכֵּל). **2\.** *place of knowledge, mind,* (in our idiom also) *thought* [Ec 10:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:20).

> \[Str4069] מַדּוּעַ:61 and :([Ex 18:19](https://ref.ly/Exod18:19)) מַדֻּעַ adv. wherefore? (prob. contr. from מַה־יָּדוּעַ *what being known ?* i.e. from what motive? so Ges Ew:§325 c Ol:§223 f , cf. in Gk. τί μαθών;)—*wherefore? on what account ?* [Gn 26:27](https://ref.ly/Gen26:27) מַדּוּעַ בָּאתֶם אֵלָי, [40:7](https://ref.ly/Gen40:7), [Ex 1:18](https://ref.ly/Exod1:18), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Exod2:18), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8) (in an indirect question), [5:14](https://ref.ly/Exod5:14), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Exod18:14), [Lv 10:17](https://ref.ly/Lev10:17), [Jos 17:14](https://ref.ly/Josh17:14) (all in Hex), Ju [5:28](https://ref.ly/Judg5:28), [5:28](https://ref.ly/Judg5:28), [2 S 3:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:7), [11:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:10) etc., [Is 5:4](https://ref.ly/Isa5:4), [50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), [63:2](https://ref.ly/Isa63:2) (all in Is.); in Jer. 16 t., oft, rhetorically, after a double question introduced by אִם…הֲ, expressing affected surprise: † [2:14](https://ref.ly/Jer2:14) (v. Gf) is Israel a slave (unable to defend himself)? *wherefore,* then, is he become a prey? (some other cause must therefore be found for Israel's misfortune), v:[31](https://ref.ly/Jer2:31), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Jer8:5), [8:19](https://ref.ly/Jer8:19), [8:22](https://ref.ly/Jer8:22), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19), [22:28](https://ref.ly/Jer22:28), [49:1](https://ref.ly/Jer49:1), cf. [30:6](https://ref.ly/Jer30:6); [Jb 3:12](https://ref.ly/Job3:12), [18:8](https://ref.ly/Job18:8) (never in Psalm).

## יָהַב (id: BDB03834)

\[Str3051] † \[יָהַב] **vb.** give (Aram. יְהַב, ܝܰܗܒ; Ar. وَهَبَ; Eth. ወሀበ፡: Sab. והב DHMZMG xxix. 1875, 614 f. ול יהב Idib. 603\)—only **Qal** *Imv.* הַב [Pr 30:15](https://ref.ly/Prov30:15), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Prov30:15); emph. הָ֫בָה [Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) \+ 10 t. (6 t. sq. monosyll.); הָבָ֫ה [Gn 29:21](https://ref.ly/Gen29:21) before gutt. (Di on Gn 28:2 Köi. 418\), fs. הָבִ֫י [Ru 3:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:15); mpl. הָב֫וּ; [Gn 47:16](https://ref.ly/Gen47:16) \+ 16 t.; הָ֫בוּ לִָי [Jb 6:22](https://ref.ly/Job6:22) (on these forms v. also Sta:§606 a Böi. 225 Ges:§69\. 2, R. 2\);—**1\.** *give,* sq. acc. [Gn 29:21](https://ref.ly/Gen29:21)(E) *give* (me) *my wife,* [47:16](https://ref.ly/Gen47:16) (J), [Ru 3:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:15), [Zc 11:12](https://ref.ly/Zech11:12) (*pirce*); of *giving* (i.e. causing to come forth, in deciding by lot) Urim and Thummim [1 S 14:41](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:41), [14:41](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:41) (LXX We Dr, v. אוּרִים); sq. acc. rei \+ לִי [Gn 30:1](https://ref.ly/Gen30:1)(E), Ju [1:15](https://ref.ly/Judg1:15); acc. rei \+ לָנוּ [Gn 47:15](https://ref.ly/Gen47:15) (J), [Psalm 60:13](https://ref.ly/Ps60:13) \= [108:13](https://ref.ly/Ps108:13); שָהבוּ לִָי [Jb 6:22](https://ref.ly/Job6:22) (no acc. expr.); abs. שַה֫ב \| הַ֤ב [Pr 30:15](https://ref.ly/Prov30:15). **2\.** \= *set,* sq. acc. (Uriah) \+ אֶל־מוּל פְּנֵי הַמִּלְחָמָה [2 S 11:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:15) *set Uriah in the fore\-front of the battle.* **3\.** with reflex. לִ (v. ל, **5 h**) \= *provide,* c. acc. הָבוּ לָכֶם אֲנָשִׁים חֲכָמִים [Dt 1:13](https://ref.ly/Deut1:13); [Jos 18:4](https://ref.ly/Josh18:4) (JE), Ju [20:7](https://ref.ly/Judg20:7); [2 S 16:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:20). **4\.** *ascribe* glory, etc., to ´י (God): c. כבוד לְ [Psalm 29:1](https://ref.ly/Ps29:1), [29:1](https://ref.ly/Ps29:1); [29:2](https://ref.ly/Ps29:2) \= [96:7](https://ref.ly/Ps96:7), [96:7](https://ref.ly/Ps96:7), [96:8](https://ref.ly/Ps96:8) \= [1 Ch 16:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:28), [16:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:28), [16:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:29); גֹדֶל לאלהינו ´ה [Dt 32:3](https://ref.ly/Deut32:3) (poem) *ascribe greatness to, our God.* **5\.** \= *come now* (orig. *grant, permit*), before voluntat.: [Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3), [11:4](https://ref.ly/Gen11:4), [11:7](https://ref.ly/Gen11:7), [38:16](https://ref.ly/Gen38:16) (all J), [Ex 1:10](https://ref.ly/Exod1:10) (E).

396:b. \[יְהָב] 1\. I: ? cf. Targum יַהֲבַת [Psalm 11:6](https://ref.ly/Ps11:6), יְהָבִית [Ec 5:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:10).

> \[Str3053] † \[יְהָב] **n.\[m.]** lot (as that which is *given*)—יְהָֽבְ ´הַשְׁלֵח עַל י [Psalm 55:23](https://ref.ly/Ps55:23) *cast on* ´י *thy lot* (the care, anxiety, etc. which are thy portion; cf. ´גּוֺל עַל־י דרכך [Psalm 37:5](https://ref.ly/Ps37:5)).

## יהץ (id: BDB03852)

יהץ (√ of foll.; Ar. وَهَصَ *break, split; valide calcavit;* وَهْصَةٌ *terra depressa* et *rotunda*).

> \[Str3096] † יַ֫הַץ, יַ֫הְצָה **n.pr.loc.** in Moab, site un\-known (MI19:20 יהץ)—יהץ [Is 15:4](https://ref.ly/Isa15:4), [Je 48:34](https://ref.ly/Jer48:34); c. ה loc. יָהְָצָה, on border of territory of Amorites (under Sihon) [Nu 21:23](https://ref.ly/Num21:23), [Dt 2:32](https://ref.ly/Deut2:32); also בְּיָהְָצָה Ju [11:20](https://ref.ly/Judg11:20); called יַהֲצָ֫ה [Jos 13:18](https://ref.ly/Josh13:18) (assigned to Reuben); יַ֫הְצָה [1 Ch 6:63](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:63) (Levit. city in Reuben), [Je 48:21](https://ref.ly/Jer48:21).

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 11:20; 1 Chronicles 6:63; Deuteronomy 2:32; Joshua 13:18; Isaiah 15:4; Jeremiah 48:21; Numbers 21:23; Jeremiah 48:34


## יהר (id: BDB03854)

יהר (√ of foll.; cf. NH יָהַר Hithp. *shew oneself haughty,* adj. יָהִיר; Aram. יְהַר Pa. *be haughty,* adj. יְהִירָא; of. Ar. اِسْتَيْهَرَ *be insane*).

> \[Str3093] † יָהִיר **adj.** proud, haughty, זֵד יָהִיר לֵץ שְׁמוֺ [Pr 21:24](https://ref.ly/Prov21:24) *a presumptuous man,* (who is) *haughty, scoffer is his name* (\+ עוֺשֶה בְּעֶבְרַת זָדוֺן); גֶּבֶר יָהִיר וְלאֹ יִנְ־וֶָֽ Hb [2:5](https://ref.ly/Hab2:5).

> \[Str3068,3097, etc] ´יו \= ´יְהוֺ **n.pr.** cpd. with, v. sub יהוה supr. p. 222:—viz. יוֺאָב, יוֺאָח, יוֺאָחָז, יוֺאֵל, יוֺאָשׁ, יוֺיָכִין, יוֺיָקִים, יוֺיָרִיב, יוֺעֵד, יוֺעָשׁ, יוֺקִים, יוֺרָם, יוֺתָם, etc. p. 219–22

> \[Str3102] † יוֺב **n.pr.m.** son of Issachar [Gn 46:13](https://ref.ly/Gen46:13), but rd. rather יָשׁוּב as Sam. [Nu 26:24](https://ref.ly/Num26:24), [1 Ch 7:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:1)(Qr) SyrVer Ol al.; LXX Ιασουφ, LXXL Ιασουβ.

> \[Str3103–05] יוֺבָב **n.pr.** v. יבב; יוֺבֵל, יוּבַל v. יבל–85

> \[Str3109] † יוֺחָא **n.pr.m.** (√ \& mng. dub.; ? \= יוֺאָח)—**1\.** a Benjamite [1 Ch 8:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:16). **2\.** one of David's heroes [1 Ch 11:45](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:45).

> \[Str3194] יוּטָה \= יֻטָּה q.v. sub נטה. p. 641

> \[Str3116] יוּכַל **n.pr.m.** v. יְהוּכַל sub יהוה supr. p. 220\.

[Jb 3:3](https://ref.ly/Job3:3); also [Psalm 118:24](https://ref.ly/Ps118:24) (v. **i**). **g.** particular days defined by n.pr.loc.: יוֺם יִזְרְעֶאל [Ho 2:2](https://ref.ly/Hos2:2) i.e. of judgement, with implied restoration; v. also [Is 9:3](https://ref.ly/Isa9:3), Ez [30:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:9); יְמֵי הַגִּבְעָה [Ho 9:9](https://ref.ly/Hos9:9), [10:9](https://ref.ly/Hos10:9), i.e. of the outrage at Gibeah (Ju [19:22](https://ref.ly/Judg19:22) ff.); יוֺם ירושׁלם [Psalm 137:7](https://ref.ly/Ps137:7) i.e. of Jerusalem's calamity, cf. [Je 50:27](https://ref.ly/Jer50:27), [50:31](https://ref.ly/Jer50:31). **h.** c. sf., *thy, his,* or *their day,* in sense of (1\) day of disaster or death: Psalm בָּא יוֺמְ [Je 50:31](https://ref.ly/Jer50:31) *thy day has come;* אוֺ יומו יָבאֹ וָמֵת [1 S 26:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:10); יומו in this sense also Ez [21:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:30), [Psalm 37:13](https://ref.ly/Ps37:13), [Jb 18:20](https://ref.ly/Job18:20); † בָּא יוֺמָם [Je 50:27](https://ref.ly/Jer50:27), Ez [21:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:29). **i.** specif. a holy day: יוֺם הַשַּׁבָּת *the sabbath day* (v. also שַׁבָּת), [Ex 20:8](https://ref.ly/Exod20:8), [20:11](https://ref.ly/Exod20:11)(E) \= [Dt 5:12](https://ref.ly/Deut5:12), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Deut5:15), [Ex 31:15](https://ref.ly/Exod31:15), [35:3](https://ref.ly/Exod35:3) \+; \= יוֺם קָדְשִׁי [Is 58:13](https://ref.ly/Isa58:13); repetition בְּיוֺם הַשַּׁבָּת בְּיוֺם הַשַּׁבָּת [Lv 24:8](https://ref.ly/Lev24:8) *every Sabbath day;* also † יוֺם הַכִּפֻּרים [Lv 23:27](https://ref.ly/Lev23:27), [25:9](https://ref.ly/Lev25:9); יוֺם כִּפֻּרִים [23:28](https://ref.ly/Lev23:28); יוֺם הַבִּכּוּרִים [Nu 28:26](https://ref.ly/Num28:26); יוֺם חַגֵּנוּ [Psalm 81:4](https://ref.ly/Ps81:4); יוֺם מַלְכֵּנוּ [Ho 7:5](https://ref.ly/Hos7:5); also of false gods, יִמֵי הַבְּעָלִים [2:11](https://ref.ly/Hos2:11). **3\.** ´יוֺם י *day of Yahweh,* chiefly as time of his coming in judgment, involving often blessedness for righteous (v. [Jb 14:6](https://ref.ly/Job14:6); time of parturition וַיִּמְלְאוּ יָמֶיהָ לָלֶדֶת [Gn 25:24](https://ref.ly/Gen25:24). **b.** appos. to other expr. of time (Dr§ 192 (1\) DaSynt.:\#xa7; 29\. d): חֹדֶשׁ יָמִים *a month of time* [Gn 29:14](https://ref.ly/Gen29:14) (J; lit. *a month, time*), [Nu 11:20](https://ref.ly/Num11:20), [11:21](https://ref.ly/Num11:21) (JE); \= יֶרַח יָמִים [Dt 21:13](https://ref.ly/Deut21:13), [2 K 15:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:13); יָמִים ארבעה חדשׁים Ju [19:2](https://ref.ly/Judg19:2) *time, four months* (si vera l., v. > \[Str3119] יוֺמָם:51 **subst.** and **adv.** daytime, by day (cf. Aram. יְמָמָא ܐܻܝܡܳܡܳܐ *day* (as opp. to *night*); Targum יֵמָם *by day;* perh. Ph. ימם (in dates, before num.), CISI. i. 10\. 1 (see note); so NoZDMG 1886, 721: on ם—ָ v. sub חִנָּם)—**1\. subst.** *daytime* (rare) [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9) בעוד יומם while it is yet *daytime,* [33:20](https://ref.ly/Jer33:20) b לְבִלְתִּי הֱיוֺת יומם זלילה *daytime* and night, v:[25](https://ref.ly/Jer33:25) ולילה ´בְּרִיתִי י (? rd. יוֺם as v:[20](https://ref.ly/Jer33:20) a); Ez [30:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16) צָרֵי יוֺמָם foes of *daytime,* i.e. coming by day (cf. [Je 15:8](https://ref.ly/Jer15:8); but text appar. defective, v. LXX Sm); once (late) בְּנּיוֺמָם (cf. Targum בְּיֵמָם [1 S 25:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:16) [Jb 5:14](https://ref.ly/Job5:14)), [Ne 9:19](https://ref.ly/Neh9:19) (varied from [Ex 13:21](https://ref.ly/Exod13:21), [Dt 1:33](https://ref.ly/Deut1:33) יומם).—[Nu 9:21](https://ref.ly/Num9:21) the sense required is (during) *a day* and a night; rd. prob. יוֺם **2\. adv.** *in the daytime, by day,* [Nu 10:34](https://ref.ly/Num10:34), [Jb 24:16](https://ref.ly/Job24:16) (but v. חִתֵּם), [Is 4:6](https://ref.ly/Isa4:6), Ez [12:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:3), [12:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:4), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:7); mostly c. לַיְלָה, and then oft. poet. \= *continually:* so יוֺמָם וָלַיְלָה [Ex 13:21](https://ref.ly/Exod13:21), [Jos 1:8](https://ref.ly/Josh1:8), [1 K 8:59](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:59), [Psalm 1:2](https://ref.ly/Ps1:2), [32:4](https://ref.ly/Ps32:4) \+, לַיְלָה וְיוֺמָם † [Dt 28:66](https://ref.ly/Deut28:66), [Is 34:10](https://ref.ly/Isa34:10), [Je 14:17](https://ref.ly/Jer14:17), in parallel clauses (esp. in poetry) [2 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:10), [Is 21:8](https://ref.ly/Isa21:8), [Je 31:35](https://ref.ly/Jer31:35), [Psalm 22:3](https://ref.ly/Ps22:3), [42:9](https://ref.ly/Ps42:9), [91:5](https://ref.ly/Ps91:5), [121:6](https://ref.ly/Ps121:6) \+. [Psalm 13:3](https://ref.ly/Ps13:3) *by day* yields a lame sense: either add וָלַיְלָה (LXX Del Gr Ch), or rd. יֹם יֹם (יוֺם יוֺם) LagNov. Psalt. Gr. Ed. Spec.13 Now.
