## I יון (id: BDB03864)

I. יון (√ of foll.; meaning unknown).

> \[Str3121] † יָוֵן **n.\[m.]** mire;—abs. מִטִּיט הַיָּוֵן [Psalm 40:3](https://ref.ly/Ps40:3); cstr. טָבַעְתִּי בִיוֵן מְצוּלָה [69:3](https://ref.ly/Ps69:3).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 69:3; Psalms 40:3


## II יון (id: BDB03866)

II. יון (√ of foll., mng. unknown; יוֺנָה acc. to LagArmen. Stud. 7\. 53; M. i. 228 \= Pers. *wanâ* but im prob.; Sta§ 259 a conject. יוֺנָה to be fr. אָנָה *mourn,* so DlPa 157\).

> \[Str3123,3128] † I. יוֺנָה **n.f.** dove (NH *id.;* Aram. *id.,* ܝܰܘܢܳܐ)—abs. יוֺנָה [Gn 8:8](https://ref.ly/Gen8:8) \+ 20 t.; cstr. יוֺנֵת [Psalm 56:1](https://ref.ly/Ps56:1); sf. יוֺנָתִי Ct [2:14](https://ref.ly/Song2:14) \+ 2 t.; pl. יוֺנִים [Na 2:8](https://ref.ly/Nah2:8) \+ 6 t.; cstr. יוֺנֵי Ez [7:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:16) (v. infr.);—*dove* [Gn 8:8](https://ref.ly/Gen8:8), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Gen8:9), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Gen8:10), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Gen8:11), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Gen8:12) (all J); oft. of offerings, בֶּן־יוֺנָה [Lv 12:6](https://ref.ly/Lev12:6) (P; an individual of the species; ‖תֹּר); יוֺנִים(ה)בְּנֵי (‖תֹּרִים [1:14](https://ref.ly/Lev1:14), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Lev5:7), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Lev5:11), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Lev12:8), [14:22](https://ref.ly/Lev14:22), [14:30](https://ref.ly/Lev14:30), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Lev15:14), [15:29](https://ref.ly/Lev15:29), [Nu 6:10](https://ref.ly/Num6:10) (all P); חֲרִי יוֺנִים *dove's dung* [2 K 6:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:25) (Kt, yet v. GeiUrschrift 409\); in various similes: Ephr. is כְּיוֺנָה פּוֺתָה *like a silly dove* [Ho 7:11](https://ref.ly/Hos7:11) (allowing itself to be snared); sim. of return of exiles, like eager flight of doves [11:11](https://ref.ly/Hos11:11), of ships with white outspread sails [Is 60:8](https://ref.ly/Isa60:8); אֵבֶר כַּיּוֺנָה [Psalm 55:7](https://ref.ly/Ps55:7); v. also כַּנְפֵי יוֺנָה [68:14](https://ref.ly/Ps68:14); sim. of fugitive Moab [Je 48:28](https://ref.ly/Jer48:28); also כְּיוֺנֵי הַגֵּאָיוֺת Ez [7:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:16) *like the doves of the valleys* (but on txt. v. Co, who emends כיונים הוֺגִיּוֺת, but regards phr. as gloss): sim. of mourning כְּקוֺל יוֺנִים [Na 2:8](https://ref.ly/Nah2:8); without קוֺל, הגה כיונים [Is 38:14](https://ref.ly/Isa38:14), [59:11](https://ref.ly/Isa59:11) (from mournful note); fig. of beauty (only Ct): עֵינַיִח יוֺנִים *thine eyes are* (those of) *doves* Ct [1:15](https://ref.ly/Song1:15), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Song4:1), v. also v:[12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12); term of endearment, יוֺנָתִי *my dove* Ct [2:14](https://ref.ly/Song2:14), [5:2](https://ref.ly/Song5:2), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Song6:9). Elsewh. only [Psalm 56:1](https://ref.ly/Ps56:1) (title) יוֺנַת אֵלֶם רְחֹקִים (עַל) prob. name of melody: *To ‘ the dove of distant* *terebinths ’* (rd. אֵלִים for אֵלֶם; v. אֵלֶם supr. p. 48, and Bae Psalm Psalm, p. xvii).—On sanctity of dove among Shemites, v. RS Sem. i. 202, 275\.

> \[Str3124] † II. יוֺנָה **n.pr.m.** prophet, acc. to [2 K 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:25) he was הַנָּבִיא son of אֲמִתַּי, fr. גַּת הַחֵפֶר and predicted the recovery of Isr.'s territory which Jerob. II effected; he is also the principal figure of the Book of Jonah: [1:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:1) (בֶּן־אֲמִתַּי), v:[3](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:3), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:5), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:7), v:[15](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:15), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:1), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:1), [2:2](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:2), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:11), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:1), [3:3](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:3), [3:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:4), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:1), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:5), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:6), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:6), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:8), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:9).

> \[Str3238] III. הַיּוֺנָה v. יָנָה Pt. p. 413

> \[Str3120] † יָוָן **n.pr.gent.** Ionia(ns), Greece (Gk. Ἰάονες (i.e. orig. Ἴ«ονες), v. also LXX infr.; OPers. *Yauna* SpiegAPK 237; As. *Iamanu (Iavanu)* DlPa 248 ff. COTGloss; Egypt. *Ye\-va\-n\-(n)a,* v. WMM369 f. cf. also 24 ff.; v. esp. on this word StaDe Populo Javan 1880\)—*Ionians,* as traders Ez [27:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:13), LXX Ἑλλάς (named with תּוּבַל, מֶשֶׁח; in v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:12) תַּרְשִׁישׁ in v:[14](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:14) תּוֺגַרְמָה); cf. יְוָנִי (adj. gent.) infr.; as distant, [Is 66:19](https://ref.ly/Isa66:19), LXX Ἑλλάς (with תַּרְשִׁישׁ, פּוּל, לוּד, תּוּבַל and הָאִיִּים הָֽרְחֹקִים; list to be emended fr. LXX Staib. 5 ff.); called ‘son’ of Japhet [Gn 10:2](https://ref.ly/Gen10:2) (P) \= [1 Ch 1:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:5), and ‘ father ’ of אֱלִישָׁא, תַּרְשִׁישׁ, כִּתִּים and רֹדָנִים (so rd. for דֹדָנִים) [Gn 10:4](https://ref.ly/Gen10:4) (P) \= [1 Ch 1:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:7) (in these LXX Ιωυναν, v. LagBN 84 Anm. \*\*); addressed (personif.) בָּנַיִח יָוָן [Zc 9:13](https://ref.ly/Zech9:13) (opp. בניך צִיּוֺן) LXX Ἔλληνες, and so in foll.; מֶלֶח יָוָן [Dn 8:21](https://ref.ly/Dan8:21) (v. מַלְכֵי מָדַי וּפָרָס v:[20](https://ref.ly/Dan8:20)) \= *king of Greece* i.e. kingdom over which Alexander ruled, whose dominion was afterward divided (see v:[22](https://ref.ly/Dan8:22) ff.); שַר־יָוָן [10:20](https://ref.ly/Dan10:20) of the guardian\-angel of Greece (v. שַר פָּרָס va); מַלְכוּת וְדָן [11:2](https://ref.ly/Dan11:2) (vb פָּרַֹס).— Ez [27:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:19) וְיָוָן is text. err., v. וְדָן supr. p. 255:a.

> \[Str3125] † \[יְוָנִי] **adj.gent.** only c. art. as n.pr.gent.: וּבְנֵי יְרוּשָׁלַסִ מְכַרְתֶּם לִבְנֵי הַיְּוִָ\+נים [Jo 4:6](https://ref.ly/Joel4:6) (LXX Ἔλληνες) *and the sons of Jerusalem ye have sold to the sons of the Ionians* (cf. יָוָן Ez [27:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:13)).—Vid. Staib.

> \[Str3130–31] יוֺסֵף, יוֺסִפְיָה v. sub יסף. p. 415

> \[Str3132] יוֺעֵאלָה v. sub יעל. p. 418

> \[Str3139] יוֺרָה v. sub ירה. p. 435
> 
> 402:a. Before יוֺרָה ins. יוֺעָשׁ v. sub ‘יהוה; יוּעַם v. II. עמם **Hoph.**’

> \[Str3142] יוּשַׁב חֶ֫סֶד v. sub שׁוּב. p. 1000

> \[Str3144–45] יוֺשָׁה, יְשַׁוְיָה v. sub ישׁה. p. 444

## יזה (id: BDB03877)

יזה (√ whence first element of foll.; perh. cf. Ar. وَزَى *congregatus, conglomeratus fuit.,* Frey).

> \[Str3149] † \[יְזִיאֵל, יזואל] **n.pr.m.** one of David's heroes וִיזִו֯אֵל [1 Ch 12:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:3).

> \[Str3150] יִזִּיָּה v. sub נזה. p. 633

## יזן (id: BDB03880)

\[Str2109] \[יזן] **vb.** only **Pu.** *Pt.* pl. מְיֻזָּנִים [Je 5:8](https://ref.ly/Jer5:8) Qr (\< Kt מוּזָנִים v. זון); mng. dub. (comp. by Schu al. with Ar. وَزَنَ *weigh,* whence *furnished with weights,* i.e. testicles, but sense remote and very uncertain).

## יזע (id: BDB03884)

יזע (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. وَذَعَ *fluxit* aqua (Frey); NH and Aram. in deriv., v. infr.)

> \[Str3154] † \[יֶ֫זַע] **n.\[m.]** sweat (v. זֵעָה infr.)—לאֹ יַחְגְּרוּ בַָ\+יּזַע Ez [44:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:18) del. Co, q.v.

> \[Str2188] † \[זֵעָה] **n.f.** sweat (NH זִיעָה (Levy), better זֵיעָה (Jastr); Aram. דֵּיעְתָּא, ܕܽܘܥܬܳܐ)—בְּזֵעַת אַפֶּיְ תּאֹכַל לֶחֶם [Gn 3:19](https://ref.ly/Gen3:19) (J).

> \[Str3155–56] יִזְרָח, יִזְרַחְיָה v. sub זרח p. 280:b supr.

> \[Str3157] יִזְרְעֶאל v. sub זרע p. 283:a supr.

> \[Str3160] יחבה v. חֻכָּה sub חבב

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 44:18; Genesis 3:19
* **Associated Articles:** חָבַב (ID: `BDB02761@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## יָחַד (id: BDB03890)

\[Str3161] † \[יָחַד] **vb.** be united (Ar. وَحَدَ *be alone,* II. *make one;* NH Pi. יִחֵד *make or declare one;* Aram. יַחֵד *unite,* ܫܰܘܚܶܕ *set alone*)—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. [Gn 49:6](https://ref.ly/Gen49:6) בִּקְהָלָם אַל־תֵּחַד כְּבוֺדִי in their assembly let my glory *not be united* (‖בְּסֹדָם אַ־ל תָּבאֹ נַפְשִׁי); 2 fs. [Is 14:20](https://ref.ly/Isa14:20) לאֹ תֵחַד אִתָּם בִּקְבוּרָה. **Pi.** *Imv.* [Psalm 86:11](https://ref.ly/Ps86:11) יַחֵד לְבָבִי לְיִרְאָה שְׁמְֶָ *unite* my heart (i.e. concentrate its affections, cf. [Je 32:39](https://ref.ly/Jer32:39)) to fear thy name (but LXX SyrVer Vulgate Gr BI Ch יִחַדְּ (from חָדָה) *let* my heart *rejoice,* etc.)

> \[Str3173] † יָחִיד **adj.** and **subst.** only, only one, solitary (NH *id.;* Aram. יָחִיד, יְחִידַי, ܝܺܚܺܝܕܳܐ, ܝܺܚܺܝܕܳܝܳܐ *id.;* Arab. *solitary*)—**1\.** *only one,* esp. of an only son, [Gn 22:2](https://ref.ly/Gen22:2), [22:12](https://ref.ly/Gen22:12), [22:16](https://ref.ly/Gen22:16) את בנך את יְחִידְ thy son, thine *only one,* אֵבֶל יָחִיד [Am 8:10](https://ref.ly/Amos8:10), [Je 6:26](https://ref.ly/Jer6:26) mourning for *an only son,* כמספד על חיחיד [Zc 12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10), [Pr 4:3](https://ref.ly/Prov4:3) רַח וְיָחִיד לפני אמי; so fem. יְחִידָה Ju [11:34](https://ref.ly/Judg11:34). **2\.** fem. יְחִידָה as subst. [Psalm 22:21](https://ref.ly/Ps22:21), [35:17](https://ref.ly/Ps35:17) יְחִידָתִי my *only one,* poet. for *my life,* as the one unique and priceless possession which can never be replaced (in each ‖נַפְשִׁי). **3\.** *solitary,* [Psalm 25:16](https://ref.ly/Ps25:16) כי יחיד ועני אני; [68:7](https://ref.ly/Ps68:7) מוֺשִׁיב יְחִידִים בַּיְתָה causing *solitary, isolated* ones (i.e. friendless wanderers or exiles; ‖אֲסִירִים) to dwell at home (Lag Ch al. מֵשִׁיב *bringing back* home).

> \[Str3162] † יַ֫חַד:45 **n.\[m.]** unitedness. **1\.** as **subst.** only [1 Ch 12:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:17) (peculiar) יִהְיֶה־לִּי עֲלֵיכֶֹם לֵבָב לֳ\+יחַד I will have a heart toward you *for unitedness,* i.e. my heart shall be ready to become one with yours (cf. v:[38](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:38) סלב אחד). **2\.** elsewhere always in acc. as **adv.** *in union, together* (cf. Ar. وَحْدٌ in acc. with sf. *in his solitariness \= alone*)—**a.** *together,* of community in action, place, or time (oft. combined, but one usu. more prominent than the others); (1\) in action, [Jb 38:7](https://ref.ly/Job38:7) בְּרָן־יֵחַד כּוֺכְבֵי אֵל when the stars of God shouted *together;* [Ezr 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:3) we together will build (opp. to *you*), with נִלְחַם [1 S 17:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:10), נִשְׁפַּט [Is 43:26](https://ref.ly/Isa43:26); (2\) in place [1 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:11) ולא נשׁארו בם שׁנים ָ\+יחַד; [2 S 10:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:15) וַיֵּאָֽסְפוּ יָחַָד [Psalm 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:2), [31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14), [88:18](https://ref.ly/Ps88:18), [133:1](https://ref.ly/Ps133:1) שֶׁבֶת יַחַד [Is 50:8](https://ref.ly/Isa50:8), as pred. [Mi 2:12](https://ref.ly/Mic2:12) יַחַד אֲשִימֶנּוּ; (3\) in place and time at once. [2 S 21:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:9) וַיִּפְּלוּ ָ\+יחַד and they perished *together,* [14:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:16) to destroy אתי ואת בני יחד; (4\) of time alone (poet.) [Is 42:14](https://ref.ly/Isa42:14) אֶשֹּׁם וְאֶשְׁאַף יחד I will gasp and pant *together,* [45:8](https://ref.ly/Isa45:8), [Jb 6:2](https://ref.ly/Job6:2), [17:16](https://ref.ly/Job17:16), [Psalm 141:10](https://ref.ly/Ps141:10) יחד אנכי עד אעבור while I at the same time pass on. **b.** *all together, altogether* (poet. syn. of כֻּלָּם but more forcible, suggesting oft., esp. with כל, *all at once,* as well as *altogether*), [Is 44:11](https://ref.ly/Isa44:11) יפחדו יבשׁו יחד (cf. [Psalm 40:15](https://ref.ly/Ps40:15)), [Psalm 62:10](https://ref.ly/Ps62:10) הֵמָּה מֵהֶבֶל יַחַד they are *all together* (made) of vanity, [Is 22:3](https://ref.ly/Isa22:3) כל קציניך נדדו יחד (with כל also [Psalm 41:8](https://ref.ly/Ps41:8), [Jb 34:15](https://ref.ly/Job34:15)): oft. in poet. beginning a clause with emph., [Dt 33:5](https://ref.ly/Deut33:5), [Jb 3:18](https://ref.ly/Job3:18) יחד אסירים שׁאננו [16:10](https://ref.ly/Job16:10), [19:12](https://ref.ly/Job19:12) יחד יבאו גדודיו, [24:4](https://ref.ly/Job24:4), [31:38](https://ref.ly/Job31:38), [Psalm 41:8](https://ref.ly/Ps41:8), [98:8](https://ref.ly/Ps98:8), [Ho 11:7](https://ref.ly/Hos11:7) (with a neg.), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Hos11:8); in connexion with a sf., or obj. of a vb., [Is 27:4](https://ref.ly/Isa27:4) אציתנה ָ\+יחַד I will burn it *altogether,* [Psalm 33:15](https://ref.ly/Ps33:15) הַיֹּצֵר יַחַד לִבָּם who formeth the hearts of them *all together,* [74:6](https://ref.ly/Ps74:6), [74:8](https://ref.ly/Ps74:8), [Jb 10:8](https://ref.ly/Job10:8) יַחַד סָבִיב *altogether* round about (but rd. perh. with LXX SyrVer Del Di אַחַר תִסּוֺב), [40:13](https://ref.ly/Job40:13). **c.** *together,* in the sense of *alike,* the one as well as the other, [Psalm 49:3](https://ref.ly/Ps49:3), [49:11](https://ref.ly/Ps49:11) יחד כסיל ובער יאבדו [Jb 21:26](https://ref.ly/Job21:26), [34:29](https://ref.ly/Job34:29). More frequent is
> 
> 403:a. יִחַד **2** (4\): [Jb 17:16](https://ref.ly/Job17:16) rd. נֵחָת for יַחַָת (v. Addenda, on pp. 94:b, 629:a); יַחַד here will then belong to (1\).

> \[Str3162] יַחְדָּו:92 ([Je 46:12](https://ref.ly/Jer46:12), [46:21](https://ref.ly/Jer46:21) [49:3](https://ref.ly/Jer49:3) יַחְדָּיו), **adv.** together (prop. (in) *his* or *its unitednesses* (cf. Ar. وَحْدَهُ *alone;* and for the form אַשְׁרָיו), but, the orig. of the term. being forgotten, applied gen. as an adv., without regard to number, gender, or person: so Ges O1:§ 135c Sta§ 370a)—*together:* **a.** of community in action, [Psalm 34:4](https://ref.ly/Ps34:4) ונרוממה שׁמו יחדו let us exalt his name *together,* [Is 52:9](https://ref.ly/Isa52:9), with נוֺעַץ *take counsel,* [45:21](https://ref.ly/Isa45:21), [Psalm 71:10](https://ref.ly/Ps71:10), [83:6](https://ref.ly/Ps83:6), [Ne 6:7](https://ref.ly/Neh6:7); נִצָּה to *struggle* [Dt 25:11](https://ref.ly/Deut25:11); in place, as with ישׁב [Gn 13:6](https://ref.ly/Gen13:6), [Dt 25:5](https://ref.ly/Deut25:5), הלך [Gn 22:6](https://ref.ly/Gen22:6), [22:8](https://ref.ly/Gen22:8), [Am 3:3](https://ref.ly/Amos3:3), בוא [Je 3:18](https://ref.ly/Jer3:18), [Jb 9:32](https://ref.ly/Job9:32), קרב [Is 41:1](https://ref.ly/Isa41:1), נפל [2 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:16), אכל Ju [19:6](https://ref.ly/Judg19:6), [Je 41:1](https://ref.ly/Jer41:1), התקבץ [Jos 9:2](https://ref.ly/Josh9:2), נאסף Ju [6:33](https://ref.ly/Judg6:33), etc.; [Dt 22:10](https://ref.ly/Deut22:10) בשׁור ובחמור יחדו coupling pairs, and so strengthening וְ (mostly poet.), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Deut22:11), [Am 1:15](https://ref.ly/Amos1:15) הוא ושריו יחדו (cf. [Je 48:7](https://ref.ly/Jer48:7) Qr \[Kt יחד], [49:3](https://ref.ly/Jer49:3)) [Je 6:11](https://ref.ly/Jer6:11), [6:12](https://ref.ly/Jer6:12) ´שדות ונשׁים י, v:[21](https://ref.ly/Jer6:21) (accents), [13:14](https://ref.ly/Jer13:14), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Jer31:8), [31:13](https://ref.ly/Jer31:13), [50:4](https://ref.ly/Jer50:4), [50:33](https://ref.ly/Jer50:33), [Is 41:19](https://ref.ly/Isa41:19), cf. [65:7](https://ref.ly/Isa65:7); in time (rare: but v. infr.), [Psalm 4:9](https://ref.ly/Ps4:9) in peace יחדו אשׁכבה ואישׁן will I *at once* lie down and sleep (i.e. lie down and fall asleep immediately). **b.** emph. (esp.poet.) \= *all together* [1 S 31:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:6), [Is 10:8](https://ref.ly/Isa10:8) הלא שרי יחדו מלכים are not my princes *all together,* all alike, kings? [18:6](https://ref.ly/Isa18:6), [40:5](https://ref.ly/Isa40:5), [41:20](https://ref.ly/Isa41:20), [41:23](https://ref.ly/Isa41:23), [45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16) (‖כֻּלָּם), [46:2](https://ref.ly/Isa46:2) קרסו כרעו יחדו, [48:13](https://ref.ly/Isa48:13), [66:17](https://ref.ly/Isa66:17), [Psalm 14:3](https://ref.ly/Ps14:3) (‖הַכֹּל), [19:10](https://ref.ly/Ps19:10) צדקו יחדו (of J.'s ordinances), [35:26](https://ref.ly/Ps35:26), [37:38](https://ref.ly/Ps37:38); sts. (like יַחַד **b**) suggesting *all at once,* as well as *all together,* [Ex 19:8](https://ref.ly/Exod19:8) ויענו כל העם יחדו (so, with כל, [Is 22:3](https://ref.ly/Isa22:3), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3), [Zc 10:4](https://ref.ly/Zech10:4)), [Is 1:28](https://ref.ly/Isa1:28), [1:31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:31) ובערו שׁניהם יחדו. Prefixed to a clause (like יחד), [Dt 33:17](https://ref.ly/Deut33:17) (cf. יחד v:[5](https://ref.ly/Deut33:5)), [Is 9:20](https://ref.ly/Isa9:20), [11:7](https://ref.ly/Isa11:7), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Isa11:14), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3), [43:17](https://ref.ly/Isa43:17), [45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16), [52:8](https://ref.ly/Isa52:8), [Je 46:12](https://ref.ly/Jer46:12), [51:38](https://ref.ly/Jer51:38), [La 2:8](https://ref.ly/Lam2:8), [Jb 24:17](https://ref.ly/Job24:17) (strengthening לָמוֺ) **c.** *alike,* the one as well as the other, [Dt 12:22](https://ref.ly/Deut12:22) (\= [15:22](https://ref.ly/Deut15:22)) the unclean and the clean *alike* may eat it, [1 S 30:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:24) :יַחְדָּו יַחֲלֽקוּ they shall share *together,* i.e. *alike.*

> \[Str3163] † יַחְדֹּי Baer, יַחְדּוֺ van d. H, **n.pr.m.** a Gileadite, [1 Ch 5:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:14), LXX Ιουραι, A Ιεδδαι, LXXL Ιεδδω.

> \[Str3171] יְחַוְאֵל v. sub חיה. p. 313

> \[Str3170,273] † יַחְזֵרָה **n.pr.m.** a priest [1 Ch 9:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:12) (for which אַחְזַי (q.v.) [Ne 11:13](https://ref.ly/Neh11:13)), v. now also Ryle on [Ne 11:13](https://ref.ly/Neh11:13). p. 28, 306

> \[Str3171–72,3174] יְחִיאֵל, יְחִיאֵלִי, יְחִיָּה v. sub חיה. p. 313

## יָחַל (id: BDB03901)

\[Str3176] † \[יָחַל] **vb. Niph.** wait; **Pi.** await (cf. NH יִחוּל *expectation*);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נוֺחֲלָה Ez [19:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:5) (yet v. infr.); *Impf.* וַיִּיָּ֫חֶל [Gn 8:12](https://ref.ly/Gen8:12) \+ [1 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:18) Kt (v. Dr; Qr Hiph. וַיּוֺחֶל);—*wait:* [Gn 8:12](https://ref.ly/Gen8:12) *and he waited* yet seven days; so [1 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:18) Kt; of Isr. under fig. of lioness, וַתֵּרֶא כִּי נוֺחֲלָה אָֽבְדָה תִּקְוָתָהּ Ez [19:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:5) *when she saw that her hope tarried, was lost* (but dub.; Sm comp. Aram. ܐܘܚܠ *be weak,* whence perh. *sink down;* Hi (perh. after LXX) כִּי נִדַּח לָהּ *that he was thrust away from her;* Co plausibly נוֺאֲלָה that *she had acted foolishly*). **Pi.** *Pf.* 2 ms. sf. יִחַלְתָּנִי [Psalm 119:49](https://ref.ly/Ps119:49); 1 S. יִחָלְתִּי v:[48](https://ref.ly/Ps119:48) \+ 4 t. ## יָחַם (id: BDB03906)

\[Str3179] † \[יָחַם] **vb.** be hot, **Pi.** conceive (Ar. وَحَمَ v. *inclauerunt* pecora, Frey; Aram. יְחַם *be hot,* usu. of sexual impulse of animals)—**Qal** v. חמם. **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. sf. יֶחֱמַתְנִי [Psalm 51:7](https://ref.ly/Ps51:7) in sin my mother *conceived me* (‖חוֺלָלְתִּי); *Inf. cstr.* of *heat* of cattle in breeding, conception; בְּכָל־יַחֵם [Gn 30:41](https://ref.ly/Gen30:41) *at every breeding\-heat* of the flock (J); בְּעֵת יַחֵם [31:10](https://ref.ly/Gen31:10) *at the time of* the flock's *being hot in breeding* (E); sf. 3 fpl. לְיַחֲמֶנָּה [30:41](https://ref.ly/Gen30:41) *in order that they might have breeding\-heat* (J).

## יחף (id: BDB03910)

יחף (√ of foll.; orig. mng. unknown; NH Aram. יָחֵף adj. as Heb.; Syr. Aph. ܐܰܚܶܦ *discalceatus fuit;* cf. perh. Ar. حَفِىَ (transp.) *walk barefoot*, also *become chafed*, of foot or hoof).

> \[Str3182] † יָחֵף **adj.** barefoot, alw. abs. יָחֵף [Is 20:2](https://ref.ly/Isa20:2) \+ 4 t.;—הלך עָרוֺם וְיָחֵף [Is 20:2](https://ref.ly/Isa20:2) (רַגְּלְֶָ נַעַלְ תַחֲלֽץ מֵעַל in preceding cl.), v:[3](https://ref.ly/Isa20:3); הֹלֵח יָחֵף [2 S 15:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:30); נָהַג יָחֵף [Is 20:3](https://ref.ly/Isa20:3); appar. \= subst., מִנְעִי רַגְלֵח מִיָּחֵף [Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25) *withhold thy foot from bareness* (Syr. ܝܽܘܚܳܦܳܐ *discalceatio*).

> \[Str3183–85] יַחְצְאֵל, יַחֲצִיאֵל, יַחְצְאֵלִי v. sub חצה. p. 345

## יחש (id: BDB03914)

יחש (√ of foll.; meaning unknown; deriv. common in NH and Aram.)

(Ithpa.))—*Pf.* 3 mpl. הִתְיַחֲשוּ [1 Ch 5:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:17), [9:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:1); *Inf. cstr.* הִתְיַחֵש [5:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:1) \+ 5 t.; sf. הִתְיִחֲשָם [1 Ch 4:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:33) \+ 7 t.; *Pt.* pl. הַמִּתְיִחֲשִים [Ezr 2:62](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:62), [Ne 7:64](https://ref.ly/Neh7:64); —*be enrolled by genealogy* [1 Ch 5:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:17), [9:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:1), [Ne 7:5](https://ref.ly/Neh7:5); perh. also ´לְהִתְי [2 Ch 12:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:15) (tr. to [11:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:16) acc. to Hi Be, v. VB); לַבְּכֹרָה ´וְלאֹ לְהִתְי [1 Ch 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:1) *but he is not to be enrolled in the place of first born;* sq. בְּ (*among, of, consisting in*) [7:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:40), without לְ [7:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:7), [Ezr 8:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:1); הַכֹּהֲנִים ´הִתְי [2 Ch 31:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:17); *Inf. \= genealogical enrolment* [1 Ch 4:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:33) these were their dwellings and they had (לָהֶם) *genealogical enrolment*, cf. [7:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:5), [7:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:9); ´בְּהִתְי [5:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:7) *at the enrolment*, of. (לְ) their generations; הֵמָּה בְּחַצְרֵיהֶם הִתְיַחֲשָם [1 Ch 9:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:22) *as for them, in their villages was their enrolment;* nearly \= *genealogical list* [2 Ch 31:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:15) (לִזְכָרִים; i.e. the males whose names were in the list), so [Ezr 8:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:3), cf. [2 Ch 31:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:18), [31:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:19) (all sq. בְּ *among, of*) *Pt.* pl. c. art. בִּקְשׁוּ כְתָבָם הַמִּתְיַחֲשִים [Ezr 2:62](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:62) \= [Ne 7:64](https://ref.ly/Neh7:64) *they sought their writing* (their book), namely *the enrolled*, i.e. their genealogical record.

## יָטַב (id: BDB03918)

\[Str3190,2895] † \[יָטַב] **vb.** be good, well, glad, pleasing (Aram. יְטַב Zinj. יטב DHMSendsch. 57\)—**Qal** (*Pf.* not in use, v. טוֺב **vb.**) *Impf.* יִיטַב [Gn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Gen12:13) \+ 34 t., יִטַב [1 S 24:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:5) \+ 3 t.; תִּיטַב [Est 2:4](https://ref.ly/Esth2:4) \+ 2 t.; תֵּיטְבִי (incorrect for תִּיטְבִי Ges§ 70 R) [Na 3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8); pl. יִיטְבוּ [Gn 34:18](https://ref.ly/Gen34:18); for *Inf.* and *Pt.* v. טוֺב—**1\.** *be glad, joyful*, לֵב Ju [18:20](https://ref.ly/Judg18:20), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Judg19:6), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Judg19:9), [1 K 21:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:7), [Ru 3:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:7), [Ec 7:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:3). **2\.** *be well placed*, הֲתֵיטְבִי מִנּאֹ אָמוֺן [Na 3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8) *art thou better placed than No of Amon?* **3\.** impers. c. לְ, **be well for** or *with, go well with* [Gn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Gen12:13) (J), [40:14](https://ref.ly/Gen40:14) (E), [Dt 4:40](https://ref.ly/Deut4:40) [5:16](https://ref.ly/Deut5:16), [5:26](https://ref.ly/Deut5:26), [6:3](https://ref.ly/Deut6:3), [6:18](https://ref.ly/Deut6:18), [12:25](https://ref.ly/Deut12:25), [12:28](https://ref.ly/Deut12:28), [22:7](https://ref.ly/Deut22:7), [2 K 25:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:24), [Ru 3:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:1), [Je 7:23](https://ref.ly/Jer7:23), [38:20](https://ref.ly/Jer38:20), [40:9](https://ref.ly/Jer40:9), [42:6](https://ref.ly/Jer42:6). **4\.** *be pleasing* בְּעֵינֵי [Gn 34:18](https://ref.ly/Gen34:18) (J), [41:37](https://ref.ly/Gen41:37), [45:16](https://ref.ly/Gen45:16) (E), [Dt 1:23](https://ref.ly/Deut1:23), [Lv 10:19](https://ref.ly/Lev10:19), [10:20](https://ref.ly/Lev10:20), [Jos 22:30](https://ref.ly/Josh22:30), [22:33](https://ref.ly/Josh22:33) (P), [1 S 18:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:5), [24:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:5), [2 S 3:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:36), [18:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:4), [1 K 3:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:10), [Est 1:21](https://ref.ly/Esth1:21), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Esth2:4), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Esth2:4), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9); c. לְ, *be pleasing to*, [Psalm 69:32](https://ref.ly/Ps69:32); c. לִפְנֵי (late) [Ne 2:5](https://ref.ly/Neh2:5), [2:6](https://ref.ly/Neh2:6), [Est 5:14](https://ref.ly/Esth5:14). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֵיטִיב [Gn 12:16](https://ref.ly/Gen12:16), [Jos 24:20](https://ref.ly/Josh24:20); הֵיטִב [1 S 25:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31); sf. הֵיטִָבְ [Dt 30:5](https://ref.ly/Deut30:5); הֵיטַבְתָּ [Je 1:12](https://ref.ly/Jer1:12); הֵיטַבְתְּ [Ru 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:10), etc.; וְהֵטִָבֹתִ֫י Ez [36:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:11) (for הֵיטַבְתִּי as if from טוב Ges§ 70R); *Impf.* יֵיטִיב [Nu 10:32](https://ref.ly/Num10:32) \+ 3 t.; יֵיטִב [Pr 15:13](https://ref.ly/Prov15:13) \+ 2 t.; יְיֵטִיב [Jb 24:21](https://ref.ly/Job24:21) (Ges§ 70R); יֵיטֵיב [1 K 1:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:47) (Ges§ 70R); וַיִּ֫יטֶב [Ex 1:20](https://ref.ly/Exod1:20); sf. וִיטִיבְ [Ec 11:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:9), etc.; *Imv.* הֵיטִ֫יבָה [Psalm 51:20](https://ref.ly/Ps51:20); הֵיטִ֫יבִי [Is 23:16](https://ref.ly/Isa23:16), etc.; *Inf. abs.* הֵיטֵיב [Je 7:5](https://ref.ly/Jer7:5), [10:5](https://ref.ly/Jer10:5); הֵיטֵב [Gn 32:13](https://ref.ly/Gen32:13) \+ 10 t.; *Inf. cstr.* הֵיטִיב [Lv 5:4](https://ref.ly/Lev5:4) \+ 7 t.; הֵטִיב [Je 32:41](https://ref.ly/Jer32:41); sf. הֵיטִיבִי [Je 32:40](https://ref.ly/Jer32:40), etc.; *Pt.* מֵיטִיב [1 S 16:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:17); מֵטִיב [Psalm 119:68](https://ref.ly/Ps119:68); מֵטִב Ez [33:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:32), etc.;—**1\.** *make glad, rejoice* לֵב Ju [19:22](https://ref.ly/Judg19:22); פנים [Pr 15:13](https://ref.ly/Prov15:13); the person [Ec 11:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:9). **2\.** *do good to, deal well* with, a person usu. c. prep.: c. לְ, [Ex 1:20](https://ref.ly/Exod1:20), [Jos 24:20](https://ref.ly/Josh24:20) (both E), [Gn 12:16](https://ref.ly/Gen12:16), [Nu 10:29](https://ref.ly/Num10:29), [10:32](https://ref.ly/Num10:32) (all J), Ju [17:13](https://ref.ly/Judg17:13), [1 S 25:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31), [Psalm 49:19](https://ref.ly/Ps49:19), [125:4](https://ref.ly/Ps125:4); c. עִם, [Gn 32:10](https://ref.ly/Gen32:10), [32:13](https://ref.ly/Gen32:13), [32:13](https://ref.ly/Gen32:13), [Nu 10:32](https://ref.ly/Num10:32) (all J), [Mi 2:7](https://ref.ly/Mic2:7); c. acc. [Dt 8:16](https://ref.ly/Deut8:16), [28:63](https://ref.ly/Deut28:63), [30:5](https://ref.ly/Deut30:5), [1 S 2:32](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:32), [Je 18:10](https://ref.ly/Jer18:10), [32:40](https://ref.ly/Jer32:40), [32:41](https://ref.ly/Jer32:41), [Zc 8:15](https://ref.ly/Zech8:15), [Jb 24:21](https://ref.ly/Job24:21), [Psalm 51:20](https://ref.ly/Ps51:20); abs. Ez [36:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:11); opp. הֵרַע (in prov. phr., cannot *do good or ill*, \= cannot do anything at all; test of deity) [Zp 1:12](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:12), [Is 41:23](https://ref.ly/Isa41:23), [Je 10:5](https://ref.ly/Jer10:5). **3\.** *do well* or *thoroughly:* אשׁר דבּרוּ(כל)הֵטִיבוּ *they have done well* (all) *that they have spoken* [Dt 5:25](https://ref.ly/Deut5:25), [18:17](https://ref.ly/Deut18:17); על הרע כפים להיטיב [Mi 7:3](https://ref.ly/Mic7:3) *their hands are upon the evil to do it well*,i.e.*diligently, thoroughly*, lit. *make (it) good*, with play on הרע; esp. as auxil. sq. inf.; דעת ´היט [Pr 15:2](https://ref.ly/Prov15:2) \= *know well;* לראות [Je 1:12](https://ref.ly/Jer1:12) *see well;* נַגֵּן(לְ) *play well, skilfully* [1 S 16:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:17), [Is 23:16](https://ref.ly/Isa23:16), Ez [33:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:32); cf. pt. cstr. sq. subst. מֵטִבֵי צָעַד [Pr 30:29](https://ref.ly/Prov30:29) *marching well*, in a stately manner, so מֵיטִבֵי לֶכֶת v:[29](https://ref.ly/Prov30:29); inf. abs. הֵיטֵיב as adv. \= *thoroughly,* c. vbs.: כתת [Dt 9:21](https://ref.ly/Deut9:21), שׁאל [13:15](https://ref.ly/Deut13:15), דרשׁ [17:4](https://ref.ly/Deut17:4), [19:18](https://ref.ly/Deut19:18), באר [27:8](https://ref.ly/Deut27:8), שׁבּר [2 K 11:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:18). **4\.** *make a thing good, right, beautiful:* c. acc. rei: the head [2 K 9:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:30); *dress, trim:* a lamp [Ex 30:7](https://ref.ly/Exod30:7) (P); מַצֵּבוֺת [Ho 10:1](https://ref.ly/Hos10:1); גֵּהָה [Pr 17:22](https://ref.ly/Prov17:22) a glad heart *maketh a good cure;* sq. מִן compar., obj. שֵׁן [1 K 1:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:47) ( \= *make it more glorious then*); חֶסֶד [Ru 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:10); sq. דֶּרֶךְ \= *course of life or action* [Je 2:33](https://ref.ly/Jer2:33) (in order to win love); *amend* one's ways and doings [Je 7:3](https://ref.ly/Jer7:3), [18:11](https://ref.ly/Jer18:11), [26:13](https://ref.ly/Jer26:13), [35:15](https://ref.ly/Jer35:15); ´הֵיטֵיב תֵּיטִיבוּ את־ד [7:5](https://ref.ly/Jer7:5). **5\.** *do well, right,* ethically, abs.: **a.** of men, [Gn 4:7](https://ref.ly/Gen4:7), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Gen4:7) (J), [Lv 5:4](https://ref.ly/Lev5:4) (P), [Psalm 36:4](https://ref.ly/Ps36:4), [Is 1:17](https://ref.ly/Isa1:17), [Je 4:22](https://ref.ly/Jer4:22), [13:23](https://ref.ly/Jer13:23). **b.** of God, טוֺב מֵטִיב *good and doing good* [Psalm 119:68](https://ref.ly/Ps119:68); so inf. abs. as adv. הַהֵיטֵב חָרָה לך [Jon 4:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:4), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:9) *art thou rightly angry ? sq.* לי v:[9](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:9). In כִּי־יֵיטִב אֶל־אָבִי [1 S 20:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:13) vb. is appar. intrans., \= **Qal,** *if it be pleasing unto my father,* but **Hiph.** not elsewh. in this sense; point rather יִיטַב; on this and on foll. אֶ־ת v. We Dr.

> \[Str3192] † יָטְבָה **n.pr.loc.** (*pleasantness*) city prob. in Judah [2 K 21:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:19), site unknown.

> \[Str3193] † יָטְבָ֫תָה **n.pr.loc.** (pleasantness) station of Isr. in wilderness [Nu 33:33](https://ref.ly/Num33:33), [33:34](https://ref.ly/Num33:34) (P), [Dt 10:7](https://ref.ly/Deut10:7), site unknown.

> \[Str4105] † מְהֵיטַבְאֵל **n.pr.Pers.** ( \= מֵיטִיב אֵל *God benefits*)—**1\. f.** an Edomite princess [Gn 36:39](https://ref.ly/Gen36:39) (P), [1 Ch 1:50](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:50). **2\. m.** ancestor of the false prophet Shemaiah [Ne 6:10](https://ref.ly/Neh6:10).

> \[Str4315] † (מֵיטָב) **n.\[m.]** the best, only cstr. מֵיטַב and as superl.; *the best* of a thing: ´שָדֵהוּ וּמ ´מ כָּרְמוֺ [Ex 22:4](https://ref.ly/Exod22:4) (JE) *the best* of his field and *the best* of his vineyard (choicest fruit); הַצּאֹן ´מ *the best of the sheep* [1 S 15:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:9), [15:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:15); הָאָרֶץ ´בְּמ *in the best of the land* [Gn 47:6](https://ref.ly/Gen47:6), [47:11](https://ref.ly/Gen47:11) (P).

## יָכַח (id: BDB03928)

Pa וַכַּח Aph. אוֺכַח, *id.*)—**Hiph.** *Pf.* הוֺכִיחַ [Is 2:4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:4) \+ 5 t.; הֹכִיחַ [Gn 24:44](https://ref.ly/Gen24:44); הוֺכִחַ [Gn 21:25](https://ref.ly/Gen21:25); 2 ms. חֹכַחְתָּ [Gn 24:14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14); 1 s. sf. הוֺכַחְתִּיו [2 S 7:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:14); *Impf.* יוֺכִיחַ [Is 11:3](https://ref.ly/Isa11:3) \+ 5 t.; יוֺכַח [Ho 4:4](https://ref.ly/Hos4:4) \+ 4 t.; יוֺכָח [1 Ch 12:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:17); sf. יוֺכִיחֵנִי [Psalm 141:5](https://ref.ly/Ps141:5) \+ 12 t. Impf.; *Imv.* הוֺכַח [Pr 9:8](https://ref.ly/Prov9:8); *Inf. abs.* הוֺכֵחַ [Lv 19:17](https://ref.ly/Lev19:17) \+ 5 t.; cstr. הוֺכִיחַ Hb [1:12](https://ref.ly/Hab1:12); הַלְהוֺכַח [Jb 6:26](https://ref.ly/Job6:26); *Pt.* מוֺכִיחַ Ez [3:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:26) \+ 8 t.; pl. מוֺכִיחִים [Pr 24:25](https://ref.ly/Prov24:25); —**1\.** *decide, judge,* abs. [Jb 22:4](https://ref.ly/Job22:4), [Psalm 50:8](https://ref.ly/Ps50:8), [105:14](https://ref.ly/Ps105:14) \= [1 Ch 16:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:21); c. בּ obj. [2 K 19:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:4) \= [Is 37:4](https://ref.ly/Isa37:4). **b.** of man, abs. [Ho 4:4](https://ref.ly/Hos4:4), Ez [3:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:26), [Jb 6:25](https://ref.ly/Job6:25), [6:25](https://ref.ly/Job6:25), [Pr 24:25](https://ref.ly/Prov24:25), [25:12](https://ref.ly/Prov25:12); מוֺכִיחַ בַּשַּׁעַר [Am 5:10](https://ref.ly/Amos5:10), [Is 29:21](https://ref.ly/Isa29:21); c. acc. Pers. [Gn 21:25](https://ref.ly/Gen21:25) (E), [Lv 19:17](https://ref.ly/Lev19:17), [19:17](https://ref.ly/Lev19:17) (H), [Pr 9:8](https://ref.ly/Prov9:8), [28:23](https://ref.ly/Prov28:23), [Je 2:19](https://ref.ly/Jer2:19); מוֺכִיחַ אֱלוהַּ [Jb 40:2](https://ref.ly/Job40:2); acc. rei [Jb 6:26](https://ref.ly/Job6:26); c. לְ, [Pr 9:7](https://ref.ly/Prov9:7), [9:8](https://ref.ly/Prov9:8), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Prov15:12), [19:25](https://ref.ly/Prov19:25). **6\.** *correct, rebuke*, of God, abs. Hb [1:12](https://ref.ly/Hab1:12); c. acc. Pers. [Psalm 141:5](https://ref.ly/Ps141:5), [Jb 13:10](https://ref.ly/Job13:10), [13:10](https://ref.ly/Job13:10); בְּשֵׁבֶט [2 S 7:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:14); happy the man whom God corrects [Jb 5:17](https://ref.ly/Job5:17); for God loves him [Pr 3:12](https://ref.ly/Prov3:12); God is entreated not to correct in anger [Psalm 6:2](https://ref.ly/Ps6:2), [38:2](https://ref.ly/Ps38:2). **Hoph.** *Pf*. וְהוּכַח בְּמַכְאוֺב *he is chastened also with pain* [Jb 33:19](https://ref.ly/Job33:19). **Niph.** *Impf.* לְכוּ־נָא וָּֽכְחָה [Is 1:18](https://ref.ly/Isa1:18) *come now and let us reason together; Pt.* שָׁם יָשָׁר נוֺכָח עִמּוֺ [Jb 23:7](https://ref.ly/Job23:7) *there an upright man might reason with him;* f. (pass.) וְנֹכָחַת [Gn 20:16](https://ref.ly/Gen20:16) (E), but Di SS rd. וְנֹכַחַתְּ 2 fs. *Pf. and thou art set right, righted, justified.* **Hithp.** *Impf.* עִם־וִשְרָאֵל יִתְוַכָּח [Mi 6:2](https://ref.ly/Mic6:2) *with Israel he will argue* (‖עִם ´רִיב לי).

> \[Str3207] † יַלְדָּה **n.f.** girl, damsel;—abs. יִלְדָּה [Gn 34:4](https://ref.ly/Gen34:4), [Jo 4:3](https://ref.ly/Joel4:3); וִילָדוֺת [Zc 8:5](https://ref.ly/Zech8:5); —*marriageable girl:* of Dinah, daughter of Jacob [Gn 34:4](https://ref.ly/Gen34:4); opp. יֶלֶד [Zc 8:5](https://ref.ly/Zech8:5), [Jo 4:3](https://ref.ly/Joel4:3).

> \[Str3208] † יַלְדוּת **n.f.** childhood, youth;—abs. יַלְדוּת [Ec 11:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:10) (‖שַׁחֲרוּת); יַלְדוּתְֶָ [11:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:9); \= young men טַל יַלְדֻתְֶָ [Psalm 110:3](https://ref.ly/Ps110:3).

> \[Str3209] † יִלּוֺד **adj.** born. (irreg. punctuation for יָלוּד, Dr [2 S 5:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:14))—יִלּוֺד [Ex 1:22](https://ref.ly/Exod1:22), [2 S 12:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:14); יִלּֽדִים [Jos 5:5](https://ref.ly/Josh5:5), [2 S 5:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:14); יִלּוֺדִים [Je 16:3](https://ref.ly/Jer16:3); ´הַבֵּן הַיִּלּ [Ex 1:22](https://ref.ly/Exod1:22) (E), [2 S 12:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:14) (\+ לְ); בַּמִּדְבָּר ´הָעָם הַיִּלּ [Jos 5:5](https://ref.ly/Josh5:5) (D); עַל־הַבָּנִים וְעַל־הַבָּנוֺת הַיִּלּוֺדִים בַּמָּקוֺם הַזֶּה [Je 16:3](https://ref.ly/Jer16:3); הַיִּלּוֺדִים לוֺ [2 S 5:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:14).

> \[Str3211] † \[יָלִיד] **adj.** born, only *cstr.* וְלִיד [Gn 17:12](https://ref.ly/Gen17:12) \+ 4 t.; pl. יְלִידֵי [14:14](https://ref.ly/Gen14:14) \+ 6 t.; בִּילִדֵי [2 S 21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18); —*born,* esp. of slave וְלִיד בַּיִת born in (one's) house (opp. purchased by money) [Gn 17:12](https://ref.ly/Gen17:12), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Gen17:13), [17:27](https://ref.ly/Gen17:27), [Lv 22:11](https://ref.ly/Lev22:11) (all P), cf. [Je 2:14](https://ref.ly/Jer2:14) where denied of Israel; יְלִידֵי בַיִת *id.* [Gn 14:14](https://ref.ly/Gen14:14); pl. elsewh. subst. \= *children, sons* הרפה ´יל [2 S 21:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:16), [21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18) cf. הָרְפָאִים ´יל [1 Ch 20:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:4); הענק ´יל [Nu 13:22](https://ref.ly/Num13:22), [13:28](https://ref.ly/Num13:28), [Jos 15:14](https://ref.ly/Josh15:14) (all JE).

> \[Str4038] † מוֺלֶ֫דֶת **n.f.** kindred, birth, offspring;—´מ cstr. [Lv 18:9](https://ref.ly/Lev18:9), [18:11](https://ref.ly/Lev18:11); sf. מוֺלַדְתִּי [Gn 24:4](https://ref.ly/Gen24:4) \+ 3 t.; etc.; pl. sf. מוֺלְדוֺתַיִח Ez [16:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:4); מֹלְדֹתַיִח v:[3](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:3); —**1\.** *kindred* [Gn 12:1](https://ref.ly/Gen12:1) (‖ארצך, בית אביך), [24:4](https://ref.ly/Gen24:4) (‖ארץ), [Nu 10:30](https://ref.ly/Num10:30) (‖ *id.*), [Gn 31:3](https://ref.ly/Gen31:3) (‖ארץ אבותיך), [43:7](https://ref.ly/Gen43:7) (all J); ‖עַם [Est 2:10](https://ref.ly/Esth2:10), [2:20](https://ref.ly/Esth2:20), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Esth8:6); esp. ´אֶרֶץ מ *land of one's kindred* [Gn 11:28](https://ref.ly/Gen11:28), [24:7](https://ref.ly/Gen24:7) (both J), [31:13](https://ref.ly/Gen31:13) (E), [Je 22:10](https://ref.ly/Jer22:10), [46:16](https://ref.ly/Jer46:16), Ez [23:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:15), [Ru 2:11](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:11). **2\.** pl. *circumstances of birth, birth* (fig. of origin of Jerus.) מֵאֶרֶץ הַכְּנַעֲנִי ´מְכֹרֹתַיִח וּ Ez [16:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:3), cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:4). **3\.** (female) *offspring, one born* [Lv 18:9](https://ref.ly/Lev18:9), [18:9](https://ref.ly/Lev18:9), *begotten* v:[11](https://ref.ly/Lev18:11) (all H); coll. \= *issue, offspring* מוֺלַדְתְּךָ אֲשֶׁר הוֺלַ֫דְתָּ [Gn 48:6](https://ref.ly/Gen48:6) (P).

> \[Str4137] † מוֺלָדָה, מֹלָדָה **n.pr.loc.** town in Simeon ´מוֺל [Jos 19:2](https://ref.ly/Josh19:2) \= 1 Ch 28; inhabited after exile [Ne 11:26](https://ref.ly/Neh11:26); name occurs also [Jos 15:26](https://ref.ly/Josh15:26) (where prob. interpol. from [Ne 11:26](https://ref.ly/Neh11:26) v. Di); LXX Μωλαδα, etc.; identified by RobBR ii, 201 GuêrinJudêe iii, 184 ff. with Tel *Tilḥ,* 4 hours E. from Beersheba \= Μαλαθα JosAnt. xviii. 6, 2; Μαλα(α)θων, *Malatha* LagOnom. 214\. 266\. 87\. 119, v. also Di

## יָלַל (id: BDB03947)

\[Str3213] † \[יָלַל] **vb. Hiph.** howl, make a howling (onomatop.)—**Hiph.** *Pf.* וְהֵילִל consec. [Je 47:2](https://ref.ly/Jer47:2); וְהֵילִיל֫וּ consec. [Am 8:3](https://ref.ly/Amos8:3); *Impf.* יְיֵלִיל [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) \+ 3 t. (Ges§ 70\. 2, R. 2 Köi. 437, 421\); אֲיֵלִיל [Je 48:31](https://ref.ly/Jer48:31); וְאֵילִי֫לָה [Mi 1:8](https://ref.ly/Mic1:8); יְיֵלִיל֫וּ [Ho 7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14) (Köi. 421\); יְהֵילִי֫לוּ [Is 52:5](https://ref.ly/Isa52:5); תִּיִלִי֫ל [Is 65:14](https://ref.ly/Isa65:14); *Imv.* הֵילֵל Ez [21:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:17), [Zc 11:2](https://ref.ly/Zech11:2); הֵילִי֫לִי [Is 14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31), [49:3](https://ref.ly/Isa49:3) \+ [Je 48:20](https://ref.ly/Jer48:20) Kt; הֵילִי֫לוּ [Is 13:6](https://ref.ly/Isa13:6) \+ 13 t. \+ [Je 48:20](https://ref.ly/Jer48:20) Qr, \+ Ez [30:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:2) (del. LXX Co);—*utter* or *make a howling, give a howl,* in distress, ‖זעק [Ho 7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14), [Je 47:2](https://ref.ly/Jer47:2), [Is 14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31), [Je 25:34](https://ref.ly/Jer25:34), [48:20](https://ref.ly/Jer48:20), [48:31](https://ref.ly/Jer48:31) (sq. על), Ez [21:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:17); ‖צעק [Is 65:14](https://ref.ly/Isa65:14) (sq. מִן of occasion, source); ‖ספד [Mi 1:8](https://ref.ly/Mic1:8), [Je 4:8](https://ref.ly/Jer4:8), [Jo 1:13](https://ref.ly/Joel1:13); ‖הֹבִישׁ [Jo 1:11](https://ref.ly/Joel1:11); ‖בכה [1:5](https://ref.ly/Joel1:5) (cf. [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Isa15:3) \+ בֶָּכִי); sq. על [Je 51:8](https://ref.ly/Jer51:8), [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Isa15:3); We prop. ילילו for יגילו [Ho 10:5](https://ref.ly/Hos10:5) (others יחילו, v. גיל supr. p. 162\); sq. לְ [Is 16:7](https://ref.ly/Isa16:7); abs. והילילו שׁירות היכל [Am 8:3](https://ref.ly/Amos8:3) *and palace\-songs shall become howlings,* [Is 13:6](https://ref.ly/Isa13:6), [16:7](https://ref.ly/Isa16:7), [23:1](https://ref.ly/Isa23:1), [23:6](https://ref.ly/Isa23:6), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Isa23:14), [Je 48:39](https://ref.ly/Jer48:39), [49:3](https://ref.ly/Jer49:3) (Ez [30:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:2) v. supr.), [Zp 1:11](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:11), [Zc 11:2](https://ref.ly/Zech11:2), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Zech11:2); in cruel exultation [Is 52:5](https://ref.ly/Isa52:5) cf. De Che di.—On הֵילֵל בֶּן־שָׁחַר [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12) cf. sub I. הלל supr. p. 237\.

410:a. Bef. \[יָלַל] ins. ‘יָלוֺן **n.pr.m.** in Judah 1 Ch[4:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:17); LXX Αμων, A LXXL Ιαλων.’

> \[Str3214] † יְלֵל **n.\[m.]** howling (of beasts);—וּבְתֹהוּ יְלֵל יְשִׁמָֹן [Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10) *in a waste of howling of a desert (\= in the howling waste of a desert;* v. Dr).

> \[Str3215] † יְלָלָה **n.f.** howling;—וִילָלָה [Zp 1:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:10); cstr. יִֽלֲלַת [Je 25:36](https://ref.ly/Jer25:36), [Zc 11:3](https://ref.ly/Zech11:3); יִלְלָתָהּ [Is 15:8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8); —*howling* in distress [Is 15:8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8) (‖זְעָקָה), [Je 25:36](https://ref.ly/Jer25:36) (of leaders of flock, metaph. for princes; ‖קול צעקה), [Zp 1:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:10) (‖ *id.*); קול יִֽלֲלַת הָרֹעִים [Zc 11:3](https://ref.ly/Zech11:3) (רעים metaph. for princes).

> \[Str3216] יָלַֽע [Pr 20:25](https://ref.ly/Prov20:25) v. לעע. p. 534

## ילף (id: BDB03951)

ילף (√ of foll; mng. dub.; Ar. وَلَفَ III. is *conjunctus fuit* cum aliquo, etc., Frey; whence ילפת as an *accretion?* so Thes ‘ab adhaerendo’).

> \[Str3217] † יַלֶּ֫פֶת **n.f.** scab, scurf, an eruptive disease, [Lv 21:20](https://ref.ly/Lev21:20), [22:22](https://ref.ly/Lev22:22) (in both ‖גָּרָב).

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 22:22; Leviticus 21:20


## ילק (id: BDB03953)

ילק (√ of following; meaning dubious; Thes al. comp. לקק *lick;* Ar. وَلَقَ is *hasten,* etc.)

> \[Str3218] † יֶ֫לֶק **n.m.**:[Na 3:16](https://ref.ly/Nah3:16) a kind of locust, abs. יֶלֶק [Na 3:16](https://ref.ly/Nah3:16) \+ 5 t.; ָ\+ילֶק [Jo 1:4](https://ref.ly/Joel1:4) \+ 2 t.;—alw. coll.: **a.** as devouring ‖גָּזָם, אַרְבֶּה, חָסִיל, [Jo 1:4](https://ref.ly/Joel1:4), [1:4](https://ref.ly/Joel1:4), [2:25](https://ref.ly/Joel2:25); ‖אַרְבֶּה [Psalm 105:34](https://ref.ly/Ps105:34) (of Egyptian plague), cf. [Na 3:15](https://ref.ly/Nah3:15) a (but del. We). **b.** in sim. of multitude of men [Na 3:15](https://ref.ly/Nah3:15) b, [Je 51:14](https://ref.ly/Jer51:14); of horses v:[27](https://ref.ly/Jer51:27) (יֶלֶק סָמָר). **c.** in sim. of scattering and disappearing [Na 3:16](https://ref.ly/Nah3:16).

> \[Str3219] יַלְקוּט v. לקט

> \[Str3223] † יְמוּאֵל **n.pr.m.** son of Simeon [Gn 46:10](https://ref.ly/Gen46:10), [Ex 6:15](https://ref.ly/Exod6:15) (LXX Ιεμουηλ) \= נְמוּאֵל (q.v.) [Nu 26:12](https://ref.ly/Num26:12), [1 Ch 4:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:24).

> \[Str3224] † יְמִימָה **n.pr.f.** (cf. perh. Ar. هِمَاقَةٌ *dove,* nom. unit. of يَمَامٌ Frey)—daughter of Job [Jb 42:14](https://ref.ly/Job42:14).

> \[Str3229] יִמְלָה **n.pr.** v. יִמְלָא sub מלא

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 51:27; Nahum 3:15; 1 Chronicles 4:24; Jeremiah 51:14; Joel 2:25; Genesis 46:10; Job 42:14; Joel 1:4; Psalms 105:34; Numbers 26:12; Exodus 6:15; Nahum 3:16
* **Associated Articles:** לָקַט (ID: `BDB04948@BDBHebrewLexicon`); מָלֵא (ID: `BDB05311@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## ימם (id: BDB03959)

ימם (√ assumed for foll.; actual existence and meaning dubious, v. infr.)

> \[Str3220] יָם:390 **n.m.**:[Ex 14:27](https://ref.ly/Exod14:27) sea (Ph. ים; Ar. يَمٌّ; Palm. בימא *on the sea,* VogNo. 79; on As. *iâmu, (āmu), sea,* v. DlHWB 307 M\-ACD 52 HptBAS i. 171 n.)—abs. יָם [1 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:5) \+; יָ֫מָּה [Gn 28:14](https://ref.ly/Gen28:14) \+; cstr. יָם [Gn 14:3](https://ref.ly/Gen14:3) \+ 23 t., also יָ֫מָּה סוּף [Ex 10:19](https://ref.ly/Exod10:19), but alw. יַם־סוּף [Ex 13:18](https://ref.ly/Exod13:18) \+ 22 t.; sf. יַמָּהּ [Je 51:36](https://ref.ly/Jer51:36); pl. יַמִּים Ju [5:17](https://ref.ly/Judg5:17) \+ 29 t.;—*sea:* esp. **1\.** *Mediterranean* [Nu 13:29](https://ref.ly/Num13:29) (E), [34:5](https://ref.ly/Num34:5) (P), [Dt 1:7](https://ref.ly/Deut1:7) [Jos 5:1](https://ref.ly/Josh5:1), [1 K 5:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:23), [5:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:23), [Jon 1:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:4), [1:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:4) \+ oft.; the Mediterr. is called also הַיָּם הַגָּדוֺל [Nu 34:6](https://ref.ly/Num34:6), [34:7](https://ref.ly/Num34:7), [Jos 15:12](https://ref.ly/Josh15:12), [15:47](https://ref.ly/Josh15:47), Ez [48:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:28) \+, cf. וּרְחַב יָדַיִם ´גּ ´הַיּ [Psalm 104:25](https://ref.ly/Ps104:25); מְבוֺא ´הַיָּם חַגּ הַשָּׁמֶשׁ [Jos 1:4](https://ref.ly/Josh1:4), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Josh23:4) (v. As. name of Mediterr. *tiamtu rabitu ša sulmu šamši,* etc., SchrNamender Meere. 171 ff.); אֶל־מוּל הַלְּבָנוֺן ´הַגּ ´הַיּ [Jos 9:1](https://ref.ly/Josh9:1); הָאַחֲרוֺן ´הַיּ *the hinder sea* [Dt 11:24](https://ref.ly/Deut11:24), [34:2](https://ref.ly/Deut34:2), [Zc 14:8](https://ref.ly/Zech14:8), [Jo 2:20](https://ref.ly/Joel2:20) (in the last two opp. הַקַּדְמֹנִי ´הַיּ, v. infr.); of Mediterr. in particular part יָם פְּלִשְׁתִּים [Ex 23:31](https://ref.ly/Exod23:31) (JE); יָם יָפוֺ [2 Ch 2:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:15) \= יָם יָפוֺא [Ezr 3:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:7). **2\.** יַם־סוּף *\&;ls;Red Sea’* (v. סוּף) [Ex 13:18](https://ref.ly/Exod13:18) (cf. [10:19](https://ref.ly/Exod10:19)), [15:4](https://ref.ly/Exod15:4), [Nu 14:25](https://ref.ly/Num14:25), [Dt 1:40](https://ref.ly/Deut1:40), [Jos 2:10](https://ref.ly/Josh2:10), Ju [11:16](https://ref.ly/Judg11:16) \+ oft.; also הַיָּם [Ex 14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2), [14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Exod14:9), [Is 51:10](https://ref.ly/Isa51:10), [51:10](https://ref.ly/Isa51:10), [63:11](https://ref.ly/Isa63:11) \+; prob. also יָם־מִצְרַיִם [11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15); ‘ Red Sea ’ named or referred to c. 66 t.; יִם־סוּף clearly of Ælanitic Gulf [1 K 9:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:26) (cf. [2 Ch 8:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:17)). **3\.** *Dead Sea,* יָם מֶלַח *sea of salt* [Gn 14:3](https://ref.ly/Gen14:3), [Nu 34:3](https://ref.ly/Num34:3), [34:12](https://ref.ly/Num34:12), [Dt 3:17](https://ref.ly/Deut3:17), [Jos 3:16](https://ref.ly/Josh3:16), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Josh12:3), [15:2](https://ref.ly/Josh15:2), [15:5](https://ref.ly/Josh15:5), [18:19](https://ref.ly/Josh18:19); יָם הָעֲרָבָה [Dt 3:17](https://ref.ly/Deut3:17), [4:49](https://ref.ly/Deut4:49), [Jos 3:16](https://ref.ly/Josh3:16), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Josh12:3), [2 K 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:25); הַקַּדְמֹנִי ´הַי Ez [47:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:18), [Zc 14:8](https://ref.ly/Zech14:8), [Jo 2:29](https://ref.ly/Joel2:29) (v. supr.); simply יָם [Is 16:8](https://ref.ly/Isa16:8), [Je 48:32](https://ref.ly/Jer48:32); —in יָם יַעְזֵר [Je 48:32](https://ref.ly/Jer48:32) יָם is text. error, del. c. Gf Gr Che Gie. **4\.** *Sea of Galilee* יָם כִּנֶּרֶת [Nu 34:11](https://ref.ly/Num34:11), [Jos 13:27](https://ref.ly/Josh13:27); יָם כִּנְּרוֺת [12:3](https://ref.ly/Josh12:3); simply יָם [Dt 33:23](https://ref.ly/Deut33:23). **5\.** more gen. *sea,* opp. earth and (or) sky [Gn 1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26), [1:28](https://ref.ly/Gen1:28), [9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) (P), [Ex 20:11](https://ref.ly/Exod20:11) (E), [Hg 2:6](https://ref.ly/Hag2:6) \+ oft. Jb Psalm Is:2 etc.; [Am 6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12) rd. בַּבָּקָר יָם for בקרים (v. בָּקָר); *sea* as under earth [Psalm 24:2](https://ref.ly/Ps24:2) (cf. [Gn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Gen1:10), [6:11](https://ref.ly/Gen6:11), [Ex 20:5](https://ref.ly/Exod20:5) \= [Dt 5:3](https://ref.ly/Deut5:3)); fig. of *flood* of invaders, עָלָה עַל־בָּבֶל הַיָּם [Je 51:42](https://ref.ly/Jer51:42). **6\.** of a *mighty river,* the Nile [Na 3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8), [Is 19:5](https://ref.ly/Isa19:5) (‖נָהָר); cf. הַתַּנִּין אֲשֶׁר בַּיָּם [27:1](https://ref.ly/Isa27:1) and כַּתַּנִּים בַּיַּמִּים Ez [32:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2) (sim. of Pharaoh); of Euphrates [Is 21:1](https://ref.ly/Isa21:1), [Je 51:36](https://ref.ly/Jer51:36) (acc. to Che Gf al.; [Is 21:1](https://ref.ly/Isa21:1) perhaps better of Persian Gulf, v. Di). **7\.** the great basin in temple\-court, called *the sea:* וַיַּעַש אֶת־הַיָּם מוּצָק [1 K 7:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:23) \= [2 Ch 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:2); יָם הַנְּחשֶׁת [2 K 25:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:13), [1 Ch 18:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:8), [Je 52:17](https://ref.ly/Jer52:17); הַיָּם alone [1 K 7:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:24), [7:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:25) \+ 10 t. K Ch \+ [Je 52:20](https://ref.ly/Jer52:20). **8\.** combinations are: **a.** *shore of sea, sea\-shore* שְפַת הַיָּם [Jos 11:4](https://ref.ly/Josh11:4), [1 K 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:9), and in sim. [Gn 22:17](https://ref.ly/Gen22:17), Ju [7:12](https://ref.ly/Judg7:12), [1 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:5); ´חוֺף הַי [Jos 9:1](https://ref.ly/Josh9:1), Ez [25:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:16); חוֺף יַמִּים [Gn 49:13](https://ref.ly/Gen49:13), Ju [5:17](https://ref.ly/Judg5:17); cf. חֶבֶל הַיָּם [Zp 2:5](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:5) *region by the sea;* so v:[6](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:6) but dub., v. I. חֶבֶל **3\. b.** *sand of the sea (shore)* חוֺל הַיָּם (in sim.) [Gn 32:13](https://ref.ly/Gen32:13), [41:49](https://ref.ly/Gen41:49), [Ho 2:1](https://ref.ly/Hos2:1), [Is 10:22](https://ref.ly/Isa10:22) \+ oft. (v. חוֺל); חוֺל גְּבוּל לַיָּם [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22). **c.** לְשׁוֺן הַיָּם *tongue (arm* or *gulf) of sea* [Jos 15:5](https://ref.ly/Josh15:5), [18:19](https://ref.ly/Josh18:19), [Is 11:5](https://ref.ly/Isa11:5). **d.** מִיָּם עַד־יָם [Am 8:12](https://ref.ly/Amos8:12), [Zc 9:10](https://ref.ly/Zech9:10), [Psalm 72:8](https://ref.ly/Ps72:8), cf. Ez, [Zc 14:4](https://ref.ly/Zech14:4), [Dn 8:4](https://ref.ly/Dan8:4), [Psalm 107:3](https://ref.ly/Ps107:3) (מִמִּזְרָח וּמִמַּעֲרָב מִצָּפוֺן וּמִיָּם; but rd. perh. וּמִיָּמִן *from the south,* Hu Pe BI Che); opp. east [Jos 11:2](https://ref.ly/Josh11:2), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Josh16:6) \+ 16 t. > 410:b. יָם: of Mediterr. in pl. [Dn 11:45](https://ref.ly/Dan11:45) \[cf. 8 d] Hi Ke Meinh Bev Dr (world\-encircling seas Behrm).

## ימן (id: BDB03962)

\[Str541] ימן (√of foll.; found in deriv. in all cogn., but orig. mng. dubious; Thes al. comp. אמן *confirm,* whence*right hand* as the *stronger;* others fr. use of right hand in *confirming* by an oath).

> \[Str3225] I. יָמִין:137 **n.f.**:[Ex 15:6](https://ref.ly/Exod15:6) ([Pr 27:16](https://ref.ly/Prov27:16) no exc., v. Now Str) right hand (NH *id.;* SI3 מימן *on the right;* Aram. יַמִּינָא, ܝܰܡܺܝܢܳܐ; Ar. يَمِينٌ *right, right side, right hand, south;* Eth. የክኣን፡ *right hand,* ይምን፡ *right side,* Sab. ימן *right hand,* ימנת *soth,* are also quoted; As. *imnu, right, right side, on the right,* DlHWB 307\)—abs. יָמִין [Gn 13:9](https://ref.ly/Gen13:9) \+ 40 t.; cstr. יְמִין [1 S 23:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:24) \+ 19 t.; sf. יְמִינִי [Je 22:24](https://ref.ly/Jer22:24) \+ 6 t., etc.; always sg.:—**1\.** *right hand:* **a.** lit. of man, oft. opp. שמאֹול [Gn 48:13](https://ref.ly/Gen48:13), [48:14](https://ref.ly/Gen48:14) (cf. v:[18](https://ref.ly/Gen48:18)), [Dn 12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7); יָדַע בֵּין־יְמִינוֺ לִשְמאֹלוֺ [Jon 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:11) *distinguish between one's right hand and one's left;* שׁלח ימים Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26) (‖יד); as holding the lot Ez [21:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:27); also of right hand as skilful [Psalm 137:5](https://ref.ly/Ps137:5); as receiving bribes [26:10](https://ref.ly/Ps26:10) (‖יָדַיִן); used in false swearing יְמִינָם וְמִין שֶׁקֶר [144:8](https://ref.ly/Ps144:8), [144:11](https://ref.ly/Ps144:11) *their right hand is a right hand of falsehood;* oft also c. יַד, יַד־יִמִינוֺ [Gn 48:17](https://ref.ly/Gen48:17) (E) *hand of his right \= his right hand* Ju [7:20](https://ref.ly/Judg7:20), [2 S 20:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:9); אִטֵּר יַד־יְמִינוֺ Ju [3:15](https://ref.ly/Judg3:15), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Judg20:16) *bound as to one's right hand,* i.e. left\-handed. **b.** fig., of right hand as held by ´י [Is 41:13](https://ref.ly/Isa41:13), [45:1](https://ref.ly/Isa45:1); so יַד־ימיני [Psalm 73:23](https://ref.ly/Ps73:23). **c.** *right hand of* ´י, as instr. of delivering Israel [Ex 15:6](https://ref.ly/Exod15:6), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Exod15:6), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Exod15:12) \+, cf. בִּגְכוּרוֺת יֵשַׁע יְמִינוֺ [Psalm 20:7](https://ref.ly/Ps20:7); שְׁנוֺת יְמִין עֶלְיוֺן [77:11](https://ref.ly/Ps77:11); ‖זְרוֺעַ [98:1](https://ref.ly/Ps98:1); as acquiring the temple\-site [78:54](https://ref.ly/Ps78:54); as holding his servant [18:36](https://ref.ly/Ps18:36) (line om. in ‖ [2 S 22:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:36)), cf. [63:9](https://ref.ly/Ps63:9) \& בִּימִין צִדְקִי [Is 41:10](https://ref.ly/Isa41:10); as finding, lighting upon (מצא) his enemies [Psalm 21:9](https://ref.ly/Ps21:9) (‖יד); as full of צֶדֶק [48:11](https://ref.ly/Ps48:11); as dispensing blessings [16:11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:11) נעימות בימינך (cf. [Pr 3:16](https://ref.ly/Prov3:16) of wisdom, personified); used in divine oath Is [62:8](https://ref.ly/Isa62:8) (‖זְרוֺעַ). **2\.** of situation on, or direction toward, *the right:* **a.** of situation, c. מִן, מִימִינָם וּמְשְּמאֹלָם [Ex 14:22](https://ref.ly/Exod14:22), [14:29](https://ref.ly/Exod14:29) a wall *on their right hand and on their left hand;* similarly [2 S 16:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:6), [1 K 7:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:39), [7:49](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:49) \= [2 Ch 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:17) [4:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:6), [1 K 22:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:19) (\= [2 Ch 18:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:18) c. על), [2 Ch 4:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:7); [4:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:8), [Psalm 91:7](https://ref.ly/Ps91:7) (‖מִצִּדְּ); for protection [16:8](https://ref.ly/Ps16:8); מימין ל [2 K 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:13), Ez [10:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:3); c. לְ, וַתֵּשֶׁב לִימִינוֺ [1 K 2:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:19), so [Psalm 110:1](https://ref.ly/Ps110:1), [45:10](https://ref.ly/Ps45:10), [109:31](https://ref.ly/Ps109:31); c. עַ־ל [Zc 3:1](https://ref.ly/Zech3:1), [1 Ch 6:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:24), [2 Ch 18:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:18) (\= [1 K 22:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:19) c. מן), [Ne 8:4](https://ref.ly/Neh8:4), [Jb 30:12](https://ref.ly/Job30:12), [Psalm 109:6](https://ref.ly/Ps109:6) cf. [110:5](https://ref.ly/Ps110:5); without prep. יְמִין הָעִיר [2 S 24:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:5), [Jb 23:9](https://ref.ly/Job23:9). **b.** of direction toward, lit. נָטָה ימין [Nu 20:17](https://ref.ly/Num20:17), [22:26](https://ref.ly/Num22:26) (both JE); סוּר ימין [Dt 2:27](https://ref.ly/Deut2:27), [1 S 6:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:12); ´פָּרַץ ימין וש [Is 54:3](https://ref.ly/Isa54:3); also (vb. הִפָּרֶד in prev. cl.) [Gn 13:9](https://ref.ly/Gen13:9); הַבֵּיט ימין [Psalm 142:5](https://ref.ly/Ps142:5); and *looking* is implied also in Ez [1:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:10) (c. אֶ־ל; opp. מֵהַשְּמאֹול); c. עַל, על הַיָּמִין (לָלֶכֶת)נטה [2 S 2:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:19), [2:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:21); פנה על־ימין [Gn 24:49](https://ref.ly/Gen24:49); sq. אֶל, of course (הלך) of boundary [Jos 17:1](https://ref.ly/Josh17:1); c. לְ, לַיָמִין מֵעַל לַחוֺמָה [Ne 12:31](https://ref.ly/Neh12:31) (vb. om., *went) to the right above the wall;* fig. of moral deviation, ימִין…סוּר מִן־הַדָּבָר וּשְמאֹול [Dt 17:11](https://ref.ly/Deut17:11), so [5:29](https://ref.ly/Deut5:29), [17:20](https://ref.ly/Deut17:20), [28:14](https://ref.ly/Deut28:14), [Jos 1:7](https://ref.ly/Josh1:7), [23:6](https://ref.ly/Josh23:6), [2 K 22:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:2) \= [2 Ch 34:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:2); ´נטה ימין וש [Pr 4:27](https://ref.ly/Prov4:27); also (very late) of *the right* as morally good, לֵב חָכָן לִימִינוֺ (opp. שמאל) [Ec 10:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:2) De Reuss Now. † **3\.** of other parts of the body, besides hand (v. יַד־ימינו etc. supr.): *right* thigh יֶרֶךְ יְמִינוֺ Ju [3:16](https://ref.ly/Judg3:16), [3:24](https://ref.ly/Judg3:24); eye עֵין יָמִין [1 S 11:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:2); עין ימינו [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17); esp. שׁוֺק הַיָּמִין i.e. *the right* (upper) *leg* of sacrificial animal [Ex 29:22](https://ref.ly/Exod29:22), [Lv 7:32](https://ref.ly/Lev7:32), [7:33](https://ref.ly/Lev7:33), [8:25](https://ref.ly/Lev8:25), [8:26](https://ref.ly/Lev8:26), [9:21](https://ref.ly/Lev9:21), [Nu 18:18](https://ref.ly/Num18:18). † **4\.** \= *south,* because when facing east the right hand is toward the south: מִימִין הַיְשִׁימוֺן [1 S 23:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:19); אֶל־יְמִין v:[24](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:24); ישׁב מִימִינֵךְ Ez [16:46](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:46) (‖על־שמאוֺלך); צָפוֺן וְיָמִין [Psalm 89:13](https://ref.ly/Ps89:13); perh. also [107:3](https://ref.ly/Ps107:3), v. יָם **9\.**

> \[Str3227] † I. יְמִינִי **adj.** Kt *right hand, on the right:* שֵׁם הימיגי יָכִין [2 Ch 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:17) the name of *the one on the right* was Yakin; צִדְּ הימיני Ez [4:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:6) *thy right side* (Qr in both הַיְמָנִי, v. infr.)

> \[Str3228,1145] † II. יְמִינִי **adj. gent.** fr. בִּנְיָמִן, abbrev. for בֶּן־יְמִינִי (q.v.);—אֶרֶץ יְמִינִי [1 S 9:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:4); אִישׁ יְמִינִי [2 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1), [Est 2:5](https://ref.ly/Esth2:5); בֶּן־אִישׁ יְמִינִי [1 S 9:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:1).

> \[Str541,3231] † II. \[יָמַן] **vb. denom. Hiph.** go to or choose the right, use the right hand;—*Impf.* 1 s. coh. יְאֵימִ֫נָה [Gn 13:10](https://ref.ly/Gen13:10) (J) *then I will go to the right* (opp. וְאַשְמְאִילָה); 2 mpl. תַּאֲמִינוּ [Is 30:21](https://ref.ly/Isa30:21), fig. of turning aside from right course of life (‖תַּשְמְאִילוּ); *Inf. cstr.* in phr. אִם־אִשׁ לְהֵמִין וּלְהַשְמִיל מִכֹּל אֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר אֲדֹנִי הַמֶּלֶח [2 S 14:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:19) it is surely impossible *to turn the right or to the left,* etc.; *Imv.* fs. הֵימִ֫ינִי Ez [21:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:21) *go to the right!* (opp. הַשְמִ֫ילִי); *Pt.* pl. *use the right hand:* מַיְמִינִים וּמִשְמִאלִים בָּאֲבָנִים וּבַחִצִּים בַּקָּשֶׁת [1 Ch 12:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:2) *using the right hand and the left hand with stones and with arrows in the bow* (i.e. in throwing and shooting).

> \[Str3233] † יְמָנִי **adj.** right hand, right—m. of pillar הָעַמּוּד הַיְמָנִי [1 K 7:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:21) \= הַיְמָנִי in ‖ [2 Ch 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:17) Qr (Kt הימיני); f. of side of house (temple): כֶּתֶף הַבַּיִת הַיְמָנִתַ [1 K 6:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:8), [7:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:39) (‖כֶּתֶף הבית מִיָּמִין), [2 K 11:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:11) (opp. הַשְּמָאלִית), Ez [47:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:1), cf. v:[2](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:2), [2 Ch 4:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:10), [23:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:10) (opp. הַשְּמָאלִית); elsewhere of parts of body (P): *right ear, hand and foot* אזן הַימָנִית\[ה] \+ ´הַי (ם)יד \+ ´הַי (ם)רגל [Ex 29:20](https://ref.ly/Exod29:20), [Lv 8:23](https://ref.ly/Lev8:23), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Lev8:24), [14:14](https://ref.ly/Lev14:14), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Lev14:17), [14:25](https://ref.ly/Lev14:25), [14:28](https://ref.ly/Lev14:28); *right finger* ´אֶצְבָּעוֺ הַי [14:16](https://ref.ly/Lev14:16), [14:27](https://ref.ly/Lev14:27); cf. also *right side,* m. צִדְּ הַיְמָנִי Ez [4:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:6) Qr (Kt הימיני).

> \[Str3226] † II. יָמִין **n.pr.m. 1\.** son of Simeon [Gn 46:10](https://ref.ly/Gen46:10), [Ex 6:15](https://ref.ly/Exod6:15), [Nu 26:12](https://ref.ly/Num26:12), [1 Ch 4:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:24). **2\.** man of Judah [1 Ch 2:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:27). **3\.** Levite (?) name, time of Ezra [Ne 8:7](https://ref.ly/Neh8:7).

> \[Str3228] † יָֽמִינִי **adj. gent.** of II. ימין **1;** only c. art. as subst. coll. [Nu 26:12](https://ref.ly/Num26:12).

> \[Str3232] † יִמְנָה **n.pr.m.** (cf. perh. Ar. يُمْنٌ *good fortune,* يَمْنَةٌ *right side*)—**1\.** a son of Asher [Gn 46:17](https://ref.ly/Gen46:17), [Nu 26:44](https://ref.ly/Num26:44), also (for adj. gent.) v:[44](https://ref.ly/Num26:44) \= the family of *the Yimnites* [1 Ch 7:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:30). **2\.** a Levite [2 Ch 31:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:14).

Anthrop. Ges. 1878, 390\)—abs. ´ת [Zc 9:14](https://ref.ly/Zech9:14) \+ 6 t. (´הַתּ only [Zc 6:6](https://ref.ly/Zech6:6)); תֵּמָן [Jb 9:9](https://ref.ly/Job9:9); c. ה loc. תֵּימָ֫נָה [Ex 26:18](https://ref.ly/Exod26:18) \+ 12 t.;—**1\. a.** *south, southern quarter* of sky, לְתֵימָן [Jb 39:26](https://ref.ly/Job39:26) (of flight of bird); of constellations ´חַדְרֵי ת [9:9](https://ref.ly/Job9:9); ´סַעֲרוֺת ת [Zc 9:14](https://ref.ly/Zech9:14) *whirlwinds of the south;* of territory, *the south* [Is 43:6](https://ref.ly/Isa43:6) (opp. צָפוֺן), also ´אֶרֶץ הַתּ [Zc 6:6](https://ref.ly/Zech6:6) (opp. אֶרֶץ צָפוֺן); ´נֶגְכָּה מִקְצֵה ת [Jos 15:1](https://ref.ly/Josh15:1) *southward at the end of the south,* i.e. in the remotest south (of the land);—see also II. ´ת. **b.** c. ה loc. *toward the south,* esp. in topograph. description (oft. with other points of compass), פְּאַת בֶ֫גְבָּה תֵּימָ֫נָה [Ex 26:18](https://ref.ly/Exod26:18) (P), ´נ ´ת ´פ Ez [47:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:19), ´נֶגֶב ת ´פ [Ex 27:9](https://ref.ly/Exod27:9), [36:33](https://ref.ly/Exod36:33), [38:9](https://ref.ly/Exod38:9) (all P), Ez [47:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:19), [48:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:28); \+ צלע *side* [Ex 26:35](https://ref.ly/Exod26:35), ירך [Nu 3:29](https://ref.ly/Num3:29) (both P); also [Nu 2:10](https://ref.ly/Num2:10), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Num10:6) (P); וּמִזְרָחָה ´יָ֫מָּה וְחָםפֹ֫נָה וְת [Dt 3:27](https://ref.ly/Deut3:27); ה loc. in redundant in דֶּרֶח תֵּימָ֫נָה Ez [21:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:2) turn they face *southward* (‖דָּרוֺם and נֶגֶב). **2\.** *south wind* [Psalm 78:26](https://ref.ly/Ps78:26) (קָדִים in ‖ cl.), Ct [4:16](https://ref.ly/Song4:16) (צָפוֺן in ‖ cl.)

> \[Str8487] † II. תֵּימָן **n.pr.** (**m.** et) **loc.** a N. district of Edom, poet. often \= Edom [Am 1:12](https://ref.ly/Amos1:12) (‖אֱדוֺם v:[11](https://ref.ly/Amos1:11), and בָּצְרָה), Ob:[9](https://ref.ly/Obad1:9) (‖הַר־עֵשָו), [Je 49:7](https://ref.ly/Jer49:7), [49:20](https://ref.ly/Jer49:20), Ez [25:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:13) (all ‖אֱדוֺם); Hb [3:3](https://ref.ly/Hab3:3) (‖הַר־פָּארָן); prob. also [Jos 12:3](https://ref.ly/Josh12:3), [13:4](https://ref.ly/Josh13:4) (both D); in [Gn 3:6](https://ref.ly/Gen3:6) (P) as name of an Edomite chief, son of אֱלִיפַז and grandson of Esau v:[11](https://ref.ly/Gen3:11) \= [1 Ch 1:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:36); v:[42](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:42) \= [1 Ch 1:53](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:53); so תֵּו֯מָן [Gn 36:15](https://ref.ly/Gen36:15).—Vid. further BuhlEdomiter 30\.

## ימר (id: BDB03976)

\[Str3235] ימר **vb.** assumed in Thes to expl. **Hiph.** הֵימִיר \= הֵמִיר [Je 2:11](https://ref.ly/Jer2:11), and **Hithp.** תִּתְיַמָּרוּ [Is 61:6](https://ref.ly/Isa61:6); but on הימיר v. מור (Köi. 457\), and תִּתְיַמָּרוּ is prob. from אדר q.v. supr. p. 56:b.

> \[Str3236] יִמְרָה v. sub מרה. p. 598

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:6; Jeremiah 2:11

## יָנָה (id: BDB03979)

\[Str3238] † \[יָנָה] **vb.** suppress (?), **oppress, maltreat** (NH Hiph. *vex* with words; so Aram. Aph. אוֺנִי, but also Targum for Heb. הוֺנָה *maltreat;* Ar. وَنَى is *laxus, debilis fuit,* IV. *debilitavit, defatigavit,* but connex. dub.)—**Qal** *Impf.* 1 pl. sf. נִינָם [Psalm 74:8](https://ref.ly/Ps74:8); *Pt.* יוֺנָה [Je 25:38](https://ref.ly/Jer25:38) \+ 3 t. \+ [Psalm 123:4](https://ref.ly/Ps123:4) (v. infr.);—*suppress* (?) sq. acc. [Psalm 74:8](https://ref.ly/Ps74:8) (text dub.); elsewhere *oppress; Pt.* abs. as adj. הָעִיר הַיּוֺנָה [Zp 3:1](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:1); subst. f. coll. הַיּוֺנָה *oppressors* [Je 25:38](https://ref.ly/Jer25:38) (but rd. חֶרֶב for חֲרוֺן, with LXX Ew Hi Gf Ke etc., as [46:16](https://ref.ly/Jer46:16), [50:16](https://ref.ly/Jer50:16)); [Psalm 123:4](https://ref.ly/Ps123:4) Qr לִגְאֵי יוֺנִים *proudest oppressors* (st. cstr. in superlat., cf. Ges§ 133, 3, R. 1; yet most follow Kt לּגּאייונים, and der. from גַּאֲיוֺן q.v. p. 145 supr.) **Hiph.** *Pf.* הוֺנָה Ez [18:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:12), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:16); הוֺנוּ [22:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:7), [22:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:29); *Impf.* יוֺנֶה [18:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:7); 2 ms. תּיֹנֶה [Ex 22:20](https://ref.ly/Exod22:20); תּוֺנֶנּוּ [Dt 23:17](https://ref.ly/Deut23:17); יוֺנוּ Ez [45:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:8); תּוֺנוּ [Lv 19:33](https://ref.ly/Lev19:33) \+ 2 t.; תֹּנוּ [Je 22:3](https://ref.ly/Jer22:3); *Inf.* sf. הוֺנֹתָם Ez [46:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:18); *Pt.* מוֺנַ֫יִךְ [Is 49:26](https://ref.ly/Isa49:26); —*oppress, maltreat,* sq. acc., esp. of ill\-treatment of poor and weak, partic. of the גֵּר, the ‘stranger,’ sojourner, by the rich and powerful, [Ex 22:20](https://ref.ly/Exod22:20) (JE), [Lv 19:33](https://ref.ly/Lev19:33), [25:14](https://ref.ly/Lev25:14), [25:17](https://ref.ly/Lev25:17) (H), [Dt 23:17](https://ref.ly/Deut23:17), [Je 22:3](https://ref.ly/Jer22:3), Ez [18:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:7), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:12), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:16), [22:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:7), [22:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:29), [45:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:8), [46:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:18); of a foreign oppressor only [Is 49:26](https://ref.ly/Isa49:26).

> \[Str3239,3241] יָנוֺחַ v. נוח; יָנוּם v. נום–30

## יָנַק (id: BDB03981)

\[Str3243,5143] † \[יָנַק] **vb.** suck (NH *id.;* Aram. יְנַק, ܝܺܢܰܩ, and deriv.; As. *êniḳu, suck,* SASmAsrb. i, Gloss, *mušeniḳtu, nurse,* JenZA, 1886, 402\)—**Qal** *Pf.* וְיָנַקְתְּ consec. [Is 60:16](https://ref.ly/Isa60:16); וִינַקְתֶּם [66:12](https://ref.ly/Isa66:12); *Impf.* יִיָ\+נק [Jb 20:16](https://ref.ly/Job20:16); אִיָ\+נק [3:12](https://ref.ly/Job3:12); תִּיָ\+נקִי [Is 60:16](https://ref.ly/Isa60:16); יִיָ\+נקוּ [Dt 33:19](https://ref.ly/Deut33:19); תִּינְקוּ [Is 66:11](https://ref.ly/Isa66:11); *Pt.* יוֺנֵק [Dt 32:25](https://ref.ly/Deut32:25) \+ 7 t.; יֹנֵק [Nu 11:12](https://ref.ly/Num11:12); יוֺנְקִים [Psalm 8:3](https://ref.ly/Ps8:3); יוֺנְקֵי [Jo 2:16](https://ref.ly/Joel2:16); —*suck,* of infant at mother's breast, abs. [Jb 3:12](https://ref.ly/Job3:12); metaph. of abundance and honour of Jerusalem in future, [Is 66:11](https://ref.ly/Isa66:11) abs.; sq. acc. of breast [60:16](https://ref.ly/Isa60:16), acc. of milk v:[16](https://ref.ly/Isa60:16), [66:12](https://ref.ly/Isa66:12); sq. acc. ראֹשׁ פְּתָנִים [Jb 20:16](https://ref.ly/Job20:16) of punishment of wicked; pt. sq. acc. יוֺנֵק שְׁדֵי אִמִּי Ct [8:1](https://ref.ly/Song8:1); cstr. יוֺנְקֵי־שָׁדַיִם [Jo 2:16](https://ref.ly/Joel2:16); elsewh. as subt., *suckling, babe* [Nu 11:12](https://ref.ly/Num11:12), [Dt 32:25](https://ref.ly/Deut32:25) (opp. אִישׁ שֵיבָה); ‖עוֺלֵל [1 S 15:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:3), [22:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:19), [Psalm 8:3](https://ref.ly/Ps8:3), [Je 44:7](https://ref.ly/Jer44:7), [La 2:11](https://ref.ly/Lam2:11); ‖עוֺלָל [4:4](https://ref.ly/Lam4:4); cf. [Jo 2:16](https://ref.ly/Joel2:16); ‖גָּמוּל [Is 11:8](https://ref.ly/Isa11:8); —on [Is 53:2](https://ref.ly/Isa53:2) v. יוֺנֵק infr. **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֵינִ֫יקָה [Gn 21:7](https://ref.ly/Gen21:7); הֵנִ֫יקוּ [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3); *Impf.* sf. וַיֵּנִקֵ֫הוּ [Dt 32:13](https://ref.ly/Deut32:13); 3 fs. תֵּינִק [Ex 2:7](https://ref.ly/Exod2:7); וַתֵּ֫נֶק [1 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:23); וַתְּנִיקֵ֫הוּ [Ex 2:9](https://ref.ly/Exod2:9); *Imv.* fs. sf. הֵינִקִ֫הוּ v:[9](https://ref.ly/Exod2:9); *Inf.* לְהֵינִיק [1 K 3:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:21); *Pt.* מֵינֶ֫קֶת [Ex 2:7](https://ref.ly/Exod2:7); cstr. *id.* [Gn 35:8](https://ref.ly/Gen35:8); מֵנִקְתּוֺ [2 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:2); מֵינִקְתּוֺ [2 Ch 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:11); מֵנִקְתָּהּ [Gn 24:59](https://ref.ly/Gen24:59); מֵינִיקוֺת [32:16](https://ref.ly/Gen32:16); מֵינִיקִתַיִח [Is 49:23](https://ref.ly/Isa49:23); —*give suck to, nurse,* sq. acc. [Gn 21:7](https://ref.ly/Gen21:7), [1 K 3:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:21), [Ex 2:7](https://ref.ly/Exod2:7), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Exod2:9), [1 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:23); cause to suck honey, fig. [Dt 32:13](https://ref.ly/Deut32:13) (2 acc.); of animals [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3) sq. acc.; pt. fem.\= *nursing,* אִשָּׁה מֵינֶקֶת [Ex 2:7](https://ref.ly/Exod2:7) lit. *a nursing woman, a nurse;* ´מ alone as subst. \= *nurse* [Gn 24:59](https://ref.ly/Gen24:59) (J), [35:8](https://ref.ly/Gen35:8) (E; דְּבֹרָה), [2 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:2), [2 Ch 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:11); metaph. [Is 49:23](https://ref.ly/Isa49:23); of camels גְּמַלִּים מֵינִיקוֺת [Gn 32:16](https://ref.ly/Gen32:16).

> \[Str3126] † יוֺנֵק **n.m.** young plant, sapling (*sucker*)—[Is 53:2](https://ref.ly/Isa53:2) (‖שרֶשׁ) in sim. of the suffering servant of ´י.

> \[Str3127] † \[יוֺנֶ֫קֶת] **n.f.** young shoot, twig (\= foregoing)—יוֺנַקְתּוֺ [Jb 8:16](https://ref.ly/Job8:16) \+ 2 t.; יוֺנְקוֺתָיו [Ho 14:7](https://ref.ly/Hos14:7); יֹנְקוֺתָיו Ez [17:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:22); יוֺנְקוֺתֶיהָ [Psalm 80:12](https://ref.ly/Ps80:12); —*shoot, twig,* of tree [Jb 14:7](https://ref.ly/Job14:7); cf wicked under fig. of tree [Jb 8:16](https://ref.ly/Job8:16), [15:30](https://ref.ly/Job15:30); Israel under fig. of olive\-tree [Ho 14:7](https://ref.ly/Hos14:7), of cedar Ez [17:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:22), of vine [Psalm 80:12](https://ref.ly/Ps80:12).

> \[Str3242] † \[יְנִיקָה] **n.f.** id., only pl. sf. יְנִיקוֺתָיו Ez [17:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:4) of Israel under figure of cedar.

> \[Str3244] יַנְשׁוּף, יַנְשׁוֺף v. sub נשׁף. p. 676

## יָסַד (id: BDB03986)

\[Str3245] † יָסַד **vb.** establish, found, fix (NH *id.,* esp. Pi.; Aram. יְסַד; perh. orig. *fix firm* or *close;* Ar. وِسَادٌ *pillow;* vb. denom. II. *fix as a pillow against one;* v. *lean against, recline,* Lane2940 Saad [Gn 28:11](https://ref.ly/Gen28:11); in Heb. usu. *fix firm* so as to *found*)—**Qal** *Pf.* יָסַד [Psalm 104:5](https://ref.ly/Ps104:5), [Pr 3:19](https://ref.ly/Prov3:19); sf. יְסָדָהּ [Psalm 24:2](https://ref.ly/Ps24:2) \+ 3 t.; 3 fs. יָסְֽדָה [Is 48:13](https://ref.ly/Isa48:13); יָסַדְתָּ [Psalm 104:8](https://ref.ly/Ps104:8); יָסַָ֫דְתָּ [102:26](https://ref.ly/Ps102:26); sf. יְסַדְתּוֺ [Hab 1:12](https://ref.ly/Hab1:12); יְסַדְתָּם [Psalm 89:12](https://ref.ly/Ps89:12), [119:152](https://ref.ly/Ps119:152); וִיסַדְתִּיךְ consec. [Is 54:11](https://ref.ly/Isa54:11); *Inf.* וִיסוֺד [2 Ch 24:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:27); לִסֻּוֺד [31:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:7) (v. Ges§ 69, 2, R. 1; Baer לְיִסּוֺד); לִיסֹד [Is 51:16](https://ref.ly/Isa51:16); יָסְדִי [Jb 38:4](https://ref.ly/Job38:4); יָסְדוֺ [Ezr 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:12); *Pt.* יִסֵד [Is 51:13](https://ref.ly/Isa51:13), [Zc 12:1](https://ref.ly/Zech12:1) \+ [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16) (Checrit. n., MT יִסַּד);—*found, establish,* c. acc., the earth [Psalm 24:2](https://ref.ly/Ps24:2) (‖כונן), cf. [78:69](https://ref.ly/Ps78:69) (obj. om.), [89:12](https://ref.ly/Ps89:12), [102:26](https://ref.ly/Ps102:26), [104:5](https://ref.ly/Ps104:5) cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Ps104:8) (sq. לְ), [Pr 3:19](https://ref.ly/Prov3:19), [Is 48:13](https://ref.ly/Isa48:13), [Jb 38:4](https://ref.ly/Job38:4), [Is 51:13](https://ref.ly/Isa51:13), [51:16](https://ref.ly/Isa51:16), [Zc 12:1](https://ref.ly/Zech12:1), vault of heaven [Am 9:6](https://ref.ly/Amos9:6); of *founding* the second temple [Ezr 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:12), of restoration under Joash, Inf. יְסוֺד [2 Ch 24:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:27); metaph. of future Israel [Is 54:11](https://ref.ly/Isa54:11); of ´י, *establish* the Chaldaean (as his instrument) להוכיח for correction Hb [1:12](https://ref.ly/Hab1:12) (‖שִׁים), his commandments [Psalm 119:152](https://ref.ly/Ps119:152); in weakened sense (si vera l.) יָסַד לְצִיִּים [Is 23:13](https://ref.ly/Isa23:13) *appoint* a city for desert\-creatures (on meaning in context, v. Di); *appoint, fix* [Ezr 7:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:9) emend. (v. יְסֻד); *found, begin*, heaps of offerings in ´י's house [2 Ch 31:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:7). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. נוֺסְדוּ [Psalm 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:2); *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּוָּסֵד [Is 44:28](https://ref.ly/Isa44:28); *Inf.* הִוָּסְֽדָהּ [Ex 9:18](https://ref.ly/Exod9:18); הִיָּסְֽדָם [Psalm 31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14); —**1\.** *fix or seat themselves close together sit in conclave* [Psalm 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:2), [31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14) ( \> Bae from ܣܘܳܕܳܐ, δμιλἰα ), but Lag's emend. נוֺעֲדוּ is prob. (cf. [Ne 6:2](https://ref.ly/Neh6:2)). **2\.** *be founded,* [Is 44:28](https://ref.ly/Isa44:28) (temple), [Ex 9:18](https://ref.ly/Exod9:18) (Egypt). **Pi.** *Pf.* יִסַּד [Is 14:32](https://ref.ly/Isa14:32) \+ 3 t.; יִסְּדָהּ [1 K 16:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:34); יִסַּ֫דְתָּ [Psalm 8:3](https://ref.ly/Ps8:3); יִסְּדוּ [Zc 4:9](https://ref.ly/Zech4:9), [Ezr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:10); *Impf.* יְיַסְּדֶנָּה [Jos 6:26](https://ref.ly/Josh6:26); *Inf.* לְיַסֵּד [1 K 5:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:31); —**1\.** *found* (\= **Qal**), sq. act., city [Jos 6:26](https://ref.ly/Josh6:26) (JE), [1 K 16:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:34), temple [1 K 5:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:31), [Zc 4:9](https://ref.ly/Zech4:9), [Ezr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:10); Zion [Is 14:32](https://ref.ly/Isa14:32), [28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16) הִנְנִי יִסַּד (rd. יֹסֵד; v. supr.) **2\.** *establish* [Psalm 8:3](https://ref.ly/Ps8:3) sq. עֹז; *appoint, ordain* (late) [1 Ch 9:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:22) (sq. acc.), [Est 1:8](https://ref.ly/Esth1:8) (abs.) **Pu.** *Pf.*, יֻסַּד [1 K 6:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:37) \+ 2 t.; יֻסָּד [Ezr 3:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:6) *Pt.* מְיֻסָּד [1 K 7:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:10); מְיֻסָּדִים Ct [5:15](https://ref.ly/Song5:15); *be founded, be laid, of a foundation* [1 K 6:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:37), [Ezr 3:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:6), [Hg 2:18](https://ref.ly/Hag2:18), [Zc 8:9](https://ref.ly/Zech8:9); *Pt.* \= subst. foundation [1 K 7:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:10), cf. מוסד infr.; pillars set on bases, sim. of legs Ct [5:15](https://ref.ly/Song5:15). **Hoph.** *be founded: Inf.* הוּסַד [2 Ch 3:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:3), [Ezr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:11); *Pt.* מוּסָּד [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16); —*the being founded, founding,* ´עַל הוּסַד בֵּית י [Ezr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:11) *because of the founding (laying the foundation) of the house of* ´י; וְאֵלֶּה ´הוּסַד שְׁלֽמֹה לִבְנוֺת וגו [2 Ch 3:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:3) and this is (lit. these are) *the founding of Solomon to build* the house of God, i.e. *this is the foundation which Solomon laid for building,* etc.; *Pt.* מוּסָד מוּסָּד [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16) *a foundation founded,* i.e. *well laid* (cf. חֵפֶש מְחֻפָּש, חָכָם מְחֻכָּם).

> \[Str3246] † יְסֻד **n.\[m.]** foundation, beginning, [Ezr 7:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:9) יְסֻד הַמַּעֲלָה, but rd. rather, c. LXX, יָסַד (הוּא \= Ezra subj.), v. Ry.

> \[Str3247] † יְסוֺד **n.f.**:Ezr 13:17 (**m.**:[Jb 22:16](https://ref.ly/Job22:16)) foundation, base, abs. יְסוֺד Hb [3:13](https://ref.ly/Hab3:13) \+ 2 t.; cstr. *id.* [Ex 29:12](https://ref.ly/Exod29:12) \+ 9 t.; sf. יְסֹדוֺ; Ez [13:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:14); יְסוֺדָם [Jb 4:19](https://ref.ly/Job4:19), [22:16](https://ref.ly/Job22:16); וִיסֹדֶיהָ [Mi 1:6](https://ref.ly/Mic1:6); יְסֹדֹתֶיהָ [La 4:11](https://ref.ly/Lam4:11); יְסוֺדוֺתֶיהָ Ez [30:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:4); —**1\.** *foundation of city (wall)* [Mi 1:6](https://ref.ly/Mic1:6), [Psalm 137:7](https://ref.ly/Ps137:7), cf. ## יָסַר (id: BDB04000)

\[Str3256] † \[יָסַר] **vb.** discipline, chasten, admonish (Talm. Targum סּוֺר(י)יִ *chastisement;* but Aram. יְסַר *is bind* )—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 m. sf. ו֔יִסְּרֵנִי [Is 8:11](https://ref.ly/Isa8:11) (Di De SS see Bo:§ 1103 Ew§ 249 d, but Thes MV Che al. **Pi.** Pf.); 1 s. sf. וְאֶסְֳּרֵם [Ho 10:10](https://ref.ly/Hos10:10) (Ges§ 71\); *Inf. abs.* יָסֹר [1 Ch 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:22) (noun Ke, point as pt. Öt); *Pt.* יֹסֵר [Pr 9:7](https://ref.ly/Prov9:7), [Psalm 94:10](https://ref.ly/Ps94:10); יסורי [Je 17:13](https://ref.ly/Jer17:13) Kt v. סוּר;—**1\.** *admonish* [Is 8:11](https://ref.ly/Isa8:11) (sq. sf. \+ מִלֶּכֶת *away from walking*, i. e. not to walk), [Pr 9:7](https://ref.ly/Prov9:7) (‖מוֺכִיחַ). **2\.** *instruct*, [1 Ch 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:22). **3\.** *discipline*, of God [Ho 10:10](https://ref.ly/Hos10:10), [Psalm 94:10](https://ref.ly/Ps94:10). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִוָּ֫סֶר עֶ֫בֶד [Pr 29:19](https://ref.ly/Prov29:19); אִוָּסֵר [Je 31:18](https://ref.ly/Jer31:18); תִּוָּסְֽרוּ [Lv 26:23](https://ref.ly/Lev26:23); *Imv.* f. הִוָּסְֽרִי [Je 6:8](https://ref.ly/Jer6:8); mpl. הִיָסְרוּ [Psalm 2:10](https://ref.ly/Ps2:10); —(Niph. *tolerate.*, Ges§ 51\. 2\), *let oneself be corrected, admonished* by words of man [Psalm 2:10](https://ref.ly/Ps2:10), [Pr 29:19](https://ref.ly/Prov29:19)); *let oneself be chastened* by discipline of God [Je 6:8](https://ref.ly/Jer6:8), [31:18](https://ref.ly/Jer31:18), [Lv 26:23](https://ref.ly/Lev26:23). **Pi.** *Pf.* יִסַּר [1 K 12:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:11) \+ 3 t.; sf. יִסְּרַנִּי [Psalm 118:18](https://ref.ly/Ps118:18); יִסְּרוֺ [Is 28:26](https://ref.ly/Isa28:26); 3 f. sf. יִסְּרַתּוּ [Pr 31:1](https://ref.ly/Prov31:1); 2 m. יִסַּרְתָּ [Psalm 39:12](https://ref.ly/Ps39:12), [Jb 4:3](https://ref.ly/Job4:3), etc. \+ 8 t. Pf.; *Impf.* יְיַסֵּר [Dt 8:5](https://ref.ly/Deut8:5); 2 m. sf. תְּיַסְּרֶנּוּ [Psalm 94:12](https://ref.ly/Ps94:12), etc. \+ 5 t. Impf.; *Imv.* יַסֵּר [Pr 19:18](https://ref.ly/Prov19:18), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Prov29:17); sf. יַסְּרֵנִי [Je 10:24](https://ref.ly/Jer10:24); *Inf. abs.* יַסֹּר [Psalm 118:18](https://ref.ly/Ps118:18); *cstr.* יַסְּרָה [Lv 26:18](https://ref.ly/Lev26:18); sf. יַסְּרֶָָ֑[Dt 4:36](https://ref.ly/Deut4:36); *Pt.* מְיַסְּרֶָָ֑ [Dt 8:5](https://ref.ly/Deut8:5); —**1\.** *discipline, correct* (the moral nature, with more or less severity acc. to circumstances): **a.** of God, c. acc. [Ho 7:15](https://ref.ly/Hos7:15) (of *training* arms), [Dt 4:36](https://ref.ly/Deut4:36) (v. Dr), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Deut8:5) (Israel as son), [Is 28:26](https://ref.ly/Isa28:26), happy the man אשׁר תיסרנו יהּ [Psalm 94:12](https://ref.ly/Ps94:12), [118:18](https://ref.ly/Ps118:18), [118:18](https://ref.ly/Ps118:18) **b.** of man, c. acc. [Dt 8:5](https://ref.ly/Deut8:5), [Pr 19:18](https://ref.ly/Prov19:18), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Prov29:17) (all of a father his son), [Jb 4:3](https://ref.ly/Job4:3), [Pr 31:1](https://ref.ly/Prov31:1), [Psalm 16:7](https://ref.ly/Ps16:7) my reins (the emotions of my own heart) *correct, admonish* me. **2\.** more severely, *chasten, chastise:* **a.** of God, sq. acc. Pers., [Je 31:18](https://ref.ly/Jer31:18); אַל־בַּחֲמָֽתְ תְיַסְּרֵנִי [Psalm 6:2](https://ref.ly/Ps6:2), [38:2](https://ref.ly/Ps38:2); בְּמשׁפט אל באף [Je 10:24](https://ref.ly/Jer10:24); למשׁפט [30:11](https://ref.ly/Jer30:11) \= [46:28](https://ref.ly/Jer46:28); על חטאת [Lv 26:18](https://ref.ly/Lev26:18), [26:28](https://ref.ly/Lev26:28); בתוכחות על עון [Psalm 39:12](https://ref.ly/Ps39:12). **b.** of man, a father his son [Dt 21:18](https://ref.ly/Deut21:18); elders a man [Dt 22:18](https://ref.ly/Deut22:18) (including, as perh. [21:18](https://ref.ly/Deut21:18), bodily chastisement); a king his subjects בַּשּׁוֺטִים and בָּעַקְרַבִּים [1 K 12:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:11), [12:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:14) \= [2 Ch 10:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:11), [10:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:14). **c.** תְּיַסְּרֵח רָֽעָתֵח [Je 2:19](https://ref.ly/Jer2:19) *thy badness will chastise thee.* **Nithp.** *Pf.* נִוַּסְּרוּ Ez [23:48](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:48) (for נִתְוַסְּרוּ Ges§ 55 R. 7\); *pass. be disciplined, corrected.* **Hiph.** *Impf.* 1 s. sf. אַיְסִירֵם (Ew§ 131 c, but error for אֲיַסְּרֵם Bö§ 437 f SS), c. acc. *chasten* [Ho 7:12](https://ref.ly/Hos7:12).

> \[Str3250] † יִסּוֺר **n.m.** one who reproves, fault\-finder, הֲרֹשׁ עִם־שַׁדַּי יִיסּוֺר [Jb 40:2](https://ref.ly/Job40:2) *shall a reprover contend with Shadday?*
