## יָעַד (id: BDB04005)

\[Str3259] † יָעַד **vb.** appoint (NH *id.*, *appoint, assign*, esp. of acquiring or designating as wife; Aram. *id.;* Ar. وَعَدَ *promise, threaten, predict*, III. *appoint a time* or *place;* perh. As. *ādu, decide*, M\-A17 DlHWB 230; Ph. n. pr. אשמניעד)—**Qal** *Pf.* sf. יְעָדוֺ [2 S 20:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:5); יְעָדָהּ [Ex 21:8](https://ref.ly/Exod21:8) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* 3 m. sf. יִיעָדֶנָּה [Ex 21:9](https://ref.ly/Exod21:9); —*appoint*, a time [2 S 20:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:5); place [Je 47:7](https://ref.ly/Jer47:7); a rod [Mi 6:9](https://ref.ly/Mic6:9); *assign* or *designate* as concubine [Ex 21:8](https://ref.ly/Exod21:8), [21:9](https://ref.ly/Exod21:9) (JE). **Niph.** *Pf.* נוֺעַדְתִּי [Ex 25:22](https://ref.ly/Exod25:22); נִעַדְתִּי [Ex 29:43](https://ref.ly/Exod29:43); pl. נוֺעֲדוּ [Psalm 48:5](https://ref.ly/Ps48:5), [Nu 10:3](https://ref.ly/Num10:3), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Num10:4); נוֺעָדוּ [Am 3:3](https://ref.ly/Amos3:3); *Impf.* אִוָּעֵד [Ex 29:42](https://ref.ly/Exod29:42) \+ 3 t.; 3 pl. וַיִּוָּעֲדוּ [Jos 11:5](https://ref.ly/Josh11:5), [Jb 2:11](https://ref.ly/Job2:11); 1 pl. נִוָּעֵד [Ne 6:10](https://ref.ly/Neh6:10); וְנִוָּעֲדָה v:[2](https://ref.ly/Neh6:2); *Pt.* pl. נוֺעָדִים [Nu 14:35](https://ref.ly/Num14:35) \+ 3 t.; נֹעָדִים [16:11](https://ref.ly/Num16:11); —**1\.** reflexive, *meet at an appointed place*, with לְ, of Yahweh *meeting* Moses at the Tent of ‘Meeting’ [Ex 29:42](https://ref.ly/Exod29:42), [29:43](https://ref.ly/Exod29:43), [30:36](https://ref.ly/Exod30:36) (P); at the throne of the Kapporeth [25:22](https://ref.ly/Exod25:22), [30:6](https://ref.ly/Exod30:6), [Nu 17:19](https://ref.ly/Num17:19) (P). **2\.** *meet by appointment* [Am 3:3](https://ref.ly/Amos3:3), [Jb 2:11](https://ref.ly/Job2:11); with בְּ of place, [Ne 6:2](https://ref.ly/Neh6:2); אֶל of place v:[10](https://ref.ly/Neh6:10). **3\.** *gather, assemble by appointment*, kings for a campaign (abs.) [Jos 11:5](https://ref.ly/Josh11:5) (D), [Psalm 48:5](https://ref.ly/Ps48:5); with אֶל, unto Moses [Nu 10:4](https://ref.ly/Num10:4) (P); to the door of the tent of meeting v:[3](https://ref.ly/Num10:3) (P); with עַל, unto Solomon [1 K 8:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:5) \= [2 Ch 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:6); against Yahweh [Nu 14:35](https://ref.ly/Num14:35), [16:11](https://ref.ly/Num16:11), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Num27:3) (P). **Po‘ēl** *Pf.* 1 s. יוֺעַדְתִּי (for MT יוֺדַעְתִּי) [1 S 21:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:3) sq. acc. Pers. \+ אֶל of place, acc. to LXX We Dr Kit Bu. **Hiph.** *Impf.* מִי יוֺעִדֶנִּי [Je 50:44](https://ref.ly/Jer50:44) \= מִי יֹעִידֶנִּי [49:19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19) \= מִי יוֺעִידֵנִי [Jb 9:19](https://ref.ly/Job9:19) *who will make me meet him at the appointed place* (of judgement)? i.e. *who will summon* or *arraign me?* **Hoph.** *Pt.* מוּעָדִים לִפְנֵי [Je 24:1](https://ref.ly/Jer24:1) *be set, placed before;* אָנָה פָנַיִח מֻעָדוֺת Ez [21:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:21) *whither thy face is set.*

[Ex 13:10](https://ref.ly/Exod13:10), [23:15](https://ref.ly/Exod23:15), [34:18](https://ref.ly/Exod34:18)), *sacred season*, RV usu. *set feast* or *appointed season* (wider than חַג, which was only a feast celebrated by a *pilgrimage*), ´מ (ימי)יוֺם *day* (*s*) *of appointed season* (i.e. *festivals*) [Ho 9:5](https://ref.ly/Hos9:5), [12:10](https://ref.ly/Hos12:10), [La 2:7](https://ref.ly/Lam2:7), [2:22](https://ref.ly/Lam2:22); sg. indef. [1:4](https://ref.ly/Lam1:4); of the feast of booths [Dt 31:10](https://ref.ly/Deut31:10); ‖חג חדשׁ שׁבת [Ho 2:13](https://ref.ly/Hos2:13); ‖שׁבת [La 2:6](https://ref.ly/Lam2:6); usu. pl. יהוה ´מ [Lv 23:2](https://ref.ly/Lev23:2), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Lev23:4), [23:37](https://ref.ly/Lev23:37), [23:44](https://ref.ly/Lev23:44) (P), [2 Ch 2:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:3), [Ezr 3:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:5); with sfs. referring to ´י [Lv 23:2](https://ref.ly/Lev23:2) (P); ´בּמ [Nu 15:3](https://ref.ly/Num15:3), [29:39](https://ref.ly/Num29:39) (P), Ez [36:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:38); [46:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:9); קרית ´מ *city of our solemnities* (sacred seasons) [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20); טובים ´למ [Zc 8:19](https://ref.ly/Zech8:19); ‖שׁבתות Ez [44:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:24); ‖חדשׁים [Is 1:14](https://ref.ly/Isa1:14), [1 Ch 23:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:31), [2 Ch 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:13), [31:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:3), [Ne 10:34](https://ref.ly/Neh10:34); ‖חנים Ez [46:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:11); חנים חדשׁים Ez [45:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:17); ‖ראשׁי חָדְשֵׁכֶם [Nu 10:10](https://ref.ly/Num10:10) (P).—*Notes.* It is most probable that in [Gn 1:14](https://ref.ly/Gen1:14) (P), where ´מ ‖אֹתֹת, the reference is to the sacred seasons as fixed by moon's appearance; and so also ´עשה ירח למ *he made the moon for sacred seasons* [Psalm 104:19](https://ref.ly/Ps104:19), although many Lexx. \& Comm. refer these to the *seasons* of the year.—למועד מועדים וחצי [Dn 12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7) *for a set time, times, and a half* \= 3 1/2 appointed times \= half the prophetic week of years, cf. BrMP 453 f.—´ויאכלו את המ they ate throughout the *sacred season* (of Maṣṣoth) [2 Ch 30:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:22) AV RV, but Thes SS Be Ke Öt they ate *the offerings of the sacred season;* LXX וַיְכַלּוּ. **2\.** *appointed meeting:* לְכָל־חַי ´בֵּית מ [Jb 30:23](https://ref.ly/Job30:23) *house of meeting for every living* (of She'ol); ´קְרִאֵי מ [Nu 16:2](https://ref.ly/Num16:2) (P) *called to the assembly* \= קְרִאֵי הָעֵדָה; ´קרא עלי מ [La 1:15](https://ref.ly/Lam1:15) *called a festal meeting against me;* ´בקרב מ [Psalm 74:4](https://ref.ly/Ps74:4) *in the midst of thine assembly;* הַר מוֺעֵד [Is 14:13](https://ref.ly/Isa14:13) *mount of meeting* or *assembly* (of the gods; the mountain of the gods in the extreme north, the oriental Olympus, Persian *Alborg*, Hindu *Meru*, Babylon. *Aralli*, cf. LenOrigines ii, ch. ix). **3\.** *appointed place:* **a.** the temple, ‖שכּוֺ [La 2:6](https://ref.ly/Lam2:6), ´נוּגֵי ממ *afflicted* (and driven) *from the place of assembly* [Zp 3:18](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:18) (cf. BrMP 225\). **b.** synagogues, כָּל־מוֺעֲדֵי־אֵל בָּאָרֶץ *all* *the appointed places of Ēl in the land* [Psalm 74:8](https://ref.ly/Ps74:8) \= בֵּית וַעַד *Sota* [9:15](https://ref.ly/Ps9:15). **4\.** *appointed sign, signal*, Ju [20:38](https://ref.ly/Judg20:38). **5\.** אֹהֶל מוֺעֵד *tent of meeting* (of God with his people: see יָעַד Niph. **1**); the sacred tent of the Exodus (see אֹהֶל **3**) [Ex 33:7](https://ref.ly/Exod33:7), [33:7](https://ref.ly/Exod33:7), [Nu 12:4](https://ref.ly/Num12:4), [Dt 31:14](https://ref.ly/Deut31:14), [31:14](https://ref.ly/Deut31:14) (E), [Nu 11:16](https://ref.ly/Num11:16) (J); oft. in P, as [Ex 27:21](https://ref.ly/Exod27:21), [29:4](https://ref.ly/Exod29:4), [Lv 1:1](https://ref.ly/Lev1:1), [1:3](https://ref.ly/Lev1:3), [Jos 18:1](https://ref.ly/Josh18:1), [19:51](https://ref.ly/Josh19:51), etc. (131 t.); elsewhere only [1 S 2:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:22) b (om. LXX), [1 K 8:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:4) \= [2 Ch 5:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:5), [1 Ch 6:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:17), [9:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:21), [23:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:32), [2 Ch 1:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:3), [1:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:6), [1:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:13).

## יָעָה (id: BDB04012)

\[Str3261] † יָעָה **vb.** sweep together (with collat. idea of *carrying away*) (Ar. وَعَى is *collect, gather*)—**Qal** *Pf.* וְיָעָה consec. [Is 28:17](https://ref.ly/Isa28:17) subj. בָּרָד c. acc. (מַחְסֵה כָזָב ´ויעה ב).

418:a. יָעָה: cf. also Ar. وُعَاءٌ *vessel* [Gn 42:25](https://ref.ly/Gen42:25) Saad, cf. BaldenspergerPEF 1904, 53, who cp. Mod. Ar. ωἁἀ (وَعَا).

> \[Str3257] † \[יָע] **n.\[m.]** shovel (TargumJer יָעֵי [Ex 27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3))—pl. הַיָּעִים [Ex 38:3](https://ref.ly/Exod38:3) \+ 7 t.; יָעָיו [27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3); —utensils for cleaning altar, *shovels* [Ex 27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3), [38:3](https://ref.ly/Exod38:3), [Nu 4:14](https://ref.ly/Num4:14) (all P), [1 K 7:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:40), [7:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:45), [2 K 25:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:14), [2 Ch 4:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:11), [4:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:16), [Je 52:18](https://ref.ly/Jer52:18) (only in lists of utensils).

> \[Str3262,3273] † יְעוּאֵל, יְעִיאֵל, יְעִו֯אֵל **n.pr.m. 1\.** יְעוּאֵל son of Zerah [1 Ch 9:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:6), LXX Επειηλ, A LXXL Ιεηλ. **2\.** יְעִיאֵל: **a.** a companion of Ezra [Ezr 8:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:13), LXXL Ιειηλ. **b.** a chief of the Reubenites [1 Ch 5:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:7), LXX Ιωηλ. **c.** Levites, LXX Ιωηλ, Ιειηλ, etc.: (I) [1 Ch 15:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:18), [15:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:21), [16:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:5), [16:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:5), [2 Ch 20:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:14); (2\) [2 Ch 35:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:9). **d.** one of those who took strange wives [Ezr 10:43](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:43), LXX Ιαηλ, Ιειηλ, etc. **3\.** יעואל Kt, יְעִיאֵל Qr: **a.** man of Gibeon, ancestor of Saul [1 Ch 9:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:35), LXX Ιηλ, Ιε(ι)ηλ. **b.** one of David's heroes [11:44](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:44), LXX (Ι)εια, A LXXL Ιειηλ. **c.** the סוֺפֵר (q.v.) of King Uzziah [2 Ch 26:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:11), LXX Ιειηλ. **d.** a Levite [29:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:13) A LXXL Ιειηλ.

> \[Str3263–65] יעוץ v. sub עוץ; יעור v. sub עור. p. 420, 734–35\.

> \[Str3264] יְעוֺרִים v. יַעַר

> \[Str3266] יְעוּשׁ v. sub עוּשׁ. p. 736

## יָעַז (id: BDB04018)

\[Str3267] † \[יָעַז] **vb.** only **Niph.** *Pt.* as adj. (precise mng. dub.; Ar. وَعَزَ is *give a nod* or *sign*, then *command*, whence Heb. might have meaning *nodding, making signs* (not intelligibly speaking), so Hi—Che *barbarous*, De *‘ungeberdig’*—which context favours, or *arrogant;* Thes prop. *durus, saevus*, and comp. עזז)—in phr. עַם נוֺעָז [Is 33:19](https://ref.ly/Isa33:19) *a barbarous* (?) *people*, of foreign invader.

> \[Str3268–69] יעזיאל, יעזיהו v. sub עזה. p. 739\.

> \[Str3270] יַעְזִרִ v. sub עזר. p. 741\.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 33:19

## I יָעַל (id: BDB04025)

\[Str3276] † I. \[יָעַל] **vb.** only **Hiph.** profit, avail, benefit;—**Hiph.** *pf.* הוֺעִיל Hb [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hab2:18); *Impf.* יוֺעִיל [Je 2:11](https://ref.ly/Jer2:11) \+ 2 t.; אֹעִיל [Jb 35:3](https://ref.ly/Job35:3); יוֺעִילוּ [1 S 12:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:21) \+ 6 t.; יוֺעִ֫לוּ [Je 2:8](https://ref.ly/Jer2:8), [12:13](https://ref.ly/Jer12:13); יוֺעִילוּח [Is 57:12](https://ref.ly/Isa57:12); נוֺעִיל [Jb 21:15](https://ref.ly/Job21:15); *Inf. abs.* הוֺעֵיל [Je 23:22](https://ref.ly/Jer23:22); cstr. הוֺעִיל [7:8](https://ref.ly/Jer7:8) \+ 4 t.; *Pt.* מוֺעִיל [16:19](https://ref.ly/Jer16:19); —*profit, avail, benefit*, always (exc. [Jb 30:13](https://ref.ly/Job30:13) where in bad sense, [Is 47:12](https://ref.ly/Isa47:12)) c. neg., or in question implying neg.; esp. of idols or false gods (as *unprofitable*), so Hb [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hab2:18), [Is 44:9](https://ref.ly/Isa44:9), [44:10](https://ref.ly/Isa44:10), [57:12](https://ref.ly/Isa57:12), [Je 2:8](https://ref.ly/Jer2:8) אחרי לא יועילו הבו, v:[11](https://ref.ly/Jer2:11) המיר כבידו בלוא יועיל, [16:19](https://ref.ly/Jer16:19) ואין בם מועיל, [1 S 12:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:21), of vain confidences [Je 7:8](https://ref.ly/Jer7:8), or promises [23:32](https://ref.ly/Jer23:32), [23:32](https://ref.ly/Jer23:32) (sq. לְ); of Egypt as ally [Is 30:5](https://ref.ly/Isa30:5) (sq. לְ), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Isa30:5), v:[6](https://ref.ly/Isa30:6); of *wickedness* [Pr 10:2](https://ref.ly/Prov10:2); *wealth* [11:4](https://ref.ly/Prov11:4); *worthless* men [Jb 30:13](https://ref.ly/Job30:13) (לְהַוָּתִי יועִילוּ i.e. promote it); of words [Jb 15:3](https://ref.ly/Job15:3) (‖דָּבָר לאֹ יִסְכּוֺן); in gen. *gain profit* [Is 47:12](https://ref.ly/Isa47:12), [48:17](https://ref.ly/Isa48:17), [Je 12:13](https://ref.ly/Jer12:13), [Jb 21:15](https://ref.ly/Job21:15), [35:3](https://ref.ly/Job35:3).

> \[Str3132] † יוֺעֵאלָה **n.pr.m.** (perh. from …יוֺעֵלָה *may he avail!*)—one of David's heroes [1 Ch 12:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:8) (Baer; v:[7](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:7) van d. H).

I. יַעֲלָה).

> \[Str3278] † II. יָעֵל **n.pr.f.** wife of Heber the Kenite, slayer of Sisera Ju [4:17](https://ref.ly/Judg4:17), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Judg4:18), [4:21](https://ref.ly/Judg4:21), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Judg4:22), [5:24](https://ref.ly/Judg5:24), prob. also v:[6](https://ref.ly/Judg5:6) (others find here name of a man, a ‘judge’).

> \[Str3279] † II. יַעֲלָא, יַעֲלָה **n.pr.m.** *head of a family of returning exiles*, יַעֲלָא [Ne 7:58](https://ref.ly/Neh7:58) \= יַעֲלָה [Ezr 2:56](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:56).

## יען (id: BDB04035)

יען (√ of foll.; acc. to Ges EwGGA 1864, No. 27 Aram. ܝܺܥܶܢ, ܝܰܥܢܳܐ *avidus, cupidus;* hence בַּת יַעֲנָה \= *daughter of greed*, of *ostrich* as *voracious* bird; but WetzstDel [Jb 31:39](https://ref.ly/Job31:39) \= *daughter of the desert* or *steppe*, from وَعْنَةٌ *hard, unproductive soil;* cf. the Arab. name أَبُو ٱلصَّحَارَى *father of the plains.*)

> \[Str3283] † \[יָעֵן] **n.\[m.]** ostrich (i.e. *voracious* one ?);—only pl. abs. ענים בַּמִּדְבָּר ´כ [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3) Kt; Qr כַּיְעֵנִים.

> \[Str3284] † יַעֲנָה **n.f.** greed (?);—only in בַּת־הַיַּעֲנָה [Lv 11:16](https://ref.ly/Lev11:16), [Dt 14:15](https://ref.ly/Deut14:15); בְּנוֺת־יַעֲנָה [Mi 1:8](https://ref.ly/Mic1:8) \+ 5 t.;—*ostrich*, (vid. √ יען supra); as wailing (sim. of mourning) [Mi 1:8](https://ref.ly/Mic1:8) (‖תַּנִּים); symbol. of loneliness [Jb 30:29](https://ref.ly/Job30:29) (´יַעֲ ´אָח הָיִיתִי לְתַנִּים וְרֵעַ לב); of desolation, as dwelling among ruins [Is 13:21](https://ref.ly/Isa13:21), [34:13](https://ref.ly/Isa34:13) (‖תַּנִּים), [Je 50:39](https://ref.ly/Jer50:39); dwelling in desert [Is 43:20](https://ref.ly/Isa43:20) (‖תַּנִּים); unclean fowl [Lv 11:16](https://ref.ly/Lev11:16), [Dt 14:15](https://ref.ly/Deut14:15).

> \[Str3285] יעני **n.pr.** v. sub. ענה. p. 774 ff

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 50:39; Job 30:29; Micah 1:8; Deuteronomy 14:15; Job 31:39; Lamentations 4:3; Isaiah 43:20; Isaiah 13:21; Leviticus 11:16; Isaiah 34:13


## I יָעֵף (id: BDB04039)

\[Str3286] † I. \[יָעֵף] **vb.** be weary, faint (Ar. وَغَفَ *run* and *shew weariness*)—**Qal** *Pf.* וְיָעֵָפוּ consec. [Je 51:58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58), [51:64](https://ref.ly/Jer51:64); *Impf.* יִיעַף [Is 40:28](https://ref.ly/Isa40:28); וַיִּעָף [44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12); יִֽעֲפוּ [40:30](https://ref.ly/Isa40:30); יִיעָפוּ v:[31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31), [Je 2:24](https://ref.ly/Jer2:24); יִעָפוּ Hb [2:13](https://ref.ly/Hab2:13); —*be* or *grow weary* [Je 2:24](https://ref.ly/Jer2:24) (in seeking); \= exhaust onself fruitlessly Hb [2:13](https://ref.ly/Hab2:13) (‖יגע), hence [Je 51:58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58), [51:64](https://ref.ly/Jer51:64) (repeated by error from v:[58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58)); of ´י (neg.) [Is 40:28](https://ref.ly/Isa40:28) (‖יגע); youth [40:30](https://ref.ly/Isa40:30) (‖ *id.*); the god\-fearing v:[31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31) (‖ *id.*); *be faint* from lack of water [44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12) (‖אֵין כֹּחַ from hunger). **Hoph.** *Pt. wearied* [Dn 9:21](https://ref.ly/Dan9:21) מֻעָף בִּיעָף (v. יְעָף).

419:a. Bef. I. \[יָעֵף] ins. ‘יְעֹעֵ֫רוּ [Is 15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5) v. I. \[עוּר] **Pilp.**’

> \[Str3287] † יָעֵף **adj.** weary, faint;—always as subst., exc. Ju [8:15](https://ref.ly/Judg8:15) אנשׁיך הַיְּפֵעִים (of physical fatigue from lack of bread); הַיָּעֵף [2 S 16:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:2) (from lack of drink); of mind and spirit [Is 40:29](https://ref.ly/Isa40:29) (‖אֵין אוֺנִין), [50:4](https://ref.ly/Isa50:4).

> \[Str3288] † יְעָף **n.\[m.]** weariness, faintness (Aramaism, acc. to LagBN 175\);—מֻעָף בִּיעָף *weary with weariness*, utterly weary [Dn 9:21](https://ref.ly/Dan9:21) (from winged flight, said of Gabriel).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Samuel 16:2; Jeremiah 51:58; Habakkuk 2:13; Jeremiah 51:64; Daniel 9:21; Isaiah 40:29; Isaiah 40:31; Isaiah 50:4; Isaiah 40:28; Isaiah 44:12; Isaiah 40:30; Jeremiah 2:24; Judges 8:15


## II יעף (id: BDB04042)

II. יעף (√ of following; cf. Ar. يَفَعَ *ascend* a mountain, يَفَعٌ *hill*).

> \[Str8443] † \[תּוֺעָפָה] **n.f.** eminence; of *towering* horns (? v. Di) כְּתוֺעֲפֹת רְאֵם לוֺ [Nu 23:22](https://ref.ly/Num23:22), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Num24:8), sim. of strength of Israel; of peaks תּוֺעֲפוֺת הָרִים לוֺ [Psalm 95:4](https://ref.ly/Ps95:4) (‖מֶחְקְרֵי־אָרֶץ); of silver [Jb 22:25](https://ref.ly/Job22:25) כֶּסֶף תּוֺעָפוֺת לָךְ, very dubious, perhaps *heaps* or *bars* (ingots).

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 23:22; Numbers 24:8; Psalms 95:4; Job 22:25

## I יער (id: BDB04049)

I. יער (√ of foll.; Ar. وَعُرَ is *be rugged*, of mountain, etc., *be difficult;* وَعْرٌ *rough* or *difficult place, mountain*).

> \[Str3264,3293] † I. יַ֫עַר **n.m.**:[2 S 18:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:8) wood, forest, thicket (MI21 pl. היערן (\= n.pr.loc. ?); Ph. יער and יר, cf. DHM in MV:(10\) 983; Aram. יַעֲרָא, ܝܰܥܪܳܐ *wood, thicket;* As. *âru* DlHWB 230, cf. JägerBAS i, 476\)—abs. ַ֫ יעַר [Is 7:2](https://ref.ly/Isa7:2) \+; ָ\+יעַר [Mi 3:12](https://ref.ly/Mic3:12) \+; c. ה loc. יַעֲרָה [Jos 17:15](https://ref.ly/Josh17:15); cstr. ַ֫ יעַר [2 S 18:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:6); יַעְרָהּ [Je 46:23](https://ref.ly/Jer46:23), etc.; pl. יְעָרִים [Ezr 2:25](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:25) \+ 4 t. (incl. Ez [34:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:25) Qr, so Co); יְעָרוֺת [Psalm 29:9](https://ref.ly/Ps29:9); —**a.** *wood, forest, wooded height*, with trees to be felled [Jos 17:15](https://ref.ly/Josh17:15) (c. עלה *go up* to), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Josh17:18) (J), [Dt 19:5](https://ref.ly/Deut19:5) (not elsewhere Hex); עֵץ מִיַּעַר כְּרָתוֺ [Je 10:13](https://ref.ly/Jer10:13) *as wood out of a forest he hath cut it;* יחטבו מן־היערים Ez [39:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:10) (עצים מן־השדה in ‖ cl.); as producing trees יער צוֺמֵחַ עֵצִים [Ec 2:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:6); in designation of Solomon's palace בֵּעַר הַלְּבָנוֺן [1 K 7:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:2) (on structure of this house v. StaSalomos Bauten, ZAW 1883, 150\), [10:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:17), [10:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:21) \= [2 Ch 9:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:16), [9:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:20); cf. בֵּית הַיָּ֫עַר [Is 22:8](https://ref.ly/Isa22:8); fig. of foes to be cut down and destroyed נִקַּף סִבְכֵי היער [Is 10:34](https://ref.ly/Isa10:34); כָּֽרְתוּ יַעְרָהּ [Je 46:23](https://ref.ly/Jer46:23); so [Is 32:19](https://ref.ly/Isa32:19) and יער הַבָּצִו֯ר [Zc 11:2](https://ref.ly/Zech11:2) (rd. Kt הַבָּצוּר *inaccessible forest*). **b.** as hiding\-place for fugitive [1 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:5); lurking\-place of wild beasts [Am 3:4](https://ref.ly/Amos3:4), [Mi 5:7](https://ref.ly/Mic5:7), [2 K 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:24), [Je 5:6](https://ref.ly/Jer5:6), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Jer12:8), [Is 56:9](https://ref.ly/Isa56:9), [Psalm 50:10](https://ref.ly/Ps50:10), [80:14](https://ref.ly/Ps80:14), [104:20](https://ref.ly/Ps104:20), cf. Ez [34:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:25) (rd. Qr, v. supr.) **c.** stripped by voice of ´י [Psalm 29:9](https://ref.ly/Ps29:9); devoured by fire [Is 9:17](https://ref.ly/Isa9:17) (´סִבְכֵי הַיּ; fig. of the people); so in metaph. or sim. of ´י's judgements [Is 10:18](https://ref.ly/Isa10:18) (כְּבוֺד יַעְרוֺ, fig. of Assyr.), [Je 21:14](https://ref.ly/Jer21:14), Ez [21:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:2), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3) (v.infr.) [Psalm 83:15](https://ref.ly/Ps83:15). **d.** opp. כַּרְמֶל (*garden\-land*) [Is 29:17](https://ref.ly/Isa29:17), [32:15](https://ref.ly/Isa32:15); but also יַעַר כַּרְמִלּוֺ *his garden woodland* [2 K 19:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:23) \= [Is 37:24](https://ref.ly/Isa37:24), and יַעַר בְּתוֺךְ כַּרְמֶל [Mi 7:14](https://ref.ly/Mic7:14) (secluded and fertile abode for flock, fig. of people, v. Che; Hi\-St thinks of *sacred grove*). **e.** \= *thicket*, esp. as symbol of desolation [Ho 2:14](https://ref.ly/Hos2:14); בָּמוֺת יער *thicket\-covered heights* (overgrown with bushes and trees) [Mi 3:12](https://ref.ly/Mic3:12) \= [Je 26:18](https://ref.ly/Jer26:18); also בַּיַּעַר בּעֲרַב [Is 21:13](https://ref.ly/Isa21:13) *in the thicket* (or *bushes*, so VB) *in Arabia* must ye lodge, caravans of Dedanites. **f.** יער(ה)עֲצֵי *trees of the forest* [Is 7:2](https://ref.ly/Isa7:2) (in sim), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Isa10:19) (יַעְרוֺ שְׁאָר עֵץ, fig.), [44:14](https://ref.ly/Isa44:14), Ez [15:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:2), also v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:6) (עֵץ היער), Ct [2:3](https://ref.ly/Song2:3) (in sim.); fig., as singing before ´י [Psalm 96:12](https://ref.ly/Ps96:12) \= [1 Ch 16:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:33), Is [44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23) (יער וְכָל־עֵץ בּוֺ). **g.** particular forests are: יער אפרים [2 S 18:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:6), see אֶפְרַיִם p. 68 supr.; cf. v:[18](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:18), v:[17](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:17) (v. also [Jos 17:15](https://ref.ly/Josh17:15), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Josh17:18)); יַעַר חָרֶת [1 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:5) (v. supr.); יַעַר לְבָנוֺן (v. supr.); יַעַר הַשָּדֶה נֶגֶב and יַעַר הַנָּגֶב Ez [21:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:2), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3) (v. supr.) fig. of Judah (land and people); v. also יְעָרִים infr.—שְדֵ־יָ\+ יעַר [Psalm 132:6](https://ref.ly/Ps132:6) is a n.pr.loc. (but Bae thinks appellat. ‘auf waldigem Gefilcle’ ), v. קִרְיַת יְעָרִים. On [1 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:25), [14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26), v. II. יער.—יעורים Ez [34:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:25) Kt, rd. יְעָרִים Qr, v. supr.

## II יער (id: BDB04051)

II. יער ( √ of following; meaning dubious; MV identif. with I, from roughness and porousness (?) of honeycomb, cf. Buhl.

> \[Str3293] † II. יַ֫עַר **n.\[m.]** honeycomb,—abs. יַעַר [1 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:25), [14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26); sf. יַעְרִי Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1); —*honeycomb*, containing honey [1 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:25) (where rd. וְיַעַר הָיָה עַל־פְּנֵּי הַשָּדֶה with LXX We Dr Kit in KauAT, instead of v. as in MT); וַיָּבוֺא העם אֶל־הַיַּעַר [1 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26) (‖דבשׁv:[25](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:25)) *and when the people came to the honeycomb* behold its bees had departed (v. LXX We Dr Kit VB); אָכַלְתִּי יַעְרִי עִם־דִּבְשִׁי Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1); v. also I. יַעֲרָה.

> \[Str3295] † I. \[יַעְרָה] **n.f.** honeycomb, only cstr. הַדְּבָשׁ יַעְרַת [1 S 14:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:27) (v. II. יער).—יְעָרוֺת v. I. יַעַר.

> \[Str3294] † II. יַעְרָה **n.pr.m.** a descendant of Saul [1 Ch 9:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:42), [9:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:42), prob. corrupt, v. יְהוֺעַדָּה supr.

> \[Str3296] † יַעֲרֵי אֹרְגִים **n.pr.m.**father of Elḥanan [2 S 21:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:19) (\= יָעִו֯ר [1 Ch 20:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:5)); ´א prob. scribal err. anticipating ´א in foll. line (We Dr), and יַעֲרֵי error for יָעִיר (We) q. v. sub עור.

> \[Str3297] † יְעָרִים **n.pr.mont.** only הַר־יְעָרִים [Jos 15:10](https://ref.ly/Josh15:10) (LXX πόλιν Ιαρειν), where explained as \= כְּסָלוֺן (LXX Χας(α)λων), mod. *Kesla*, NE. of Beth Shemesh, cf. RobBR ii. 30 n.; iii. 154\.—קִרְיַת יְעָרִים v. sub קִרְיָת.

> \[Str3298] יַעֲרֶשְׁיָה v. sub. ערשׁ. p. 793

> \[Str3299–300] יַעֲשַי, יעשו, יַעֲשִיאֵל v. עשה

> \[Str3301] יִפְדְּיָה v. sub. פדה. p. 804

## יָפָה (id: BDB04060)

\[Str3302] † \[יָפָה] **vb.** be fair, beautiful (NH *id.* Pi. and deriv.; Aram. Aph. ܐܰܘܦܺܝ is *suffice, finish, fail*; cf. Aram. ܦܶܐܐ (PS3015) *beautiful, fit*, Nö§ 172 c. Anm. G H \[offmann] LCB1882, 321\. Ar. وَفَى *fulfil, perform;* Eth. አወፈየ፡ II. **2\.** *give* (entirely) *over to, into power of,* DI349)—**Qal** *Pf.* יָפִיתְ Ct [7:7](https://ref.ly/Song7:7); יָפוּ [4:10](https://ref.ly/Song4:10) [7:2](https://ref.ly/Song7:2) *Impf.* 2 fs. וַתִּ֫יפִי (KöLgh. i. 581\) Ez [16:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:13); 3 ms. apoc. וַיִּיף [31:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:7); —*be beautiful*, subj. Pers. Ct [4:10](https://ref.ly/Song4:10), [7:7](https://ref.ly/Song7:7); of feet v:[2](https://ref.ly/Song7:2); of Jerus. under fig. of woman Ez [16:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:13); Egypt under fig. of tree [31:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:7). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יְיַפֵָּהוּ [Je 10:4](https://ref.ly/Jer10:4) *beautify* an idol. The (**Pe‘al‘al** ?) *Pf.* form אָדָם יָפְיָפִיתָ מִבְּנֵי [Psalm 45:3](https://ref.ly/Ps45:3) is contrary to all anal.: rd. either יָפִיתָ or יְפֵיפִיתָ Ges: § 55\. 3 Sta§ 156 R Now (Köi. 583 f. defence is artificial): *thou art more beautiful than,* etc. **Hithp.** *Impf.* 2 fs. תִּתְיַפִּי *beautify thyself* [Je 4:30](https://ref.ly/Jer4:30).

[Dt 14:28](https://ref.ly/Deut14:28). **e.** *bring out* to (or for) a person [Gn 14:18](https://ref.ly/Gen14:18), Ju [6:19](https://ref.ly/Judg6:19) cf. v:[18](https://ref.ly/Judg6:18), [1 K 17:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:13), [2 K 10:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:22), [10:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:22). **f.** *bring out* from (מִן) a place to people [Nu 17:24](https://ref.ly/Num17:24) (P). **g.** *bring out* in payment, *pay* (money, tribute, etc.) [2 K 12:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:12), [15:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:20) (עַל \= in behalf of: or *put it forth, imposed it, on* Israel). **h.** *bring forth* (\= cause to rise or appear) the heavenly bodies [Is 40:26](https://ref.ly/Isa40:26), [Jb 38:32](https://ref.ly/Job38:32); see also וְתַעֲלֻמָה יֹצִא אוֺר [28:11](https://ref.ly/Job28:11) *and hidden things he* (the miner) *bringeth forth tolight.* **i.** *bring forth* by miracle: Moses. water out of rock [Nu 20:8](https://ref.ly/Num20:8), [20:10](https://ref.ly/Num20:10) (JE); ´י id. [Dt 8:15](https://ref.ly/Deut8:15), [Ne 9:15](https://ref.ly/Neh9:15), [Psalm 78:16](https://ref.ly/Ps78:16). **j.** \= *produce, generate, bring into being:* of magicians trying to produce lice [Ex 8:14](https://ref.ly/Exod8:14) (P); a smith producing weapon [Is 54:16](https://ref.ly/Isa54:16); see the threefold הוציא [Pr 30:33](https://ref.ly/Prov30:33), [30:33](https://ref.ly/Prov30:33), [30:33](https://ref.ly/Prov30:33); of ´י causing לֶחֶם to proceed out of (מִן) earth [Psalm 104:14](https://ref.ly/Ps104:14); elsewhere of earth as producing [Gn 1:12](https://ref.ly/Gen1:12), [1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24) (both P), [Is 61:11](https://ref.ly/Isa61:11), [Hg 1:11](https://ref.ly/Hag1:11); of Aaron's rod bringing forth blossoms [Nu 17:28](https://ref.ly/Num17:28) (P). **k.** *bring forth* words (out of mouth, etc.), i.e. *speak* [Jb 8:10](https://ref.ly/Job8:10), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Job15:3), [Pr 10:18](https://ref.ly/Prov10:18), [29:11](https://ref.ly/Prov29:11), [Ne 6:19](https://ref.ly/Neh6:19), [Is 48:20](https://ref.ly/Isa48:20) (‖הגיד, השׁמיע); abs. [Ec 5:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:1); of *publishing* a report עָיֶהָ שֵׁם רָע ´הו [Dt 22:14](https://ref.ly/Deut22:14) (‖שָם לָהּ עֲלִילֽת דְּבָרִים) cf. v:[19](https://ref.ly/Deut22:19); (רָעָה )דִּבַּת הָאָרֶץ ´הו [Nu 13:32](https://ref.ly/Num13:32), [14:37](https://ref.ly/Num14:37); so [14:36](https://ref.ly/Num14:36) (דִּבָּה עַל־הָאָרֶץ). **5\.** fig., subj. ´י, *bring forth* from (מִן) Bel's mouth what he has swallowed [Je 5:44](https://ref.ly/Jer5:44); wind, out of treasuries [10:13](https://ref.ly/Jer10:13), [51:16](https://ref.ly/Jer51:16), [Psalm 135:7](https://ref.ly/Ps135:7); fire, out of Tyre Ez [28:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:18); curse, over the land [Zc 5:4](https://ref.ly/Zech5:4); of Jeremiah, מִוּוֺלֵל אִם תּוֺצִיא יָקָר [Je 15:19](https://ref.ly/Jer15:19) *if thou bring forth the precious from* (Gie *without*) *the base,* i.e. if thou free that which is pure in thee from base admixture (Gf; Gie *if thou produce the noble without the base*); bring forth, i.e. *exhibit,* righteousness (nnocence Che) [Psalm 37:6](https://ref.ly/Ps37:6), [Je 51:10](https://ref.ly/Jer51:10) (cf. **Qal 1 f** end); וַיֹּצֵא לָאוֺר צַלְמָוֶת [Jb 12:22](https://ref.ly/Job12:22), cf. [Mi 7:9](https://ref.ly/Mic7:9); subj. servant of ´י of publishing מִשְׁפָּט (i.e. religion) to the world [Is 42:1](https://ref.ly/Isa42:1), [42:3](https://ref.ly/Isa42:3). † **Hoph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. הוּצָאָה Ez [38:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:8) (del.Co); *Pt.* fs. מוּצֵאת [Gn 38:25](https://ref.ly/Gen38:25); m.pl. מוּצָאִים [14:22](https://ref.ly/Gen14:22), [47:8](https://ref.ly/Gen47:8) (but v. infr.); f.pl. מוּצָאוֺת [Je 38:22](https://ref.ly/Jer38:22); —*be brought forth* [Gn 38:25](https://ref.ly/Gen38:25) ( J;of Tamar); of women as captives [Je 38:22](https://ref.ly/Jer38:22); remnant out of Jerusalem Ez [14:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:22) MT (but rd. Hiph., v. supr.); exiles, from among the peoples [38:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:8) (om. SerVer Co); of waters, אֶל־הַיָּ֫מָּה הַמּוּצָאִים Ez [47:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:8) *which are caused to flow into the sea* (but SerVer Co אֶל־הַמַּיִם הַחֲמוּאִים *into the sour (bitter, salt) waters,* see Field VB). 

> \[Str3329] † \[יָצִיא] **adj.** coming forth; only c. מִן and sf., ומיציאו מֵעָיו [2 Ch 32:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:21) Kt (וּמִיצִיאֵי Qr), *and some of those who came forth from his loins* (בניו in ‖ [Is 37:38](https://ref.ly/Isa37:38)).

## יָצַב (id: BDB04083)

\[Str3320] † \[יָצַב] **vb.** only **Hithp.** set or station oneself, take one's stand ( Targum יצב Pa. \& deriv.; Ar. وَصَبَ *be constant, firm*)—**Hithp.** *Pf.* 3 pl. הִתְיַצְּבוּ [2 Ch 11:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:13); ´וְהִת consec. [Nu 11:16](https://ref.ly/Num11:16); *Impf.* יִתְיַצֵּב [Dt 7:24](https://ref.ly/Deut7:24) \+ 6 t.; יִתְיַצָּב [Jb 41:2](https://ref.ly/Job41:2); יִתְיַצַָּב [Pr 22:29](https://ref.ly/Prov22:29); וַיִּתְיַצֵּב [1 S 3:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:10) \+ 5 t.; 3 fs. וַתֵּתַצֵּב [Ex 2:4](https://ref.ly/Exod2:4) (but read וַתִּתְיַצֵּב cf. Sam Ges§ 71 Köi, 430\); 2 ms. תִּתְיַצֵּב [2 S 18:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:13); וְאֶתְיַצְּבָה Hb [2:1](https://ref.ly/Hab2:1); יִתְיַצְּבוּ [Psalm 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:2) \+ 2 t.; וַיִּתְיַצְּבוּ [Ex 19:17](https://ref.ly/Exod19:17) \+ 4 t.; *Imv.* הִתְיַצֵּב [8:16](https://ref.ly/Exod8:16) \+ 5 t.: הִתְיַצְּבוּ [1 S 10:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:19) \+ 6 t.; *inf. cstr.* הִתְיַצֵּב(מֵ) [2 S 21:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:5) \+ 4 t.;—*station oneself, take one's stand, stand:* **a.** c. phr. of place, [Ex 2:4](https://ref.ly/Exod2:4) (E, c. מֵרָחוֺק), [2 S 18:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:13) (c. מִנֶּגֶד), fig. \= *stand aloof* (VB Dr); v:[30](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:30) (c. כֹּה, ‖עָמַד): c. ב loc, [Ex 19:17](https://ref.ly/Exod19:17), [Nu 22:22](https://ref.ly/Num22:22) (both E), Ju [20:2](https://ref.ly/Judg20:2), [Dt 31:14](https://ref.ly/Deut31:14), [31:14](https://ref.ly/Deut31:14), also (in sense of *having a place* or *position*) נִשְׁמַדְנוּ מֵהִתְיַצֵּב בְּכָל־גְּבֻל יִשְרָאֵל [2 S 21:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:5); c. בְּתוֺח Pers. [1 S 10:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:23), loc. ## יָצַג (id: BDB04084)

\[Str3322] † \[יָצַג] **vb. Hiph.** set, place, a vivid and forcible syn. of שָם (Köi. 430 Ges§ 71\)—**Hiph.** *Pf.* sf. הִצִּיגַּ֫נִי [Je 51:34](https://ref.ly/Jer51:34) Qr (Kt הציגנו), [Jb 17:6](https://ref.ly/Job17:6); 1 S sf. הִצַּגְתִּיו [Gn 43:9](https://ref.ly/Gen43:9); וְהִצַּגְּתִּיהָ consec. [Ho 2:5](https://ref.ly/Hos2:5); *Impf.* וַיַּצֵּג [Gn 30:38](https://ref.ly/Gen30:38), Ju [8:27](https://ref.ly/Judg8:27); sf. וַיַּצִּגֵם [Gn 47:2](https://ref.ly/Gen47:2); 2 ms. תַּצִּיג Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5), etc.; *Imv.* הַצִּ֫יגוּ [Am 5:15](https://ref.ly/Amos5:15); *Inf. cstr.* הַצֵּג [Dt 28:56](https://ref.ly/Deut28:56) (Ges§ 53, 3, R. 2 Kö1. c.); *Pt.* מַצִּיג Ju [6:37](https://ref.ly/Judg6:37); —*set, place,* c. acc.: \+ ב loc. [Gn 30:28](https://ref.ly/Gen30:28) (J; \+ לְנֹכַח צאֹן), Ju [6:37](https://ref.ly/Judg6:37), [8:27](https://ref.ly/Judg8:27), [2 S 6:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:17) ( \+ בְּתוֺח), c. בְּתוֺח in ‖ [1 Ch 16:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:1); \+ אֵצֶל [1 S 5:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:2); of setting foot on (לְ) ground [Dt 28:56](https://ref.ly/Deut28:56); [Gn 33:15](https://ref.ly/Gen33:15) (J; sq. עִם) \= *station with* thee some of my retinue; \+ לִפְנֵי [Gn 43:9](https://ref.ly/Gen43:9) (J); so with idea of *presenting, introducing to* [Gn 47:2](https://ref.ly/Gen47:2) (J); *set* (so that all may see) \+ לְ [Jb 17:6](https://ref.ly/Job17:6); sq. 2 acc. ( \= *set as*) [Je 51:34](https://ref.ly/Jer51:34); וְהִצַּגְתִּיהָ כְּיוֺם הִוָּֽלְדָהּ [Ho 2:5](https://ref.ly/Hos2:5) *and* (lest) *I set* ( \= *exhibit*) *her as in the day when she was born* (‖אַפְשִׁיטֶנָּה עֲרֻמָּה); fig. *set up, establish,* הציגר בשׁער משפט [Am 5:15](https://ref.ly/Amos5:15) (opp. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Amos5:7) לארץ הניחו). **Hoph.** *Impf.* יֻצָּג [Ex 10:24](https://ref.ly/Exod10:24) (E) *be stayed, stopped, detained* (of herds, etc.)

## יָצַע (id: BDB04086)

\[Str3331] † \[יָצַע] **vb. Hiph.** lay, spread, late (Ges§ 71; Aram. יְצַע Pa. Aph. *id.,* also *expound,* ‘ auslegen;’ Ar. وَضَعَ is *put* or *lay* (down, on a place, etc.), but ض \= Aram. ע)—**Hiph.** *Impf.* יַצִּיעַ [Is 58:5](https://ref.ly/Isa58:5); אַצִּ֫יעָה [Psalm 139:8](https://ref.ly/Ps139:8); *lay, spread out,* sq. acc. שק ואפר [Is 58:5](https://ref.ly/Isa58:5); שׁאול [Psalm 139:8](https://ref.ly/Ps139:8) *if I spread out She’ôl* (as my couch). **Hoph.** *Impf.* יֻצַּע (**Pu.** *Pf.* De Di al.) *be laid, spread;* subj. שק ואפר [Est 4:3](https://ref.ly/Esth4:3); subj. רִמָּה worm, as couch for king of Babylon [Is 14:11](https://ref.ly/Isa14:11).

> \[Str3326] † \[יָצוּעַ] **n.\[m.]** couch, bed (poet.);—sf. יְצוּעִי [Gn 49:4](https://ref.ly/Gen49:4); pl. cstr. יְצוּעֵי [1 Ch 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:1); sf. יְצוּעָי [Jb 17:13](https://ref.ly/Job17:13) \+ 2 t. (\+ 1 K [6:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:5), [6:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:6), [6:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:10) Kt, v. יָצִיעַ); *couch, bed,* of wedlock or concubinage, [Gn 49:4](https://ref.ly/Gen49:4) (J), hence [1 Ch 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:1); in gen. [Psalm 63:7](https://ref.ly/Ps63:7), עֶרֶש יְצוּעָי [132:3](https://ref.ly/Ps132:3); בַּחשֶׁח רִפַּדְתִּי יְצוּעָי [Jb 17:13](https://ref.ly/Job17:13) (‖אִם־אֲקַיֶּה שְׁאוֺל בֵּתִי; cf. ## יָצַר (id: BDB04094)

\[Str3335] † יָצַר **vb.** form, fashion (NH in pt. יוֺצֵר *potter, creator,* and deriv.; Ph. יצר *potter;* Targum יצר Syr. ܝܰܨܪܳܐ \= יֵצֶר etc.; Ar. وِصْرٌ *covenant, contract;* As. *êṣêru,* as Heb., DlHWB 309\)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´י [Is 44:10](https://ref.ly/Isa44:10); יָצָר [Gn 2:8](https://ref.ly/Gen2:8); sf. יְצָרָהּ [Is 45:18](https://ref.ly/Isa45:18); 2 m. יָצַרְתָּ [Psalm 104:26](https://ref.ly/Ps104:26) \+ 8 t. Pf.; *Impf.* 3 m. וַיִּ֫יצֶר [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7); וַיִּ֫צֶר v:[19](https://ref.ly/Gen2:19); sf. יִצְּרֵהוּ [Is 44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12); 1 s. sf. אֶצֳּו֯רְ [Je 1:5](https://ref.ly/Jer1:5); *Pt.* יוֺצֵר [Is 41:25](https://ref.ly/Isa41:25) \+ 20 t.; יֹצֵר [45:18](https://ref.ly/Isa45:18) \+ 6 t.; sf. יֹצְרִי [49:5](https://ref.ly/Isa49:5) \+ 11 t. sf.; pl. יוֺצְרִים [30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14) 1 Ch [4:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:23); cstr. יֹצְרֵי [Is 44:9](https://ref.ly/Isa44:9); —**1\.** of *human activity:* **a.** of a potter who forms out of clay a vessel [Is 29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16), [41:25](https://ref.ly/Isa41:25), [Je 18:4](https://ref.ly/Jer18:4), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Jer18:4), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Jer18:6), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Jer18:6), [1 Ch 4:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:23), [La 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lam4:2), [Zc 11:13](https://ref.ly/Zech11:13), [11:13](https://ref.ly/Zech11:13) (these last two acc. to Thes SS et al. error for אוֺצָר); ´י(ה)כְּלִי *potter's vessel* 2 S [17:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:28), [Psalm 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ps2:9), [Je 19:11](https://ref.ly/Jer19:11); ´חֹמֶר הי *potter's clay* [Is 29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16); Israel as a people [Is 27:11](https://ref.ly/Isa27:11), [43:1](https://ref.ly/Isa43:1), [43:21](https://ref.ly/Isa43:21), [44:21](https://ref.ly/Isa44:21), [45:9](https://ref.ly/Isa45:9), [45:9](https://ref.ly/Isa45:9), [45:11](https://ref.ly/Isa45:11), [64:7](https://ref.ly/Isa64:7), even from the womb [44:2](https://ref.ly/Isa44:2), [44:24](https://ref.ly/Isa44:24); the servant of Yahweh from the womb [49:5](https://ref.ly/Isa49:5); of the formation of the individual man [43:7](https://ref.ly/Isa43:7); Jeremiah in the womb [Je 1:5](https://ref.ly/Jer1:5); the eye of man [Psalm 94:9](https://ref.ly/Ps94:9); the locust [Am 7:1](https://ref.ly/Amos7:1); Leviathan [Psalm 104:26](https://ref.ly/Ps104:26); the dry land [95:5](https://ref.ly/Ps95:5); the earth [Is 45:18](https://ref.ly/Isa45:18), [45:18](https://ref.ly/Isa45:18); the mountains [Am 4:13](https://ref.ly/Amos4:13); הַכֹּל [Je 10:16](https://ref.ly/Jer10:16) \= [51:19](https://ref.ly/Jer51:19). Fig. perh. lost sight of in some of the above, and quite certainly in the forming of light [Is 45:7](https://ref.ly/Isa45:7), of summer and winter [Psalm 74:17](https://ref.ly/Ps74:17), the רוּחַ of man [Zc 12:1](https://ref.ly/Zech12:1), and the לב of men [Psalm 33:15](https://ref.ly/Ps33:15). **b.** fig. for *frame, pre\-ordain, plan* (in divine purpose), of a situation מֵרָחוֺק [Is 22:11](https://ref.ly/Isa22:11); of an occurrence מִימֵי קֶדֶם [37:26](https://ref.ly/Isa37:26) \= [2 K 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:25); יָצַרְתִּי אַף אֶעֱשֶנָּה *I have planned, I will also do it* [46:11](https://ref.ly/Isa46:11); רעה על ´י [Je 18:11](https://ref.ly/Jer18:11); לַהֲכִינָהּ ´י *devised it to establish it* [33:2](https://ref.ly/Jer33:2). **Niph.** *Pf.* לְפָנַי לאֹ־נוֺצַר אֵל [Is 43:10](https://ref.ly/Isa43:10) *before me a god was not formed* (created). **Pu.** *Pf.* יָמִים יֻצָּרוּ *days* (that) *were pre\-ordained* (in the divine purpose) [Psalm 139:16](https://ref.ly/Ps139:16) (cf. **Qal, 2 b**). **Hoph.** *Impf.* כָּל־כְּלִי יוּצַר עָלַיִח לאֹ יִצְלָח [Is 54:17](https://ref.ly/Isa54:17) *any weapon that is formed against thee will not prosper*.

> \[Str3336] † I. יֵ֫צָר **n.m.**:[Is 26:3](https://ref.ly/Isa26:3) form, framing, purpose;—´י [Gn 6:5](https://ref.ly/Gen6:5) \+ 5 t.; יִצְדוֺ [Dt 31:21](https://ref.ly/Deut31:21), Hb [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hab2:18); יִצְרֵנוּ [Psalm 103:14](https://ref.ly/Ps103:14); —**1\.** *pottery*, formed by the potter [Is 29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16). **2\.** form of a graven image Hb [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hab2:18). **3\.** form of man as made of the dust [Psalm 103:14](https://ref.ly/Ps103:14). **4\.** of what is framed in the mind (cf. יָצַר **1 c, 2 b**), *imagination, device, purpose:* (ב)ת לב(ו)יצר מחשׁב [Gn 6:5](https://ref.ly/Gen6:5) (J), [1 Ch 29:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:18); מחשׁבות ´י [28:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:9); לב ´י [Gn 8:21](https://ref.ly/Gen8:21) (J); יֵצֶר alone [Dt 31:21](https://ref.ly/Deut31:21) (J); יֵצֶר סָמוּךְ [Is 26:3](https://ref.ly/Isa26:3) a *stedfast purpose* (or *frame* of mind). (In NH יֵצֶר is common in sense of *impulse:* יצר הטוב and יצר הרע of good and bad tendency in man.)

> \[Str3337] † II. יֵּ֫צֶר **n.pr.m.** son of Naphtali [Gn 46:24](https://ref.ly/Gen46:24) \= [1 Ch 7:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:13), [Nu 26:49](https://ref.ly/Num26:49) (P).

> \[Str3339,3340] † יִצְרִי **n.pr.m. a.** chief of one of the Levitical choirs of singers [1 Ch 25:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:11). **b. adj.gent.** of II. יֵצֶר, c. art. \= subst. coll. [Nu 26:49](https://ref.ly/Num26:49) (P).

## יָצַת (id: BDB04099)

(NH *id.* rare))—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתִּצַּת [Is 9:17](https://ref.ly/Isa9:17); יִצַָּתּוּ [33:12](https://ref.ly/Isa33:12), [Je 51:58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58) (Köi. 431\); 3 fpl. תִּצַּתְנָה [Je 49:2](https://ref.ly/Jer49:2); —*kindle*, intr., fig. of wickedness, [Is 9:17](https://ref.ly/Isa9:17); sq. בָּאֵשׁ *be kindled with fire* ( \= set on fire), of thorns (in sim.) [33:12](https://ref.ly/Isa33:12), [Je 49:2](https://ref.ly/Jer49:2) (of dependent towns and villages of Rabbah, under fig. of daughters), [51:58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58) (of gates of Babylon). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִצְּתָה [2 K 22:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:13), [Je 9:11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:11) \+ [2:15](https://ref.ly/Jer2:15) Kt (Qr נִצְּתוּ); וְנִצְּתָה consec. [2 K 22:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:17) [Je 46:19](https://ref.ly/Jer46:19); נִצְּתוּ [Ne 1:3](https://ref.ly/Neh1:3) \+ 2 t. \+ [Je 2:15](https://ref.ly/Jer2:15) Qr (cf. supr.);—*be kindled*, fig. of wrath of ´י, [2 K 22:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:13), [22:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:17); *be burned*, of gates of Jerusalem, sq. בָּאֵשׁ, [Ne 1:3](https://ref.ly/Neh1:3), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Neh2:17); \= *desolated* [Je 2:15](https://ref.ly/Jer2:15) (of cities of Israel), cf. [46:19](https://ref.ly/Jer46:19), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Jer9:9) (of mountains and pastures); of land [9:11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:11) (‖אָֽבְדָה). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִצִּית [Je 11:16](https://ref.ly/Jer11:16); 1 S. וְהִצַּתִּי consec. [Je 17:27](https://ref.ly/Jer17:27) \+ 5 t.; והוחירתיה [2 S 14:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:30) Kt; (\< Qr וְהַצִּיתוּהָ *Imv.*, Ges§ 71 Köi. 431\); הִצִּיתוּ v:[31](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:31), [Je 51:30](https://ref.ly/Jer51:30); וְהִצִּיתוּ consec. Gesl. c.); וַיַָּצִּיתוּ [Jos 8:19](https://ref.ly/Josh8:19) \+ 2 t.; תַּצִּיתוּ v:[8](https://ref.ly/Josh8:8); *Imv.* [2 S 14:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:30) Qr, cf. supr.; *Pt.* מַצִּית Ez [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3); —*kindle, set on fire*, sq. acc., עִיר \+ בָּאֵשׁ [Jos 8:8](https://ref.ly/Josh8:8), [8:19](https://ref.ly/Josh8:19) (JE), [Je 32:29](https://ref.ly/Jer32:29) ( \+ שרף); field of corn, \+ בָּאֵש [2 S 14:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:30), [14:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:30), [14:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:31); dwellings (no באשׁ) [Je 51:30](https://ref.ly/Jer51:30), cf. also Ju [9:49](https://ref.ly/Judg9:49); also sq. acc. אֵשׁ \+ prep. לְ, [Je 11:16](https://ref.ly/Jer11:16) (of Jerusalem under fig. of olive\-tree); elsewh. \+ בְּ, [Am 1:14](https://ref.ly/Amos1:14), ´והצתי אשׁ ב (hence [Je 17:27](https://ref.ly/Jer17:27), [21:14](https://ref.ly/Jer21:14), [43:12](https://ref.ly/Jer43:12), [49:27](https://ref.ly/Jer49:27), [50:32](https://ref.ly/Jer50:32)) [La 4:11](https://ref.ly/Lam4:11), Ez [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3).

## יקב (id: BDB04100)

יקב (√ of foll.; comp. Ar. وَقَبَ *be sunk, depressed;* وَقْبٌ *hollow, cavity*).

> \[Str3342] יֶ֫קֶב **n.m.**:[Pr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Prov3:10) wine\-vat (a trough or hollow excavated (חצב [Is 5:2](https://ref.ly/Isa5:2)) in the rock for receiving the juice trodden out in the גַּת: Benz212 f.), sometimes also wine\-press (the trough in which the grapes were trodden out);—abs. יֶ֫קֶב [Is 5:2](https://ref.ly/Isa5:2) \+ 4 t.; ָ\+יקֶב [Nu 18:30](https://ref.ly/Num18:30); cstr. ֶ י֫קֶב Ju [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25); sf. יִקְבְֶָ [Dt 15:14](https://ref.ly/Deut15:14), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Deut16:13); pl. יְקָבִּים [Jb 24:11](https://ref.ly/Job24:11) \+ 4 t.; יִקְבֵּי [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10); יְקָבֶיְ [Pr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Prov3:10); —wine\- *vat,* [Is 5:2](https://ref.ly/Isa5:2), [Hg 2:16](https://ref.ly/Hag2:16), [Jo 4:13](https://ref.ly/Joel4:13) (‖גַּת), [Pr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Prov3:10); oft. ‖גֹּרֶן, [Nu 18:27](https://ref.ly/Num18:27), [18:30](https://ref.ly/Num18:30), [Dt 15:14](https://ref.ly/Deut15:14), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Deut16:13), [2 K 6:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:27), [Ho 9:2](https://ref.ly/Hos9:2), [Jo 2:24](https://ref.ly/Joel2:24); appar. of the *wine\-press* [Jb 24:11](https://ref.ly/Job24:11) יְקָבִים דָּֽרְכוּ, [Is 16:10](https://ref.ly/Isa16:10) (hence [Je 48:33](https://ref.ly/Jer48:33)). Designating particular localities, Ju [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25) יֶקֶב זְאֵב, [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10) יִקְבֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ (near Jerusalem).

> \[Str3343] יְקַבְצְאֵל v. sub קבץ. p. 868

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 18:30; Job 24:11; Deuteronomy 16:13; Proverbs 3:10; Isaiah 5:2; Joel 2:24; Zechariah 14:10; 2 Kings 6:27; Deuteronomy 15:14; Haggai 2:16; Judges 7:25; Isaiah 16:10; Jeremiah 48:33; Numbers 18:27; Joel 4:13; Hosea 9:2


## יָקַד (id: BDB04103)

\[Str3344] † \[יָקַד] **vb.** be kindled, burn (Aram. יְקַד, ܝܺܩܶܕ, *burn*, intrans.; Ar. وَقَدَ, *id.*)—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יֵקַד [Is 10:16](https://ref.ly/Isa10:16); 3 fs. וַתִּיקַד [Dt 32:22](https://ref.ly/Deut32:22); *Pt. act.* f. יֹקֶ֫דֶת [Is 65:5](https://ref.ly/Isa65:5); *pass.* יָקוּד [30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14); —*be kindled*, fig. of judgment, יֵקַד יְקֹד כִּיקוֺד אֵשׁ [Is 10:16](https://ref.ly/Isa10:16); so *burn*, [Dt 32:22](https://ref.ly/Deut32:22) *a fire hath been kindled* (קָֽדְחָה) *in my wrath*, וַּתִּיקַר עַד־שְׁאוֺל *and it burneth unto shₑ’ôl;* fig. of people displeasing to ´י, אֵלֶּה עָשָׁן בְּאַפִּי אֵשׁ יֹקֶדֶת כָּל־הַיּוֺם [Is 65:5](https://ref.ly/Isa65:5); pt. יָקוּד *kindled*, as subst. לַחְהתּוֺת אֵשׁ מִיָּקוּד [30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14) *to take fire from that which is knidled*, i.e. from the hearth (LgBN 60\). **Hoph.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תּוּקַד [Lv 6:2](https://ref.ly/Lev6:2), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Lev6:5), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Lev6:6); תּוּקָד [Je 15:14](https://ref.ly/Jer15:14), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Jer17:4); —*be burning, burn*, of (perpetual) altar\-fire [Lv 6:2](https://ref.ly/Lev6:2), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Lev6:5), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Lev6:6) (P); fig. of ´י's judgment, אֵשׁ קָֽדְחָה בְּאַפִּי עֲלֵיכֶם תּוּקָר [Je 15:14](https://ref.ly/Jer15:14), similarly [17:4](https://ref.ly/Jer17:4) (cf. [Dt 32:22](https://ref.ly/Deut32:22) supr., as prob. source of both).

> \[Str3350] † יְקוֺד **n.\[m.]** a burning (properly *Inf. cstr.* of יָקַד)—only [Is 10:16](https://ref.ly/Isa10:16), abs. and cstr., fig. of ´י's judgment, v. \[יָקַד] supr.

> \[Str4168] † מוֺקֵד **n.\[m.]** a burning mass;—abs. עַצְמוֺתַי כְּמוֺקֵד נִחָרוּ [Psalm 102:4](https://ref.ly/Ps102:4) my bones are scorched through like a *burning mass;* pl. cstr., fig. of ´י's judgment, מוֺקְדֵי עוֺלָם [Is 33:14](https://ref.ly/Isa33:14) (‖אֵשׁ אוֺכֵלָ֔ה).

> \[Str4169,3347] † מוֺקְדָה **n.f.** hearth ( \= *place of burning*), only of *altar\-hearth,* the plate or top of altar, on which burnt\-offering was laid and consumed, עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ ´הִוא הָעוֺלָה עַל מ [Lv 6:2](https://ref.ly/Lev6:2) (sq. וְאֵשׁ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ תּוּקַד בּוֺ).

> † יָקְדְעָם **n.pr.loc.** a city of Judah [Jos 15:56](https://ref.ly/Josh15:56); site unknown. LXX ΙαρειΚαμ, A Ιεκδααμ, LXXL ΙεΚνααμ.

## יקה (id: BDB04108)

יקה (√ of following; cf. Ar. وَقَى *preserve* (from evil, or fear); VIII. *be pious, careful of one's religious duties* (Lane3059)).

> \[Str3348] † יָקֶה **n.pr.m.** father of Agur, אָגוּר בִּן־יָקֶה הַמַּשָּא [Pr 30:1](https://ref.ly/Prov30:1) (read probably הַמַּשָּאִי or מִמַּשָּא: v. Be\-Nowxvi ff.)

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 30:1; 1 Chronicles 4:18


## יקהּ (id: BDB04111)

יקהּ (√ of following; cf. Ar. وَقِهَ *be obedient;* so As. *âḳû* (וקה?) DlHWB 123; Sab. וקה *hear* (favourably), *hear* (and answer), also in n.pr. וקהאל, יקהאל, יקהמלך DHM in MV).

> \[Str3349] † \[יִקְהָה] **n.f.** obedience;—cstr. יִקְּהַת עַמִּים [Gn 49:10](https://ref.ly/Gen49:10) c. subject. genit. (Daghesh forte dirim. Ges§ 20, 2 b LagBN 82\), לִיֶקְּהַת אֵם [Pr 30:17](https://ref.ly/Prov30:17) c. object. genit.

> יָקוֺט [Jb 8:14](https://ref.ly/Job8:14) v. קוט. p. 876

> \[Str3351,3359–61,3365] יְקוּם, יְקִּים, יְקַמְיָה, יְקַמְעָם, יָקְמְעָם v. sub קּום. p. 879–80

> \[Str3354] יְקוּתִיאֵל v. sub יקה. above

> \[Str3355] † יָקְטָן **n.pr.m.** son of Eber, descendant of Shem (DHM in MV comp. his name in Arab. tradition, viz. *Ḳaḥtân:* the √ √ قحط and وقط being both expl. in *Muḥîṭ* by ضرب *strike, beat;* v. Lane1777ff.)—[Gn 10:25](https://ref.ly/Gen10:25), [10:26](https://ref.ly/Gen10:26), [10:29](https://ref.ly/Gen10:29) \= [1 Ch 1:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:19), [1:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:20), [1:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:23), ‘father’ of various tribes of Yemen; LXX ΙεΚταν.

> \[Str3362] † יָקְנְעָם **n.pr.loc.** Canaanitish city, with a king, defined by לַכַּרְמֶל [Jos 12:22](https://ref.ly/Josh12:22); in Zebulun [19:11](https://ref.ly/Josh19:11); Levitical city [21:34](https://ref.ly/Josh21:34).

## יָקַע (id: BDB04118)

\[Str3363] † יָקַע **vb.** be dislocated, alienated (Ar. وَقَعَ is *fall, fall down, fall upon, befal, happen*)—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. תֵּקַע [Je 6:8](https://ref.ly/Jer6:8); וַתֵּ֫קַע [Gn 32:26](https://ref.ly/Gen32:26) \+ 2 t.; *dislocated* [Gn 32:26](https://ref.ly/Gen32:26) (Jacob's thigh); elsewhere fig. of נפשׁ; *torn away, alienated* from any one, sq. מִן [Je 6:8](https://ref.ly/Jer6:8), Ez [23:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:17); sq. מֵעַל v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:18). **Hiph.** *Pf.* וְהוֺקַעֲנוּם consec. [2 S 21:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:6); *Impf.* וַיֹּקִיעֻם v:[9](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:9); *Imv.* הוֺקַע [Nu 25:4](https://ref.ly/Num25:4); of some solemn form of execution, but mng. uncertain: Aq Ges *impale;* LXX SyrVer *expose* (ἐξηλιάζειν, παραδειγματἰζειν); Targum Vulgate *crucify;* RSRel. Sem. 398 (419\) *throw down* (Ar. أَوْقَعَ) a rock (cf. [2 Ch 25:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:12)): \+ ליהזה [2 S 21:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:6) (´לפני י v:[9](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:9)), [Nu 25:4](https://ref.ly/Num25:4). **Hoph.** *Pt.* הַמּוּקָעִים [2 S 21:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:13); pass. of Hiph.

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 25:12; Numbers 25:4; 2 Samuel 21:13; 2 Samuel 21:9; Ezekiel 23:17; 2 Samuel 21:6; Genesis 32:26; Jeremiah 6:8; Ezekiel 23:18


## יָקַץ (id: BDB04119)

\[Str3364] † \[יָקַץ] **vb.** awake (Ar. يَقِظَ *wake, be awake*)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִקָץ [1 K 18:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:27); וַיִּיקַץ [Gn 28:16](https://ref.ly/Gen28:16) \+ 3 t.; וַיִּקַץ Ju [16:20](https://ref.ly/Judg16:20), [Psalm 78:65](https://ref.ly/Ps78:65), [1 K 3:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:15) (v. Baer); וָאִיקָץ [Gn 41:21](https://ref.ly/Gen41:21); יִֽקְצוּ Hb [2:7](https://ref.ly/Hab2:7); —*awake,* [Gn 41:4](https://ref.ly/Gen41:4), [41:7](https://ref.ly/Gen41:7), [41:21](https://ref.ly/Gen41:21) (E), [1 K 3:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:15); of Baal [18:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:27); sq. מִשְּׁנָתוֺ [Gn 28:16](https://ref.ly/Gen28:16) (J), Ju [16:14](https://ref.ly/Judg16:14), [16:20](https://ref.ly/Judg16:20); of Noah, מִיֵּינוֺ [Gn 9:24](https://ref.ly/Gen9:24) (J), i.e. from drunken sleep; fig. of ´י [Psalm 78:65](https://ref.ly/Ps78:65), i.e. *become suddenly active;* of enemies Hb [2:7](https://ref.ly/Hab2:7).—Vid. also קיץ **Hiph.**

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 41:7; Psalms 78:65; Genesis 9:24; Genesis 28:16; Habakkuk 2:7; Judges 16:14; Genesis 41:4; 1 Kings 3:15; Genesis 41:21; Judges 16:20; 1 Kings 18:27


## יָקַר (id: BDB04120)

\[Str3365] † \[יָקַר] **vb.** be precious, prized, appraised (NH *id.,* Pi., Hiph.; Aram. יְקַר, ܝܺܩܰܪ, *be heavy, precious;* Pa. *honour;* יַקִּיר, ܝܰܩܺܝܪ *weighty, precious, honoured;* cf. As. *aḳâru, be precious, costly,* and deriv., DlHWB 240; Ar. وَقَرَ *be heavy,* II. *honour;* وَقَارٌ *dignity;* Sab. n. יקר *honour* DHMZMG 1883, 402\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. יָֽקְרָה [1 S 16:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:21); 2 ms. יָקַרְתָּ [Is 43:4](https://ref.ly/Isa43:4); 1 S. יָקַרְתִּי [Zc 11:13](https://ref.ly/Zech11:13); 3 pl. יָקְֽרוּ [Psalm 139:17](https://ref.ly/Ps139:17); *Impf.* וַיִּיקַר [1 S 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:30); וְיֵקַר [Psalm 49:9](https://ref.ly/Ps49:9); וְיֵיקַר [72:14](https://ref.ly/Ps72:14); juss. (־נָא)תִּיקַר [2 K 1:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:13), [1:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:14); —**1\.** *be precious:* **a.** \= *highly valued, esteemed,* David's name, וַיִּיקַר שְׁמוֺ מְאֹד [1 S 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:30); of Israel, יָקַרְתָּ בְעֵינַי [Is 43:4](https://ref.ly/Isa43:4) (\+ נִכְבַּדְתָּ, אֲהַבְתִּיְ); of God's thoughts וְלִי מַה־יָּֽקִרוּ רֵעֶיְ [Psalm 139:17](https://ref.ly/Ps139:17) (Ew Hi Hup render *hard, difficult:* cf. יַקִּירָה [Dn 2:11](https://ref.ly/Dan2:11)). **b.** esp. of life (נֶפֶשׁ), in phr. יָֽקִרָה נַפְשִׁי בְּעֵינֶיְ [1 S 26:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:21), i.e. thou hast spared it; so (juss.) [2 K 1:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:13), [1:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:14); similarly יֵיקַר דָּמָם בְּעֵינָיו [Psalm 72:14](https://ref.ly/Ps72:14) (‖מִתּוֺח וּמֵחָמָס יִגְאַל נַפְשָׁס). **c.** *be costly,* וְיֵקַר פִּדְיוֺן נַפְשָׁם [Psalm 49:9](https://ref.ly/Ps49:9). **2\.** *be appraised, valued,* אֶדֶר הַיְקָר אֲשֶׁר יָקַרְתִּי מֵעֲלֵיהֶם [Zc 11:13](https://ref.ly/Zech11:13) *the magnificence of the price at which I was appraised (and dismissed)* *from them!* **Hiph.** *Impf.* 1 s. אוֺקִיר אֱנוֺשׁ מִפָּז [Is 13:12](https://ref.ly/Isa13:12) *I will make men more rare* (lit. *precious*) *than fine gold. Imv.* הֹקַר [Pr 25:17](https://ref.ly/Prov25:17) *make rare* (i.e. *withhold*) thy foot from the house of thy friend.

> \[Str3368] † יָקָר **adj.** precious, rare, splendid, weighty;—abs. יָקָר [1 S 3:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:1) \+ 9 t.; cstr. יְקַ־ר [Pr 17:27](https://ref.ly/Prov17:27) Qr (Kt וקר; v. infr.); כִּיקַר [Psalm 37:20](https://ref.ly/Ps37:20); f. יְקָרָה [2 S 12:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:30) \+ 14 t.; cstr. יִקְרַת [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16); mpl. יְקָרִים [La 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lam4:2); fpl. יְקָדוֺת [1 K 5:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:31) \+ 2 t. \+ [Zc 14:6](https://ref.ly/Zech14:6) (v. infr.); יְקָרֹת [1 K 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:9); sf. בְּיִקְּרוֺהֶָיְ [Psalm 45:10](https://ref.ly/Ps45:10) (so Baer; van d. H ´בִּיקְּ);—**1\.** *precious:* **a.** *costly,* אֲבָנִים יְקָרוֺת of costly building\-stones [1 K 5:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:31), [7:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:9), [7:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:10), [7:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:11) cf. פִּנַּת יִקְרַת מוּסָד מוּסָּד [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16) *a costly corner* (\-stone) of a foundation,—on cstr. v. DaSynt. § 28, R. 3, 6; הוֺן יָקָר *costly wealth* [Pr 1:13](https://ref.ly/Prov1:13), [12:27](https://ref.ly/Prov12:27); הוֺן יָקָר וְנָעִים [24:1](https://ref.ly/Prov24:1). **b.** *precious, highly valued:* נֶפֶשׁ יְקָרָה [Pr 6:26](https://ref.ly/Prov6:26) *precious life;* opp. זוֺלֵל [Je 15:19](https://ref.ly/Jer15:19) (of choice elements of character); הַמָּֽוְתָה לַחֲסִידָיו ´יָקָר בְּעֵינֵי י [Psalm 116:15](https://ref.ly/Ps116:15); יְקָרָה הִיא מפניים [Pr 3:15](https://ref.ly/Prov3:15) *she* (wisdom) *is more precious than rubies* (v. also **c**); *prized,* of ´חסד י [Psalm 36:8](https://ref.ly/Ps36:8); \= subst. בְּנוֺת מְלָכִים בְּיִקְּרותֶָיְ [Psalm 45:10](https://ref.ly/Ps45:10) *king's daughters are among thy precious* ( \= *dear*) *ones.* **c.** אֶבֶן יְקָרָה coll. *precious stones, jewels* [2 S 12:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:30) \= [1 Ch 20:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:2), [1 K 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:2), [10:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:10), [1:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:11) \= [2 Ch 9:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:1), [9:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:9), [9:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:10), [1 Ch 29:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:3), [2 Ch 3:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:6), [32:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:27), Ez [27:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:22), [28:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:13) (list of precious stones follows), [Dn 11:38](https://ref.ly/Dan11:38); so also [La 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lam4:2), read אַבְנֵי צִיּוֺן הַיְקָרִים (for MT ´בְּנֵי וגו), see VB; שֹׁהַם יָקָר [Jb 28:16](https://ref.ly/Job28:16) (v. also [Pr 3:15](https://ref.ly/Prov3:15) sub **b**). **2\.** *rare,* [1 S 3:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:1) (cf. √ Hiph. [Is 13:12](https://ref.ly/Isa13:12)). **3\.** *glorious, splendid* (cf. Aram.), of the moon [Jb 31:26](https://ref.ly/Job31:26); as subst. \= *glory,* the wicked perish, כִּיקַר כָּרִים [Psalm 37:20](https://ref.ly/Ps37:20) *like the glory of the pastures* (i.e. like gay, but short\-lived, flowers). **4\.** (late and Aram.) *weighty, influential,* יָקָר מֵחָכְמָה [Ec 10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1) *weightier than wisdom* and honour is a little folly.—In [Pr 17:27](https://ref.ly/Prov17:27) read Kt וְקַר־רוּחַ *and one cool of spirit* (see VB); in [Zc 14:6](https://ref.ly/Zech14:6) read לאֹ יִהְיֶה אוֺר וְקָרוֺת וְקִפָּאוֺן *there shall not be light but cold and congelation* (see *id.* and Vrss.); v., however, also Addenda.

> \[Str3366] † יְקָר **n.m.** preciousness, price, honour (late: cf. BA Targum יְקָר, ܐܻܝܩܳܪ *glory*);—abs. יְקָר [Zc 11:13](https://ref.ly/Zech11:13) \+ 5 t.; בִּיקָר etc. [Psalm 49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:13) \+ 3 t.; cstr. יְקָר [Est 1:4](https://ref.ly/Esth1:4); sf. בִּיקָרוֺ [6:6](https://ref.ly/Esth6:6) \+ 4 t.; יְקָרָהּ [Je 20:5](https://ref.ly/Jer20:5); —**1\.** *preciousness:* **a.** \= *precious* (*costly*) *things* (coll.) [Je 20:5](https://ref.ly/Jer20:5) (om. LXX), חֹסֶן וִיקָר Ez [22:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:25); כָּל־יְקָר [Jb 28:10](https://ref.ly/Job28:10). **b.** כְּלִי יְקָר [Pr 20:15](https://ref.ly/Prov20:15) *a jewel of preciousness* \= *precious jewel* (‖זָהָב, פְּנִינִים). **2\.** *price* [Zc 11:13](https://ref.ly/Zech11:13), v. יָקַר **2\. 3\.** *honour,* [Psalm 49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:13), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Ps49:20); elsewhere only Est.: יְקָר תִּפְאֶרֶת גְּדוּלָּתוֺ [Est 1:4](https://ref.ly/Esth1:4) (‖כְּבוֺד מַלְכוּתוֺ); יִתְּנוּ יְקָר לְ v:[20](https://ref.ly/Esth1:20) all wives *give honour to* their lords; מַה־נַּעֲשָה יְקָר וּגְדוּלָּה לְמָרְדְּכַי [6:3](https://ref.ly/Esth6:3), c. עשה לְ also v:[6](https://ref.ly/Esth6:6); לַיְּהוּדִים הָֽיְתָה אוֺרָה וְשִמְחָה וְשָשן וִיקָר [8:16](https://ref.ly/Esth8:16); אִישׁ(הָ) אֲשֶׁר הַמֶּלֶח חָפֵץ בִּיקָרוֺ *the man in whose honour the king delighteth* [6:6](https://ref.ly/Esth6:6), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Esth6:7), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Esth6:9), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Esth6:9), [6:11](https://ref.ly/Esth6:11).
> 
> 430:a. יָקָר end: [Zc 14:6](https://ref.ly/Zech14:6) ‘*but*’ is very dub.; rd. prob. צוֺם וְקָרוֺת *there shall be neither heat nor cold,* etc. (We al.).

> \[Str3357] † יַקִּיר **adj.** intrans. very precious, dear; only הֲבֵן יַקִּיר לִי אֶפְרַיִם [Je 31:20](https://ref.ly/Jer31:20) *is Ephraim a very precious son unto me* ? (‖יֶלֶד שַׁעֲשׁוּעִים).

## יָקשׁ (id: BDB04124)

\[Str3369] † \[יָקשׁ] **vb.** lay a bait or lure (v. מוֺקֵשׁ), then gen. lay snares (v. also נקשׁ, קוֺשׁ)—**Qal** *Pf.* יָק֫שְׁתִּי [Je 50:24](https://ref.ly/Jer50:24); יָֽקְשׁוּ [Psalm 141:9](https://ref.ly/Ps141:9); *Pt.* pl. יוֺקְשִׁים [124:7](https://ref.ly/Ps124:7); —*lay snares,* fig. of devices of wicked [141:9](https://ref.ly/Ps141:9) (c. acc. cogn.); of ´י's plan to destroy Babylon [Je 50:24](https://ref.ly/Jer50:24) יקשׁתי לך וגם נלכדר; *Pt.* as subst. *bait\-layers, fowlers* [Psalm 124:7](https://ref.ly/Ps124:7) (cf. [141:9](https://ref.ly/Ps141:9)) sim. **Niph.** *Pf.* נוֺקַ֫שְׁתָּ [Pr 6:2](https://ref.ly/Prov6:2); וְנוֺקְשׁוּ consec. [Is 8:15](https://ref.ly/Isa8:15), [28:13](https://ref.ly/Isa28:13); *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּוָּקֵשׁ [Dt 7:25](https://ref.ly/Deut7:25); *be caught by a bait, ensnared,* in business entanglements [Pr 6:2](https://ref.ly/Prov6:2); in disastrous consequences of idolatry [Dt 7:25](https://ref.ly/Deut7:25) (‖נלכד); of those ensnared by ´י's plans (sq. ונלכדו) [Is 8:15](https://ref.ly/Isa8:15) [28:13](https://ref.ly/Isa28:13). **Pu.** *Pt.* יוּקָשִׁים (for מְיוּקָשִׁים, unless this should be read: Ges§ 52\. R. 6; Sta§ 220\): *entrapped,* in circumstances of life [Ec 9:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:12).

> \[Str3352] † יָקוֺשׁ **n.\[m.]** bait\-layer, fowler, פַּח יָקוֺשׁ [Ho 9:8](https://ref.ly/Hos9:8) *the snare of fowler.*

> \[Str3353] † יָקוּשׁ **n.\[m.]** id.; פַּח יָקוּשׁ [Psalm 91:3](https://ref.ly/Ps91:3); כְּצִפּוֺר מִיַּד יָקוּשׁ [Pr 6:5](https://ref.ly/Prov6:5); pl. יְקוּשִׁים [Je 5:26](https://ref.ly/Jer5:26) (in sim.)

> \[Str4170] † מוֺקֵשׁ **n.m.**:[Pr 12:13](https://ref.ly/Prov12:13) prop. a bait or lure in a fowler's net; then fig. snare—abs. מוֺקֵשׁ [Ex 10:7](https://ref.ly/Exod10:7) \+ 15 t.; cstr. [Pr 18:7](https://ref.ly/Prov18:7), [20:25](https://ref.ly/Prov20:25); pl. מוֺקְשִׁים [Psalm 64:6](https://ref.ly/Ps64:6), [Jb 40:24](https://ref.ly/Job40:24); מֹקְשִׁים [Psalm 140:6](https://ref.ly/Ps140:6); cstr. מוֺקְשֵי [18:6](https://ref.ly/Ps18:6) \+ 3 t.; מֹקְשֵׁי [2 S 22:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:6); *f.* מֹקְשׁוֺת [Psalm 141:9](https://ref.ly/Ps141:9); *bait* or *lure,* in a net for birds [Am 3:5](https://ref.ly/Amos3:5); will not pierce nostril of hippopotamus [Jb 40:24](https://ref.ly/Job40:24); elsewhere fig. of what allures and entraps any one to disaster or ruin; Moses a *snare* to Egyptians [Ex 10:7](https://ref.ly/Exod10:7) (J); מוֺקְשֵׁי עָם [Jb 34:30](https://ref.ly/Job34:30), of men who are the ruin of their people; idols and idol\-worship a pernicious *lure* to Israel [Ex 23:33](https://ref.ly/Exod23:33) (JE), [Dt 7:16](https://ref.ly/Deut7:16), Ju [2:3](https://ref.ly/Judg2:3), [8:27](https://ref.ly/Judg8:27), [Psalm 106:36](https://ref.ly/Ps106:36); so alliances with Canaanites [Ex 34:12](https://ref.ly/Exod34:12) (JE), [Jos 23:13](https://ref.ly/Josh23:13) (D); Michal, to David [1 S 18:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:21); of ´י as cause of ruin to evildoers [Is 8:14](https://ref.ly/Isa8:14); of plots of wicked [Psalm 64:6](https://ref.ly/Ps64:6), [140:6](https://ref.ly/Ps140:6) (vb. שִׁית; ‖פַּח, חֲבָלִים, רֶשֶׁת), [141:9](https://ref.ly/Ps141:9) (‖פַּח); a *lure* or *snare* for wicked in their transgressions [Pr 29:6](https://ref.ly/Prov29:6), [Psalm 69:23](https://ref.ly/Ps69:23) (‖פַּח); consisting in transgressions of lips [Pr 12:13](https://ref.ly/Prov12:13), cf. [18:7](https://ref.ly/Prov18:7), [20:25](https://ref.ly/Prov20:25); in wrathfulness [22:25](https://ref.ly/Prov22:25); in fear of man [29:25](https://ref.ly/Prov29:25); מָוֶת ´מ [Psalm 18:6](https://ref.ly/Ps18:6) \= [2 S 22:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:6) (‖חֶבְלֵי שְׁאוֺל), [Pr 13:14](https://ref.ly/Prov13:14), [14:27](https://ref.ly/Prov14:27).

> \[Str3370] † יָקְשָׁן **n.pr.m.** son of Abraham and Keturah [Gn 25:2](https://ref.ly/Gen25:2), [25:3](https://ref.ly/Gen25:3) \= [1 Ch 1:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:32), [1:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:32); LXX Ιεξαν; LXXL [Gn 25:2](https://ref.ly/Gen25:2), [25:3](https://ref.ly/Gen25:3) ΙεΚταν.

> \[Str3371] † יָקְתְאֵל **n.pr.loc.** (acc. to Ol:§ 277 k. 3 יקת is Impf. from a √ קות, meaning dubious; poss. \= יְקוּתִיאֵל q.v.; see Wetzst in De:[Is 3:703](https://ref.ly/Isa3:703) f.)—**1\.** in the *Shephelah* of Judah [Jos 15:38](https://ref.ly/Josh15:38), site unknown; LXX Ιακαρεηλ A Ιεκθαηλ, LXXL Ιεχθαηλ. **2\.** name given to סֶלַע (\= Petra) by King Amaziah, its captor [2 K 14:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:7); LXX Καθοηλ; A Ιεκθοηλ.

## יָרָא (id: BDB04137)

\[Str3384] † \[יָרָא] **vb.** shoot, pour (Aram. orthogr. of יָרָה q.v.)—**Qal** *Inf. cstr.* לִירוֺא בַּחִצִּים [2 Ch 26:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:15) *shoot with arrows.* **Hiph.** *Impf.* וַיֹּרא֯וּ [2 S 11:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:24) *and they shot at* (אֶ־ל); *Pt.* pl. הַמּוֺרִא֯ים v:[24](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:24) *the shooters, archers;* Kt to be read וַיֹּרִאוּ, הַמּוֺרַאִים; but Mas. nothing א as superfluous, would read וַיֹּרוּ, הַמּוֺרִים from יָרָה (Bö§ 1088 (8\), (9\)). **Hoph.** *Impf.* only in וּמַרְוֶה גַּם הוּא יוֺרֶָא [Pr 11:25](https://ref.ly/Prov11:25) he that watereth *shall* himself also *be watered* (Fl De MV al. derive—‘non bene,’ Thes—from √ רוה, q. v., יוֺרֶא for …יָרְוֶה; but v. Thes Ew§ 131 f. Bö§ 161 (4\) SS Buhl Köi. 585; cf. ירה **4**).

> \[Str3376] יִרְאִיָּיה֯ **n.pr.m.** v. sub ראה. p. 909

> \[Str3377–80] יָרֵב, יְרֻבַּ֫עַל, יָרָבְעָם, יְרֻבֶּ֫שֶׁת v. sub ריב. p. 914, 937

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 26:15; 2 Samuel 11:24; Proverbs 11:25

in P peculiarly יַרְדֵּן יְרֵחוֺ *the Jordan of* (at) *Jericho,* with עַל [Nu 26:3](https://ref.ly/Num26:3), [26:63](https://ref.ly/Num26:63), [31:12](https://ref.ly/Num31:12), [33:48](https://ref.ly/Num33:48), [33:50](https://ref.ly/Num33:50), [35:1](https://ref.ly/Num35:1), [36:13](https://ref.ly/Num36:13), with מֵעֵבֶר לְ [Nu 22:1](https://ref.ly/Num22:1), [34:15](https://ref.ly/Num34:15), [Jos 13:32](https://ref.ly/Josh13:32), [20:8](https://ref.ly/Josh20:8) (\= [1 Ch 6:63](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:63)), with מִן [Jos 16:1](https://ref.ly/Josh16:1) (E?); ´מַעְבְּרוֺת הַיַּר Ju [3:28](https://ref.ly/Judg3:28), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Judg12:5), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Judg12:6) *fords of the Jordan;* אֶל־גְּגְּלִילוֺת הַיַּרְדֵּן אֶל־עֵבֶר בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל [Jos 22:11](https://ref.ly/Josh22:11), cf. v:[10](https://ref.ly/Josh22:10) (both P; v. גלילות supr. p. 165\); אֶרֶץ יַרְדֵּן [Psalm 42:7](https://ref.ly/Ps42:7) (without art. only here and in foll.); יָגִּיחַיַרְדֵּן אֶל־פִּיהוּ [Jb 40:23](https://ref.ly/Job40:23) *a Jordan bursteth forth into his mouth.*

## יָרָה (id: BDB04144)

\[Str3384,4175] † יָרָה **vb.** throw, shoot (NH *shoot,* Hiph. *teach;* Aram. Aph. אוֺרִי *teach;* Eth. ወረወ፡ *jecit;* Amh. *warê* is *information, news,* v. NöZMG 1886, 724; As. *ârû, lead, guide,* DlHWB 241; *têrtu, law,* DlHA 49; Zinj. ירי *set, found,* DHMSendsch. 57, cf. **2** infr.—Buhl distinguishes **3** √ √ ירה: I. \= *throw,* cf. mod. Ar. *warra;* II. Hiph. \= *moisten,* cf. רוה; III. Hiph. \= *teach,* cf. ראה, Ar. رأى, رَوَى; but evidence for this division hardly sufficient)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´יר [Ex 15:4](https://ref.ly/Exod15:4) \+ 3 t.; 1 s. יָרִיתִי [Gn 31:51](https://ref.ly/Gen31:51), [Jos 18:6](https://ref.ly/Josh18:6); *Impf.* 1 pl. sf. וַנִּירָם [Nu 21:30](https://ref.ly/Num21:30) (SS after LXX וְנִינָם, see Ges§ 76 (2\) e); *Imv.* יְרֵה [2 K 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:17); *Inf. abs.* יָרֹה [Ex 19:13](https://ref.ly/Exod19:13) cstr. ת(וֺ)לִיר [Psalm 11:2](https://ref.ly/Ps11:2), [64:5](https://ref.ly/Ps64:5); *Pt.* יֹרֶה [Pr 26:18](https://ref.ly/Prov26:18); pl. יוֺרִים [1 Ch 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:3); יֹרִים [2 Ch 35:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:23); —**1\.** *throw, cast,* with acc.: cast lots [Jos 18:6](https://ref.ly/Josh18:6) (E); army into (בְּ) the sea [Ex 15:4](https://ref.ly/Exod15:4) (song). **2\.** *cast* (\= *lay, set*), corner\-stone [Jb 38:6](https://ref.ly/Job38:6); pillar [Gn 31:51](https://ref.ly/Gen31:51) (E; v. Zinj. supr.) **3\.** *shoot arrows*, abs. [Ex 19:13](https://ref.ly/Exod19:13) (E) [2 K 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:17); acc. of arrows [1 S 20:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:36), [20:37](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:37), [Pr 26:18](https://ref.ly/Prov26:18); acc. Pers. [Nu 21:30](https://ref.ly/Num21:30) (song, E) [Psalm 64:5](https://ref.ly/Ps64:5); with לְ Pers. [11:2](https://ref.ly/Ps11:2); רִים(וֺ)י *archers* [1 Ch 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:3), [2 Ch 35:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:23). **4\.** *throw water, rain:* [Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3), but v. יוֺרֶה *early rain* (cf. **Hiph. 3**). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִיָּרֶה *shot through* (with arrows) [Ex 19:13](https://ref.ly/Exod19:13) (E). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הֹרַנִי [Jb 30:19](https://ref.ly/Job30:19) (הֹרָנִי Baer); הוֺרָהוּ [2 K 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:3); הוֺרֵתָנִי [Psalm 119:102](https://ref.ly/Ps119:102); 1 s. וְהוֺרֵיתִ֫י [Ex 4:15](https://ref.ly/Exod4:15), [1 S 12:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:23); sf. הֹרֵיתִיְ [Pr 4:11](https://ref.ly/Prov4:11); הוֺרֵיתִיְ [Ex 4:12](https://ref.ly/Exod4:12); *Impf.* יוֺרֶה [Psalm 25:8](https://ref.ly/Ps25:8) \+ 5 t.; וַיּוֺר [2 K 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:17) (for this and other forms see Ges§ 76 (2\) e); וַיֹּרֵנִי [Pr 4:4](https://ref.ly/Prov4:4); יוֺרֵנוּ Ju [13:8](https://ref.ly/Judg13:8) \+ 2 t.; יוֺרֶנּוּ [Psalm 25:12](https://ref.ly/Ps25:12), [Is 28:26](https://ref.ly/Isa28:26); וַיּוֺרֵהוּ [Ex 15:25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25); יֹרֵם [2 K 17:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:27), [Psalm 64:8](https://ref.ly/Ps64:8); 2 f. sf. תּוֺרְ [Psalm 45:5](https://ref.ly/Ps45:5); תֹּרֶָָ֑ [Jb 12:7](https://ref.ly/Job12:7), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Job12:8); 2 m. sf. תּוֺרֵם [1 K 8:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:36) \= [2 Ch 6:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:27); 1 s. אוֺרֶה [1 S 20:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:20), [Jb 27:11](https://ref.ly/Job27:11); sf. אוֺרְ [Psalm 32:8](https://ref.ly/Ps32:8); 3 mpl. יוֺרוּ [Dt 24:8](https://ref.ly/Deut24:8) \+ 3 t.; יֹרוּ [2 S 11:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:20), [2 Ch 35:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:23); יֹרֻהוּ [Psalm 64:5](https://ref.ly/Ps64:5); יוֺרוְּ [Dt 17:10](https://ref.ly/Deut17:10) \+ 2 t.; *Imv.* sf. הֹרֵנִי [Jb 34:32](https://ref.ly/Job34:32); הוֺרֵנִי [Psalm 27:11](https://ref.ly/Ps27:11) \+ 2 t.; pl. sf. הוֺרוּנִי [Jb 6:24](https://ref.ly/Job6:24); *Inf. cstr.* הוֺרֹת [Gn 46:28](https://ref.ly/Gen46:28) \+ 3 t.; הוֺרֹתָם [Ex 24:12](https://ref.ly/Exod24:12); *Pt.* מוֺרֶה [1 S 20:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:36) \+ 5 t.; מֹרֶה [Pr 6:13](https://ref.ly/Prov6:13); pl. מוֺרִים [1 S 31:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:3) \+ 2 t.; sf. מוֺרָי [Pr 5:13](https://ref.ly/Prov5:13); מוֺרֶיְ [Is 30:20](https://ref.ly/Isa30:20), [30:20](https://ref.ly/Isa30:20); —**1\.** *throw, cast,* with ל, into the mire [Jb 30:19](https://ref.ly/Job30:19). **2\.** *shoot* (arrows) [1 S 20:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:20), [20:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:36), [2 S 11:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:20), [2 K 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:17), [19:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:32) \= [Is 37:33](https://ref.ly/Isa37:33); with ל, of Pers. [2 Ch 35:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:23); acc. Pers. [Psalm 64:5](https://ref.ly/Ps64:5), [64:8](https://ref.ly/Ps64:8); מוֺרִים *archers* [1 S 31:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:3), [31:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:3), [1 Ch 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:3). **3\.** *throw water, rain:* וְיוֺרֶה צֶדֶק לָכֶם [Ho 10:12](https://ref.ly/Hos10:12) *and rain righteousness for you* (Thes al. under **5**); hence מוֺרֶה *early rain* (cf. **Qal 4**). **4\.** *point out, shew:* לְהוֺרֹת לְפָנָיו גּשְׁנָה [Gn 46:28](https://ref.ly/Gen46:28) (J) *to point out before him* (the way) *to Goshen;* מֹרֶח בְּאֶצְבְּעֹתָיו [Pr 6:13](https://ref.ly/Prov6:13) *pointing out with his fingers;* acc. Pers. et rei [Ex 15:25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25) (JE) [Psalm 45:5](https://ref.ly/Ps45:5), [Jb 6:24](https://ref.ly/Job6:24). **5\.** *direct, teach, instruct:* **a.** of men, abs. Bezalel in handicraft [Ex 35:34](https://ref.ly/Exod35:34) (P); c. acc. Pers., a father his son [Pr 4:4](https://ref.ly/Prov4:4); the ancients Job [Jb 8:10](https://ref.ly/Job8:10); the animals and the earth, the friends of [Job 12:7](https://ref.ly/Job12:7), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Job12:8); c. 2 acc. [Is 28:9](https://ref.ly/Isa28:9); בּדֶרֶח in the way [1 S 12:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:23), [Psalm 25:8](https://ref.ly/Ps25:8), [32:8](https://ref.ly/Ps32:8), [Pr 4:11](https://ref.ly/Prov4:11); בְּיַד־אֵל *concerning the hand of Ēl* [Jb 27:11](https://ref.ly/Job27:11).—מוֺרֶה שֶׁקֶר *teaching lies* is used of prophet [Is 9:14](https://ref.ly/Isa9:14). **b.** specially of the authoritative *direction* (v. תּוֺרָה) given by priests on matters of ceremonial observance, with acc. rei and לְ Pers. [Dt 33:10](https://ref.ly/Deut33:10) (song), they *teach* thy judgments to Jacob, and thy direction (law) to Israel; double acc. [17:10](https://ref.ly/Deut17:10), [17:11](https://ref.ly/Deut17:11) according to the direction, wherewith they *direct thee*; [24:8](https://ref.ly/Deut24:8) (on leprosy), [Lv 10:11](https://ref.ly/Lev10:11) (P), abs. [14:57](https://ref.ly/Lev14:57) (P), [2 Ch 15:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:3) כֹּהֵן מוֺרֶה, Ez [44:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:23) the Zadokite priests את־עמי יֹרוּ בֵּין קֹדֶשׁ לְחֹל; [Mi 3:11](https://ref.ly/Mic3:11) the priests give such ‘direction\&rs for hire; less, technically, of Moses [Ex 24:12](https://ref.ly/Exod24:12) (E), of Jehoiada [2 K 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:3), of the Samaritan priests [2 K 17:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:27), [17:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:28). **c.** of God: c. acc. Pers. [Is 28:26](https://ref.ly/Isa28:26), [Psalm 119:102](https://ref.ly/Ps119:102); double acc. [Ex 4:12](https://ref.ly/Exod4:12), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Exod4:15) (J), Ju [13:8](https://ref.ly/Judg13:8), [Jb 34:32](https://ref.ly/Job34:32), [1 K 8:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:36) (\= אֶל הַדֶּרֶח [2 Ch 6:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:27)), [Psalm 27:11](https://ref.ly/Ps27:11), [86:11](https://ref.ly/Ps86:11), [119:33](https://ref.ly/Ps119:33); acc. Pers. בְּדֶרֶח [Psalm 25:12](https://ref.ly/Ps25:12); מִדְּרָכָיו *of his ways* [Is 2:3](https://ref.ly/Isa2:3) \= [Mi 4:2](https://ref.ly/Mic4:2). **d.** of idol\-image Hb [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hab2:18) (מוֺרֶה שָׁקֶר), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Hab2:19).

> \[Str3139] † יוֺרָה **n.pr.m.** ´בְּנֵי י one of the families of the restoration [Ezr 2:18](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:18) (LXX Ουρα, Ιωρα) \= חָרִיף [Ne 7:24](https://ref.ly/Neh7:24) (LXX Αρειφ).

> \[Str3138] † יוֺרֶה **n.\[m.]** early rain, which falls in Palestine from the last of October until the first of December, opp. מַלְקוֺשׁ: [Dt 11:14](https://ref.ly/Deut11:14), [Je 5:24](https://ref.ly/Jer5:24), [Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3) (where MT makes ´יו Pt., or Hiph. Impf., but v. We); cf. also I. מוֺרֶה. Vid. further RobBR i, 429 f. ChaplinPEF 1883, 8 ff. KleinZPV iv, 72 f.

> \[Str4175] † I. מוֺרֶה **n.m.**:Psalm 84. 7 (early) rain (cf. יוֺרֶה);—[Jo 2:23](https://ref.ly/Joel2:23) (‖גֶּשֶׁם, מַלְקוֺשׁ) v:[23](https://ref.ly/Joel2:23) (del. We), [Psalm 84:7](https://ref.ly/Ps84:7).

> \[Str4175,4176] † II. מוֺרֶה **n.m.**:[Is 30:20](https://ref.ly/Isa30:20) teacher;—abs. in אֵלוֺן מוֺרֶה [Gn 12:6](https://ref.ly/Gen12:6) (J) \= אֵלוֺנֵי מֹרֶה [Dt 11:30](https://ref.ly/Deut11:30) (Sam LXX אֵלוֺן), *the teacher's terebinth* (see אֵלוֺן) near Shechem; cf. גִּבְעַת הַמּוֺרֶה Ju [7:1](https://ref.ly/Judg7:1) *teacher's hill* near the plain of Jezreel, prob. Little Hermon, *Nebî Daḥî* BDPal 244; the terebinth being a holy tree from which divine teaching was given, and the hill of the teacher the seat of a holy place whence divine teaching was given; see also (of God) [Jb 36:22](https://ref.ly/Job36:22); here belong prob. likewise מוֺרָי [Pr 5:13](https://ref.ly/Prov5:13), and מוֺרֶיְ [Is 30:20](https://ref.ly/Isa30:20), [30:20](https://ref.ly/Isa30:20).
