## יָרַהּ (id: BDB04154)

\[Str7297] † \[יָרַהּ] **vb.** only **Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. תִּרְהוּ [Is 44:8](https://ref.ly/Isa44:8) (van d. H Baer, but prob. תִּֽרְהוּ si vera l., so Thes); ? *be stupefied* (cf. Ar. وَرِهَ, Thes al.), but Frey *fatuus et stolidus fuit*; \< Ew al. who rd. תִּֽרְאוּ fr. ירא (‖פחד).

> \[Str3389] יֲרוּשָׁלַ֫םִ, יְרוּשָׁלַ֫יִם:644 **n.pr.loc.** Jerusalem (in As. *Urusalim,* Tel Amarna, ZimZA, 1891, 252, 254; *Ursalimmu,* COTGloss.; LXX Ιερουσαλημ; √ and mng. dub.; Rel Ew al. der. fr. יְרוּשׁ שׁלם *possession of peace* (or *Salem's possession*); Thes al. fr. יָרָה, i.e. יְרוּ \+ שָׁלֵם *a foundation of peace;* GrillZAW, 1884, 134 ff. *foundation of Shalem* (Sh. \= God of peace, \= ´י); but name not certainly Hebr.; acc. to SayceAcad. Feb. 7, 1891, 138; Higher Crit. 176 (opposed by ZimZA. 1891, 263\), JastrJBL xi, 1892, 185 \= *Uru* (*city*) \+ *Salim*, n. pr. div.);—usu. יְרוּשָׁלַ֫םִ (*Qr perpetuum*), [Jos 10:10](https://ref.ly/Josh10:10) \+; יְרוּשָׁלָםִ [1 S 17:54](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:54) \+; לַ֫יִם\- 5 t. acc. to Mas. (vid. FrensdorffMass. Magna, 293\), viz. [Je 26:18](https://ref.ly/Jer26:18), [1 Ch 3:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:5), [2 Ch 25:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:1), [32:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:9) (c. ה loc.), [Est 2:6](https://ref.ly/Esth2:6), (but לָ֫םִ\- [1 Ch 3:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:5) van d. H Baer), so Maccab. coins, LevyGesch. d. jüd. Münz. 42 f.; c. ה loc. יְרוּשָׁלַ֫מֳה [1 K 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:2), [Is 36:2](https://ref.ly/Isa36:2), Ez [8:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:3); (לַ֫יְמָה\- [2 Ch 32:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:9) supr.); לָמֳה\- [2 K 9:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:28); with prefixes: ´בִּירו [2 S 9:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:13) \+; ´לִירו [2 K 18:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:22) \+; ´מִירו [2 S 15:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:11) \+; ´וִירו [1 K 23:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:1) \+;—*Jerusalem,* renowned as capital of all Israel, afterwards of southern kingdom, seat of central worship in temple, first named as city of Canaanite Adoni\-Ṣedek † [Jos 10:1](https://ref.ly/Josh10:1), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Josh10:3), [10:5](https://ref.ly/Josh10:5), [10:23](https://ref.ly/Josh10:23) (all JE), cf. [12:10](https://ref.ly/Josh12:10) (D); inhabited by Jebusites [Jos 15:63](https://ref.ly/Josh15:63), [15:63](https://ref.ly/Josh15:63) (P), Ju [1:21](https://ref.ly/Judg1:21), [1:21](https://ref.ly/Judg1:21), cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Judg1:7) (Adoni\-Bezek); identif. with יְבוּס Ju [19:10](https://ref.ly/Judg19:10), and הַיְבוּסִי (q.v.) [Jos 15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8), [18:28](https://ref.ly/Josh18:28) (both P); captured by Judah Ju [1:8](https://ref.ly/Judg1:8); first named in connexion with David [1 S 17:54](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:54) †; taken possession of by David as king [2 S 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:6); David's royal seat v:[5](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:5), v:[13](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:13), v:[14](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:14), [8:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:7), [11:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:1) \+; it remained the capital until taken by Nebuchadrezzar, B. C. 588, [2 K 25:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:1) \+; it became the chief home of the returned exiles [Ezr 1:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:11), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:11), [Ne 2:11](https://ref.ly/Neh2:11), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Neh2:17) \+; mentioned S 31 t., K 92 t., Ch 151 t., Ezr 25 t., Ne 38 t., Is:1 27 t., Is:2, Is:3 22 t., Je 107 t., Ez 26 t., Zc 41 t., etc.—Vid. also n.pr.loc. יְבוּס, שָׁלֵם.

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 3:5; 2 Samuel 5:14; Joshua 10:10; Joshua 15:8; Nehemiah 2:11; Joshua 18:28; Joshua 10:23; 2 Kings 18:22; Ezra 2:11; 2 Samuel 11:1; 2 Kings 23:1; Joshua 12:10; Jeremiah 26:18; 2 Chronicles 25:1; 2 Samuel 5:13; Isaiah 36:2; Joshua 15:63; 2 Kings 25:1; Joshua 10:1; Judges 1:7; 1 Samuel 17:54; 2 Samuel 5:6; Esther 2:6; Judges 1:8; 2 Samuel 15:11; 2 Samuel 9:13; Judges 1:21; Nehemiah 2:17; Isaiah 44:8; 2 Samuel 5:5; Joshua 10:3; Ezekiel 8:3; Judges 19:10; 2 Kings 9:28; Ezra 1:11; 1 Kings 10:2; Joshua 10:5; 2 Chronicles 32:9; 2 Samuel 8:7


## ירח (id: BDB04156)

ירח (√ of foll.; meaning dub.; acc. to MV Buhl \= ארח, of moon as *wanderer,* so LagBN 46\).

> \[Str3394] † יָרֵחַ **n.m.**:[Jos 10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13) moon, esp. poet. (NH *id.;* Ph. ירח; As. *iriḥu,* acc. to PinchesBOR Aug. 1888, 207; Eth. ወርሓ፡ *moon, month;* cf. sub יֶרַח)—abs. יָרֵחַ [Gn 37:9](https://ref.ly/Gen37:9) \+ 25 t.; sf. וִיֽרֵחֵח [Is 60:20](https://ref.ly/Isa60:20):—*moon,* usually named with sun [Jos 10:12](https://ref.ly/Josh10:12), [10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13) (poem in JE), [Psalm 72:5](https://ref.ly/Ps72:5) (עִם־שָׁמֶשׁ וְלִפְנֵי יָרֵחַ), [89:38](https://ref.ly/Ps89:38) (in these two, a symbol of permanence), [121:6](https://ref.ly/Ps121:6), [Is 60:19](https://ref.ly/Isa60:19), Hb [3:11](https://ref.ly/Hab3:11), [Jo. 3:4](https://ref.ly/Joel3:4); obj. of idolatrous worship [Jb 31:26](https://ref.ly/Job31:26) (\+ sun); in same sense also \+ sun and stars [Dt 4:19](https://ref.ly/Deut4:19), [17:3](https://ref.ly/Deut17:3), [2 K 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:5), [Je 8:2](https://ref.ly/Jer8:2); as determiner of feast\-times [Psalm 104:19](https://ref.ly/Ps104:19) (‖ sun); \+ stars, as shining , by night [Psalm 136:9](https://ref.ly/Ps136:9) (‖ sun, by day), so חֻקֹּת יָרֵחַ [Je 31:35](https://ref.ly/Jer31:35); (‖ *id.*); elsewh. \+ sun and stars [Gn 37:9](https://ref.ly/Gen37:9) (E), [Is 13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10), Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7), [Jo 2:10](https://ref.ly/Joel2:10), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Joel4:15), [Psalm 148:3](https://ref.ly/Ps148:3), [Ec 12:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:2); \+ stars [Psalm 8:4](https://ref.ly/Ps8:4), [Jb 25:5](https://ref.ly/Job25:5); with neither sun nor stars only עַד־בְּלִי יָרֵחַ [Psalm 72:7](https://ref.ly/Ps72:7).

> \[Str3391] † I. יֶ֫רַח **n.m.**:[Zc 11:8](https://ref.ly/Zech11:8) month (Aram. יַרְחָא, ܝܰܪܚܳܐ *new moon, month;* Palm. ירח VogNo. 1; As. *arḫ u* COTGloss. Muss\-ArnoltJBL xi. 1892, 73, 163; cf. Ph. Eth. sub יָרֵחַ; Sab. ורחַ *month,* DHMZMG 1876, 603; 1883, 369 HalJAS vii, 1, 516\)—abs. יֶ֫רַח [1 K 6:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:37) \+ 3 t.; cstr. *id.* [Dt 21:13](https://ref.ly/Deut21:13), [2 K 15:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:13); pl. יְרָחִים [Ex 2:2](https://ref.ly/Exod2:2) \+ 3 t.; cstr. יַרְחֵי [Jb 7:3](https://ref.ly/Job7:3), [29:2](https://ref.ly/Job29:2); —**1\.** *month,* as measure of time, during, or in which [Ex 2:2](https://ref.ly/Exod2:2) (E), [Zc 11:8](https://ref.ly/Zech11:8), [Jb 39:2](https://ref.ly/Job39:2); יַרְחֵי שָׁוְא [Jb 7:3](https://ref.ly/Job7:3); יַרְחֵי קֶדֶם [29:2](https://ref.ly/Job29:2); pleon. יֶרַת יָמִים *a month* (*of*) *days* \= *a month of time* [Dt 21:13](https://ref.ly/Deut21:13), [2 K 15:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:13). **2\.** *calendar month,* with name יֶרַח זִו [1 K 6:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:37) (2nd mo., cf. v:[1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:1)); יֶרַח בּוּל v:[38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:38) (8th mo.); יֶרַךְ הָאֵתָנִים [1 K 8:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:2) (7th mo.); cf. בְּמִסְפַּר יְרָחִים אַל־יָבאֹ [Jb 3:6](https://ref.ly/Job3:6); גֶּרֶשׁ יְרָתִים [Dt 33:4](https://ref.ly/Deut33:4) *produce of months,* i.e. of various seasons of year.—Cf. synon. חֹדֶשׁ.

> \[Str3392] † II. \[יֶ֫רַח] **n.pr.m.** ‘son’ of Joktan, only ָ\+ירַךְ [Gn 10:26](https://ref.ly/Gen10:26) (LXX Ιαραδ, LXXL Ιεραχ) \= [1 Ch 1:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:20) (LXXL Ιαρε).

> \[Str3386] † יָרוֺחַ **n.pr.m.** a Gadite, [1 Ch 5:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:14); LXX Ιδαι, LXXL Αρουε.

> \[Str3405] יְרֵחוֺ v. יְרִיחוֺ below

> \[Str3395–96] יְרֹחָם, יְרַחְמְאֵל v. sub רחם. p. 934

> \[Str3398] † יַרְחָֹע **n.pr.m.** an Egyptian slave [1 Ch 2:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:34), [2:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:35); LXX Ιωχηλ, LXXL Ιερεε.

## יָרַט (id: BDB04164)

\[Str3399] † יָרַט **vb.** be precipitate, precipitate (trans.) (cf. Ar. وَرَّطَ *conjecit, praecipitem dedit* in puteum, exitium, Frey.)—**Qal** *Pf.* יָרַט [Nu 22:32](https://ref.ly/Num22:32), app. c. subj. הַדֶּרֶח; the way *is precipitate,* (RVm *headlong*) *before me,* but transit. על־ידי רשׁעים ירטני [Jb 16:11](https://ref.ly/Job16:11) *into the hands of wicked men he precipitates me* (v. Di; יִרְטֵנִי \= יִֽרְטֵנִי; Baer's text יִרְטֵנִי points to √ רטה q. v.); hence perh. rd. יָרַטְתָּ [Nu 22:32](https://ref.ly/Num22:32); *thou hast precipitated the journey* in front of me, i.e. rushed recklessly in front of me; v. Di; KauAT leaves untransl.

> \[Str3405] יְרִיחוֺ, יְרִיחֹה, יְרֵחוֺ:57 **n.pr.loc.** Jericho (on form see Baer on [Jos 2:1](https://ref.ly/Josh2:1), [Je 39:5](https://ref.ly/Jer39:5) KöEinl. 49; √ and mng. dub.; acc. to Thes al. from רָוַח, \= *regio fragrans,* the district abounding in palms, rose\-gardens, balsam, etc.);—יְרִיחוֺ [Jos 2:1](https://ref.ly/Josh2:1) \+ 27 t. Jos. (19 t. JE, 3 t. D, 6 t. P), \+ 5 t. 2 K 2; יְרִחוֺ [Jos 18:21](https://ref.ly/Josh18:21), [2 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:5), [Je 39:5](https://ref.ly/Jer39:5), [52:8](https://ref.ly/Jer52:8); יְרִחֹה [1 K 16:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:34); יְרֵחוֺ [Dt 34:1](https://ref.ly/Deut34:1) (P), v:[3](https://ref.ly/Deut34:3) (D), [Nu 22:1](https://ref.ly/Num22:1) (P) \+ 9 t. P, [2 K 25:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:5), [Ezr 2:34](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:34), [Ne 3:2](https://ref.ly/Neh3:2), [7:36](https://ref.ly/Neh7:36) \+ 3 t. Ch;—Canaanit. city taken by Josh. [Jos 6:1](https://ref.ly/Josh6:1) (JE) \+; having a king [2:2](https://ref.ly/Josh2:2), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Josh2:3), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Josh8:2), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Josh10:1) (all JE), [10:28](https://ref.ly/Josh10:28), [10:30](https://ref.ly/Josh10:30), [12:9](https://ref.ly/Josh12:9) (all D); near Jordan, whence (יְרִיחוֺ) יַרְדֵּן יְרֵחוֺ, v. יַרְדֵּן supr.; Mt. Nebo is described as עַל־פְּנֵי יְרֵחוֺ [Dt 32:49](https://ref.ly/Deut32:49), [34:1](https://ref.ly/Deut34:1) (P); (called עִיר הַתְּמָרִים [Jud 1:16](https://ref.ly/Deut1:16), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Deut3:13); so in appos. [Dt 34:3](https://ref.ly/Deut34:3), [2 Ch 28:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:15)); the adjacent plain is called בִּקְעַת יְרֵחוֺ [Dt 34:3](https://ref.ly/Deut34:3) (JE); עַרְבוֺת יְרֵחוֺ [2 K 25:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:5) \= [Je 39:5](https://ref.ly/Jer39:5) \= [52:8](https://ref.ly/Jer52:8); יְרִיחוֺ ´ע [Jos 4:13](https://ref.ly/Josh4:13), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Josh5:10) (both P); among returning exiles appear בְּנֵי יְרֵחוֺ [Ezr 2:34](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:34) \= [Ne 7:36](https://ref.ly/Neh7:36), and among builders of wall אַנְשֵׁי יְרֵחוֺ [Ne 3:2](https://ref.ly/Neh3:2) (Be Ry Ryle *ad loc.* and [Ezr 2:34](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:34));—mod. (*E*)*rî ḥâ;* see also GASmGeogr. 266 ff.

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Samuel 10:5; 2 Kings 25:5; Joshua 4:13; Joshua 8:2; 2 Chronicles 28:15; Job 16:11; Nehemiah 7:36; Joshua 12:9; Joshua 6:1; Deuteronomy 3:13; Joshua 10:30; Joshua 2:3; Jeremiah 39:5; Deuteronomy 34:1; Deuteronomy 1:16; Joshua 18:21; Numbers 22:1; Nehemiah 3:2; 1 Kings 16:34; Joshua 2:1; Deuteronomy 32:49; Joshua 5:10; Joshua 10:28; Joshua 2:2; Numbers 22:32; Jeremiah 52:8; Deuteronomy 34:3; Ezra 2:34; Joshua 10:1


## ירך (id: BDB04166)

ירך (√ of foll.; meaning dubious).

> \[Str3409] † יָרֵךְ **n.f.**:[Nu 5:27](https://ref.ly/Num5:27) thigh, loin, side, base (NH *id.;* Aram. יַרְכָּא; Zinj. ירך *loin*; As. *arku, arkâtu, back, rear, hereafter* DlHWB 242; Ar. وَرِكٌ *hip*)—abs. יָרֵךְ Ju [15:8](https://ref.ly/Judg15:8) \+ 6 t.; cstr. יֶ֫רֶךְ [Gn 24:9](https://ref.ly/Gen24:9) \+ 11 t. \+ [2 S 3:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:27) (v. infr.); sf. יְרֵכִי [Gn 24:2](https://ref.ly/Gen24:2), [47:29](https://ref.ly/Gen47:29); יְרֵכֵח [Nu 5:21](https://ref.ly/Num5:21); יְרֵכוֺ [Gn 32:26](https://ref.ly/Gen32:26) \+ 6 t.; יְרֵכָהּ [Ex 25:31](https://ref.ly/Exod25:31)\+ 3 t.; du. יְרֵכַיִם [Ex 28:42](https://ref.ly/Exod28:42); sf. יְרֵכַיִח Ct [7:2](https://ref.ly/Song7:2); —**1\.** *thigh,* **a.** outside of thigh, where sword was worn, שִים חֶרֶב עַל־יְרֵכוֺ [Ex 32:27](https://ref.ly/Exod32:27) (E); וַיַחְגֹּר אֹתָהּ עַל־יֶרֶח יְמִינוֺ Ju [3:16](https://ref.ly/Judg3:16) *and he girded it upon his right thigh,* v:[21](https://ref.ly/Judg3:21), [Psalm 45:4](https://ref.ly/Ps45:4), Ct [3:8](https://ref.ly/Song3:8); כַּף הַיָּרֵח [Gn 32:33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:33) *hollow of the thigh,* v:[26](https://ref.ly/Gen32:26), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Gen32:26), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32) (all J); חַמּוּקֵי יְרֵכַיִח Ct [7:2](https://ref.ly/Song7:2) *the roundings of thy thighs;* מִמָּתְנַיִם וְעַד־יְרֵכַיִם [Ex 28:42](https://ref.ly/Exod28:42) (P); ‖בֶּטֶן [Nu 5:21](https://ref.ly/Num5:21), [5:22](https://ref.ly/Num5:22), [5:27](https://ref.ly/Num5:27) (P); צֹלֵעַ עַל־יְרֵכוֺ [Gn 32:32](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32) (J), *limping upon his thigh;* סָפַק עַל־יָרֵח [Je 31:19](https://ref.ly/Jer31:19) and (c. אֶ־ל) Ez [21:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:17), *smite upon, thigh*, in token of consternation; וַיַּח אוֺחָם שׁוֺק עַ־ל יָרֵךְ Ju [15:8](https://ref.ly/Judg15:8) *and he smote them, hip upon thigh,* a great slaughter. **b.** *thigh* \= *loins,* as seat of procreative power (RSK. 34; Sem. i, 360 (380\)) יֹצְאֵי ירך *those proceeding from the loins of* any one [Gn 46:26](https://ref.ly/Gen46:26), [Ex 1:5](https://ref.ly/Exod1:5) (both P), Ju [8:30](https://ref.ly/Judg8:30); hence שִים יַד תַּחַת ירך *place the hand under thigh*, in taking oath [Gn 24:2](https://ref.ly/Gen24:2), [24:9](https://ref.ly/Gen24:9) (J), [47:29](https://ref.ly/Gen47:29). **2\.** *side* (flank) of altar [2 K 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:14), [Lv 1:11](https://ref.ly/Lev1:11) (P); of tabernacle [Ex 40:22](https://ref.ly/Exod40:22), [40:24](https://ref.ly/Exod40:24), [Nu 3:29](https://ref.ly/Num3:29), [3:35](https://ref.ly/Num3:35) (all P); also יֶרֶךְ הַשַּׁעַר (for MT ´תּוֺח הַשּׁ) LXX Th We Dr Bu Kit. **3\.** *base* (loins) of candlestick [Ex 25:31](https://ref.ly/Exod25:31), [37:17](https://ref.ly/Exod37:17), [Nu 8:4](https://ref.ly/Num8:4) (all P).

> \[Str3411] † \[יַרְכָה, or יְרֵכָה Ol:§ 167\. g] flank, side, du. extreme parts, recesses;—sf. יַרְכַָתוֺ [Gn 49:13](https://ref.ly/Gen49:13); du. יַרְכָתַיִם [Ex 26:27](https://ref.ly/Exod26:27), [36:32](https://ref.ly/Exod36:32) \+ Ez [46:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:19) Qr (Kt ירכתם); יַרְכָתָיִם [Ex 26:23](https://ref.ly/Exod26:23), [36:28](https://ref.ly/Exod36:28); cstr. יַרְכְּתֵי Ju [19:1](https://ref.ly/Judg19:1) \+ 20 t. \+ [1 K 6:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:16) Qr (Kt ירכותי);—**1\.** *side,* i.e. *further side* of Zebulun, poet. for more distant border of his territory [Gn 49:13](https://ref.ly/Gen49:13). **2\.** elsewhere always du. *the two things,* i.e. fig. *angle, recess, extreme parts: as recesses* of Mt. Ephr. Ju [19:1](https://ref.ly/Judg19:1), [19:18](https://ref.ly/Judg19:18); of Lebanon [2 K 19:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:23) \= [Is 37:24](https://ref.ly/Isa37:24); of *recesses* or *innermost part* of a cave [1 S 24:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:4); a house [Am 6:10](https://ref.ly/Amos6:10), [Psalm 128:3](https://ref.ly/Ps128:3); the pit (בּוֺר) [Is 14:15](https://ref.ly/Isa14:15) (‖שְׁאוֺל ָ), Ez [32:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:23); a ship [Jon 1:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:5); יַרְכְּתֵי צָפוֺן *remote parts of the north* [Is 14:13](https://ref.ly/Isa14:13), Ez [38:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:6), [38:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:15), [39:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:2), [Psalm 48:3](https://ref.ly/Ps48:3) (but here Lag. prop. רצון ´יר); יַרְכְּתֵי אָרֶץ *remote parts of earth* [Je 6:22](https://ref.ly/Jer6:22), [25:32](https://ref.ly/Jer25:32), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Jer31:8), [50:41](https://ref.ly/Jer50:41); of a long building, *extreme* or *hinder part,* so of tabernacle [Ex 26:22](https://ref.ly/Exod26:22), [26:23](https://ref.ly/Exod26:23), [26:27](https://ref.ly/Exod26:27), [36:27](https://ref.ly/Exod36:27), [36:28](https://ref.ly/Exod36:28), [36:32](https://ref.ly/Exod36:32) (all P); of temple [1 K 6:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:16); of Ezek.'s temple Ez [46:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:19).

> \[Str3412] † יַרְמוּת **n.pr.loc. 1\.** LXX Ιερ(ε)ιμουθ, Canaanitish city, with a king, named between Hebron and Lachish [Jos 10:3](https://ref.ly/Josh10:3), [10:5](https://ref.ly/Josh10:5), [10:23](https://ref.ly/Josh10:23) (JE), [12:11](https://ref.ly/Josh12:11) (D); named with Adullam [15:35](https://ref.ly/Josh15:35) (P), cf. [Ne 11:29](https://ref.ly/Neh11:29). **2\.** LXX Ρεμμαθ, A LXXL Ιερμωθ, Levitical city in Issachar [Jos 21:29](https://ref.ly/Josh21:29) (P).
> 
> 438:a. יַרְמוּת ad fin.: add ‘see מוֺת(א)רָ **1 c,** p. 928:b.’

> \[Str3413] † יְרֵמַי **n.pr.m.** one of those who had strange wives [Ezr 10:33](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:33); LXX Ιεσαμει(μ).

> \[Str3406] † יְרֵימוֺת, יְרֵמוֺת, יְרִימוֺת **n.pr.m.** LXX Αρειμωθ, Ιεριμουθ, etc.;—**1\.** Benjamites: **a.** יְרִימוֺת [1 Ch 7:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:7). **b.** יְרֵימוֺת [1 Ch 7:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:8). **c.** יְרֵמוֺת [1 Ch 8:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:14). **2\.** Levites: **a.** יְרֵמוֺת [1 Ch 23:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:23). **b.** *id.* [1 Ch 25:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:22) \= **c.** יְרִימוֺת [24:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:30). **d.** יְרִימוֺת [1 Ch 25:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:4). **e.** *id.* [2 Ch 31:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:13).
> 
> **3\.** *id.* Naphtalite [1 Ch 27:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:19). **4\.** *id.* son of David and father of Rehoboam's wife [2 Ch 11:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:18). **5\.** יְרֵמוֺת, men who had strange wives: **a.** [Ezr 10:26](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:26), LXX Ιερειμοιθ(\-μωθ). **b.** [Ezr 10:27](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:27), LXX Αμων, א Αρμων, A Ιαρμωθ, LXXL Ιεριμωθ. **c.** [Ezr 10:29](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:29) Kt (Qr וְרָמוֺת), LXX Μημων, A Ρημωθ, LXXL Αριμωθ.

> \[Str3414] יִרְמְיָהוּ, יִרְמְיָה v. sub רמה. p. 941

(1\) of judgment of ´י, all c. פרשׁ: [Ho 7:12](https://ref.ly/Hos7:12) (‖כְּעוֺף הַשָּׁמַיִם אוֺרִידֵם), Ez [12:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:13), [17:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:20) (both ‖מְצוּדָתִי), [32:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:3) (of ´י catching Pharaoh, under fig. of sea\-monster; ‖ II. חֵרֶם); (2\) of nations capturing king of Israel under fig. of lion [19:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:8) (c. פרשׁ; ‖שַׁחְתָּם); (3\) of priests and rulers (as entangling people in sin) [Ho 5:1](https://ref.ly/Hos5:1) (‖פַּח). **b.** spread for feet of man (fig.): (1\) by ´י [La 1:13](https://ref.ly/Lam1:13) (c. פָּרַשׁ); (2\) by wicked (\= *plot*) [Pr 29:5](https://ref.ly/Prov29:5) (c. פרשׁ), [Psalm 140:6](https://ref.ly/Ps140:6) (c. *id.;* ‖טָמַן פַּךְ), [10:9](https://ref.ly/Ps10:9), [25:15](https://ref.ly/Ps25:15), שֻׁלַּח בְּדֶשֶׁת בְּרַגְלָיו [Jb 18:8](https://ref.ly/Job18:8) (‖שְבָכָה); elsewh. c. טמן [Psalm 9:16](https://ref.ly/Ps9:16) (‖שַׁחַת), [31:5](https://ref.ly/Ps31:5), [35:7](https://ref.ly/Ps35:7) (שַׁחַת רִשְׁתָּם), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Ps35:8); c. הֵכִין [57:7](https://ref.ly/Ps57:7) (‖שִׁיחָה). **2\.** brazen *network* for altar of tabern. הָרֶשֶׁת [Ex 27:4](https://ref.ly/Exod27:4), [27:5](https://ref.ly/Exod27:5); נְחשֶׁת ´מִכְבָּר מַעֲשֵה ר [27:4](https://ref.ly/Exod27:4) \= [38:4](https://ref.ly/Exod38:4) (all P).—On Inf. רֶשֶׁת, v. ירשׁ supr.

> \[Str4180] † \[מוֺרָשׁ] **n.\[m]** a possession;—cstr. מוֺרַשׁ קִפֹּד [Is 14:23](https://ref.ly/Isa14:23) *a possession for bitterns;* וְיָרְֽשׁוּ בֵּית יַעֲקֹב אֵת מוֺרָשֵׁיהֶם Ob:[17](https://ref.ly/Obad1:17) *and the house of Jacob shall possess their possessions* (but rd. perhaps מורישׁיהם *their dispossessors,* LXX Vulgate Targum al.); fig. מוֺרָשֵׁי לְבָבִי [Jb 17:11](https://ref.ly/Job17:11), i.e. *my cherished thoughts* (‖זִמּוֺתַי), but fig. is questionable, v. Di, who derives from ארשׁ desire, so Buhl.

> \[Str4182,1661] † מוֺרֶ֫שֶׁת **n.pr.loc.** apparently in neighbourhood of Gath [Mi 1:14](https://ref.ly/Mic1:14) (גַּת vocative, acc. to We; \> most, who render *M. of Gath*); prob. home of prophet Micah, v. foll.

## יָשַם (id: BDB04196)

\[Str3455] \[יָשַם] **vb.** *Impf.* וַיִּישֶם [Gn 50:26](https://ref.ly/Gen50:26), and so [24:33](https://ref.ly/Gen24:33) Kt, v. שום, שים.

> \[Str3478] יִשְרָאֵל **n.pr.** and deriv., v. sub I שרה. p. 975

> \[Str3480] יְשַרְאֵ֫לָה **n.pr.m.** a son of Asaph [1 Ch. 25:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:14) \= אֲשַראֵ֫לָה (q.v.) v:[2](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:2) (etym. dubious).

> \[Str3485] † יִשָּשכָר:48 (so always MT, Qr perpet.; Ben Napht. יִשְשָכָר BaerGn p. 84 f.) **n.pr.m.** Issachar (etym. and mng. dub.; MT as if Niph. Impf. √ שכר; Kt \= יֵשׁ שָכָר *there is recompense* (cf. [Gn 30:18](https://ref.ly/Gen30:18)), so Thes al.; this the true etym. acc. to Ol:§§ 69 c; 277 f. Köi. 120 v. יֵשׁ שָכָר [Je 31:16](https://ref.ly/Jer31:16), [2 Ch 15:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:7); \= אִישׁ שָכָר (a more prob. combination in n.pr.) WeSam 95; \> יִשָּכָר (MT), or יִשָּא שכר; LXX Ισσαχαρ, see esp. [Gn 30:18](https://ref.ly/Gen30:18) Ισσχαρ, ὄ ἐστιν μισθός)—**1\.** fifth son of Jacob and Leah (appar. ninth son of Jacob) [Gn 30:18](https://ref.ly/Gen30:18), [35:23](https://ref.ly/Gen35:23), [46:13](https://ref.ly/Gen46:13), [49:14](https://ref.ly/Gen49:14), [Ex 1:3](https://ref.ly/Exod1:3), [Nu 1:8](https://ref.ly/Num1:8); ´בְּנֵי יש [Nu 1:28](https://ref.ly/Num1:28), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Num2:5), [26:23](https://ref.ly/Num26:23), [Jos 19:17](https://ref.ly/Josh19:17), [1 Ch 7:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:1), [12:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:33) (v:[32](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:32) van d. H.); ´מִשְׁפְּחוֺת יש [Nu 26:25](https://ref.ly/Num26:25), [1 Ch 7:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:5); ´מַטֵּה יש [Nu 1:29](https://ref.ly/Num1:29), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Num2:5), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Num13:7), [Jos 21:6](https://ref.ly/Josh21:6), [21:28](https://ref.ly/Josh21:28), [1 Ch 6:47](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:47), [6:57](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:57); ´מטה בני יש [Nu 10:15](https://ref.ly/Num10:15), [34:26](https://ref.ly/Num34:26), [Jos 19:23](https://ref.ly/Josh19:23); ´בֵּית יש [1 K 15:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:27); hence ´יש alone \= *tribe* of Issachar, [Nu 7:18](https://ref.ly/Num7:18), [Dt 27:12](https://ref.ly/Deut27:12), [33:18](https://ref.ly/Deut33:18), [Jos 19:17](https://ref.ly/Josh19:17), Ju [5:15](https://ref.ly/Judg5:15), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Judg5:15), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Judg10:1), Ez [48:33](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:33) (´שׁער יש), [1 Ch 27:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:18), [2 Ch 30:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:18); \= *territory* of the tribe [Jos 17:10](https://ref.ly/Josh17:10), [17:11](https://ref.ly/Josh17:11), [1 K 4:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:17), [1 Ch 12:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:41) (v:[40](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:40) van d. H.); transit. fr. *tribe* to *territory* Ez [48:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:25), [48:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:26). **2\.** 7th son of Obed\-Edom [1 Ch 26:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:5).

> \[Str3426] יֵשׁ:187 subst. being, substance, existence (on etym. v. **2**)—יֵשׁ [Gn 18:24](https://ref.ly/Gen18:24) etc.; יֶ־שׁ [31:29](https://ref.ly/Gen31:29) \+ oft.; הֲיֵשׁ [24:23](https://ref.ly/Gen24:23) \+ 18 t. (so אִשׁ [2 S 14:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:19) †, הַאִשׁ [Mic 6:10](https://ref.ly/Mic6:10) †, v. p. 78\), with sf. יֶשְׁ v:[42](https://ref.ly/Gen24:42) \+ 2 t.; יֶשְׁכֶם v:[49](https://ref.ly/Gen24:49) †; הֲיִשְׁכֶם [Dt 13:4](https://ref.ly/Deut13:4) †; יֶשְׁנוֺ (Ol:§ 97 b Sta§ 370 b) [29:14](https://ref.ly/Deut29:14), [1 S 14:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:39) (\+ v:[41](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:41) LXX We Dr), [23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23), [Est 3:8](https://ref.ly/Esth3:8) †;—**1\.** *substance*, only [Pr 8:21](https://ref.ly/Prov8:21) לְהַנְחִיל אֹהֲבַי יֵשׁ to cause them that love me to inherit  *substance* (so [Sir 42:3](https://ref.ly/Sir42:3)). **2\.** elsewhere (prop. as a subst. in the *st. c.*), it asserts *existence*, and so corresponds to the *verb substantive*, is (are, was, were, will be), lit. *the being, presence of...* (so BA אִתַי; Aram. איתא, אית, ܐܝܺܬ \[whence ܐܻܝܬܝܳܐ (self\-) *existent*, ܐܝܺܬܘܽܬܳܐ *essence, substance*], with לָא contr. לַיִת, לֵית, ܠܰܝܺܬ; Mand. עת, עית, לאיית; Ar. \[with irreg. س] لَيْسَ (inflected as a verb, لَيْسَتْ, لَسْتَ, etc.; WAG i. § 182, ii. § 42\) *is not* (أَيْسَ is known only in two proverbial sayings, as a secondary form FlKl. Schr. i. 146 f.); As. *išû, be, have* DlHWB 310; with affix 1 s. *la\-a i\-ša\-a\-ku, I have not*, TPi. 57 f.. On this word, see esp. NöM. § 213, who exemplifies its different constructions in Semitic, and shews how it tends to pass into a verb;—(1\) in BAram. Syr. Bab. Targum, with possessive sf.; (2\) in later Pal. diall., Jer. Targum, Jer. Talm., sts. also in Bab. Targum and Syr., with independent Pers. pron. (as לית אנא, לית הוא); (3\) in Mand. Bab. Talm., sometimes in Syr. and Ar., and in Heb. יֶשְׁנוֺ, with object. (verbal) sf.; (4\) in Ar., as a true verb. In Eth. ቦ፡ lit. *therein*, Germ. *es gibt*, is similar in use (DIGr. § 167, l), though not of course in origin. In Heb. the corresponding neg. is אַיִן, q.v., the construction of which is quite similar)—is, are, was, were, etc., not, however, as a mere copula, but implying existence with emph. (hence in Engl. to be often represented by the subst. verb in italics): **a.** with a pred. following, [Gn 28:16](https://ref.ly/Gen28:16) במקום הזה ´אכן ישׁ י surely Y. *is* (emph.) in this place! [44:26](https://ref.ly/Gen44:26) אִם יֵשׁ אָחִינוּ אִתְּכֶם; [Ex 17:7](https://ref.ly/Exod17:7) הֲיֵשׁ יהוה בְּקִרְבֵּנוּ אִם אַיִן, [Nu 22:29](https://ref.ly/Num22:29) לו ישׁ חרב בידי Oh, that there *were* a sword in my hand! [Dt 13:4](https://ref.ly/Deut13:4) הֲיִשְׁכֶם אֹהֲבִים whether you *do* love, [29:17](https://ref.ly/Deut29:17) פן ישׁ בכם אישׁ, v:[18](https://ref.ly/Deut29:18), [1 S 9:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:11) f. and they said, *Is* the seer here? and they said יֵשׁ  *He is*, [20:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:8), [23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23), [Je 27:18](https://ref.ly/Jer27:18). Alone, in answer to a question (asked with יֵשׁ), *He* (*it*) *is:* † [1 S 9:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:12), [2 K 10:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:15), [Je 37:17](https://ref.ly/Jer37:17). But [Je 23:26](https://ref.ly/Jer23:26) (where ישׁ has no subst. or sf.) text must be corrupt: cf. Gie. **b.** absolutely, *there is* (*es. gibt, il y a*), [Gn 18:24](https://ref.ly/Gen18:24) אולי ישׁ חמשׁים צדיקים בעיר perhaps there *are* fifty righteous in the city, [24:23](https://ref.ly/Gen24:23) הישׁ בית אביך מקום לנו ללון, [42:1](https://ref.ly/Gen42:1) כי ישׁ שׁבר ´במצ that there *was* corn in Egypt, Ju [4:20](https://ref.ly/Judg4:20) הישׁ פה אישׁ, [2 S 9:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:1), [2 K 5:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:8) he shall know that there *is* (emph.) a prophet in Israel, [Ru 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:12) there *is* a kinsman nearer than I, [Psalm 58:12](https://ref.ly/Ps58:12) surely there *are* gods judging on the earth: so in aphorisms, asserting the existence of a partic. character, quality, etc., [Pr 11:24](https://ref.ly/Prov11:24) ישׁ מפזר ונוסף עוד, [12:18](https://ref.ly/Prov12:18), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Prov13:7), [13:23](https://ref.ly/Prov13:23), [14:12](https://ref.ly/Prov14:12), [16:25](https://ref.ly/Prov16:25), [18:24](https://ref.ly/Prov18:24), [20:15](https://ref.ly/Prov20:15), [Ec 2:21](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:21), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:8), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:12), [6:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:1), [6:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:11), [7:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:15), [7:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:15), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:14), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:14), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:14), [10:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:5). In questions, or protestations, יֵשׁ often implies a doubt whether what is asked about is to be found or exists: [1 K 18:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:10), [Je 5:1](https://ref.ly/Jer5:1) and see אם ישׁ אישׁ if there *is*(emph.) a man doing justice, etc. (cf. [Psalm 14:2](https://ref.ly/Ps14:2)), [14:22](https://ref.ly/Jer14:22), [Is 44:8](https://ref.ly/Isa44:8) הישׁ אלהים *is* there a god beside me? [Psalm 7:4](https://ref.ly/Ps7:4) אם ישׁ עול בכפי if there *is* iniquity in my hands! [73:11](https://ref.ly/Ps73:11) *is* there knowledge in the Most High? [Jb 5:1](https://ref.ly/Job5:1), [6:30](https://ref.ly/Job6:30), [La 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lam1:2). **c.** special phrases:—(*a*) after אִם and a ptcp., where an abiding *intention* is to be emphasized, † [Gn 24:42](https://ref.ly/Gen24:42) אם־ישׁך־נא מצליח דרכי if thou *art* (really), prospering my way, v:[49](https://ref.ly/Gen24:49), [43:4](https://ref.ly/Gen43:4), Ju [6:36](https://ref.ly/Judg6:36) (cf. II. אין **2 b**). (*b*) יֵשׁ לְ \= *has* (*had*), esp. with prons. יֵשׁ לִי, יֵשׁ לְ; etc., [Gn 33:9](https://ref.ly/Gen33:9) יֶשׁ־לִי רָב *I have* plenty, v:[11](https://ref.ly/Gen33:11), [43:7](https://ref.ly/Gen43:7) הישׁ לכם אח, [44:20](https://ref.ly/Gen44:20) ישׁ לנו אב זקן, [1 S 17:40](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:40) that they may know כי ישׁ אלהים לישראל that Israel *has* (emph.) a god, [2 K 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:2) מַה־יֶּשׁ־לָכְי what *hast* thou ? [Jb 14:7](https://ref.ly/Job14:7) for a tree *has* (emph.) hope (cf. [Ru 1:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:12)), [25:3](https://ref.ly/Job25:3), [28:1](https://ref.ly/Job28:1), [38:28](https://ref.ly/Job38:28) הישׁ למטר אב: [Gn 39:5](https://ref.ly/Gen39:5), [39:5](https://ref.ly/Gen39:5), [39:8](https://ref.ly/Gen39:8), \* F f כל אשׁר יֶשׁ־לו all that had (v:[4](https://ref.ly/Gen39:4) without אשׁר, prob. error). (*c*) with inf. and לְ, *is it possible to...* ? [2 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:13); הישׁ לדבר־לך *can* (1\) *speak* for thee to the king? [2 Ch 25:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:9); so אִשׁ לְ [2 S 14:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:19) (cf. אֵין לְ, sub II. אַיִן **5**). (*d*)... יֵשׁ אֲשֶׁר (if) *it was* that... † [Nu 9:20](https://ref.ly/Num9:20), [9:21](https://ref.ly/Num9:21); there *were* some who... (with ptcp.) † [Ne 5:2](https://ref.ly/Neh5:2), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Neh5:3), [5:4](https://ref.ly/Neh5:4) (cf. Syr. ܐܝܺܬ ܕ for ὅ μὲν... ὅ δὲ … [Mt 13:8](https://ref.ly/Matt13:8); ܐܝܺܬ ܕܐܴܡܪܺܝܢ PS172). (*e*) יֶשׁ־לְאֵל יָדִי [Gn 31:29](https://ref.ly/Gen31:29) al.; v. II. אֵל **7,** p. 43\. (*f*) [2 K 10:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:15) וָיֵשׁ *and* (if) *it be*... (cf. וָלאֹ [5:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:17), [2 S 13:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:26)); so Ju [6:13](https://ref.ly/Judg6:13) אִתָּנוּ ´וְיֵשׁ י. (*g*) pleon. אֵין יֵשׁ † [Psalm 135:17](https://ref.ly/Ps135:17). (*h*) לאֹ יֵשׁ † [Jb 9:33](https://ref.ly/Job9:33) (cf. لَيْسَ, ܠܰܝܺܬ: but LXX SyrVer Me al. לֻא).—As a rule, יֵשׁ precedes its subst. (from which, however, like אין, it may be separated: [Gn 24:23](https://ref.ly/Gen24:23), [43:7](https://ref.ly/Gen43:7) הֲיֵשׁ לָכֶם אָח, [44:19](https://ref.ly/Gen44:19), [44:20](https://ref.ly/Gen44:20), [1 S 20:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:8) etc.); but occasionally, for greater emphasis, this is prefixed: [1 S 21:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:5) כי אם לחם קדשׁ ישׁ but holy bread *there is!* [Is 43:8](https://ref.ly/Isa43:8) the blind people, ועינים ישׁ though it *has* eyes, Ju [19:19](https://ref.ly/Judg19:19) לחם ויין ישׁ לי (cf. אַיִן **2 c**).

## יָשַׁב (id: BDB04201)

Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6), but also עִיר מוֺשָׁב [Psalm 107:4](https://ref.ly/Ps107:4), [107:7](https://ref.ly/Ps107:7), [107:36](https://ref.ly/Ps107:36); of Zion as ´י ´מ [132:13](https://ref.ly/Ps132:13). **b.** alm. \= abstr. *dwelling* עַיר בֵּית מוֺשַׁב [Lv 25:29](https://ref.ly/Lev25:29) (P); ´אֶרֶץ מ [Nu 15:2](https://ref.ly/Num15:2) *land of your dwelling\-places* or *dwelling* (P). **c.** \= house [Ex 12:20](https://ref.ly/Exod12:20) (‖ בית v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod12:19)),, [35:3](https://ref.ly/Exod35:3), [Lv 3:17](https://ref.ly/Lev3:17), [7:26](https://ref.ly/Lev7:26), [13:46](https://ref.ly/Lev13:46), [23:3](https://ref.ly/Lev23:3), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Lev23:14), [23:21](https://ref.ly/Lev23:21), [23:31](https://ref.ly/Lev23:31), [Nu 35:29](https://ref.ly/Num35:29) (all P), [Lv 23:17](https://ref.ly/Lev23:17) (H). **3\.** *situation* of city [2 K 2:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:19); *location* of image Ez [8:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:3). **4\.** *time f dwelling* [Ex 12:40](https://ref.ly/Exod12:40) (P). **5\.** coll. \= *those dwelling*, ´כֹּל מוֺשַׁב וני [2 S 9:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:12) *all those dwelling in* the house of Ṣiba.—Ez [37:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:28) v. supr. and מְשׁוּבָה sub שׁוּב.

> \[Str8453] † תּוֺשָׁב **n.m.**:[Lv 25:45](https://ref.ly/Lev25:45) sojourner, only P (H) and late; abs. ´ת [Gn 23:4](https://ref.ly/Gen23:4) \+ 6 t; cstr. תּוֺשַׁב [Lv 22:10](https://ref.ly/Lev22:10); sf. תּוֺשָֽׁבְ [25:6](https://ref.ly/Lev25:6); pl. תּוֺשָׁבִים v:[6](https://ref.ly/Lev25:6) \+ 2 t.; cstr. תּשָׁבֵי [1 K 17:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:1), but read חִשְׁבֶּה (q.v.) LXX Ew Th Hi;—*sojourner*, appar. of a more temporaty and dependent (Lv [22:10](https://ref.ly/Lev22:10), [25:6](https://ref.ly/Lev25:6)) kind than the גֵּר (with which it is often joined): ‖שָכִיר [Ex 12:45](https://ref.ly/Exod12:45) (P), [Lv 22:10](https://ref.ly/Lev22:10) (כֹּהֵן ´ת apriest's *sojourner*), [25:6](https://ref.ly/Lev25:6), [25:40](https://ref.ly/Lev25:40) (all H); הַחשְָׁבִים הַגָּרִים עִמָּכֶם v:[45](https://ref.ly/Lev25:45) (c. pt. גָּד also v:[6](https://ref.ly/Lev25:6)); c. עִם, also [Lv 25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47), [25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47) (H); c. בְּחוֺךְ [Nu 35:15](https://ref.ly/Num35:15) (P). Fig. of one enjoying only a temporary tenure, c. עִם [Gn 23:4](https://ref.ly/Gen23:4), with ´י [Lv 25:23](https://ref.ly/Lev25:23), [Psalm 39:13](https://ref.ly/Ps39:13), c. לִפְנֵי [1 Ch 29:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:15).—[1 K 17:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:1) v. supr.

## ישׁה (id: BDB04214)

ישׁה (√ of following; meaning uncertain; acc. Fl De:[Pr 2:7](https://ref.ly/Prov2:7) \= Ar. أَسَا, III. أَاسَنا, var. وَاسَا, *assist, support:* nut this dubious; v. Lane60 )cf. WetzstZDMG 1868, 119\) acc. to whom this is a second. sense from *to make equal* (viz. by giving to another of one's own property, etc.))

> \[Str8454] † תּוּשִׁיָּה **n.f.** sound, efficient wisdom, abiding success (on der., v. supr.; acc. to Fl De prop. *advancement*, or mental aptitude that *advances:* for the form, cf. חוּגָה and תַּאֲנִיָה; Sta§ 262\), a technical term of theWisdLT;—**a.** *sound, efficient wisdom* [Is 28:29](https://ref.ly/Isa28:29) (of ´י) הפליא עצח חגדיל חושׁיה; [Pr 3:21](https://ref.ly/Prov3:21) וּמְזִמָּה ´נְצֹר תּ; [8:14](https://ref.ly/Prov8:14) (Wisdom speaks) ´לִי עֵצָח וח; [Pr 18:1](https://ref.ly/Prov18:1) יִחֽגַּלָּע ´לכל תּ; [Jb 11:6](https://ref.ly/Job11:6) (of ´י: v.Di), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Job12:6) ´עִמּוֺ עֹז ות; [26:3](https://ref.ly/Job26:3) לָרֹב ´ות הוָֺ\+רעֽתָּ (‖תָעַעְחָ לְלאֹ חָכְמָה **b.** of the *effect* of sound wisdom, *abiding success* (for the combination of meanings, cf. הִשְכִּיל *to shew wisdom.* and also to *achieve success*). [Jb 5:12](https://ref.ly/Job5:12) ולא חעשינה ´ידיהם ח do not achieve *abiding success;* [6:13](https://ref.ly/Job6:13) and *abiding success* (‖עַזְרָחִי) is driven from me; [Pr 2:7](https://ref.ly/Prov2:7) ´יִעְפֹּן לַיִשָׁרִים ת; [Mi 6:9](https://ref.ly/Mic6:9) וחושׁיה יראה שֹׁמך, i.e (si vera l.) he that seeth (heedeth) thy name is *well\-advised* (Ges§ 155, 2 b3; Dav:§ 29 e).—חֻשִׁיָה [Jb 30:22](https://ref.ly/Job30:22), v. sub. שׁוא.

> \[Str3144] † יוֺשָׁה **n.pr.m.** a Simeonite [1 Ch 4:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:34); LXX Ιως(ε)ια, A Ιωσιας, LXXL Ιωας.

## יָשַׁט (id: BDB04222)

As. *asûṭu, extended, mighty* LyonSargontexte 60 DlHWB 144; Ar. وَسَطَ is penetrate into the middle of)—יוֺשִׁיט שַׁרְבִיט הַזָּהָב לְ [Est 4:11](https://ref.ly/Esth4:11)*hold out the golden sceptre to* , so (וֵי֫וֺשֶׁט) [5:2](https://ref.ly/Esth5:2), [8:4](https://ref.ly/Esth8:4).

> \[Str3448] יִשַׁי:41 **n.pr.m.** father of David (etym. dub.; Ol:§ 277 f. conj. יֵשׁ יָהּ; We:[1 S 14:49](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:49) ישׁי \= אישׁי \= יִשָׁי (but see this))—יְשַׁי [1 S 16:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:3) \+ 29 t.; יִשָׁי v:[19](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:19) \+ 10 t.; LXX Ιεσσαι; יִשַׁי(וְ) [1 Ch 2:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:13); —† יִשַׁי בּי־ח הַלַּחְמִי *jesse the Bethlehemite* [1 S 16:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:1), [16:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:18) (´בֶּן־ישׁי וגו, of David), [17:58](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:58) †; ישׁי also [16:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:3) \+ 14 t. [1 S 16:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:17); † son of עוֺבֵד acc. to [Ru 4:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:17), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:22), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:22) \= [1 Ch 2:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:12); ד(י)דָּוִ בֶּן־ישׁי [2 S 23:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:1), [1 Ch 10:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:14), [29:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:26), [Psalm 72:20](https://ref.ly/Ps72:20); אֱלִיאָב בֶּן־ישׁי [2 Ch 11:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:18); elsewhere בֶּן־ישׁי alone \= David, [1 S 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:27), [20:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:30), [20:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:31), [22:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:7), [22:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:8), [22:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:9), [22:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:13), [25:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:10), [2 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1), [1 K 12:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:16) \= [2 Ch 10:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:16), [1 Ch 12:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:18); נֶּוַּע יִשָׁי [Is 11:1](https://ref.ly/Isa11:1); שֹׁרֶשׁ יְשֵׁי v:[10](https://ref.ly/Isa11:10) of Jesse as ancestor of future (Messianic) king†.

## יָשַׁם (id: BDB04226)

\[Str3456] † \[יָשַׁם] **vb.** be desolate (cf. שָׁמַם)—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. תֵּשַׁס Ez [12:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:19); תֵּשָׁס [Gn 47:19](https://ref.ly/Gen47:19); וַתֵּשַׁם Ez [19:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:7); 3 fpl. תִּישָׁמְנָה [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6) (Co תֵּשׁמנה);—*be desolate* , subj. ארץ Ez [12:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:19), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:7); subj. אֲדָמָץ [Gn 47:19](https://ref.ly/Gen47:19) (J); subj. במות Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6).

> \[Str3451] † \[יְשִׁימָה] **n.f.** desolation, only intens. pl. יְשִׁימוֺת [Psalm 55:16](https://ref.ly/Ps55:16) Kt (Qr יַשִּׁיא מָוֶֺת), so Ges Hup al., cf. n.pr.loc. בית הישׁימות supr. p. 111 b; LXX Ew Ol Pe De Bae al. follow Qr, *let death come deceitfully upon them*, v. נשׁא.

> \[Str3452] † יְשִׁימוֺן **n.m.** waste, wilderness;—abs. יְשִׁימוֺן [1 S 23:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:19) \+ 5 t.; יְשִׁימֹן [Nu 21:20](https://ref.ly/Num21:20) \+ 3 t.; יְשִׁמוֺן [Is 43:19](https://ref.ly/Isa43:19); יְשִׁמֹן [Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10); cstr. יְשִׁימוֺן [Psalm 107:4](https://ref.ly/Ps107:4); —*waste, wilderness:* **a.** c. art., alm. n.pr. [Nu 21:20](https://ref.ly/Num21:20) (commanded by Pisgah), [23:28](https://ref.ly/Num23:28) (by Pe‘or; both JE), frequented by David as an outlaw [1 S 23:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:19), [23:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:24), [26:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:1), [26:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:3). Probably some part of the wilderness of Judah, bordering on Dead Sea (ConderTent work, ch. 15; BuhlGeogr. § 61\); acc. to others, at least in Nu. (v. Di), part of the ‘Arábah, NE. of the Dead Sea (in which was בֵּית הַיְשִׁימֹת [Nu 33:49](https://ref.ly/Num33:49) al.) b. without art., a *waste* or *wilderness* (only poet.), of scene of Israel's wanderings (after the Exodus), ´בְּחֹהוּ יְלֵל יְשׁ [Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10) (‖בְּאֶרֶץ מִדְבָּר), [Psalm 68:8](https://ref.ly/Ps68:8), [78:40](https://ref.ly/Ps78:40), [106:14](https://ref.ly/Ps106:14); with ref. to returning exiles [Is 43:19](https://ref.ly/Isa43:19), [43:20](https://ref.ly/Isa43:20); דֶּרֶךְ ´י [Psalm 107:4](https://ref.ly/Ps107:4) *waste of a way* ( \= desert way).

> \[Str3457] † יִשְׁמָא **n.pr.m.** a name in Judah [1 Ch 4:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:3); LXX Ραγμα, A Ιεσμα LXXL Σαμαα.

## יָשֵׁן (id: BDB04232)

\[Str3462,8153] † \[יָשֵׁן] **vb.** *sleep* (NH id.; Aram. n. ܫܶܢܬܳܐ *sleep*; Ar. وَسِنَ *be sleepy*, وَسَنٌ, سِنَةٌ *sleep*; As. *šittiu, sleep,* DlHWB 246, *šunatu, šuttu, dream* , Idib, Floodiv. 22 AsrbAnnals,v R. 2, 97\)—**Qal** *Pf.* יָשַׁנְתִּי [Jb 3:13](https://ref.ly/Job3:13); וְיָֽשְׁנוּ consec. [Je 51:39](https://ref.ly/Jer51:39) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* יִישָׁן [Is 5:27](https://ref.ly/Isa5:27), [Psalm 121:4](https://ref.ly/Ps121:4); וַיִישַׁן [1 K 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:5), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* לִישׁוֺן [Ec 5:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:11); —*sleep, go to sleep,* and *be asleep*, [Gn 2:21](https://ref.ly/Gen2:21) (J), [41:5](https://ref.ly/Gen41:5) (E), Ez [34:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:25), [Jb 3:13](https://ref.ly/Job3:13), [Psalm 3:6](https://ref.ly/Ps3:6), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Ps4:9), [Pr 4:16](https://ref.ly/Prov4:16), [Ec 5:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:11), also [2 S 4:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:6) LXX We Dr Kit Bu, vid. חֹמֶשׁ; c. לאֹ, of Ass. army [Is 5:27](https://ref.ly/Isa5:27) (‖נוּם); of Baal [1 K 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:5); of ´י, עוּרָה לָמָּה חִישַׁן אֲדֹנָי † Psalm [44:24](https://ref.ly/Ps44:24); לאֹ יִישָׁן [121:4](https://ref.ly/Ps121:4) (‖נוּס); c. acc. cogn. שְׁנַת עוֺלָס ´ישׁ [Je 51:39](https://ref.ly/Jer51:39), [51:57](https://ref.ly/Jer51:57) (v. שֵׁנָה), of death, cf. אִישַׁן הַמָּוֶת [Psalm 13:4](https://ref.ly/Ps13:4). *Niph.* only in deriv. sense of *become inactive or stationary; Pf.* וְנוֺשַׁנְחֶס consec. [Dt 4:25](https://ref.ly/Deut4:25) *and ye be grown old* בָּאָדֶץ, i.e. (v. Di Dr) have lost freshness of first impressions; of inanimate things, *be old, stale* , only *Pt.* יָשָׁן נוֺשָׁן [Lv 26:10](https://ref.ly/Lev26:10) (H), f. צרעת נוֺשֶׁנֶת [Lv 13:11](https://ref.ly/Lev13:11) \= *old leprosy* (P); these poss. denom. from יָשָׁן **Pi.** causat. *Impf.* וַתְּיַשְּׁנֵהוּ Ju [16:19](https://ref.ly/Judg16:19)*and she made him sleep*.

> \[Str3463] † I. יָשֵׁן **adj.sleeping** ;—ms. יָשֵׁן [1 S 26:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:7) \+ 3 t., fs. יֲשֵׁנֵי [1 K 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:20), Ct [5:2](https://ref.ly/Song5:2); pl. יְשֵׁנִיס [1 S 26:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:12), Ct [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10); cstr. יֲשֵׁנֵי [Dn 12:2](https://ref.ly/Dan12:2); —*asleep, sleeping* [1 S 26:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:7), [26:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:12), [1 K 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:20), Ct [5:2](https://ref.ly/Song5:2), [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10); so, fig., [Ho 7:6](https://ref.ly/Hos7:6), expl. as \= יֶעְשַׁן *smoketh*, cf. [Dt 29:19](https://ref.ly/Deut29:19), by RSJ Phil. xvi. 1888, 72; Proph. iv. n. 19 Che with אַפְּהֶם (Ges§ 1\. R. 1 a) for נֹפֵהֶם; but \< We, *their anger sleepeth;* of Baal [1 K 18:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:27); in sim. of ´י [Psalm 78:65](https://ref.ly/Ps78:65); of dead, as sleeping in dust, [Dn 12:2](https://ref.ly/Dan12:2).

> \[Str3464] † II. יָשֵׁן **n.pr.m.** one of David's heroes, 2 S \= הָשֵׁס [1 Ch 11:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:34) (in both del. בְּנֵי as dittogr., We Dr Bu); v. הָשֵׁס, and גוּנִי.

> \[Str3465] † יָשָׁן **adj.** old (orig., as it seems, *withered, flabby* , like a lifeless plant with top hanging down, as if in sleep; cf. **Niph.** supr., and NH יָשַׁן Pi.*let a thing grow old* )—יָשָׁן [Lv 25:22](https://ref.ly/Lev25:22) \+ 3 t.; f. יָשָׁנָה [Is 22:11](https://ref.ly/Isa22:11) \+ 2 t.; pl. יְשָׁנִיס Ct [7:14](https://ref.ly/Song7:13); —*old:* הַבְּרֵכָה הַיְשָׁנָה [Is 22:11](https://ref.ly/Isa22:11); שַׁעַר הַיְשָׁנָה [Ne 3:6](https://ref.ly/Neh3:6); so [12:39](https://ref.ly/Neh12:39); of choice fruits, Ct [7:14](https://ref.ly/Song7:13) (opp. חדשׁ); subst. *old harvest, store,* [Lv 25:22](https://ref.ly/Lev25:22), [25:22](https://ref.ly/Lev25:22), [26:12](https://ref.ly/Lev26:12), [26:12](https://ref.ly/Lev26:12) (H; opp. חדשׁ).

> \[Str3466] † יְשָׁנָה **n.pr.loc.** town on southern border of N. Israel, near Bethel, [2 Ch 13:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:19); so rd. also [1 S 7:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:12) for MT הַשֵּׁן We Dr Klo Kit Bu; mod.*‘Ain Siniya*, 5 m. N. of Beitun, Cl. GanneauJ As Avr.\-Juin, 1870, 490–501 SocinZPV i, 1878, 41 BuhlGeogr. § 95, p. 173\.

> \[Str8142,8153] † שֵׁנַה, שֵׁנָא, שְׁנָת **n.f. sleep**—שֵׁנָה [Psalm 90:5](https://ref.ly/Ps90:5) \+ 3 t.; שֵׁנָא [Psalm 127:2](https://ref.ly/Ps127:2), שְׁנָת [Psalm 132:4](https://ref.ly/Ps132:4) (Ges§ 80 g. h.); cstr. שְׁנַת [Je 51:39](https://ref.ly/Jer51:39) \+ 3 t.; שְׁנָתִי [Gn 31:40](https://ref.ly/Gen31:40), [Je 31:26](https://ref.ly/Jer31:26), etc.; pl. abs. שֵׁנוֺת [Pr 6:10](https://ref.ly/Prov6:10), [24:33](https://ref.ly/Prov24:33) ;—*sleep* [Gn 28:16](https://ref.ly/Gen28:16) (J), Ju [16:14](https://ref.ly/Judg16:14), [16:20](https://ref.ly/Judg16:20), [Pr 3:24](https://ref.ly/Prov3:24), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Prov6:9), [20:13](https://ref.ly/Prov20:13), [Ec 5:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:11), [Je 31:26](https://ref.ly/Jer31:26), [Zc 4:1](https://ref.ly/Zech4:1); [Pr 6:4](https://ref.ly/Prov6:4) (‖תְּנוּמָה); מְעַט שֵׁנוֺת מְעַט תְּנוּמוֺת [Pr 6:10](https://ref.ly/Prov6:10) \= [24:33](https://ref.ly/Prov24:33) (these two of sluggard אִישׁ עָצֵל; v. also [6:9](https://ref.ly/Prov6:9), [20:13](https://ref.ly/Prov20:13)); note also וַתִּדּד שְׁנָתִי מֵעֵיָ\+ני [Gn 31:40](https://ref.ly/Gen31:40) (E) *and my sleep fled from mine eyes;* ָ\+נדֲדָה שְׁנַת הַמֶּלֶךְ [Est 6:1](https://ref.ly/Esth6:1); וְנִגְזְלָה שְׁנָתָם [Pr 4:16](https://ref.ly/Prov4:16); וּשְׁנָתוֺ נִהְיְתָה עָלָיו [Dn 2:1](https://ref.ly/Dan2:1); שֵׁנָה בְּעֵינָיו אֵינֵנּוּ רֹאֵה [Ec 8:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:16); יִתֵּן לִידִידוֺ שֵָׁ\+ני [Psalm 132:4](https://ref.ly/Ps132:4) (all of lack of sleep); יִתֵּן לִידִידוֺ שֵָׁ\+נא [Psalm 127:2](https://ref.ly/Ps127:2) \= *he giveth to his beloved in sleep,* cf. Ges§ 118, 3; of sleep of death (so Ar. وَسَنٌ Dozyii. 806\) [Jb 14:12](https://ref.ly/Job14:12), [Psalm 90:5](https://ref.ly/Ps90:5) (cf. De Che); so, as acc. cogn., [Psalm 76:6](https://ref.ly/Ps76:6), [Je 51:39](https://ref.ly/Jer51:39), [51:57](https://ref.ly/Jer51:57) (in these two שְׁנַת־עוֺלָם).

## יָשַׁע (id: BDB04238)

\[Str3467] † \[יָשַׁע] **vb. Hiph.** deliver; **Niph.** intrans. and pass. (Ar. وَسِعَ *be capacious,* II. *make wide, spacious,* IV. *make sufficient*, V. VIII. *be or live in abundance* (v. Dr:[1 S 14:45](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:45)) Ph. n.pr. ישע; Sab. יתַע (royal epith.), n.pr. אליתַע, יתַעאל, עמיתַע, e.g. MordtZMG 1876, 87; 1893, 409\. 416\. 417, etc. (yet note strange equiv., תַ \= س); not in Aram.; MI4 השעני *he delivered me,* l3 משע(ב) *deliverance,* Sm and So, (but במת ClGann Dr); n.pr.m. משע l1, also l3 \[4] Sm and So, but ישע *deliverance*, ClGann Dr)—**Niph.** *pf.* 3 m. נוֺשַׁע [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29), [Is 45:17](https://ref.ly/Isa45:17); 2 pl. נוֺשַׁעְתֶּם [Nu 10:9](https://ref.ly/Num10:9); 1 pl. נוֺשָׁעְנוּ [Je 8:20](https://ref.ly/Jer8:20); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִוָּשֵׁעַ [Je 30:7](https://ref.ly/Jer30:7), [Pr 28:18](https://ref.ly/Prov28:18) \+ [1 S 14:47](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:47) (for MT יַרְשִׁיעַ) LXX We Klo Dr Kit Bu; 3 fs. תִּוָּשַׁע [Je 23:6](https://ref.ly/Jer23:6), [33:16](https://ref.ly/Jer33:16); אִוָּשֵָׁעָה [Psalm 119:117](https://ref.ly/Ps119:117), [Je 17:14](https://ref.ly/Jer17:14) \+ 8 t. Impf.; *Imv.* pl. הִוָּֽשְׁעוּ [Is 45:22](https://ref.ly/Isa45:22); *Pt.* נוֺשָׁע [Psalm 33:16](https://ref.ly/Ps33:16), [Zc 9:9](https://ref.ly/Zech9:9); —**1\.** *be liberated, saved* (prop. *placed in freedom*; cf. for the fig. הִרְחִיב, מֶרְחָב), from external evils [Pr 28:18](https://ref.ly/Prov28:18), by God [Is 30:15](https://ref.ly/Isa30:15), [45:22](https://ref.ly/Isa45:22), [64:4](https://ref.ly/Isa64:4), [Je 4:14](https://ref.ly/Jer4:14), [8:20](https://ref.ly/Jer8:20), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Jer17:14), [23:6](https://ref.ly/Jer23:6), [33:16](https://ref.ly/Jer33:16), [Psalm 80:4](https://ref.ly/Ps80:4), [80:8](https://ref.ly/Ps80:8), [80:20](https://ref.ly/Ps80:20), [119:117](https://ref.ly/Ps119:117); with מִן, [Je 30:7](https://ref.ly/Jer30:7); מֵאֹיְבִים [Nu 10:9](https://ref.ly/Num10:9) (P), [2 S 22:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:4) \= [Psalm 18:4](https://ref.ly/Ps18:4). **2\.** *be saved* in battle, *victorious* [Zc 9:9](https://ref.ly/Zech9:9), [Psalm 33:16](https://ref.ly/Ps33:16) \+ [1 S 14:47](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:47) v. supr.; עַם נוֺשַׁע ביהוה [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29) *a people victorious in* ´י (poem), cf. [Is 45:17](https://ref.ly/Isa45:17). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הוֺשִׁעַ [1 S 9:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:16), [Psalm 20:7](https://ref.ly/Ps20:7); הוֺשִׁעַ [Zc 12:7](https://ref.ly/Zech12:7); sf. הוֺשִׁיעוֺ [Psalm 34:7](https://ref.ly/Ps34:7); 1 S. הוֺשַׁעְתִּי [Is 43:12](https://ref.ly/Isa43:12) \+ 4 t., \+ 14 t. Pf.; *Impf.* הוֺשִׁיעַ [1 S 17:47](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:47) (v. Dr) [Psalm 116:6](https://ref.ly/Ps116:6); יוֺשִׁיעַ [Is 45:20](https://ref.ly/Isa45:20) \+ 6 t.; וְישַׁע (juss.) [Pr 20:22](https://ref.ly/Prov20:22); וַיּוֺשַׁע [Ex 14:30](https://ref.ly/Exod14:30) \+ 7 t.; וַיּשַׁע [1 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:5) \+ 2 t.; sf. יוֺשִׁיעְֲ [Ho 13:10](https://ref.ly/Hos13:10); יוֺשִׁיעֶנּוּ [Is 46:7](https://ref.ly/Isa46:7); ישִׁעֵנוּ [1 S 10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27); ישַׁעֲכֶם [Is 35:4](https://ref.ly/Isa35:4) (juss., Dr§ 47\. n. 4\), etc.; *Imv.* הוֺשַׁע [Je 31:7](https://ref.ly/Jer31:7), [Psalm 86:2](https://ref.ly/Ps86:2); הוֺשִׁיעָה [2 K 6:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:26) \+ 8 t.; הוֺשִׁעָה [2 S 14:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:4) \+ 19 t. Imv.; *Inf. abs.* הוֺשֵׁעַ [1 S 25:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:26) \+ 2 t.; cstr. הוֺשִׁיעַ [1 S 14:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:6) \+ 14 t. \+ [2 S 3:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:18) (where rd. אוֺשִׁיעַ Vrss. Ke We Klo Dr Bu al.), \+ 7 t. Inf.; *Pt.* מוֺשִׁיעַ [Dt 22:27](https://ref.ly/Deut22:27) \+ 21 t.; sf. מוֺשִׁיעָם [Psalm 106:21](https://ref.ly/Ps106:21) \+ 8 t. sf.; pl. מוֺשִׁיעִים Ob:[21](https://ref.ly/Obad1:21) [Ne 9:27](https://ref.ly/Neh9:27); —**1\.** *deliver, save* (prop. *give width and breadth to, liberate*), in peril, c. acc. Pers. velloc. [Ex 2:17](https://ref.ly/Exod2:17) (E) Ju [6:31](https://ref.ly/Judg6:31), [1 S 23:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:2), [23:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:5), [2 S 10:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:19) \= [1 Ch 19:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:19), [2 K 6:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:27), [6:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:27), [Psalm 36:7](https://ref.ly/Ps36:7), [72:18](https://ref.ly/Ps72:18), [Jb 26:2](https://ref.ly/Job26:2); with לְ Pers. Specif. *save,* from evils and troubles : **a.** of heroic men, saving the nation in war Ju [3:31](https://ref.ly/Judg3:31), [6:15](https://ref.ly/Judg6:15), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Judg10:1), [1 S 10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27), [Ho 13:10](https://ref.ly/Hos13:10), [Je 14:9](https://ref.ly/Jer14:9); these are named מוֺשִׁיעַ *saviour* Ju [3:9](https://ref.ly/Judg3:9), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Judg3:15), [2 K 13:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:5), [Is 19:20](https://ref.ly/Isa19:20), Ob:[21](https://ref.ly/Obad1:21), [Ne 9:27](https://ref.ly/Neh9:27); Yahweh [1 Ch 18:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:6), with acc. v:[13](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:13); abs. *gain victory* [1 S 14:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:6), [17:47](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:47); וַיּוֺשַׁע תְּשׁוּעָה גְדוֺלָה [1 Ch 11:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:14).

> \[Str3468] † יֵ֫שַׁע **n.m.**:[Is 51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5) deliverance, rescue, salvation, also safety, welfare;—[Psalm 20:7](https://ref.ly/Ps20:7) \+ 4 t.; יֶ֫שַׁע [Jb 5:11](https://ref.ly/Job5:11) \+ 4 t.; sf. יִשְׁעִי [2 S 22:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:3) \+ 11 t., \+ 14 t. sfs.;—**1\.** *safety, welfare, prosperity* [2 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:5), [Psalm 12:6](https://ref.ly/Ps12:6), [Jb 5:4](https://ref.ly/Job5:4), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Job5:11). **2\.** *salvation,* i.e. primarily physical rescue, by God, oft. with added spiritual idea: [Is 62:11](https://ref.ly/Isa62:11), [Psalm 69:14](https://ref.ly/Ps69:14), [85:8](https://ref.ly/Ps85:8), [85:10](https://ref.ly/Ps85:10); יֵשַׁע אֱלֽהִים *salvation from God* [Psalm 50:23](https://ref.ly/Ps50:23); used as infin. with acc. לְיֵשַׁע Hb [3:13](https://ref.ly/Hab3:13), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Hab3:13) (see Ew§ 239 a); accordingly Yahweh is אוֺרִי וְיִשְׁעִי *my light and my salvation* [Psalm 27:1](https://ref.ly/Ps27:1); צוּר ישׁע [95:1](https://ref.ly/Ps95:1); קֶרֶן ישׁע [18:3](https://ref.ly/Ps18:3) \= [2 S 22:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:3); אֱלֽהֵי ישׁע [Is 17:10](https://ref.ly/Isa17:10), [Mi 7:7](https://ref.ly/Mic7:7), Hb [3:18](https://ref.ly/Hab3:18), [Psalm 18:47](https://ref.ly/Ps18:47) \= אֱלֽהֵי צוּר ישׁע [2 S 22:47](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:47), [Psalm 24:5](https://ref.ly/Ps24:5), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Ps25:5), [27:9](https://ref.ly/Ps27:9), [65:6](https://ref.ly/Ps65:6), [79:9](https://ref.ly/Ps79:9), [85:5](https://ref.ly/Ps85:5), [1 Ch 16:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:35); ´מָגֵן ישׁ [Psalm 18:36](https://ref.ly/Ps18:36) \= [2 S 22:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:36); על אלהים ישׁעי [Psalm 62:8](https://ref.ly/Ps62:8); ‖צְדָקָה [Is 45:8](https://ref.ly/Isa45:8), [51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5), [61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10); שְשוֺן יִשְׁעְֶָ [Psalm 51:14](https://ref.ly/Ps51:14) *joy of thy salvation* ´כֹּהֲנֶיהָ אַלְבִּישׁ ישׁ [132:16](https://ref.ly/Ps132:16) *her priests will clothe with salvation.* **3\.** *victory:* גְּבוּרוֺת יֵשַׁע יְמִינוֺ [Psalm 20:7](https://ref.ly/Ps20:7) *the mighty deeds of the victory of his right hand.*

with verbs of rejoicing בישׁועה [1 S 2:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:1), [Is 25:9](https://ref.ly/Isa25:9), [Psalm 9:15](https://ref.ly/Ps9:15), [13:6](https://ref.ly/Ps13:6), [35:9](https://ref.ly/Ps35:9); יְשׁוּעָתִי עַד־קְצֵה הָאָרֶץ [Is 49:6](https://ref.ly/Isa49:6) *my salvation unto the ends of the earth;* בְּכָלאגּוֺיִם יְשׁוּעָתְֶָ [Psalm 67:3](https://ref.ly/Ps67:3) *among all nations thy salvation.* **4\.** *victory:* c. עָשָה *work victory* [1 S 14:45](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:45), [Is 26:18](https://ref.ly/Isa26:18): elsewhere of victories wrought by Yahweh for his people [Ex 15:2](https://ref.ly/Exod15:2) (song) [Is 12:2](https://ref.ly/Isa12:2), Hb [3:8](https://ref.ly/Hab3:8), [Psalm 20:6](https://ref.ly/Ps20:6), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Ps21:2), [21:6](https://ref.ly/Ps21:6), [44:5](https://ref.ly/Ps44:5), [68:20](https://ref.ly/Ps68:20), [118:14](https://ref.ly/Ps118:14), [118:15](https://ref.ly/Ps118:15), [118:21](https://ref.ly/Ps118:21); > \[Str7771,7772] † I. שׁוֺעַ **adj. (free), idependent, noble** (in station) (acc. to most from second. √ שׁוע \= ישׁע in sense of Ar. وَسِع (cf. תְּשׁוּעָה infr.); but actual existence of such a √ not proven; Thes allows שׁוֺעַ \= יְשׁוֺעַ)—*noble,* of rank (and, by implic., of character) [Is 32:5](https://ref.ly/Isa32:5) (‖נָדִיב opp. כִּילַי), [Jb 34:19](https://ref.ly/Job34:19) (Di al. *rich,* but ‖שָרִים opp. דַּל). —

> II. שׁוֺעַ [Is 22:5](https://ref.ly/Isa22:5), v. sub \[שָׁוַע].

> III. שׁוֺעַ **n.pr.gent.** Ez [23:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:23), v. ´שׁ p. 1003

> \[Str7770] † I. שׁוּעַ **n.pr.m.** father of Judah's wife, LXX Σαυα, [Gn 38:2](https://ref.ly/Gen38:2) and (after cstr. בַּת) v:[12](https://ref.ly/Gen38:12), [1 Ch 2:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:3); v. בַּת־שׁוּע p. 124 supr., and cf. on mng. שׁוּעַ(אֲבִי) p. 4, שׁוּעַ(אֱלִי) p. 4:6\.

> \[Str7769] II. שׁוּעַ **n.\[m.]** opulence ? (cf. Ar. سَعَةٌ)—so AE al. [Jb 30:24](https://ref.ly/Job30:24), [36:19](https://ref.ly/Job36:19); but \< v. sub \[שָׁוַעַ] p. 1002

> \[Str7774] † שׁוּעָא **n.pr.f.** a woman of Asher [1 Ch 7:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:32); LXX Σωλα, LXXL Σουα.

> \[Str3469] † יִשְׁעִי **n.pr.m.** (*salutary*)—**1\.** one of the line of Jerahmeel [1 Ch 2:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:31), [2:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:31), LXX Ισεμιηλ, Α Ιεσει, LXXL Ιεσσαυει. **2\.** a chief of Manasseh [1 Ch 5:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:24), LXX Σεει, Ιες(ς)ει. **3\.** a chief of Judah [1 Ch 4:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:20). **4\.** a chief of Simeon [1 Ch 4:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:42).

> \[Str3470] יְשַׁעְיָ֫הוּ **n.pr.m.** (*salvation of Yah;* cf. אֱלִישָׁע p. 46 supr.; ישעאל on scarab C1GannJAs 1883, Fev.\-Mars, 135, No. 8\)—**1\.** Isaiah, son of ’Amôṣ, the prophet: [Is 1:1](https://ref.ly/Isa1:1) \+ 15 t. Is., [2 K 19:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:2) \+ 12 t. K., [2 Ch 26:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:22), [32:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:20), [32:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:32), LXX Ηξαιας, Vulgate Isaias. †**2\.** one of the children of Jeduthun [1 Ch 25:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:3), [25:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:15), LXX Ισαια, etc. †**3\.** a Levite ancestor of one of David's treasurers [1 Ch 26:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:25), LXX Ωσαιας, LXXL Ιωσηε.

> \[Str3470] † ישׁעיה **n.pr.m.** (*salvation of Yah*)—**1\.** grandson of Zerubbabel [1 Ch 3:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:21), LXX Ιασαβα, Ιεσεια. **2\.** chief of the sons of Elam, who went up with Ezra [Ezr 8:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:7), LXX Ιοσεια, Ησαια, etc. **3\.** chief of sons of Merari in time of Ezra [Ezr 8:19](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:19), LXX Ωσαια, etc. **4\.** a Benjamite [Ne 11:7](https://ref.ly/Neh11:7), LXX Ιεσια, etc.

> \[Str1954] † הוֺשֵׁעַ **n.pr.m.** (*salvation;* on form, cf. הַשְׁמֵד on pronunciation v. HptZA ii, 261 Anm. 2 JagerBAS i, 468\)—**1\.** orig. name of Joshua acc. to [Nu 13:8](https://ref.ly/Num13:8), [13:16](https://ref.ly/Num13:16) (P), LXX Αυση, cf. [Dt 32:44](https://ref.ly/Deut32:44) (J; prob. err., v. Dr), LXX Ιησους, v. יְהוֺשׁוּעַ supr. **2\.** last king of Israel [2 K 15:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:30), [17:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:1), [17:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:3), [17:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:4), [17:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:6), [18:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:1), [18:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:9), [18:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:10), LXX Ωσηε. **3\.** the prophet Hosea [Ho 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hos1:1), [1:2](https://ref.ly/Hos1:2), [1:2](https://ref.ly/Hos1:2), LXX Ωσηε. **4\.** an Ephraimite chief under David [1 Ch 27:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:20), LXX Ωση(ε). **5\.** a chief under Nehemiah [Ne 10:24](https://ref.ly/Neh10:24), LXX Ωσηθα, Ωσηε.

> \[Str1955] † הוֺשַׁעֲיָה **n.pr.m.** (*Yah has saved*)—**1\.** a prince of Judah [Ne 12:32](https://ref.ly/Neh12:32), LXX Ωσαια. **2\.** father of Azariah or Jezaniah, a chief in time of Jeremiah [Je 42:1](https://ref.ly/Jer42:1), [43:2](https://ref.ly/Jer43:2), LXX Μαας(ς)αιος, etc.

> \[Str091,3442] יְהוֺשֻׁעַ, יֵשׁוּעַ **n.pr.,** see p. 221\.

> \[Str4190] † \[מוֺשָׁעָה] **n.f.** only pl. מוֺשָׁעוֺת *saving acts* [Psalm 68:21](https://ref.ly/Ps68:21).

> \[Str4338] † מֵישַׁע **n.pr.m.** (*deliverance*)—Mesha, king of Moab [2 K 3:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:4) \= משע MI1; LXX Μωσα.—v. Sm and SoMI, 1886 DlSm lxxxv ff.

> \[Str4337] † מֵישָׁע **n.pr.m.** (*deliverance*)—son of Caleb [1 Ch 2:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:42), LXX Μαρεισα, SyrVerL Μουσα

> \[Str8668] † תְּשׁוּעָה († תְּשֻׁעָה [2 S 19:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:3)) **n.f.** deliverance, salvation \= יְשׁוּעָה (formed by false anal., as if from √ שׁוע, in sense of ישׁע; most assign it to שׁוע, but no sufficient evidence for existence of such a √; cf. Köii. 200\)—abs. ´ת [1 S 11:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:9) \+ 19 t.; cstr. [Je 3:23](https://ref.ly/Jer3:23) \+ 5 t.; sf. תְּשׁוּעָתִי [Is 46:13](https://ref.ly/Isa46:13) \+ 2 t.; תְּשׁוּעָֽתְ [Psalm 40:11](https://ref.ly/Ps40:11) \+ 2 t.; תְּשׁוּעָתְֶָ v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ps40:17), [71:15](https://ref.ly/Ps71:15); —**1\.** *deliverance,* usually by God, through human agency, esp. from oppression [1 S 11:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:9), and in battle [1 Ch 19:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:12) (הוֺשׁיע in ‖ cl.), \= *victory* Ju [15:18](https://ref.ly/Judg15:18) (´בְּיַד וגו ´נָתַתָּ ת), [2 K 5:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:1), ´י ´עשה ת [1 S 11:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:13) ´י *wrought a great victory,* so [19:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:5), [2 S 19:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:3), [23:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:10), [23:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:12), [1 Ch 11:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:14) (as acc. cogn. after הושׁיע), [Pr 21:31](https://ref.ly/Prov21:31), [Psalm 33:17](https://ref.ly/Ps33:17) (נושׁע in ‖ v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ps33:16)); למלכים ´הַגּוֺתֵן ת [144:10](https://ref.ly/Ps144:10); so in phr. ליהוה ´חֵץ־ת [2 K 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:17), בַּאֲרָם ´חֵץ־ת v:[17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:17); of national *deliverance,* from exile, עולמים ´נוֺשַׁע יִשְרָאֵל ת [Is 45:17](https://ref.ly/Isa45:17) (DaSynt.:§ 67 b), [46:13](https://ref.ly/Isa46:13) (‖צְדָקָה), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Isa46:13) (c. נתן; ‖תִּפְאֶרֶת); in gen. of national *success* and *prosperity* [Je 3:23](https://ref.ly/Jer3:23), [Pr 11:14](https://ref.ly/Prov11:14) \= [24:6](https://ref.ly/Prov24:6); of deliverance fr. personal trouble [Psalm 37:39](https://ref.ly/Ps37:39), [La 3:26](https://ref.ly/Lam3:26) (´תְּשׁוּעַי י) or of national deliverance under fig. of personal [Psalm 40:11](https://ref.ly/Ps40:11) (‖אֱמוּנָֽתְ), v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ps40:17), [71:15](https://ref.ly/Ps71:15) (‖צְדָקָח), so תְּשׁוּעָתִי [38:23](https://ref.ly/Ps38:22); contrasted with ´י's deliverance is תְּשׁוּעַת אָדָם *deliverance of (through) man* [Psalm 60:13](https://ref.ly/Ps60:13) \= [108:13](https://ref.ly/Ps108:13), cf. [146:3](https://ref.ly/Ps146:3). **2\.** more exclusively spiritual in sense, \= *salvation,* appear to be אֱלֽהֵי תְשׁוּעָתִי [Psalm 51:16](https://ref.ly/Ps51:16), [119:41](https://ref.ly/Ps119:41) (‖חֲסָדְֶ), v:[81](https://ref.ly/Ps119:81), and ´כֹּהֲנֶיְ יִלְבְּשׁוּ ת [2 Ch 6:41](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:41) (from כהניה אלברשׁ יֵשַׁע [Psalm 132:16](https://ref.ly/Ps132:16)).

> \[Str3471] † יָֽשְׁפֶה **n.\[m.]** jasper (loan\-word fr. Pers. يَشْم so Ar. يَشْبٌ Lane2978, also (and on forms يَشْفٌ, يَشْمٌ) Frey; perh. Sm).

## יָשַׁר (id: BDB04259)

\[Str3474] † יָשַׁר **vb. be smooth, straight, right** (NH *id.*, and deriv.; Ar. يَسَرَ *be gentle, tractable easy, II. make easy; يَسَرٌ manageable, easy,* but Sab. ותַר, הותַר, אהל יתַרן OsZMG 1865, 270 CISiv, 29, 2; As. Išâu, *be* or *go straight, right, mêšêru, justice, mêšêriš, righteously,* DlHWB 310\)—**Qal** *Pf.* יָשַׁר [Je 18:4](https://ref.ly/Jer18:4) \+ 2 t.; 3 f. יָשְֽׁרָה Ju [14:3](https://ref.ly/Judg14:3), Hb [2:4](https://ref.ly/Hab2:4); 3 mpl. יָֽשְׁרוּ [1 K 9:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:12); *Impf.* יִישַׁר [Nu 23:27](https://ref.ly/Num23:27) \+ 2 t.; יִשַׁר [1 S 18:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:20), [18:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:26); 3 fs. תִּישַׁר Ju [14:7](https://ref.ly/Judg14:7); 3 fpl. יִשַּׁרְנָה [1 S 6:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:12) (Ges§ 47, n. 3; 71\)—**1\.** *go straight,* בַּדֶּרֶח *in the way* [1 S 6:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:12). **2\.** fig., *be pleasing, agreeable, right* (בְּעֵינֵי *in the yees of*); said of a woman Ju [14:3](https://ref.ly/Judg14:3), [14:7](https://ref.ly/Judg14:7); a man [Je 27:5](https://ref.ly/Jer27:5); of matters and things (הַדָּבָר) [1 S 18:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:20), [18:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:26), [2 S 17:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:4), [1 Ch 13:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:4), [2 Ch 30:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:4); of cities [1 K 9:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:12); a place בְּעֵינֵי הָאֱלֽהִים [Nu 23:27](https://ref.ly/Num23:27) (E); with כַּאֲשֶׁר [Je 18:4](https://ref.ly/Jer18:4). **3\.** in ethical sense, *straightforward, upright;* יָֽשְׁרָה נַפְשׁוֺ בּוֺ Hb [2:4](https://ref.ly/Hab2:4) *his soul is not upright in him* (opp. עֻפְּלָה *swollen*). **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 ´יִשָּׁרְתִּ [Psalm 119:128](https://ref.ly/Ps119:128) (יִשְׁרֵהוּ [Jb 37:3](https://ref.ly/Job37:3), 3 m. sf. Thes; but \< Ew Di Hi De Da, *Impf.* fr. שָׁרָה); *Impf.* יְיַשֵּׁר [Pr 3:6](https://ref.ly/Prov3:6); יְיַשֶּׁ־ר [15:21](https://ref.ly/Prov15:21); sf. וַיְיַשְּׁרֵם (Mas. abbrev. וַיַּשְּׁרֵם) [2 Ch 32:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:30); 3 fs. תְּיַשֵּׁר [Pr 11:4](https://ref.ly/Prov11:4); 1 S. אֲיַשֵּׁר [Is 45:13](https://ref.ly/Isa45:13); אֲיַשֵּׁר v:[2](https://ref.ly/Isa45:2) Qr (Kt Hiph. אוֺשִׁר Ges§ 70, 2 doubtless copyist's error); *Imv.* pl. יַשְּׁרוּ [40:3](https://ref.ly/Isa40:3); *Pt.* pl. מְיַשְּׁרִים [Pr 9:15](https://ref.ly/Prov9:15); —**1\.** *make smooth, straight,* acc. מְסִלָּה [Is 40:3](https://ref.ly/Isa40:3); הֲדוּרִים [45:2](https://ref.ly/Isa45:2); fig., with דֶּרֶח *make smooth* or *straight,* i.e. free from obstacles, successful v:[13](https://ref.ly/Isa45:13), [Pr 11:5](https://ref.ly/Prov11:5), אֹרַח [3:5](https://ref.ly/Prov3:5); in ethical sense, הַמְיַשְּׁרִים אָרְחוֺתָם [9:15](https://ref.ly/Prov9:15) *those making straight their paths,* going straightforward on their paths; יְיַשֶּׁר־לָכֶת [15:21](https://ref.ly/Prov15:21) *maketh straight his going, goeth straightforwards.* **2\.** *lead straight along direct,* waters of an aqueduct [2 Ch 32:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:30); (of the thunder [Jb 37:3](https://ref.ly/Job37:3) acc. to some; but v. rather שָׁרָה) **3\.** *esteem right, approve* [Psalm 119:128](https://ref.ly/Ps119:128) (rd. כל פקודיך לי יִשָּׁרְתִּי). **Pu.** *Pt.* זָהָב מְיֻשָּׁר [1 K 6:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:35) *gold made level, laid smoothly out, upon the graven work.* **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 pl. עַפְעַפֶּיךָ יַיְשִׁרוּ נֶגְדְֶָּ [Pr 4:25](https://ref.ly/Prov4:25) *let thine eyelids look straight before thee; Imv.* הַיְ֯שַׁר דַּרְכְֶּ *make thy way even* [Psalm 5:9](https://ref.ly/Ps5:9) Qr (Kt הוֺשַׁר; v. Ges70 (2\)).

> \[Str3477] † יָשָׁר **adj. straight, right**;—´י [1 S 29:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:6) \+ 70 t.; cstr. יְשַׁר [Pr 29:27](https://ref.ly/Prov29:27); f. יְשָׁרָה Ez [1:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:7) \+ 4 t.; pl. יְשָׁרִים [Nu 23:10](https://ref.ly/Num23:10) \+ 31 t.; cstr. יִשְׁרֵי [Psalm 7:11](https://ref.ly/Ps7:11) \+ 8 t.; f. יְשָׁרוֺת Ez [1:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:23) (del. Co);—**1\.** *straight, level,* of a way [Is 26:7](https://ref.ly/Isa26:7), [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9), [Psalm 107:7](https://ref.ly/Ps107:7), [Ezr 8:21](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:21); foot Ez [1:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:7); wings v:[23](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:23) (? v. supr.) **2\.** *right, pleasing:* **a.** *to God,* הַיָּשָׁר בעיני *that which is right, pleasing in the eyes of, agreeable to* (either cstr. before ´י, or with sfs. referring to him), esp. in Deut. writers, [Ex 15:26](https://ref.ly/Exod15:26) (R), [Dt 12:25](https://ref.ly/Deut12:25), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Deut13:19), [21:9](https://ref.ly/Deut21:9), [1 K 11:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:33), [11:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:38), [14:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:8), [15:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:5), [15:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:11), [22:43](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:43) \= [2 Ch 20:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:32), [2 K 10:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:30), [12:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:3), [14:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:3), [15:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:3), [15:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:34), [16:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:2), [18:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:3) [22:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:3) \= [2 Ch 24:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:2), [25:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:2), [26:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:4), [27:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr27:2), [28:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:1), [29:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:2) [34:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:2), [Je 34:15](https://ref.ly/Jer34:15); ´הישׁר והטוב בעיני י [Dt 6:18](https://ref.ly/Deut6:18); ´הטוב והישׁר בעיני י [12:28](https://ref.ly/Deut12:28) [2 Ch 14:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:1); ´הטוב והישׁר והאמת לפני י [31:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:20). **b.** *to man,* ישׁר בעיני(ה) [Dt 12:8](https://ref.ly/Deut12:8), Ju [17:6](https://ref.ly/Judg17:6), [21:25](https://ref.ly/Judg21:25), [2 S 19:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:7), [Je 40:5](https://ref.ly/Jer40:5), [Pr 12:15](https://ref.ly/Prov12:15), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Prov21:2); ישׁר בעיוי(ה)טוב ו(ה) [Jos 9:25](https://ref.ly/Josh9:25) (D), [Je 26:14](https://ref.ly/Jer26:14), [40:4](https://ref.ly/Jer40:4); יֵשׁ דֶּרֶח יָשָׁר לִפְנֵי־אִישׁ [Pr 14:12](https://ref.ly/Prov14:12) *there is a way which is pleasing before a man* \= [16:25](https://ref.ly/Prov16:25). **3\.** *straightforward, just upright:* **a.** *of God,* צַדִּיק וְיָשָׁר הוא [Dt 32:4](https://ref.ly/Deut32:4) (song); ´טוב וישׁר י [Psalm 25:8](https://ref.ly/Ps25:8); ´ישׁר י [92:16](https://ref.ly/Ps92:15); his ways [Ho 14:10](https://ref.ly/Hos14:10); his משׁפטים [Ne 9:13](https://ref.ly/Neh9:13), [Psalm 119:137](https://ref.ly/Ps119:137); פִּקּוּדִים [19:9](https://ref.ly/Ps19:9); דָּבָר [33:4](https://ref.ly/Ps33:4); the words of divine wisdom [Pr 8:9](https://ref.ly/Prov8:9). **b.** *of man,* God made him *upright* [Ec 7:29](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:29); but יָשָׁר בָּאָדָם אָיִן [Mi 7:2](https://ref.ly/Mic7:2) *an upright man among men there is none* and yet Job is תָּם וְיָשָׁר [Jb 1:1](https://ref.ly/Job1:1) (v. Da), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Job1:8), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Job2:3), cf. [Psalm 37:37](https://ref.ly/Ps37:37); זַח וישׁר [Jb 8:6](https://ref.ly/Job8:6); so earlier of David as an uprightman [1 S 29:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:6); of man's doings ‖זַח [Pr 20:11](https://ref.ly/Prov20:11), cf. [21:8](https://ref.ly/Prov21:8); of his way of life ‖טובה [1 S 12:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:23); יְשַׁר־דֶּרֶח [Pr 29:27](https://ref.ly/Prov29:27); יִשְׁרֵי־דֶרֶדִּ [Psalm 37:14](https://ref.ly/Ps37:14); of his heart, mind, and will, יִשְׁרֵי לֵבָב *upright of heart* [2 Ch 29:34](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:34); יִשְׁרֵי־לֵב [Psalm 7:11](https://ref.ly/Ps7:11), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Ps11:2), [32:11](https://ref.ly/Ps32:11), [36:11](https://ref.ly/Ps36:11), [64:11](https://ref.ly/Ps64:11), [94:15](https://ref.ly/Ps94:15), [97:11](https://ref.ly/Ps97:11); יְשָׁרִים בְּלִבּוֺתָם [125:4](https://ref.ly/Ps125:4). **c.** as a noun, (1\) with ref. to things, יָשָׁר הֶעֱוֵיתִי *the right I have perverted* [Jb 33:27](https://ref.ly/Job33:27); הַיְשָׁרָה יְעַקֵָּשׁוּ [Mi 3:9](https://ref.ly/Mic3:9) *pervert the right* (lit. *twist* that which is *straight*); דֹּבֵר יְשָׁרִים *speaketh right things* [Pr 16:13](https://ref.ly/Prov16:13), cf. [2 K 10:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:15). (2\) more commonly of men, in sg. יָשָׁר(ה) [Mi 2:7](https://ref.ly/Mic2:7), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Mic7:4), [2 K 10:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:3), [Pr 21:29](https://ref.ly/Prov21:29), [Jb 23:7](https://ref.ly/Job23:7); collective, [Psalm 11:7](https://ref.ly/Ps11:7); also in סֵפֶר הַיָּשָׁר *book of the upright* [Jos 10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13), [2 S 1:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:18) (cf. [1 K 8:53](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:53) LXX; DrIntr.. 182\), a collection of ancient national poetry; in pl. יְשָׁרִים *the upright,* of pious Israel [Nu 23:10](https://ref.ly/Num23:10) (song E); elsewhere of the upright among the people of God as distinguished from the wicked, in WisdLt. [Jb 4:7](https://ref.ly/Job4:7), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Job17:8), [Pr 2:7](https://ref.ly/Prov2:7), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Prov2:21), [3:32](https://ref.ly/Prov3:32), [11:3](https://ref.ly/Prov11:3), [11:6](https://ref.ly/Prov11:6), [11:11](https://ref.ly/Prov11:11), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Prov12:6), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Prov14:9), [14:11](https://ref.ly/Prov14:11), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Prov15:8), [15:19](https://ref.ly/Prov15:19), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Prov16:17), [21:18](https://ref.ly/Prov21:18), [28:10](https://ref.ly/Prov28:10), [29:10](https://ref.ly/Prov29:10), in late PsalmPsalm [33:1](https://ref.ly/Ps33:1), [49:15](https://ref.ly/Ps49:15), [107:42](https://ref.ly/Ps107:42), [111:1](https://ref.ly/Ps111:1), [112:2](https://ref.ly/Ps112:2), [112:4](https://ref.ly/Ps112:4), [140:14](https://ref.ly/Ps140:13) and [Dn 11:17](https://ref.ly/Dan11:17). **4\.** abstr., *uprightness,* בֶּאֱמֶת וְיָשָׁר [Psalm 111:8](https://ref.ly/Ps111:8) (Thes), but read rather ישֶׁר with Hi Ri Bae, after LXX SyrVer Targum Jer.

> \[Str3476] † י֫שֶׁר **n.m.**:[Psalm 25:21](https://ref.ly/Ps25:21) straightness, uprightness;—ישֶׁר [Pr 2:13](https://ref.ly/Prov2:13) \+ 11 t.; sf. יָשְׁרוֺ [Jb 33:23](https://ref.ly/Job33:23), [Pr 14:2](https://ref.ly/Prov14:2); —**1\.** *straightness, evenness,* of paths (with moral implication) [Pr 2:13](https://ref.ly/Prov2:13), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Prov4:11). **2\.** *rightness, uprightness* [Pr 17:26](https://ref.ly/Prov17:26); תֹּם וָישֶׁר [Psalm 25:21](https://ref.ly/Ps25:21); of words, spoken [Jb 6:25](https://ref.ly/Job6:25), written [Ec 12:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:10); of the moral walk (ו)בישׁר [1 K 9:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:4), [Pr 14:2](https://ref.ly/Prov14:2); ישֶׁר־לִבִּי [Jb 33:3](https://ref.ly/Job33:3); ישׁר לבב [Dt 9:5](https://ref.ly/Deut9:5), [Psalm 119:7](https://ref.ly/Ps119:7), [1 Ch 29:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:17):—on [Psalm 111:8](https://ref.ly/Ps111:8) v. יָשָׁר **4\. 3\.** *what is due, right* [Jb 33:23](https://ref.ly/Job33:23), [Pr 11:24](https://ref.ly/Prov11:24).

> \[Str3475] † יֶ֫שֶׁר **n.pr.m.** (*uprightness*) son of Caleb [1 Ch 2:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:18) (on pointing v. Baer), LXX Ιωασαρ, LXXL Σαρ.

> \[Str3483] † \[יְשָׁרָה] **n.f.** uprightness;—בְּיִשְׁרַת לֵבָב [1 K 3:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:6) (for form see Bö§ 644 a Kö§ 90 (ii, 170\)).

> \[Str3484] † יְשֻׁרוּן **n.pr.m.** (*upright one*) [Dt 32:15](https://ref.ly/Deut32:15), [33:5](https://ref.ly/Deut33:5), [33:26](https://ref.ly/Deut33:26), [Is 44:2](https://ref.ly/Isa44:2); poetic name of Israel, designating it under its *ideal* character, LXX ὁ ἠγαπημένος, Aq Sym Theod εὐθύς, Vulgate *rectissimus, dilectus;* Thes Ew§ 167 a LagBN 33 regard it as dimin. fr. יָשׁוּר \= *good little people;* but no evidence that זּן\- has a dimin. force, and most recent scholars De Di Dr MV Ges§ 86 (2\). 4 take as denom. \= *Rechtvolk;* BacherZAW v, 1885, 161 ff. and Che rightly compare סֵפֶר הַיָּשָׁר (see יָשָׁר **3 e**).

> \[Str4339] † \[מֵישָׁר] **n.m.** evenness, uprightness, equity;—only pl.: מֵשָׁרִים [Pr 1:3](https://ref.ly/Prov1:3); elsewhere מֵישָׁרִים [Is 26:7](https://ref.ly/Isa26:7) \+ 17 t.;—**1\.** *evenness, level* [Is 26:7](https://ref.ly/Isa26:7), of path of righteous (in the future), fig. for free from difficulties; *smoothness,* of the flow of wine, ´בְּמ [Pr 23:31](https://ref.ly/Prov23:31); ´למ Ct [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10). **2\.** in ethical sense, *uprightness, equity,* as taught in the school of wisdom [Pr 8:6](https://ref.ly/Prov8:6), ‖צֶדֶק [1:3](https://ref.ly/Prov1:3), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Prov2:9); of government ´מ(ב) [Psalm 9:9](https://ref.ly/Ps9:9), [58:2](https://ref.ly/Ps58:2), [75:3](https://ref.ly/Ps75:3), [96:10](https://ref.ly/Ps96:10), [99:4](https://ref.ly/Ps99:4); of speech [Is 33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15), [Pr 23:16](https://ref.ly/Prov23:16); of Yahweh's promises [Is 45:19](https://ref.ly/Isa45:19); ´רצה מ [1 Ch 29:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:17) *have pleasure in equity;* ´עשה מ [Dn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Dan11:6) *make an equitable arrangement.* **3\.** adv. *rightly,* with חזה [Psalm 17:2](https://ref.ly/Ps17:2); אהב Ct [1:4](https://ref.ly/Song1:4).

Joppa northward, noted for fertility, [Is 33:9](https://ref.ly/Isa33:9) (‖בָּשָׁן, כַּרְמֶל; LXX ὁ Σαρων), cf. הֲדַר כַּרְמֶל וְהַשָּׁרוֺן [35:2](https://ref.ly/Isa35:2); [65:10](https://ref.ly/Isa65:10) (‖עֵמֶק עָכוֺר; LXX τῷ δρυμῷ); ´חֲבַצֶּלֶת הַשּׁ Ct [2:1](https://ref.ly/Song2:1) (LXX ἄνθος τοῦ πεδίου); ´הַבָּקָר הָרֹעִים בַּשּׁ [1 Ch 27:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:29); so also [Jos 12:18](https://ref.ly/Josh12:18); where rd. ´מֶלֶח אֲפֵק לַשּׁ, so Di WeSam 55 Ges12 after LXX, cf. RSOTJC 2, 273, 435\.—On this plain v. GASmGeogr. 147 ff. ## ישׁשׁ (id: BDB04268)

ישׁשׁ (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. وَثْوَاثٌ *weak* or *impotent* man, وَثْوَثَةٌ *weakness, impotence,* Lane2919).

450:a. Bef. ישׁשׁ ins. ‘שָׁרוֺנִי **adj. gent.** of foregoing, [1 Ch 27:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:29).’

> \[Str3486] † יָשֵׁשׁ **adj. aged, decrepit**, only זָקֵן וְיָשֵׁשׁ [2 Ch 36:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:17).

[Jb 32:6](https://ref.ly/Job32:6) (opp. צָעִיר לְיָמִים); as subst. [15:10](https://ref.ly/Job15:10) (‖שָב); [29:8](https://ref.ly/Job29:8) (opp. נערים); בִּישִׁישִׁים חָכְמָה [12:12](https://ref.ly/Job12:12) *among aged men is wisdom* (‖אֹרֶח יָמִים תְּבוּנָה).

יתד (√ of foll.; mng. dub.; Ar. وَتَدَ *drive in peg, be firm* (of peg), is appar. denom.)

Ges§ 127, R. 4 a); pl. יְתֵדֹת [Ex 38:20](https://ref.ly/Exod38:20); cstr. יִתְדֹת [27:19](https://ref.ly/Exod27:19) \+ 4 t.; sf. וִיתֵידֹתַיִח [Is 54:2](https://ref.ly/Isa54:2); יְתֵדֹתָיו [Ex 27:19](https://ref.ly/Exod27:19), [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20); וִיתֵדֹתֶיהָ [Ex 39:40](https://ref.ly/Exod39:40); וִיתֵדֹתָם [Nu 3:37](https://ref.ly/Num3:37), [4:32](https://ref.ly/Num4:32); —*pin, peg,* esp. **a.** *tent\-pin* Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26), [4:21](https://ref.ly/Judg4:21); יְתַד הָאֹהֶל v:[22](https://ref.ly/Judg4:22); rd. perh. also [Jb 4:21](https://ref.ly/Job4:21) (fig.; cf. II. יֶתֶר); oft. of tabernacle [Ex 27:19](https://ref.ly/Exod27:19), [27:19](https://ref.ly/Exod27:19), [35:18](https://ref.ly/Exod35:18), [35:18](https://ref.ly/Exod35:18), [38:20](https://ref.ly/Exod38:20), [38:31](https://ref.ly/Exod38:31), [38:21](https://ref.ly/Exod38:21); ‖מֵיתָרָיו [39:40](https://ref.ly/Exod39:40), cf. [Nu 3:37](https://ref.ly/Num3:37), [4:32](https://ref.ly/Num4:32) (all P); of Zion under fig. of tent [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20), [54:2](https://ref.ly/Isa54:2); fig. of ruler as support of state [Zc 10:4](https://ref.ly/Zech10:4). **b.** a peg for hanging Ez [15:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:3); so fig. of Eliakim [Is 22:23](https://ref.ly/Isa22:23), [22:24](https://ref.ly/Isa22:24); of secure position [Ezr 9:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:8) (cf. Ar., v. Thes Hi Hariri361 Der., ثابت الأَوْتَادِ of a king). **c.** for digging [Dt 23:14](https://ref.ly/Deut23:14) (\= spade). **d.** the *pin* or *stick* used in beating up the woof in the loom Ju [16:14](https://ref.ly/Judg16:14) a, vid. GFMPAOS, Oct. 1889, clxxvi. ff.; in היתד הּארג vb, del היתד (Idib and in comm. ad loc.)

Dr); as subst. in נַהֲרוֺת אֵיתָּן [Psalm 74:15](https://ref.ly/Ps74:15) *rivers of steady flow;* as subst. also [Ex 14:27](https://ref.ly/Exod14:27) (J), the sea returned לְאֵיתָנוֺ to its *steady flow,* יֶרַת הָאֵיתָנִים [1 K 8:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:2) month of *steady flowings* \= 7th mo., Oct.–Nov. (post\-Bibl. Very uncertain is [Pr 13:15](https://ref.ly/Prov13:15) דֶּרֶח בִּגְדִים אֵיתָן, perh. *firm, hard, rugged* (Ew De Hi Now: acc. to Str. the text is corrupt).

> \[Str3492] † יַתִּיר, יַתִּר **n.pr.loc.** town in Judah (√ dub.);—יַתִּיר [Jos 15:48](https://ref.ly/Josh15:48), LXX Ι(ε)θερ; elsewh. יַתִּר, Levitical city [Jos 21:14](https://ref.ly/Josh21:14) (both P), LXX Αιλωμ, A LXXL Ιεθερ, [1 Ch 6:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:42), LXX \[v:[43](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:43)] Ιεθθαρ, A Ιεθερ; prob. \= יַתִּר [1 S 30:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:27), LXX Γεθθορ, A Ειεθερ;—acc, to Onom. \= Ιεθειρα, village 20 miles from Eleuthropolis, LagOnom. 266, 133; ed. 2, p. 268; identified by RobBR. i, 494 (who, however, questions ’ (ע) \= י) BDPal. 3, 153 with *’ Attîr*, halfway between Hebron and Milḥ; so, ‘perh.,’ BuhlGeogr. § 91, p. 164; this not certain, cf. Di.

> \[Str3507] † יִתְרְעָם **n.m.** 6th son of David [2 S 3:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:5) \= [1 Ch 3:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:3), LXX Ιθ(α)ραμ, Ιεθρααμ, etc.

> \[Str3509] † יְתֵת **n.pr.m.** an Edomite chief [Gn 36:40](https://ref.ly/Gen36:40), LXX Ιεβερ \= [1 Ch 1:51](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:51), LXX Ιεθετ; LXXL in both Ιεθερ.
